        <script>
        "use strict";
        
        const ENC_KEY='juju.enc.v1';
        const ENC_ITER=600000; 
        const PENDING_KEY='juju.pending';
        const VERS_KEY='juju.versions';
        const VERS_ENC_KEY='juju.versions.enc';
        const UI_ENC_KEY='juju.ui.enc';
        
        var IS_DESKTOP=false;
        function detectDesktop(){
          try{return !!(window.__TAURI__&&window.__TAURI__.core&&typeof window.__TAURI__.core.invoke==='function');}catch(e){return false;}
        }
        function desktopInvoke(cmd,args){
          if(!IS_DESKTOP)return Promise.reject(new Error('闈炴闈㈢幆澧?));
          return window.__TAURI__.core.invoke(cmd,args||{});
        }
        function bufToB64(buf){const bytes=new Uint8Array(buf);let bin='';for(let i=0;i<bytes.length;i++)bin+=String.fromCharCode(bytes[i]);return btoa(bin);}
        function b64ToBuf(b64){const bin=atob(b64);const bytes=new Uint8Array(bin.length);for(let i=0;i<bin.length;i++)bytes[i]=bin.charCodeAt(i);return bytes.buffer;}
        let _encSalt=null,_encSaltPwd=null;
        let _encKey=null,_encKeySig=null;
        async function deriveKey(password,salt,iter){
          const sig=password+'|'+(salt?bufToB64(salt):'')+'|'+iter;
          if(_encKey&&_encKeySig===sig)return _encKey;
          const enc=new TextEncoder();
          const base=await crypto.subtle.importKey('raw',enc.encode(password),'PBKDF2',false,['deriveKey']);
          const key=await crypto.subtle.deriveKey({name:'PBKDF2',salt,iterations:iter,hash:'SHA-256'},base,{name:'AES-GCM',length:256},false,['encrypt','decrypt']);
          _encKey=key;_encKeySig=sig;
          return key;
        }
        function _getOrCreateSalt(password){
          if(_encSalt&&_encSaltPwd===password)return _encSalt;
          _encSalt=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
          _encSaltPwd=password;
          _encKey=null;_encKeySig=null;
          return _encSalt;
        }
        async function encryptState(data,password){
          const salt=_getOrCreateSalt(password);
          const iv=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
          const key=await deriveKey(password,salt,ENC_ITER);
          const plain=new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(data));
          const cipher=await crypto.subtle.encrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv},key,plain);
          return {v:1,salt:bufToB64(salt),iv:bufToB64(iv),cipher:bufToB64(cipher),iter:ENC_ITER,updatedAt:Date.now()};
        }
        async function decryptState(payload,password){
          const salt=b64ToBuf(payload.salt);
          const iv=b64ToBuf(payload.iv);
          const key=await deriveKey(password,salt,payload.iter||ENC_ITER);
          
          _encSalt=salt;_encSaltPwd=password;
          const plain=await crypto.subtle.decrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv},key,b64ToBuf(payload.cipher));
          return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(plain));
        }
        function hasEncryptedStore(){try{return !!localStorage.getItem(ENC_KEY);}catch(e){return false;}}
        function readEncryptedStore(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ENC_KEY)||'null');}catch(e){return null;}}
        async function writeEncryptedStore(payload,silent){
          try{
            const json=JSON.stringify(payload);
            if(json.length>4500000){if(!silent)toast('瀛樺偍绌洪棿涓嶈冻锛岃瀵煎嚭澶囦唤');return false;}
            if(IS_DESKTOP){
              
              const ok=await desktopInvoke('save_state',{payload:JSON.stringify({encStore:json})}).catch(()=>{if(!silent)toast('妗岄潰鍔犲瘑瀛樺偍澶辫触');return false;});
              if(ok){try{localStorage.removeItem(ENC_KEY);}catch(e){}return true;}
              return false;
            }
            localStorage.setItem(ENC_KEY,json);
            return true;
          }catch(e){
            if(!silent)toast('瀛樺偍澶辫触锛?+(e.name==='QuotaExceededError'?'绌洪棿涓嶈冻':'鏈煡閿欒'));
            return false;
          }
        }
        function clearEncryptedStore(opts){
          const purgeVersions=!!(opts&&opts.purgeVersions===true);
          try{
            localStorage.removeItem(ENC_KEY);
            localStorage.removeItem(PENDING_KEY);
            localStorage.removeItem(VERS_ENC_KEY);
            localStorage.removeItem(VERS_KEY);
            localStorage.removeItem(UI_ENC_KEY);
          }catch(e){}
          if(IS_DESKTOP){try{desktopInvoke('clear_encrypted',{}).catch(()=>{});}catch(e){}}
          if(purgeVersions){try{ VDB.destroy(); }catch(e){}}
        }
        
        const $=(s,r=document)=>r.querySelector(s);
        const $$=(s,r=document)=>Array.from(r.querySelectorAll(s));
        const uid=()=>Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,9)+Date.now().toString(36).slice(-4);
        const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
        const stripHtml=s=>String(s||'').replace(/<[^>]*>/g,'');
        const pad=n=>n<10?'0'+n:''+n;
        const ymd=d=>d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate());
        function parseYmd(s){
          const m=/^(\d{4})-(\d{1,2})-(\d{1,2})$/.exec(String(s||''));
          if(!m)return new Date(NaN);
          const y=+m[1],mo=+m[2],d=+m[3];
          if(mo<1||mo>12||d<1||d>31)return new Date(NaN);
          
          const dt=new Date(y,mo-1,d);
          if(dt.getFullYear()!==y||dt.getMonth()!==mo-1||dt.getDate()!==d)return new Date(NaN);
          return dt;
        }
        const todayStr=()=>ymd(new Date());
        const clone=(typeof structuredClone==='function')?(o=>structuredClone(o)):(o=>JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o)));
        
        function safeClone(o){
          try{return clone(o);}
          catch(e){
            console.error('[JU] clone failed, falling back to JSON',e);
            try{return JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(o));}
            catch(e2){console.error('[JU] JSON clone also failed',e2);return o;}
          }
        }
        
        function copyPlain(src){
          const tgt={};
          for(const key of Reflect.ownKeys(src)){
            Object.defineProperty(tgt,key,{value:src[key],writable:true,enumerable:true,configurable:true});
          }
          return tgt;
        }
        
        function __juProduce(base, recipe){
          if(typeof recipe!=='function')throw new TypeError('__juProduce: recipe 蹇呴』鏄嚱鏁?);
          if(base==null||typeof base!=='object'){
            const ret=recipe(base);
            return ret===undefined?base:ret;
          }
          if(base instanceof Date||base instanceof Map||base instanceof Set||base instanceof RegExp){
            const draft=safeClone(base);
            const ret=recipe(draft);
            return ret===undefined?draft:ret;
          }
          const recMap=new WeakMap();
          function getRec(obj){
            let rec=recMap.get(obj);
            if(!rec){
              rec={base:obj,copy:null,modified:false,deleted:null,parent:null,proxy:null,aliased:false};
              recMap.set(obj,rec);
            }
            return rec;
          }
          function markModified(rec){
            let cur=rec;
            while(cur&&!cur.modified){
              cur.modified=true;
              cur=cur.parent;
            }
          }
          function ensureCopy(rec){
            if(!rec.copy)rec.copy=Array.isArray(rec.base)?rec.base.slice():copyPlain(rec.base);
            return rec.copy;
          }
          function wrap(rec){
            if(rec.proxy)return rec.proxy;
            rec.proxy=new Proxy(rec.base,{
              get(target,prop){
                if(prop==='__isJuDraft')return true;
                if(prop==='__juBase')return target;
                
                if(rec.deleted&&rec.deleted.has(prop))return undefined;
                let src=target;
                if(rec.copy&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec.copy,prop))src=rec.copy;
                const value=src[prop];
                if(value&&typeof value==='object'){
                  const childRec=getRec(value);
                  
                  if(childRec.parent&&childRec.parent!==rec){
                    childRec.aliased=true;
                    if(typeof console!=='undefined'&&console.warn){
                      console.warn('[JU] 鍚屼竴瀵硅薄鍑虹幇鍦ㄥ涓埗鑺傜偣涓嬶紝缁撴瀯鍏变韩璇箟涓嶄繚璇?,value);
                    }
                  }
                  childRec.parent=rec;
                  return wrap(childRec);
                }
                return value;
              },
              set(target,prop,value){
                
                if(rec.deleted&&rec.deleted.has(prop))rec.deleted.delete(prop);
                const copy=ensureCopy(rec);
                const isArray=Array.isArray(copy);
                const oldLen=isArray?copy.length:-1;
                const existing=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(copy,prop);
                if(existing){
                  if(existing.get||existing.set){
                    
                    copy[prop]=value;
                  }else{
                    
                    Object.defineProperty(copy,prop,{...existing,value});
                  }
                }else{
                  
                  Object.defineProperty(copy,prop,{value,writable:true,enumerable:true,configurable:true});
                }
                
                if(isArray&&prop==='length'&&typeof value==='number'&&value<oldLen){
                  if(!rec.deleted)rec.deleted=new Set();
                  for(let i=value;i<oldLen;i++)rec.deleted.add(String(i));
                }
                markModified(rec);
                return true;
              },
              deleteProperty(target,prop){
                const copy=ensureCopy(rec);
                delete copy[prop];
                if(!rec.deleted)rec.deleted=new Set();
                rec.deleted.add(prop);
                markModified(rec);
                return true;
              },
              has(target,prop){
                
                if(rec.deleted&&rec.deleted.has(prop))return false;
                if(rec.copy&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec.copy,prop))return true;
                return Reflect.has(target,prop);
              },
              ownKeys(target){
                const keys=new Set(Reflect.ownKeys(target));
                if(rec.copy)Reflect.ownKeys(rec.copy).forEach(k=>keys.add(k));
                
                if(rec.deleted)rec.deleted.forEach(k=>keys.delete(k));
                return Array.from(keys);
              },
              getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target,prop){
                
                if(rec.deleted&&rec.deleted.has(prop))return undefined;
                if(rec.copy&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(rec.copy,prop)){
                  
                  const d=Object.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(rec.copy,prop);
                  if(d)return d;
                }
                return Reflect.getOwnPropertyDescriptor(target,prop);
              },
              defineProperty(target,prop,desc){
                
                if(rec.deleted&&rec.deleted.has(prop))rec.deleted.delete(prop);
                const copy=ensureCopy(rec);
                Object.defineProperty(copy,prop,desc);
                markModified(rec);
                return true;
              }
            });
            return rec.proxy;
          }
          const rootRec=getRec(base);
          const draft=wrap(rootRec);
          const ret=recipe(draft);
          if(ret!==undefined)return ret;
          const done=new WeakMap();
          function finalize(rec){
            if(!rec.modified)return rec.base;
            if(done.has(rec))return done.get(rec);
            const copy=rec.copy||(Array.isArray(rec.base)?rec.base.slice():copyPlain(rec.base));
            done.set(rec,copy);
            for(const key of Reflect.ownKeys(copy)){
              const val=copy[key];
              if(val&&typeof val==='object'){
                const childRec=recMap.get(val);
                if(childRec)copy[key]=finalize(childRec);
              }
            }
            return copy;
          }
          return finalize(rootRec);
        }
        
        function __juProduceSafe(base,recipe){
          try{
            return{ok:true,next:__juProduce(base,recipe)};
          }catch(e){
            return{ok:false,base,error:e};
          }
        }
        function isBlankNode(n){
          if(!n)return false;
          if(stripHtml(n.text||'').trim()!=='')return false;
          if((n.children||[]).length>0)return false;
          return true;
        }
        const VIEW_KIND={LIBRARY_ROOT:'library-root',FOLDER:'folder',CALENDAR:'calendar',DATABASE:'database',WORD:'word',OUTLINE:'outline'};
        function viewOf(node){
          if(!node||node.id===state.rootId)return VIEW_KIND.LIBRARY_ROOT;
          if(node._isCalRoot)return VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR;
          if(node._isDbRoot)return VIEW_KIND.FOLDER;
          if(node.dbRoot)return VIEW_KIND.DATABASE;
          if(node._isFolder)return VIEW_KIND.FOLDER;
          if(node._isWord)return VIEW_KIND.WORD;
          return VIEW_KIND.OUTLINE;
        }
        
        const _execWarnAtMap=new Map(); 
        function _warnExecFail(cmd,quiet){
          
          const _now=Date.now();
          if(quiet){
            try{console.warn('[JU] execCommand 闈欓粯澶辫触锛堟棤瀹筹級:',cmd);}catch(e){}
            return;
          }
          const _last=_execWarnAtMap.get(cmd)||0;
          if(_now-_last>30000){
            _execWarnAtMap.set(cmd,_now);
            try{console.warn('[JU] execCommand 鎵ц澶辫触锛堝懡浠ゅ凡搴熷純鎴栧綋鍓嶅涓讳笉鏀寔锛?',cmd);}catch(e){}

          }
        }
        function safeExec(cmd,val){
          try{
            if(typeof document.execCommand!=='function'){_warnExecFail(cmd,true);return false;}
            const _ok=document.execCommand(cmd,false,val!=null?val:null);
            if(!_ok)_warnExecFail(cmd,cmd==='styleWithCSS'||cmd==='copy'||cmd==='cut');
            return _ok;
          }catch(e){_warnExecFail(cmd,cmd==='styleWithCSS'||cmd==='copy'||cmd==='cut');return false;}
        }
        const SAFE_TAGS=new Set(['B','I','U','S','STRONG','EM','BR','SPAN','FONT','DIV','P','H1','H2','H3','H4','H5','H6','UL','OL','LI','BLOCKQUOTE','CODE','PRE','TABLE','THEAD','TBODY','TR','TH','TD','A','IMG','MARK','SUB','SUP','HR','DEL','INS','SMALL']);
        const SAFE_ATTRS={'A':['href','title','target','rel'],'IMG':['src','alt','title','width','height'],'FONT':['color','size','face'],'SPAN':['style'],'DIV':['style'],'P':['style'],'*':['style']};
        
        const CSS_WHITELIST=new Set(['color','background-color','font-size','font-weight','font-style','text-decoration','text-align','line-height','border','border-color','border-width','border-style','margin','margin-top','margin-right','margin-bottom','margin-left','padding','padding-top','padding-right','padding-bottom','padding-left','width','max-width','height','max-height','border-radius','opacity']);
        function sanitizeStyle(raw){
          let s=String(raw||'')
            .replace(/expression\s*\(/gi,'')
            .replace(/behavior\s*:/gi,'')
            .replace(/-moz-binding\s*:/gi,'')
            .replace(/url\s*\(\s*['"]?\s*(?:javascript|vbscript)\s*:/gi,'url(blocked:')
            .replace(/url\s*\(\s*['"]?\s*data\s*:(?!image\/(?:png|jpe?g|gif|webp))/gi,'url(blocked:');
          const kept=[];
          const re=/([\w-]+)\s*:\s*([^;]+)/g;let m;
          while((m=re.exec(s))){
            const prop=m[1].toLowerCase().trim();
            const val=m[2].trim();
            if(!CSS_WHITELIST.has(prop))continue;
            if(/url\s*\(|expression|javascript|vbscript|behavior/gi.test(val))continue;
            if(prop==='opacity'){
              const num=parseFloat(val);
              if(isNaN(num)||num<0||num>1)continue;
              kept.push('opacity:'+num);
              continue;
            }
            
            if(/^(font-size|width|height|margin|padding|border-width|line-height|border-radius|letter-spacing)/i.test(prop)){
              const m=val.match(/(-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/);
              if(m){
                const num=parseFloat(m[1]);
                const cap=prop.toLowerCase()==='font-size'?200:10000;
                if(!isNaN(num)&&num>cap)val=val.replace(m[1],String(cap));
              }
            }
            kept.push(prop+':'+val);
          }
          return kept.join(';');
        }
        function sanitizeHtml(html){
          if(!html)return '';
          if(!/<[a-z!/]/i.test(html))return html;
          try{
            const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<div>'+html+'</div>','text/html');
            const root=doc.body.firstChild;
            if(!root)return '';
            (function walk(node){
              Array.from(node.childNodes).forEach(ch=>{
                if(ch.nodeType===1){
                  const tag=ch.tagName.toUpperCase();
                  if(!SAFE_TAGS.has(tag)){ch.parentNode.replaceChild(doc.createTextNode(ch.textContent||''),ch);return;}
                  const allowed=(SAFE_ATTRS[tag]||[]).concat(SAFE_ATTRS['*']||[]);
                  Array.from(ch.attributes).forEach(at=>{
                    const name=at.name.toLowerCase();
                    if(name.startsWith('on')){ch.removeAttribute(at.name);return;}
                    if(name==='class'){ch.removeAttribute(at.name);return;} 
                    if(!allowed.includes(name)){ch.removeAttribute(at.name);return;}
                    if(name==='href'||name==='src'){
                      const v=(at.value||'').trim().toLowerCase();
                      const rawVal=(at.value||'');
                      const okPrefix=['http:
                      const isDataImg=v.startsWith('data:image/png;')||v.startsWith('data:image/jpeg;')||v.startsWith('data:image/gif;')||v.startsWith('data:image/webp;');
                      
                      if(isDataImg&&rawVal.length>700000){ch.parentNode.replaceChild(doc.createTextNode('[鍥剧墖杩囧ぇ宸茬渷鐣'),ch);return;}
                      if(!isDataImg&&!okPrefix.some(p=>v.startsWith(p)))ch.removeAttribute(at.name);
                    }
                    if(name==='style'){
                      ch.setAttribute('style',sanitizeStyle(at.value));
                    }
                  });
                  walk(ch);
                }else if(ch.nodeType===8){ch.parentNode.removeChild(ch);}
              });
            })(root);
            return root.innerHTML;
          }catch(e){return String(html).replace(/<[^>]*>/g,'');}
        }
        
        (function(){
          const _orig = typeof document.execCommand === 'function' ? document.execCommand.bind(document) : null;
          if(!_orig) return;
          document.execCommand = function(cmd, ui, value){
            if(String(cmd||'').toLowerCase() === 'inserthtml' && typeof value === 'string'){
              value = sanitizeHtml(value);
            }
            return _orig(cmd, ui, value);
          };
        })();
        
        (function(){
          function ceTarget(e){
            const el = e && e.target;
            if(!el) return null;
            if(el.nodeType === 1) return el.closest ? el.closest('[contenteditable]') : null;
            if(el.nodeType === 3 && el.parentElement) return el.parentElement.closest('[contenteditable]');
            return null;
          }
          document.addEventListener('dragover', function(e){
            if(!ceTarget(e)) return;
            const types = e.dataTransfer && e.dataTransfer.types;
            if(types && Array.prototype.indexOf.call(types, 'text/html') !== -1){
              e.preventDefault();
              try{ e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = 'copy'; }catch(_){}
            }
          }, false);
          document.addEventListener('drop', function(e){
            if(!ceTarget(e)) return;
            const dt = e.dataTransfer;
            if(!dt) return;
            let html='';
            try{ html = dt.getData('text/html') || ''; }catch(_){ return; }
            if(!html) return;
            e.preventDefault();
            const clean = sanitizeHtml(html);
            if(clean){
              try{ document.execCommand('insertHTML', false, clean); }catch(_){}
            }
          }, false);
        })();

        function removeNodeFromMap(map,id){
          const n=map[id];if(!n)return;
          (n.children||[]).forEach(c=>removeNodeFromMap(map,c));
          if(n.parent&&map[n.parent]&&map[n.parent].children){
            const _pi=map[n.parent].children.indexOf(id);
            if(_pi>=0)map[n.parent].children.splice(_pi,1);
          }
          delete map[id];
          
          try{purgeNodeMeta({nodes:map},id);}catch(e){}
          if(ui&&ui.collapsedDocs)delete ui.collapsedDocs[id];
          
          try{if(state&&state._localCreated){const _li=state._localCreated.indexOf(id);if(_li>=0)state._localCreated.splice(_li,1);}}catch(e){}
          
          _tombstoneSet(id); 
        }
        function mergeRemoteState(remote){
          
          if(!state||!remote.nodes)return false; 
          
          __juInMerge=true;
          window.__juInMerge=true;
          try{
          
          const rnNodes = remote.nodes || {};
          if (!rnNodes[state.rootId] || Object.keys(rnNodes).length === 0) {
            console.warn('FIX-BUG01: 鎷掔粷涓嶅畬鏁磋繙绔揩鐓?rootId=', state.rootId, 'keys=', Object.keys(rnNodes).length);
            return false; 
          }
          const localNodes=state.nodes||{};
          const remoteUpdatedAt=(remote.updatedAt||0);
          const _norm=t=>(t>8.64e14)?Math.round(t/1000):t;
          let _structDirty=false; 
          let _metaDirty=false; 
          
          const pruneTombstonedChildren=(n)=>{
            if(n&&Array.isArray(n.children)&&_recentlyRemoved&&_recentlyRemoved.size){
              const filtered=n.children.filter(cid=>!_recentlyRemoved.has(cid));
              if(filtered.length!==n.children.length)n.children=filtered;
            }
          };
          for(const id in remote.nodes){
            
            if(id==='__proto__'||id==='constructor'||id==='prototype')continue;
            const rn=remote.nodes[id];
            const ln=localNodes[id];
            if(!ln){
              
              const _rmTs=_recentlyRemoved&&_recentlyRemoved.get(id);
              if(_rmTs&&(Date.now()-_rmTs)<30000&&_norm(rn.updatedAt||0)<=_rmTs)continue;
              localNodes[id]=rn;
              pruneTombstonedChildren(rn); 
              _structDirty=true; 
              rn._origin='remote'; 
              
              if(rn._deleted)continue;
              
              const pp=localNodes[rn.parent];
              if(pp&&pp.children&&!pp.children.includes(id))pp.children.push(id);
              else if((!pp)&&state.rootId&&localNodes[state.rootId]){
                
                rn.parent=state.rootId;
                if(!localNodes[state.rootId].children.includes(id))localNodes[state.rootId].children.push(id);
              }
            }
            else if(_norm(rn.updatedAt||0)>_norm(ln.updatedAt||0)
              
              ||(_norm(rn.updatedAt||0)===_norm(ln.updatedAt||0)&&_norm(rn.createdAt||0)===_norm(ln.createdAt||0)
                &&JSON.stringify(rn,(k,v)=>['_rev','_origin','_deleted'].includes(k)?undefined:v)>JSON.stringify(ln,(k,v)=>['_rev','_origin','_deleted'].includes(k)?undefined:v))){
              
              const rnChildren=rn.children||[];
              const localExtra=(ln.children||[]).filter(c=>{
                if(rnChildren.includes(c))return false;
                const lc=localNodes[c];
                if(!lc)return false;
                const lcUpd=_norm(lc.updatedAt||0);
                const lcCrt=_norm(lc.createdAt||0);
                return lcUpd>=remoteUpdatedAt||lcCrt>remoteUpdatedAt;
              });
              localNodes[id]=rn;
              _structDirty=true; 
              rn._origin='remote'; 
              
              if(rn._deleted){
                const dp=ln.parent&&localNodes[ln.parent];
                if(dp&&Array.isArray(dp.children)){
                  const di=dp.children.indexOf(id);
                  if(di>=0)dp.children.splice(di,1);
                }
              }
              if(localExtra.length)rn.children=rnChildren.concat(localExtra);
              pruneTombstonedChildren(rn); 
              
              if(ln.parent&&ln.parent!==rn.parent){
                const oldP=localNodes[ln.parent];
                if(oldP&&Array.isArray(oldP.children)){
                  const oi=oldP.children.indexOf(id);
                  if(oi>=0)oldP.children.splice(oi,1);
                }
              }
            }
          }
          
          try{
            if(state._localCreated&&state._localCreated.length){
              const lc=state._localCreated;
              for(let i=lc.length-1;i>=0;i--){if(remote.nodes[lc[i]])lc.splice(i,1);}
            }
          }catch(e){}
          
          try{
            if(remote._localCreated&&remote._localCreated.length){
              if(!state._localCreated)state._localCreated=[];
              for(const id of remote._localCreated){
                if(typeof id==='string'&&localNodes[id]&&!state._localCreated.includes(id))state._localCreated.push(id);
              }
            }
          }catch(e){}
          
          try{
            if(remote._tombstones&&typeof remote._tombstones==='object'){
              const _now=Date.now();
              for(const k in remote._tombstones){
                const _ts=+remote._tombstones[k];
                if(!_ts||_now-_ts>30000)continue;
                const old=_recentlyRemoved.get(k);
                if(!old||_ts>old){
                  _recentlyRemoved.set(k,_ts);
                  try{
                    if(typeof state._tombstones!=='object'||!state._tombstones)state._tombstones={};
                    state._tombstones[k]=_ts;
                  }catch(e){}
                }
              }
            }
          }catch(e){}
          
          if(remoteUpdatedAt>0){
            for(const id of Object.keys(localNodes)){
              
              if(id==='__proto__'||id==='constructor'||id==='prototype')continue;
              if(remote.nodes[id])continue;
              const ln=localNodes[id];
              if(!ln)continue;
              const lnUpd=_norm(ln.updatedAt||0);
              if(lnUpd>=remoteUpdatedAt)continue;   
              const lnCrt=_norm(ln.createdAt||0);
              if(lnCrt>remoteUpdatedAt)continue;    
              
              if(ln._origin==='local')continue;
              
              if(state._localCreated&&state._localCreated.includes(id))continue;
              
              if(!_diskConfirmed||!_diskConfirmed.has(id))continue;
              removeNodeFromMap(localNodes,id);
              _structDirty=true; 
            }
          }
          
          if(state.trashed&&state.trashed.length){
            state.trashed=state.trashed.filter(t=>t&&t.rootId&&localNodes[t.rootId]);
          }
          
          const remoteNewer=remoteUpdatedAt>(state.updatedAt||0);
          if(remoteNewer){
            _metaDirty=true; 
            
            for(const f of ['tagColors','dbColumnWidths']){
              if(remote[f]!==undefined){
                if(!state[f])state[f]={};
                Object.assign(state[f],remote[f]); 
              }
            }
            for(const f of ['templates','contacts']){
              if(Array.isArray(remote[f])&&remote[f].length){
                if(!Array.isArray(state[f]))state[f]=[];
                const seen=new Set(state[f].map(x=>x&&x.id));
                for(const x of remote[f]){
                  if(!x||x.id==null)continue;
                  if(seen.has(x.id)){state[f]=state[f].map(y=>y&&y.id===x.id?x:y);} 
                  else{seen.add(x.id);state[f].push(x);}
                }
              }
            }
            
            if(remote.trashed!==undefined){
              if(!Array.isArray(state.trashed))state.trashed=[];
              const tSeen=new Set(state.trashed.map(t=>t&&t.rootId));
              for(const t of (remote.trashed||[])){
                if(!t||!t.rootId)continue;
                if(tSeen.has(t.rootId)){state.trashed=state.trashed.map(y=>y&&y.rootId===t.rootId?t:y);}
                else{tSeen.add(t.rootId);state.trashed.push(t);}
              }
            }
            if(remote.dbFields!==undefined)state.dbFields=remote.dbFields; 
            
            if(remote.activity&&typeof remote.activity==='object'){
              if(!state.activity||typeof state.activity!=='object')state.activity={};
              for(const d in remote.activity){
                const rb=remote.activity[d];
                if(!rb||typeof rb!=='object')continue;
                const lb=state.activity[d];
                if(!lb||typeof lb!=='object'){state.activity[d]=rb;continue;}
                for(const t of ['add','del','mod']){
                  const ra=rb[t];
                  if(!Array.isArray(ra)||!ra.length)continue;
                  const la=lb[t]||(lb[t]=[]);
                  const seen=new Set(la.map(x=>x&&x.id));
                  for(const e of ra){
                    if(!e||e.id==null)continue;
                    if(seen.has(e.id)){
                      const idx=la.findIndex(x=>x&&x.id===e.id);
                      if(idx>=0)la[idx]=e;
                    }else{seen.add(e.id);la.push(e);}
                  }
                  if(la.length>200)la.splice(0,la.length-200);
                }
              }
            }
          }
          
          if(_structDirty)repairTreeConsistency();
          
          try{dedupNoteRoots();}catch(e){}
          
          try{dedupCalRoots();}catch(e){}
          
          try{
            const viol=[];
            for(const pid in localNodes){
              const p=localNodes[pid];
              if(!p||!Array.isArray(p.children))continue;
              for(const cid of p.children){
                const c=localNodes[cid];
                if(!c||c._deleted)continue;
                if(c.parent!==pid&&cid!==state.rootId)viol.push(cid);
              }
            }
            if(viol.length)reportError('invariant',{name:'multi-parent-after-repair',ids:[...new Set(viol)].slice(0,5)});
          }catch(_){}
          
          state._rev=Math.max((state._rev|0),(remote._rev|0))+1;
          
          
          
          if(_structDirty||_metaDirty)markPersistMerged(rnNodes,state);
          return _structDirty||_metaDirty;
          }finally{
            
            __juInMerge=false;
            window.__juInMerge=false;
            _calIndexCache=null;
            _tagIndexCache=null;
            _searchInverted=null;
            _backlinkMap=null;
            _docTitleToDocId=null;
          }

        function repairTreeConsistency(){
          if(!state||!state.nodes||!state.rootId)return;
          const ns=state.nodes;
          const root=ns[state.rootId];
          if(!root)return;
          
          const _childSets=new Map();
          const _getChildSet=pid=>{
            let s=_childSets.get(pid);
            if(!s){s=new Set(((ns[pid]&&ns[pid].children)||[]));_childSets.set(pid,s);}
            return s;
          };
          
          for(const id in ns){
            if(id===state.rootId)continue;
            const n=ns[id];
            if(!n||typeof n!=='object')continue;
            if(n._deleted)continue; 
            if(n.parent===id)delete n.parent;
            const p=(n.parent&&ns[n.parent])?ns[n.parent]:null;
            if(!p){
              n.parent=state.rootId;
              if(!_getChildSet(state.rootId).has(id)){root.children.push(id);_getChildSet(state.rootId).add(id);}
            }else if(!_getChildSet(p.id).has(id)){
              p.children.push(id);_getChildSet(p.id).add(id);
            }
          }
          
          for(const pid in ns){
            const p=ns[pid];
            if(!p||!Array.isArray(p.children))continue;
            const seen=new Set();
            
            for(let i=0;i<p.children.length;i++){
              const cid=p.children[i];
              if(seen.has(cid)){p.children.splice(i,1);i--;continue;} 
              seen.add(cid);
              const c=ns[cid];
              if(!c||c._deleted)continue; 
              if(c.parent!==pid){p.children.splice(i,1);i--;} 
            }
          }
        }

        function _tombstoneDelete(k,st){

        function _tombstoneSet(id,st){
          let target=null; 
          try{
            const _now=Date.now();
            _recentlyRemoved.set(id,_now);
            
            target=st||state;
            if(target){
              if(typeof target._tombstones!=='object'||!target._tombstones)target._tombstones={};
              target._tombstones[id]=_now;
            }
            
            if((++_tombstoneOps)%200===0){
              for(const[k,v]of _recentlyRemoved){if(_now-v>30000)_tombstoneDelete(k,target);}
            }
            
            if(_recentlyRemoved.size>2000){
              for(const[k,v]of _recentlyRemoved){if(_now-v>30000)_tombstoneDelete(k,target);}
              while(_recentlyRemoved.size>2000){
                const k=_recentlyRemoved.keys().next().value;
                if(k===undefined)break;
                _tombstoneDelete(k,target);
              }
            }
          }catch(e){}
        }
        const _REV_STRIP=(k,v)=>k==='_rev'||k==='_origin'?undefined:v;

        function makeLRU(max){
          const map=new Map();
          return {
            get size(){return map.size;},
            has:k=>map.has(k),
            get(k){
              if(!map.has(k))return undefined;
              const v=map.get(k);
              map.delete(k);map.set(k,v);
              return v;
            },
            set(k,v){
              if(map.has(k))map.delete(k);
              else if(map.size>=max){const oldest=map.keys().next().value;if(oldest!==undefined)map.delete(oldest);}
              map.set(k,v);
              return v;
            },
            delete:k=>map.delete(k),
            clear:()=>map.clear()
          };
        }
        const _WORDCOUNT_MAX=2000; 
        const _wordCountCache=makeLRU(_WORDCOUNT_MAX);
        function initStore(initial){
          state=initial;
          for (const id in state.nodes) {
            const n = state.nodes[id];
            if (!n) continue;
            if (n.createdAt > 8.64e14) n.createdAt = Math.round(n.createdAt / 1000);
            if (n.updatedAt > 8.64e14) n.updatedAt = Math.round(n.updatedAt / 1000);
          }
          if(state.trashed&&state.trashed.length){
            state.trashed=state.trashed.filter(t=>t&&t.rootId&&state.nodes[t.rootId]);
          }
          _wordCountCache.clear();
          invalidateCalIndex();
          
          try{_searchHayCache.clear();}catch(e){}
          
          try{
            if(state._tombstones&&typeof state._tombstones==='object'){
              const _now=Date.now();
              for(const k in state._tombstones){
                const _ts=+state._tombstones[k];
                if(_ts&&_now-_ts<=30000)_recentlyRemoved.set(k,_ts);
                else delete state._tombstones[k];
              }
            }
          }catch(e){}
          
          try{
            const lc=Array.isArray(state._localCreated)?state._localCreated.filter((id,i,a)=>typeof id==='string'&&state.nodes[id]&&a.indexOf(id)===i):[];
            state._localCreated=lc;
          }catch(e){try{state._localCreated=[];}catch(e2){}}
          
          _lastNowSeq=0;
          
          try{repairTreeConsistency();}catch(e){}
          
          try{_diskConfirmed=new Set(Object.keys(state.nodes||{}));}catch(e){}
        }
        function getState(){return state;}

        function invalidateCalIndex(){
          _calIndexCache=null;
          _calRecurCache=null;
          _yearCountsCache=null;
          _tagIndexCache=null;
          _calDayCache=null;
          _renderDayCache=null;

        let _graphPositions=(()=>{
          try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('juju.graphpos')||'{}')||{};}catch(e){return {};}
        })();
        function saveGraphPositions(){
          try{localStorage.setItem('juju.graphpos',JSON.stringify(_graphPositions));}catch(e){}

        function purgeNodeMeta(s,id){
          if(s.dbRowHeights)delete s.dbRowHeights[id];
          if(s.dbColumnWidths){
            const prefix=id+'::';
            Object.keys(s.dbColumnWidths).forEach(k=>{if(k.startsWith(prefix))delete s.dbColumnWidths[k];});
          }
          try{delete _graphPositions[id];}catch(e){}
        }
        function findTouchedNodeId(prev,next){
          let bestId=null,bestTs=-1;
          for(const id in next.nodes){
            const a=prev.nodes[id],b=next.nodes[id];
            if(a && b && a.updatedAt!==b.updatedAt && (b.updatedAt||0)>bestTs){bestTs=b.updatedAt||0;bestId=id;}
          }
          if(bestId)return bestId;
          for(const id in next.nodes){
            if(!prev.nodes[id] && (next.nodes[id].updatedAt||0)>bestTs){bestTs=next.nodes[id].updatedAt||0;bestId=id;}
          }
          if(bestId)return bestId;
          for(const id in prev.nodes){
            if(!next.nodes[id]){
              const p=prev.nodes[id]?.parent;
              if(p&&next.nodes[p])return p;
            }
          }
          return null;
        }
        function findOwningTopDocId(id,s){
          let cur=id;
          const visited=new Set();
          while(cur&&!visited.has(cur)){
            visited.add(cur);
            const n=s.nodes[cur];
            if(!n)return null;
            
            if(n._deleted){cur=n.parent;continue;}
            if(!n.parent||n.parent===s.rootId)return cur;
            cur=n.parent;
          }
          return null;
        }
        
        const _MERGE_WINDOW_MS=500;     
        const _MAX_PATH_DEPTH=8;        
        const _PATCH_FAIL_CAP=50;       
        
        window.__juPatchFailures=window.__juPatchFailures||[];
        window.__juPatchFailures.length=Math.min(window.__juPatchFailures.length,_PATCH_FAIL_CAP);
        
        function resolveHistoryMode(opts){
          if(opts&&typeof opts.historyMode==='string')return opts.historyMode;
          if(opts&&opts.noHistory)return 'never';
          if(opts&&opts.light)return 'never';
          if(opts&&opts.snapshot){
            
            if(opts.op==='updateText'||opts.op==='setDbCell')return 'merge';
            return 'snapshot';
          }
          return 'never';
        }
        
        function collectModifiedPaths(prev,next,basePath,depth,out){
          out=out||[];basePath=basePath||'';depth=depth||0;
          if(depth>_MAX_PATH_DEPTH){
            
            if(!out.length||out[out.length-1]!==basePath)out.push(basePath||'<root>');
            return out;
          }
          if(prev===next)return out;
          if(prev==null||next==null||typeof prev!=='object'||typeof next!=='object'
             ||Array.isArray(prev)!==Array.isArray(next)
             ||prev instanceof Date||next instanceof Date
             ||prev instanceof Map||next instanceof Map
             ||prev instanceof Set||next instanceof Set){
            
            out.push(basePath||'<root>');
            return out;
          }
          
          const keys=new Set();
          for(const k in prev)if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(prev,k))keys.add(k);
          for(const k in next)if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(next,k))keys.add(k);
          for(const k of keys){
            const pv=prev[k],nv=next[k];
            if(pv!==nv){
              const path=basePath?basePath+'.'+k:k;
              if(pv===undefined||nv===undefined||pv===null||nv===null
                 ||typeof pv!=='object'||typeof nv!=='object'
                 ||Array.isArray(pv)!==Array.isArray(nv)
                 ||pv instanceof Date||nv instanceof Date
                 ||pv instanceof Map||nv instanceof Map
                 ||pv instanceof Set||nv instanceof Set){
                out.push(path);
              }else{
                collectModifiedPaths(pv,nv,path,depth+1,out);
              }
            }
          }
          return out;
        }
        
        function extractAtPaths(s,paths){
          const out={};
          for(const p of paths){
            let cur=s,seg='',ok=true;
            const parts=p.split('.');
            for(let i=0;i<parts.length;i++){
              if(cur==null||typeof cur!=='object'){ok=false;break;}
              seg=parts[i];
              if(i===parts.length-1){
                let v=cur[seg];
                if(v instanceof Date)v=v.toISOString();
                else if(v instanceof Map){v={};for(const [k,vv] of v)v[k]=vv;}
                else if(v instanceof Set){v=[];for(const x of v)v.push(x);}
                out[p]=v;
              }else{
                cur=cur[seg];
              }
            }
            if(!ok)out[p]=undefined;
          }
          return out;
        }
        
        function setByPath(s,path,value){
          const parts=String(path).split('.');
          if(parts.length>_MAX_PATH_DEPTH)return s;  
          const r=__juProduceSafe(s,draft=>{
            let cur=draft;
            for(let i=0;i<parts.length-1;i++){
              const seg=parts[i];
              if(cur[seg]==null||typeof cur[seg]!=='object')cur[seg]={};
              cur=cur[seg];
            }
            const last=parts[parts.length-1];
            if(value===undefined)delete cur[last];
            else cur[last]=value;
          });
          return r.ok?r.next:s;
        }
        
        function invertPatch(p){
          return {
            v:1,t:p.t,
            op:(p.op||'unknown')+':undo',
            mode:p.mode,paths:p.paths.slice(),
            pre:p.post,post:p.pre,
            meta:p.meta||{},
            groupKey:p.groupKey,
            mergedCount:p.mergedCount,
          };
        }
        
        function applyPatch(s,patch,direction){
          if(direction!=='undo'&&direction!=='redo')return s;
          const values=direction==='undo'?patch.pre:patch.post;
          if(!values||!patch.paths)return s;
          let next=s;
          for(const p of patch.paths){
            if(!Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(values,p))continue;
            next=setByPath(next,p,values[p]);
          }
          
          try{if(typeof repairTreeConsistency==='function'){const fixed=repairTreeConsistency(next);if(fixed)next=fixed;}}catch(e){}
          return next;
        }
        
        function tryMergeWithLast(stack,patch){
          if(!stack.length){stack.push(patch);if(stack.length>HISTORY_MAX)stack.shift();return;}
          const last=stack[stack.length-1];
          
          if(last.mode!=='merge'||patch.mode!=='merge'){stack.push(patch);if(stack.length>HISTORY_MAX)stack.shift();return;}
          if(patch.t-last.t>_MERGE_WINDOW_MS){stack.push(patch);if(stack.length>HISTORY_MAX)stack.shift();return;}
          if(last.groupKey!==patch.groupKey){stack.push(patch);if(stack.length>HISTORY_MAX)stack.shift();return;}
          
          const pathSet=new Set([...last.paths,...patch.paths]);
          const paths=Array.from(pathSet);
          
          if(paths.length>10){stack.push(patch);if(stack.length>HISTORY_MAX)stack.shift();return;}
          
          if((last.mergedCount||1)>=50){stack.push(patch);if(stack.length>HISTORY_MAX)stack.shift();return;}
          const merged={
            v:1,t:patch.t,op:last.op,mode:'merge',paths,
            pre:Object.assign({},last.pre,patch.pre),
            post:Object.assign({},patch.post),
            meta:Object.assign({},last.meta||{},patch.meta||{}),
            groupKey:last.groupKey,
            mergedCount:(last.mergedCount||1)+1,
          };
          stack[stack.length-1]=merged;
        }
        
        function inferOp(opts){return (opts&&typeof opts.op==='string')?opts.op:'unknown';}
        
        function inferGroupKey(opts){
          if(!opts)return undefined;
          const op=opts.op||'unknown';
          if(op==='updateText'&&opts.opArgs&&opts.opArgs.id)return 'updateText:'+opts.opArgs.id;
          if(op==='setDbCell'&&opts.opArgs&&opts.opArgs.id)return 'setDbCell:'+opts.opArgs.id+':'+(opts.opArgs.field||'?');
          return op;
        }
        
        function buildPatch(opts){
          return {
            v:1,t:opts.t,op:opts.op,mode:opts.mode,
            paths:opts.paths.slice(),
            pre:Object.assign({},opts.pre),
            post:Object.assign({},opts.post),
            meta:opts.meta||{},
            groupKey:opts.groupKey,
          };
        }
        
        function reportPatchFailure(payload){
          const buf=window.__juPatchFailures;
          buf.push({op:payload.op,mode:payload.mode,pathCount:(payload.paths||[]).length,error:String(payload.error||'unknown'),ts:payload.ts||Date.now()});
          while(buf.length>_PATCH_FAIL_CAP)buf.shift();
          if(typeof console!=='undefined'&&console.warn)console.warn('[JU-HistoryAgent] patch serialize failed, fallback to never',payload);
        }
        
        const _INDEX_FAIL_CAP=50; 
        const _INDEX_FAILURE_STRIKE=3; 
        window.__juIndexFailures=window.__juIndexFailures||[];
        let __juIndexFailureStreak=0;
        let __juInMerge=false;

        function extractChangedIds(paths,meta){
          const ids=new Set();
          if(!Array.isArray(paths))return ids;
          for(const p of paths){
            const m=/^nodes\.([^.]+)(?:\.|$)/.exec(p);
            if(m)ids.add(m[1]);
          }
          if(meta&&Array.isArray(meta.ids)){
            for(const id of meta.ids)ids.add(id);
          }
          return ids;
        }
        
        function collectSubtreeIds(rootId){
          const ids=new Set();
          if(!rootId||!state||!state.nodes||!state.nodes[rootId])return ids;
          const stack=[rootId];
          while(stack.length){
            const id=stack.pop();
            if(ids.has(id))continue;
            ids.add(id);
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(n&&Array.isArray(n.children)){
              for(const cid of n.children)stack.push(cid);
            }
          }
          return ids;
        }
        
        function persistDirtyStats(){
          return {dirty:_dirtyNodes.size,removed:_removedNodes.size,mode:_persistMode,
                  seq:_persistBatchSeq,sinceFull:_dirtySinceFull,forced:_l8ForceDesktop,
                  active:l8PersistActive(),pending:_l8CheckpointPending,checkpointEvery:_l8CheckpointEvery};
        }
        
        function l8PersistActive(){
          try{
            if(ui&&ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey)return false; 
            return !!(IS_DESKTOP||_l8ForceDesktop);
          }catch(e){return false;}
        }
        function l8PersistFailure(phase,op,err){ 
          try{
            const buf=window.__juPersistFailures=window.__juPersistFailures||[];
            buf.push({phase:String(phase||''),op:String(op==null?'':op),error:String((err&&err.message)||err),ts:Date.now()});
            if(buf.length>L8_FAIL_CAP)buf.splice(0,buf.length-L8_FAIL_CAP);
            const p=window.__juPersistProbe;if(p&&p.errors!==buf)p.errors=buf;
            _persistMode='full'; 
          }catch(e){}
        }
        function l8PersistNote(msg){try{console.info('[L8]',msg);}catch(e){}}
        
        function collectSubtreeIdsFromPreState(pre,rootIds){
          const nodes=(pre&&pre.nodes)?pre.nodes:(pre||{});
          const ids=new Set();
          const roots=Array.isArray(rootIds)?rootIds:(rootIds?[rootIds]:[]);
          const stack=roots.filter(Boolean);
          while(stack.length){
            const id=stack.pop();
            if(!id||ids.has(id))continue;
            ids.add(id);
            const n=nodes[id];
            if(n&&Array.isArray(n.children)){for(const cid of n.children)if(cid&&!ids.has(cid))stack.push(cid);}
          }
          return ids;
        }
        
        function markPersistDirty(changedIds,st,ctx){
          if(!l8PersistActive())return persistDirtyStats();
          if(!changedIds||!changedIds.size)return persistDirtyStats();
          const nodes=(st&&st.nodes)||{};
          for(const id of changedIds){
            if(!id)continue;
            if(nodes[id]){_removedNodes.delete(id);_dirtyNodes.set(id,nodes[id]);}
            else{_dirtyNodes.delete(id);_removedNodes.add(id);}
          }
          return persistDirtyStats();
        }
        
        function markPersistFromPatch(patch,preState,postState,op){
          if(!l8PersistActive())return persistDirtyStats();
          try{
            if(!patch||!Array.isArray(patch.paths)||!patch.paths.length)return persistDirtyStats();
            const ids=extractChangedIds(patch.paths,patch.meta);
            const postNodes=(postState&&postState.nodes)||{};
            const _gone=[];
            for(const id of ids)if(!postNodes[id])_gone.push(id);
            if(_gone.length){
              const _full=collectSubtreeIdsFromPreState(preState,_gone);
              for(const id of _full)ids.add(id);
            }
            return markPersistDirty(ids,postState,{op});
          }catch(e){l8PersistFailure('mark',op,e);return persistDirtyStats();}
        }
        
        function markPersistMerged(remoteNodes,st){
          if(!l8PersistActive())return persistDirtyStats();
          try{return markPersistDirty(new Set(Object.keys(remoteNodes||{})),st,{op:'merge'});}
          catch(e){l8PersistFailure('mark','merge',e);return persistDirtyStats();}
        }
        
        window.__juPersistFailures=window.__juPersistFailures||[];
        window.__juPersistProbe={
          ready:true,
          defaultMode:'patch',
          lastPatch:null,
          sent:[],
          w2:{maxPerBatch:L8_MAX_PER_BATCH,failCap:L8_FAIL_CAP},
          w3:{checkpointEvery:L8_CHECKPOINT_EVERY,fullCheckpointOneShot:true},
          errors:window.__juPersistFailures,
          get mode(){return _persistMode;},
          stats(){return persistDirtyStats();},
          setMode(m){_persistMode=(m==='full')?'full':'patch';_l8CheckpointPending=false;return _persistMode;},
          forceDesktop(v){_l8ForceDesktop=!!v;return _l8ForceDesktop;},
          clearDirty(){_dirtyNodes.clear();_removedNodes.clear();return persistDirtyStats();},
          mark(changedIds,st){return markPersistDirty(changedIds,st||state,{op:'probe'});},
          markFromPatch(patch,pre,post,op){return markPersistFromPatch(patch,pre,post,op||'probe');},
          subtreeIdsFromPre(pre,roots){return Array.from(collectSubtreeIdsFromPreState(pre,roots));},
          dirtyIds(){return Array.from(_dirtyNodes.keys());},
          removedIds(){return Array.from(_removedNodes.values());},
          refOf(id){return _dirtyNodes.get(id)||null;},
