          checkpointPending(){return _l8CheckpointPending;},
          setCheckpointEvery(n){_l8CheckpointEvery=(typeof n==='number'&&n>0)?n:L8_CHECKPOINT_EVERY;return _l8CheckpointEvery;},
        };
        
        function buildPersistBatch(){
          
          if((_dirtyNodes.size+_removedNodes.size)>L8_PATCH_MAX)return {reason:'over-threshold',batches:[]};
          const deleted=[];
          for(const id of _removedNodes){if(id)deleted.push(id);}
          const _del=new Set(deleted);
          const changedIds=[];
          for(const id of _dirtyNodes.keys()){if(id&&!_del.has(id))changedIds.push(id);} 
          if(!changedIds.length&&!deleted.length)return {reason:'empty',batches:[]};
          
          const batches=[];
          const _n=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(changedIds.length/L8_MAX_PER_BATCH));
          for(let i=0;i<_n;i++){
            const slice=changedIds.slice(i*L8_MAX_PER_BATCH,(i+1)*L8_MAX_PER_BATCH);
            const changed=[];
            for(const id of slice){const node=_dirtyNodes.get(id);if(node!==undefined)changed.push({id:id,data:JSON.stringify(node)});} 
            batches.push({changed:changed,deleted:(i===0)?deleted.slice():[]});
          }
          return {reason:null,batches:batches};
        }
        
        function _l8BatchRefs(batch){
          const m=new Map();
          for(const it of (batch&&batch.changed)||[]){const n=_dirtyNodes.get(it&&it.id);if(n!==undefined)m.set(it.id,n);}
          return m;
        }
        
        function applyPatchAck(batch,ack,refs){
          try{
            const changed=(batch&&batch.changed)||[];
            const deleted=(batch&&batch.deleted)||[];
            const refMap=(refs instanceof Map)?refs:_l8BatchRefs(batch);
            for(const it of changed){
              const id=it&&it.id;if(!id)continue;
              if(_dirtyNodes.get(id)===refMap.get(id))_dirtyNodes.delete(id); 
            }
            for(const id of deleted){if(id)_removedNodes.delete(id);}
            _persistBatchSeq++;      
            _dirtySinceFull++;       
            if(_dirtySinceFull>=_l8CheckpointEvery)requestFullCheckpoint(); 
            state._rev=(state._rev|0)+1; 
            if(!_diskConfirmed)_diskConfirmed=new Set();
            for(const it of changed){if(it&&it.id)_diskConfirmed.add(it.id);}
            for(const id of deleted){if(id)_diskConfirmed.delete(id);}
            _lastPersistFingerprint=(state._rev|0)+':'+_persistBatchSeq; 
            const p=window.__juPersistProbe;
            if(p){
              p.sent.push({seq:_persistBatchSeq,changed:changed.length,deleted:deleted.length});
              if(p.sent.length>256)p.sent.splice(0,p.sent.length-256); 
              p.lastPatch={seq:_persistBatchSeq,changed:changed.length,deleted:deleted.length,rev:state._rev|0};
            }
            return true;
          }catch(e){l8PersistFailure('ack','patch_nodes',e);return false;}
        }
        
        function markFullOnce(){_persistMode='full';return _persistMode;}
        
        function requestFullCheckpoint(){_l8CheckpointPending=true;return _l8CheckpointPending;}
        
        async function l8PersistSendBatches(pb){
          const batches=(pb&&pb.batches)||[];
          if(!batches.length)return false;
          for(const batch of batches){
            const refs=_l8BatchRefs(batch);                        
            const seq=_persistBatchSeq+1;                           
            const args={changed:batch.changed,deleted:batch.deleted,seq:seq,baseRev:state._rev|0};
            let ack;
            try{ack=await desktopInvoke('patch_nodes',args);}
            catch(e){l8PersistFailure('patch','patch_nodes',e);markFullOnce();return false;} 
            if(!applyPatchAck(batch,ack,refs)){markFullOnce();return false;}
          }
          return true;
        }
        
        
        function nodeAllTags(n){
          if(!n)return new Set();
          const seen=new Set();
          const raw=stripHtml(n.text||'');
          let m;TAG_RE.lastIndex=0;
          while((m=TAG_RE.exec(raw))!==null){
            const t=m[1].slice(1);
            if(t)seen.add(t);
          }
          if(Array.isArray(n.tags)&&n.tags.length){
            for(const t of n.tags){
              if(!t||seen.has(t))continue;
              if(!(Array.isArray(n._manualTags)&&n._manualTags.includes(t)))continue;
              seen.add(t);
            }
          }
          return seen;
        }
        
        function tokenize(text){
          const out=new Set();
          if(!text)return out;
          const t=String(text).toLowerCase();
          
          const enRe=/[a-z0-9]+/g;
          let m;
          while((m=enRe.exec(t))!==null){
            const w=m[0];
            if(w.length>=1&&w.length<=32)out.add(w);
          }
          
          const cjkRe=/[\u4e00-\u9fff\u3400-\u4dbf]/g;
          while((m=cjkRe.exec(t))!==null){
            out.add(m[0]);
          }
          return out;
        }
        
        function extractWikiTitles(text){
          const out=new Set();
          if(!text)return out;
          const t=String(text);
          WIKI_RE.lastIndex=0;let m;
          while((m=WIKI_RE.exec(t))!==null){
            const title=normTitle(m[1]);
            if(title)out.add(title);
          }
          return out;
        }
        
        function resolveDocIdByTitle(title){
          if(!title)return null;
          if(!_docTitleToDocId){
            _docTitleToDocId=new Map();
            if(state&&state.nodes){
              for(const id in state.nodes){
                const n=state.nodes[id];
                if(!n||n._deleted)continue;
                const top=findOwningTopDocId(id,state);
                if(!top||top===state.rootId)continue;
                const t=normTitle(stripHtml(n.text||''));
                if(t)_docTitleToDocId.set(t,top);
              }
            }
          }
          return _docTitleToDocId.get(title)||null;
        }
        
        function calIndexUpdate(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts){
          if(!_calIndexCache)return{added:0,removed:0,errors:[]}; 
          if(opts&&opts.subtreeCleanup){
            let removed=0;
            for(const id of(opts.subtreeIds||changedIds)){
              for(const[date,idSet]of _calIndexCache){
                if(idSet.delete(id))removed++;
                if(!idSet.size)_calIndexCache.delete(date);
              }
            }
            return{added:0,removed,errors:[]};
          }
          let added=0,removed=0;
          const errors=[];
          for(const id of changedIds){
            try{
              const fromNode=fromState&&fromState.nodes&&fromState.nodes[id];
              const toNode=toState&&toState.nodes&&toState.nodes[id];
              const fromDate=fromNode&&fromNode.date;
              const toDate=toNode&&toNode.date;
              const fromDeleted=fromNode&&fromNode._deleted;
              const toDeleted=toNode&&toNode._deleted;
              
              if(fromDate&&(toDeleted||toDate!==fromDate)){
                const set=_calIndexCache.get(fromDate);
                if(set){
                  if(set.delete(id))removed++;
                  if(!set.size)_calIndexCache.delete(fromDate);
                }
              }
              
              if(toDate&&!toDeleted&&(toDate!==fromDate||fromDeleted)){
                let set=_calIndexCache.get(toDate);
                if(!set){set=new Set();_calIndexCache.set(toDate,set);}
                if(!set.has(id)){set.add(id);added++;}
              }
            }catch(e){errors.push(`id=${id}: ${e&&e.message||e}`);}
          }
          return{added,removed,errors};
        }
        
        function tagIndexUpdate(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts){
          if(!_tagIndexCache)return{added:0,removed:0,errors:[]};
          if(opts&&opts.subtreeCleanup){
            let removed=0;
            for(const id of(opts.subtreeIds||changedIds)){
              for(const[tag,idSet]of _tagIndexCache){
                if(idSet.delete(id))removed++;
                if(!idSet.size)_tagIndexCache.delete(tag);
              }
            }
            return{added:0,removed,errors:[]};
          }
          let added=0,removed=0;
          const errors=[];
          for(const id of changedIds){
            try{
              const fromNode=fromState&&fromState.nodes&&fromState.nodes[id];
              const toNode=toState&&toState.nodes&&toState.nodes[id];
              const fromDeleted=fromNode&&fromNode._deleted;
              const toDeleted=toNode&&toNode._deleted;
              const fromTags=(fromNode&&!fromDeleted)?nodeAllTags(fromNode):new Set();
              const toTags=(toNode&&!toDeleted)?nodeAllTags(toNode):new Set();
              
              for(const t of fromTags){
                if(toTags.has(t))continue;
                const set=_tagIndexCache.get(t);
                if(set){
                  if(set.delete(id))removed++;
                  if(!set.size)_tagIndexCache.delete(t);
                }
              }
              
              for(const t of toTags){
                if(fromTags.has(t))continue;
                let set=_tagIndexCache.get(t);
                if(!set){set=new Set();_tagIndexCache.set(t,set);}
                if(!set.has(id)){set.add(id);added++;}
              }
            }catch(e){errors.push(`id=${id}: ${e&&e.message||e}`);}
          }
          return{added,removed,errors};
        }
        
        function searchInvertedUpdate(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts){
          if(!_searchInverted)return{added:0,removed:0,errors:[]};
          if(opts&&opts.subtreeCleanup){
            let removed=0;
            for(const id of(opts.subtreeIds||changedIds)){
              const doc=_searchInvertedDocs&&_searchInvertedDocs.get(id);
              if(doc){
                for(const term of doc.terms){
                  const set=_searchInverted.get(term);
                  if(set){
                    if(set.delete(id))removed++;
                    if(!set.size)_searchInverted.delete(term);
                  }
                }
                _searchInvertedDocs.delete(id);
              }
            }
            return{added:0,removed,errors:[]};
          }
          let added=0,removed=0;
          const errors=[];
          for(const id of changedIds){
            try{
              const fromNode=fromState&&fromState.nodes&&fromState.nodes[id];
              const toNode=toState&&toState.nodes&&toState.nodes[id];
              const fromDeleted=fromNode&&fromNode._deleted;
              const toDeleted=toNode&&toNode._deleted;
              
              const oldDoc=_searchInvertedDocs?_searchInvertedDocs.get(id):null;
              const fromTerms=oldDoc?oldDoc.terms:((fromNode&&!fromDeleted)?tokenize(stripHtml(fromNode.text||'')):new Set());
              
              const toTerms=(toNode&&!toDeleted)?tokenize(stripHtml(toNode.text||'')):new Set();
              
              for(const t of fromTerms){
                if(toTerms.has(t))continue;
                const set=_searchInverted.get(t);
                if(set){
                  if(set.delete(id))removed++;
                  if(!set.size)_searchInverted.delete(t);
                }
              }
              
              for(const t of toTerms){
                if(fromTerms.has(t))continue;
                let set=_searchInverted.get(t);
                if(!set){set=new Set();_searchInverted.set(t,set);}
                if(!set.has(id)){set.add(id);added++;}
              }
              
              if(toNode&&!toDeleted){
                _searchInvertedDocs.set(id,{terms:toTerms,updatedAt:toNode.updatedAt||Date.now()});
              }else{
                _searchInvertedDocs.delete(id);
              }
            }catch(e){errors.push(`id=${id}: ${e&&e.message||e}`);}
          }
          return{added,removed,errors};
        }
        
        function backlinkMapUpdate(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts){
          if(!_backlinkMap)return{added:0,removed:0,errors:[]};
          if(opts&&opts.subtreeCleanup){
            let removed=0;
            for(const id of(opts.subtreeIds||changedIds)){
              const reverseSet=_backlinkReverse?_backlinkReverse.get(id):null;
              if(reverseSet){
                for(const docId of reverseSet){
                  const docMap=_backlinkMap.get(docId);
                  if(docMap){
                    docMap.delete(id);
                    removed++;
                    if(!docMap.size)_backlinkMap.delete(docId);
                  }
                }
                _backlinkReverse.delete(id);
              }
            }
            return{added:0,removed,errors:[]};
          }
          let added=0,removed=0;
          const errors=[];
          for(const id of changedIds){
            try{
              const fromNode=fromState&&fromState.nodes&&fromState.nodes[id];
              const toNode=toState&&toState.nodes&&toState.nodes[id];
              const fromDeleted=fromNode&&fromNode._deleted;
              const toDeleted=toNode&&toNode._deleted;
              const fromTxt=(fromNode&&!fromDeleted)?stripHtml(fromNode.text||''):'';
              const toTxt=(toNode&&!toDeleted)?stripHtml(toNode.text||''):'';
              const fromWikis=extractWikiTitles(fromTxt);
              const toWikis=extractWikiTitles(toTxt);
              
              for(const title of fromWikis){
                if(toWikis.has(title))continue;
                const docId=resolveDocIdByTitle(title);
                if(!docId)continue;
                const docMap=_backlinkMap.get(docId);
                if(docMap){
                  if(docMap.has(id)){
                    const c=docMap.get(id);
                    if(c>1)docMap.set(id,c-1);
                    else docMap.delete(id);
                    removed++;
                  }
                  if(!docMap.size)_backlinkMap.delete(docId);
                }
                if(_backlinkReverse){
                  const rs=_backlinkReverse.get(id);
                  if(rs){rs.delete(docId);if(!rs.size)_backlinkReverse.delete(id);}
                }
              }
              
              for(const title of toWikis){
                if(fromWikis.has(title))continue;
                const docId=resolveDocIdByTitle(title);
                if(!docId)continue;
                let docMap=_backlinkMap.get(docId);
                if(!docMap){docMap=new Map();_backlinkMap.set(docId,docMap);}
                docMap.set(id,(docMap.get(id)||0)+1);
                added++;
                if(_backlinkReverse){
                  let rs=_backlinkReverse.get(id);
                  if(!rs){rs=new Set();_backlinkReverse.set(id,rs);}
                  rs.add(docId);
                }
              }
            }catch(e){errors.push(`id=${id}: ${e&&e.message||e}`);}
          }
          return{added,removed,errors};
        }
        
        
        function _searchScanStep(ids,i,deadline,idx,docs){
          while(i<ids.length){
            const id=ids[i];i++;
            const n=(state&&state.nodes)?state.nodes[id]:null;
            if(n&&!n._deleted){
              const terms=tokenize(stripHtml(n.text||''));
              for(const t of terms){let s=idx.get(t);if(!s){s=new Set();idx.set(t,s);}s.add(id);}
              docs.set(id,{terms:terms,updatedAt:n.updatedAt||0});
            }
            if(_l7Now()>=deadline)break;
          }
          return i;
        }
        function buildSearchInverted(){
          if(_searchInverted)return _searchInverted;
          const idx=new Map(),docs=new Map();
          try{
            const nodes=(state&&state.nodes)||{};
            const ids=Object.keys(nodes);
            let i=0;
            while(i<ids.length)i=_searchScanStep(ids,i,Infinity,idx,docs);
          }catch(e){}
          _searchInverted=idx;
          _searchInvertedDocs=docs;
          return _searchInverted;
        }
        
        function _backlinkScanStep(ids,i,deadline,map,rev){
          while(i<ids.length){
            const id=ids[i];i++;
            const n=(state&&state.nodes)?state.nodes[id]:null;
            if(n&&!n._deleted){
              const titles=extractWikiTitles(stripHtml(n.text||''));
              for(const title of titles){
                const docId=resolveDocIdByTitle(title);
                if(!docId)continue;
                let dm=map.get(docId);
                if(!dm){dm=new Map();map.set(docId,dm);}
                dm.set(id,(dm.get(id)||0)+1);
                let rs=rev.get(id);
                if(!rs){rs=new Set();rev.set(id,rs);}
                rs.add(docId);
              }
            }
            if(_l7Now()>=deadline)break;
          }
          return i;
        }
        function buildBacklinkMap(){
          if(_backlinkMap)return _backlinkMap;
          const map=new Map(),rev=new Map();
          try{
            const nodes=(state&&state.nodes)||{};
            const ids=Object.keys(nodes);
            let i=0;
            while(i<ids.length)i=_backlinkScanStep(ids,i,Infinity,map,rev);
          }catch(e){}
          _backlinkMap=map;
          _backlinkReverse=rev;
          return _backlinkMap;
        }
        
        function _l6UpdateIncrementalIndexesImpl(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts){
          opts=opts||{};
          
          const _subtreeIds=opts.subtreeIds;
          if((!changedIds||!changedIds.size)&&!_subtreeIds)return{ok:true,errors:[],added:0,removed:0};
          
          const snapshot={
            calIndex:_calIndexCache,
            tagIndex:_tagIndexCache,
            searchInverted:_searchInverted,
            backlinkMap:_backlinkMap,
          };
          const errors=[];
          let added=0,removed=0;
          try{
            const calRes=calIndexUpdate(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts);
            added+=calRes.added;removed+=calRes.removed;
            if(calRes.errors&&calRes.errors.length)errors.push('calIndex: '+calRes.errors.join(';'));
            const tagRes=tagIndexUpdate(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts);
            added+=tagRes.added;removed+=tagRes.removed;
            if(tagRes.errors&&tagRes.errors.length)errors.push('tagIndex: '+tagRes.errors.join(';'));
            const searchRes=searchInvertedUpdate(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts);
            added+=searchRes.added;removed+=searchRes.removed;
            if(searchRes.errors&&searchRes.errors.length)errors.push('searchInverted: '+searchRes.errors.join(';'));
            const backRes=backlinkMapUpdate(changedIds,fromState,toState,opts);
            added+=backRes.added;removed+=backRes.removed;
            if(backRes.errors&&backRes.errors.length)errors.push('backlinkMap: '+backRes.errors.join(';'));
          }catch(e){
            
            try{_calIndexCache=snapshot.calIndex;}catch(_){}
            try{_tagIndexCache=snapshot.tagIndex;}catch(_){}
            try{_searchInverted=snapshot.searchInverted;}catch(_){}
            try{_backlinkMap=snapshot.backlinkMap;}catch(_){}
            return{ok:false,errors:[String(e&&e.message||e)],added:0,removed:0};
          }
          if(errors.length){
            return{ok:false,errors,added,removed};
          }
          return{ok:true,errors:[],added,removed};
        }
        
        function reportIndexFailure(payload){
          const buf=window.__juIndexFailures;
          buf.push({
            op:payload.op||'unknown',
            index:payload.index||'unknown',
            changedCount:(payload.changedIds&&payload.changedIds.size)||0,
            error:String(payload.error||'unknown'),
            ts:payload.ts||Date.now()
          });
          while(buf.length>_INDEX_FAIL_CAP)buf.shift();
          __juIndexFailureStreak++;
          if(__juIndexFailureStreak>=_INDEX_FAILURE_STRIKE){
            
            _calIndexCache=null;
            _tagIndexCache=null;
            _searchInverted=null;
            _backlinkMap=null;
            __juIndexFailureStreak=0;
          }
          if(typeof console!=='undefined'&&console.warn){
            console.warn('[JU-IndexAgent] incremental update failed, fallback to lazy rebuild',payload);
          }
        }
        
        function resetIndexFailureStreak(){
          __juIndexFailureStreak=0;
        }
        
        function l6InjectionFailure(tag,op,err,t){
          try{
            reportIndexFailure({op:tag+':'+(op||'unknown'),index:'injection',changedIds:null,error:err,ts:t});
          }catch(_e){}
          _calIndexCache=null;
          _tagIndexCache=null;
          _searchInverted=null;
          _backlinkMap=null;
        }
        window.__juIndexFailureStreak=0;
        
        
        const INDEX_WORKER_SRC = `
        'use strict';
        
        let STORE=null;
        
        
        function buildStore(){return {gen:0,cal:new Map(),tag:new Map(),search:new Map(),searchDocs:new Map(),backlink:new Map(),backlinkReverse:new Map()};}
        function post(msg){try{self.postMessage(msg);}catch(e){}}
        function bucketAdd(map,key,id){let s=map.get(key);if(!s){s=new Set();map.set(key,s);}if(!s.has(id)){s.add(id);return 1;}return 0;}
        function bucketDel(map,key,id){const s=map.get(key);if(!s)return 0;const had=s.delete(id);if(s.size===0)map.delete(key);return had?1:0;}
        function clearStore(){if(!STORE)return;STORE.cal=new Map();STORE.tag=new Map();STORE.search=new Map();STORE.searchDocs=new Map();STORE.backlink=new Map();STORE.backlinkReverse=new Map();}
        
        function digestMap(map){
          const arr=[];
          map.forEach(function(ids,key){const a=Array.from(ids);a.sort();arr.push([String(key),a]);});
          arr.sort(function(x,y){return x[0]<y[0]?-1:(x[0]>y[0]?1:0);});
          return JSON.stringify(arr);
        }
        
        function digestMap2(map){
          const arr=[];
          map.forEach(function(inner,key){
            const rows=[];
            inner.forEach(function(count,k2){rows.push([String(k2),Number(count)||0]);});
            rows.sort(function(x,y){return x[0]<y[0]?-1:(x[0]>y[0]?1:0);});
            arr.push([String(key),rows]);
          });
          arr.sort(function(x,y){return x[0]<y[0]?-1:(x[0]>y[0]?1:0);});
          return JSON.stringify(arr);
        }
        
        function digestAll(){
          return {cal:digestMap(STORE.cal),tag:digestMap(STORE.tag),search:digestMap(STORE.search),
            searchDocs:digestMap(STORE.searchDocs),backlink:digestMap2(STORE.backlink)};
        }
        
        function countOf(arr){
          const m=new Map();
          const a=arr||[];
          for(let i=0;i<a.length;i++){const k=String(a[i]);m.set(k,(m.get(k)||0)+1);}
          return m;
        }
        
        function applySubtreeCleanup(ids){
          const cal=STORE.cal,tag=STORE.tag,search=STORE.search,docs=STORE.searchDocs,back=STORE.backlink,rev=STORE.backlinkReverse;
          let removed=0,ks,i,j,s,id;
          ks=Array.from(cal.keys());
          for(i=0;i<ks.length;i++){s=cal.get(ks[i]);if(!s)continue;for(j=0;j<ids.length;j++){if(s.delete(ids[j]))removed++;}if(s.size===0)cal.delete(ks[i]);}
          ks=Array.from(tag.keys());
          for(i=0;i<ks.length;i++){s=tag.get(ks[i]);if(!s)continue;for(j=0;j<ids.length;j++){if(s.delete(ids[j]))removed++;}if(s.size===0)tag.delete(ks[i]);}
          for(j=0;j<ids.length;j++){
            id=String(ids[j]);
            const terms=docs.get(id);
            if(terms){
              terms.forEach(function(t){const s2=search.get(t);if(s2){if(s2.delete(id))removed++;if(s2.size===0)search.delete(t);}});
              docs.delete(id);
            }
            const docIds=rev.get(id);
            if(docIds){
              docIds.forEach(function(docId){
                const dm=back.get(docId);
                if(dm){if(dm.delete(id))removed++;if(dm.size===0)back.delete(docId);}
              });
              rev.delete(id);
            }
          }
          return removed;
        }
        
        function applyDeltaCore(m){
          const cal=STORE.cal,tag=STORE.tag,search=STORE.search,docs=STORE.searchDocs,back=STORE.backlink,rev=STORE.backlinkReverse;
          const opts=m.opts||{};
          let added=0,removed=0;
          if(opts.subtreeCleanup){
            const ids=(opts.subtreeIds&&opts.subtreeIds.length)?opts.subtreeIds:(m.changedIds||[]);
            removed+=applySubtreeCleanup(ids);
            return {added:0,removed:removed};
          }
          const pre=m.pre||{},post=m.post||{};
          const ids=m.changedIds||[];
          for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++){
            const id=String(ids[i]);
            const pn=pre[id],tn=post[id];
            const fromDate=pn&&pn.date,toDate=tn&&tn.date;
            const fromDeleted=!!(pn&&pn._deleted),toDeleted=!!(tn&&tn._deleted);
            if(fromDate&&(toDeleted||toDate!==fromDate))removed+=bucketDel(cal,String(fromDate),id);
            if(toDate&&!toDeleted&&(toDate!==fromDate||fromDeleted))added+=bucketAdd(cal,String(toDate),id);
            const fromTags=(pn&&!fromDeleted)?(pn.tags||[]):[];
            const toTags=(tn&&!toDeleted)?(tn.tags||[]):[];
            const fromSet=new Set(fromTags),toSet=new Set(toTags);
            fromSet.forEach(function(t){if(!toSet.has(t))removed+=bucketDel(tag,String(t),id);});
            toSet.forEach(function(t){if(!fromSet.has(t))added+=bucketAdd(tag,String(t),id);});
            
            const oldTerms=docs.get(id);
            const fromTerms=oldTerms||((pn&&!fromDeleted)?(pn.terms||[]):[]);
            const toTerms=(tn&&!toDeleted)?(tn.terms||[]):[];
            const fromTermSet=new Set(fromTerms),toTermSet=new Set(toTerms);
            fromTermSet.forEach(function(t){if(!toTermSet.has(t))removed+=bucketDel(search,String(t),id);});
            toTermSet.forEach(function(t){if(!fromTermSet.has(t))added+=bucketAdd(search,String(t),id);});
            if(tn&&!toDeleted)docs.set(id,toTermSet);else docs.delete(id);
            
            const fromWiki=(pn&&!fromDeleted)?(pn.wikiIds||[]):[];
            const toWiki=(tn&&!toDeleted)?(tn.wikiIds||[]):[];
            const fromCnt=countOf(fromWiki),toCnt=countOf(toWiki);
            if(fromCnt.size||toCnt.size){
              fromCnt.forEach(function(c,docId){
                const n=c-(toCnt.get(docId)||0);
                if(n<=0)return;
                const dm=back.get(docId);
                if(dm&&dm.has(id)){
                  const next=(dm.get(id)||0)-n;
                  if(next>0)dm.set(id,next);else dm.delete(id);
                  removed+=n;
                  if(dm.size===0)back.delete(docId);
                }
                const rs=rev.get(id);
                if(rs){rs.delete(docId);if(rs.size===0)rev.delete(id);}
              });
              toCnt.forEach(function(c,docId){
                const n=c-(fromCnt.get(docId)||0);
                if(n<=0)return;
                let dm=back.get(docId);
                if(!dm){dm=new Map();back.set(docId,dm);}
                dm.set(id,(dm.get(id)||0)+n);
                added+=n;
                let rs=rev.get(id);
                if(!rs){rs=new Set();rev.set(id,rs);}
                rs.add(docId);
              });
            }
          }
          return {added:added,removed:removed};
        }
        
        const PROGRESS_EVERY=2000;
        
        function fullRebuildCore(nodes,report){
          const cal=new Map(),tag=new Map(),search=new Map(),docs=new Map(),back=new Map(),rev=new Map();
          const arr=nodes||[];
          for(let i=0;i<arr.length;i++){
            const n=arr[i];if(!n||n._deleted)continue;
            const id=String(n.id);
            if(n.date)bucketAdd(cal,String(n.date),id);
            if(!n._isFolder){const ts=n.tags||[];for(let j=0;j<ts.length;j++){if(ts[j])bucketAdd(tag,String(ts[j]),id);}}
            
            const termSet=new Set();
            const tms=n.terms||[];
            for(let k=0;k<tms.length;k++){if(tms[k]){const t=String(tms[k]);bucketAdd(search,t,id);termSet.add(t);}}
            docs.set(id,termSet);
            
            const wikis=n.wikiIds||[];
            for(let k=0;k<wikis.length;k++){
              const docId=String(wikis[k]);
              if(!docId)continue;
              let dm=back.get(docId);
              if(!dm){dm=new Map();back.set(docId,dm);}
              dm.set(id,(dm.get(id)||0)+1);
              let rs=rev.get(id);
              if(!rs){rs=new Set();rev.set(id,rs);}
              rs.add(docId);
            }
            
            if(report&&((i+1)%PROGRESS_EVERY===0||i===arr.length-1))report(i+1,arr.length);
          }
          return {cal:cal,tag:tag,search:search,docs:docs,back:back,rev:rev};
        }
        function onMsg(e){
          const m=(e&&e.data)||{};
          const gen=m.gen||0;
          try{
            switch(m.type){
              case 'init':
                STORE=buildStore();STORE.gen=gen;
                post({type:'ready',gen:gen,ok:true,errors:[],stub:true,
                  digest:digestAll()});
                return;
              case 'fullRebuild':{
                if(!STORE)STORE=buildStore();
                STORE.gen=gen;
                
                post({type:'progress',gen:gen,phase:'fullRebuild',done:0,total:(m.nodes&&m.nodes.length)||0,stub:true});
                const r=fullRebuildCore(m.nodes,function(done,total){
                  post({type:'progress',gen:gen,phase:'fullRebuild',done:done,total:total,stub:true});
                });
                STORE.cal=r.cal;STORE.tag=r.tag;STORE.search=r.search;STORE.searchDocs=r.docs;
                STORE.backlink=r.back;STORE.backlinkReverse=r.rev;
                post({type:'ack',gen:gen,seq:m.seq||0,ok:true,errors:[],added:r.cal.size+r.tag.size+r.search.size+r.back.size,removed:0,stub:true,
                  rebuilt:true,digest:digestAll()});
                return;}
              case 'applyDelta':{
                if(!STORE)STORE=buildStore();
                STORE.gen=gen;
                const r=applyDeltaCore(m);
                post({type:'ack',gen:gen,seq:m.seq||0,ok:true,errors:[],added:r.added,removed:r.removed,stub:true,
                  digest:digestAll()});
                return;}
              case 'query':{
                if(!STORE)STORE=buildStore();
                STORE.gen=gen;
                const kind=m.kind,args=m.args||{};
                let data=[];
                if(kind==='calIndex'){const s=STORE.cal.get(String(args.date));data=s?Array.from(s):[];}
                else if(kind==='tagIndex'){const s=STORE.tag.get(String(args.tag));data=s?Array.from(s):[];}
                else if(kind==='search'){const s=STORE.search.get(String(args.term));data=s?Array.from(s):[];}
                else if(kind==='searchDocs'){const s=STORE.searchDocs.get(String(args.id));data=s?Array.from(s):[];}
                else if(kind==='backlink'){const dm=STORE.backlink.get(String(args.docId));data=dm?Array.from(dm.keys()):[];}
                else if(kind==='digest'){data=[digestMap(STORE.cal),digestMap(STORE.tag),digestMap(STORE.search),digestMap(STORE.searchDocs),digestMap2(STORE.backlink)];}
                else if(kind==='digestAll'){data=digestAll();}
                post({type:'result',gen:gen,reqId:m.reqId||0,ok:true,data:data,stub:true,errors:[]});
                return;}
              case 'invalidate':
                clearStore();
                post({type:'ack',gen:gen,seq:m.seq||0,ok:true,errors:[],added:0,removed:0,stub:true,
                  digest:digestAll()});
                return;
              case 'dispose':
                STORE=null;
                post({type:'done',gen:gen,ok:true,errors:[]});
                return;
              default:
                post({type:'error',gen:gen,where:'onMsg',message:'unknown type: '+String(m.type),stack:''});
                return;
            }
          }catch(err){
            post({type:'error',gen:gen,where:'onMsg:'+String(m.type),message:String((err&&err.message)||err),stack:String((err&&err.stack)||'')});
          }
        }
        self.addEventListener('message',onMsg);
        post({type:'ready',gen:0,ok:true,errors:[],stub:true,boot:true,digest:{cal:'[]',tag:'[]',search:'[]',searchDocs:'[]',backlink:'[]'}});
        `;
        let _indexWorkerUrl=null;
        function getIndexWorkerUrl(){

        function _indexWorkerOnMessage(ev){
          const m=(ev&&ev.data)||{};
          if(m.gen&&m.gen!==_indexWorkerGen)return; 
          switch(m.type){
            case 'ready':
              _indexWorkerReady=true;
              _indexWorkerReadyStrikes=0;                 
              if(_indexWorkerReadyTimer){try{clearTimeout(_indexWorkerReadyTimer);}catch(e){}_indexWorkerReadyTimer=0;}
              window.__juIndexWorker.ready=true;
              window.__juIndexWorker.readyStrikes=0;
              window.__juIndexWorker.gen=_indexWorkerGen;
              if(m.stub)window.__juIndexWorker.stub=true;
              
              if(window.__juIndexWorkerMode!=='sync')l7IndexFullRebuild();
              break;
            case 'ack':
              window.__juIndexWorker.acked++;
              if(m.ok===false)l7WorkerFailure('ack',(m.errors&&m.errors.join(';'))||'ack.ok=false');
              if(m.rebuilt)_indexWorkerStoreSynced=true;    
              if(m.digest)_indexShadowReconcile(m);         
              break;
            case 'result':{
              window.__juIndexWorker.results++;
              const p=_indexWorkerPending.get(m.reqId);
              if(p){_indexWorkerPending.delete(m.reqId);if(p.timer)clearTimeout(p.timer);p.resolve(m);}
              break;}
            case 'progress':
              
              window.__juIndexWorker.progressMsgs=(window.__juIndexWorker.progressMsgs||0)+1;
              window.__juIndexWorker.progress=(m.total>0)?Math.round((m.done/m.total)*100):0;

                pct:(m.total>0)?(m.done/m.total):0,label:'绱㈠紩鍏ㄩ噺瀵归綈'});
              break;
            case 'error':
              l7WorkerFailure(m.where||'worker',m.message||'worker error');
              break;
            case 'done':
              break;
            default:break;
          }
        }

        }

        function _l7DigestFromMap(map){
          if(!map)return null;
          const arr=[];
          try{
            map.forEach(function(ids,key){const a=Array.from(ids);a.sort();arr.push([String(key),a]);});
            arr.sort(function(x,y){return x[0]<y[0]?-1:(x[0]>y[0]?1:0);});
          }catch(e){return null;}
          return JSON.stringify(arr);
        }
        
        function _l7DigestFromBacklink(map){
          if(!map)return null;
          const arr=[];
          try{
            map.forEach(function(inner,key){
              const rows=[];
              inner.forEach(function(count,k2){rows.push([String(k2),Number(count)||0]);});
              rows.sort(function(x,y){return x[0]<y[0]?-1:(x[0]>y[0]?1:0);});
              arr.push([String(key),rows]);
            });
            arr.sort(function(x,y){return x[0]<y[0]?-1:(x[0]>y[0]?1:0);});
          }catch(e){return null;}
          return JSON.stringify(arr);
        }

              window.__juIndexWorkerMode='shadow';

        }

              const r=document.createElement('div');r.className='ju-skel-row';
              const l=document.createElement('div');l.className='ju-skel-line';l.style.width=(46+(i%4)*13)+'%';
              r.appendChild(l);wrap.appendChild(r);
            }
          }else{
            for(let i=0;i<n;i++){const l=document.createElement('div');l.className='ju-skel-line';l.style.width=(100-(i%4)*11)+'%';wrap.appendChild(l);}
          }
          const prog=document.createElement('div');prog.className='ju-skel-prog';
          const bar=document.createElement('i');prog.appendChild(bar);wrap.appendChild(prog);
          const hint=document.createElement('div');hint.className='ju-skel-hint';
          hint.textContent=String(opts.hint||'姝ｅ湪鏋勫缓绱㈠紩鈥?);wrap.appendChild(hint);
          wrap._juSkelBar=bar;wrap._juSkelHint=hint;
          st.renders++;st.active++;st.lastKind=String(kind||'block');st.phase='pending';
          return wrap;
        }
        
        
        function l7SkeletonProgress(pct,info){
          const st=window.__juSkeleton;if(!st)return;
          st.pct=Math.max(0,Math.min(1,Number(pct)||0));
          if(info){st.phase=String(info.phase||st.phase);st.done=Number(info.done)||0;st.total=Number(info.total)||0;}
          if(typeof document==='undefined'||!document.querySelectorAll)return;
          if(st.active<=0)return;                    
          const pc=Math.round(st.pct*100);
          const list=document.querySelectorAll('.ju-skel');
          for(let i=0;i<list.length;i++){
            const n=list[i];
            if(n._juSkelBar)n._juSkelBar.style.width=pc+'%';
            if(n._juSkelHint&&info)n._juSkelHint.textContent=String(info.label||'姝ｅ湪鏋勫缓绱㈠紩鈥?)+' '+pc+'%';
          }
        }

        }
        
        function l7TwoPhaseRender(host,opts){
          if(!host)return null;
          opts=opts||{};
          const sk=l7Skeleton(opts.kind||'block',opts);
          try{host.innerHTML='';host.appendChild(sk);}catch(_e){return sk;}
          const done=function(node){try{if(host&&node){host.innerHTML='';host.appendChild(node);}}catch(_e){}};
          Promise.resolve().then(function(){return opts.build?opts.build():null;}).then(function(){
            let node=null;try{node=opts.onData?opts.onData():null;}catch(_e){node=null;}
            done(node);
          },function(err){
            const st=window.__juSkeleton;if(st)st.errors++;
            let node=null;try{node=opts.onError?opts.onError(err):null;}catch(_e){node=null;}
            done(node);
          });
          return sk;
        }
        
        
        function _l7BuildOnce(name,fn){
          const cur=_l7BuildInflight[name];
          if(cur)return cur;                                 
          const p=Promise.resolve().then(fn).then(function(v){
            if(_l7BuildInflight[name]===p)_l7BuildInflight[name]=null;
            return v;
          },function(e){
            if(_l7BuildInflight[name]===p)_l7BuildInflight[name]=null;
            try{l7WorkerFailure('asyncIndex:'+name,e);}catch(_e){}   
            throw e;
          });
          _l7BuildInflight[name]=p;
          return p;
        }
        
        async function _l7ChunkedRun(label,steps,onProgress){
          for(;;){
            const deadline=_l7Now()+_L7_CHUNK_BUDGET_MS;
            const r=steps(deadline)||{done:true};
            const pr=r.progress||{done:0,total:0};
            const info={phase:label,done:pr.done||0,total:pr.total||0,
              pct:(pr.total>0)?((pr.done||0)/pr.total):0,label:label};
            try{l7ProgressEmit('main',info);}catch(_e){}
            if(onProgress){try{onProgress(info);}catch(_e){}}
            if(r.done)return;
            await _l7Yield();
          }
        }
        
        async function l7BuildTagIndexAsync(onProgress){

              i=_tagScanStep(ids,i,deadline,map);
              return {done:i>=ids.length,progress:{done:i,total:ids.length}};
            },onProgress);
            if(_tagIndexCache)return _tagIndexCache;
            if(_l7StateToken()!==token)continue;             
            _tagIndexCache=map;
            return map;
          }
          return _tagIndexCache;
        }

        async function _l7L6QueryAsync(kind,args,onProgress){
          try{
            if(kind==='calIndex'){const idx=await l7EnsureCalIndexAsync(onProgress);const s=idx?idx.get(String(args&&args.date)):null;return s?Array.from(s):[];}
            if(kind==='tagIndex'){const map=await l7EnsureTagIndexAsync(onProgress);const s=map?map.get(String(args&&args.tag)):null;return s?Array.from(s):[];}
            if(kind==='search'){const idx=await l7EnsureSearchInvertedAsync(onProgress);const s=idx?idx.get(String(args&&args.term)):null;return s?Array.from(s):[];}
            if(kind==='searchDocs'){await l7EnsureSearchInvertedAsync(onProgress);const v=_searchInvertedDocs?_searchInvertedDocs.get(String(args&&args.id)):null;return(v&&v.terms)?Array.from(v.terms):[];}
            if(kind==='backlink'){const map=await l7EnsureBacklinkMapAsync(onProgress);const dm=map?map.get(String(args&&args.docId)):null;return dm?Array.from(dm.keys()):[];}
          }catch(e){
            l7WorkerFailure('l6queryAsync',e);
            try{return _l7L6Query(kind,args);}catch(_e){}
          }
          return [];
        }

        function commit(action,{silent=false,snapshot=false,persistOnly=false,dirtyText=null,dirtyDocs=undefined,light=false,noHistory=false,noRollback=false,historyMode,op,opArgs}={}){
          if(ui.locked)return state; 
          
          const r=__juProduceSafe(state,action);
          if(!r.ok){
            console.error('[JU] commit action failed, rolling back',r.error);
            if(!silent)scheduleNotify();
            return state;
          }
          let draft=r.next;
          let _patch; 
          let _l8Patch=null; 
          
          if(draft===null||typeof draft!=='object'){
            console.error('[JU] commit action returned non-object, rolling back',draft);
            if(!silent)scheduleNotify();
            return state;
          }
          
          if(draft===state){
            draft=Array.isArray(state)?state.slice():copyPlain(state);
          }
          
          const _mode=resolveHistoryMode({historyMode,snapshot,light,persistOnly,noHistory,op});
          if(_mode!=='never'){
            const _paths=collectModifiedPaths(state,draft);
            
            if(_paths.indexOf('updatedAt')<0)_paths.push('updatedAt');
            if(_paths.length){
              const _pre=extractAtPaths(state,_paths);
              const _post=extractAtPaths(draft,_paths);
              let _patch;
              try{
                _patch=buildPatch({t:Date.now(),op:inferOp({op}),mode:_mode,paths:_paths,pre:_pre,post:_post,meta:{}});
                
                if(typeof window!=='undefined'&&window.__juInjectPatchFailure){throw new TypeError('L4-T19: simulated patch serialize failure');}
                
                if(opArgs&&opArgs.failSerialize){throw new TypeError('L4-T19: failSerialize triggered');}
                JSON.stringify(_patch);  
              }catch(e){
                
                reportPatchFailure({op:inferOp({op}),mode:_mode,paths:_paths,error:e&&e.message||String(e),ts:Date.now()});
                _patch=null;  
              }
              _l8Patch=_patch; 
              if(_patch){
                if(_mode==='merge')_patch.groupKey=inferGroupKey({op,opArgs});
                tryMergeWithLast(historyPast,_patch);
              }
            }
            
            historyFuture.length=0;
          }else{
            
            historyFuture.length=0;
          }
          
          draft.updatedAt=Math.max(Date.now(),(draft.updatedAt||0)+1);
          if(snapshot){
            try{
              const tid=findTouchedNodeId(state,draft);
              if(tid){
                const owner=findOwningTopDocId(tid,draft);
                if(owner&&owner!==draft.rootId)ui._lastTouchedDocId=owner;
              }
            }catch(e){}
          }
          
          const _oldState=state;
          state=draft;
          if(_patch&&_patch.paths&&_patch.paths.length&&!__juInMerge){
            try{
              const _changedIds=extractChangedIds(_patch.paths,_patch.meta);
              
              const _delIds=new Set();
              for(const p of _patch.paths||[]){
                const m1=/^nodes\.([^.]+)$/.exec(p); 
                const m2=/^nodes\.([^.]+)\._deleted$/.exec(p); 
                if(m1){
                  const id=m1[1];
                  const preVal=_patch.pre[p];
                  const postVal=_patch.post&&_patch.post[p];
                  
                  if(preVal!==undefined&&postVal===undefined)_delIds.add(id);
                }
                if(m2){
                  const id=m2[1];
                  const preVal=_patch.pre[p];
                  const postVal=_patch.post&&_patch.post[p];
                  
                  if(!preVal&&postVal===true)_delIds.add(id);
                }
              }
              if(_changedIds.size){
                const _res=updateIncrementalIndexes(_changedIds,_oldState,state,{op:_patch.op});
                if(!_res.ok){
                  reportIndexFailure({op:_patch.op,index:_res.errors[0]||'unknown',changedIds:_changedIds,error:_res.errors.join('; '),ts:_patch.t});
                  _calIndexCache=null;_tagIndexCache=null;_searchInverted=null;_backlinkMap=null;
                }else{
                  resetIndexFailureStreak();
                }
              }
              if(_delIds.size){
                const _res2=updateIncrementalIndexes(new Set(),_oldState,state,{subtreeCleanup:true,subtreeIds:_delIds,op:(_patch.op||'unknown')+':remove'});
                if(!_res2.ok){
                  reportIndexFailure({op:(_patch.op||'unknown')+':remove',index:_res2.errors[0]||'unknown',changedIds:_delIds,error:_res2.errors.join('; '),ts:_patch.t});
                  _calIndexCache=null;_tagIndexCache=null;_searchInverted=null;_backlinkMap=null;
                }else{
                  resetIndexFailureStreak();
                }
              }
            }catch(_e){l6InjectionFailure('commit',_patch&&_patch.op,_e,_patch&&_patch.t);}
          }
          
          markPersistFromPatch(_l8Patch,_oldState,state,_l8Patch&&_l8Patch.op);
          
          if(!light&&ui&&ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey){
            
            try{sessionStorage.setItem(PENDING_PLAIN_KEY,JSON.stringify({st:_pendingPlainState(state),ts:Date.now(),ttl:PENDING_PLAIN_TTL}));}catch(e){}
          }
          const _clearWC=(dirtyText===null)?snapshot:dirtyText;
          if(_clearWC){
            if(Array.isArray(dirtyDocs)){
              dirtyDocs.forEach(d=>{if(d)_wordCountCache.delete(d);});
            }else if(typeof dirtyDocs==='string'){
              _wordCountCache.delete(dirtyDocs);
            }else if(dirtyDocs===null){
              
            }else{
              _wordCountCache.clear();
            }
          }
          const touched=(typeof dirtyDocs==='string')?[dirtyDocs]:Array.isArray(dirtyDocs)?dirtyDocs:null;
          
          const _clearTag=(dirtyText===true);
          if(touched){
            _calRecurCache=null;_yearCountsCache=null;_calDayCache=null;_renderDayCache=null;
          }else if(_clearWC){
            _calRecurCache=null;_yearCountsCache=null;_calDayCache=null;_renderDayCache=null;
          }
          if(!silent)scheduleNotify();
          else if(persistOnly)schedulePersist();
          scheduleEncFallback(); 
          renderIntro(); 
          
          try{if(typeof _mbOnCommit==='function')_mbOnCommit(true,null);}catch(e){}
          return state;
        }
        function subscribe(fn){listeners.add(fn);return()=>listeners.delete(fn);}
        function scheduleNotify(){
          if(notifyPending)return;
          notifyPending=true;
          queueMicrotask(()=>{
            notifyPending=false;
            listeners.forEach(fn=>{try{fn(state);}catch(e){console.error(e);}});
            schedulePersist();
          });
        }
        function schedulePersist(){
          clearTimeout(persistTimer);
          persistTimer=setTimeout(runPersist,300);
        }
        
        let _persistFailCount=0;
        const _PERSIST_MAX_RETRY=6;
        async function runPersist(){
          if(ui.locked)return;
          if(_persistRunning){_persistDirty=true;return;}
          _persistRunning=true;
          _persistDirty=false;
          const mySeq=++_persistSeq;
          
          try{
            
            const snapshot=getState();
            
            const json=JSON.stringify(snapshot,_ORIGIN_STRIP);
            
            const _l8DirtyCnt=_dirtyNodes.size+_removedNodes.size;
            const _l8DesktopDirty=(IS_DESKTOP&&_l8DirtyCnt>0&&l8PersistActive());
            if(IS_DESKTOP&&_persistMode==='patch'&&_l8DirtyCnt&&l8PersistActive()&&!_l8CheckpointPending){ 
              const _pb=buildPersistBatch();
              if(_pb.reason==='over-threshold'){
                markFullOnce(); 
              }else if(_pb.batches.length){
                if(await l8PersistSendBatches(_pb)){
                  _lastWrittenJson=JSON.stringify(getState(),_ORIGIN_STRIP); 
                  _broadcastRev(snapshot);
                  return; 
                }
                return; 
              }
            }
            
            const _l8CheckpointNow=(IS_DESKTOP&&_l8CheckpointPending);
            if(json===_lastWrittenJson&&!_l8DesktopDirty&&!_l8CheckpointNow){
              return;
            }
            state._rev=(state._rev|0)+1;
            const json2=JSON.stringify(snapshot,_ORIGIN_STRIP);
            if(ui.encrypted && ui.encKey){
              const payload=await encryptState(snapshot,ui.encKey);
              _lastEncPayload=payload; 
              if(mySeq!==_persistSeq){schedulePersist();return;}
              
              try{sessionStorage.setItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY,JSON.stringify({payload,ts:Date.now(),ttl:60000}));}catch(e){}
              if(await writeEncryptedStore(payload)){
                try{localStorage.removeItem(STORE_KEY);localStorage.removeItem(PENDING_KEY);}catch(e){}
                try{sessionStorage.removeItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY);}catch(e){}
                try{sessionStorage.removeItem(PENDING_PLAIN_KEY);}catch(e){} 
                _lastWrittenJson=json2;markDiskConfirmed(snapshot.nodes);
                _persistFailCount=0; 
                _broadcastRev(snapshot);
              }else{
                
                try{toast('鍔犲瘑淇濆瓨澶辫触锛氬凡淇濈暀鏄庢枃澶囦唤锛岃灏藉揩瀵煎嚭鏁版嵁');}catch(e){}
              }
            }else{
              if(IS_DESKTOP){
                
                flushDesktopSave();
                _lastWrittenJson=json2;markDiskConfirmed(snapshot.nodes);
                _persistFailCount=0; 
                
                if(_dirtyNodes.size)_dirtyNodes.clear();
                if(_removedNodes.size)_removedNodes.clear();
                _dirtySinceFull=0;_l8CheckpointPending=false; 
                _broadcastRev(snapshot);
                saveAutoBackup();
              }else{
                if(json2.length>4500000)throw new Error('鏁版嵁杩囧ぇ');
                localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY,json2);
                try{localStorage.removeItem(PENDING_KEY);}catch(e){}
                _lastWrittenJson=json2;markDiskConfirmed(snapshot.nodes);
                _persistFailCount=0; 
                _broadcastRev(snapshot);
                saveAutoBackup();
              }
            }
          }catch(e){
            console.warn('persist failed',e);
            _persistFailCount++; 
            try{toast('淇濆瓨澶辫触锛氬瓨鍌ㄧ┖闂翠笉瓒筹紝璇峰鍑哄浠?);}catch(_){}
          }
          finally{
            _persistRunning=false;
            if(_persistFailCount>=_PERSIST_MAX_RETRY){
              
              _persistDirty=false;
              return;
            }
            if(_persistDirty||mySeq!==_persistSeq){_persistDirty=false;schedulePersist();}
          }
        }
        
        function _broadcastRev(snap){
          try{
            if(STORE_BC_INSTANCE){
              
              STORE_BC_INSTANCE.postMessage({rev:(snap._rev|0)||0,updatedAt:(snap.updatedAt||0)});
            }
          }catch(e){}
        }
        
        const PENDING_SYNC_KEY='juju.pending.v1';
        
        const PENDING_PLAIN_KEY='juju.pending.plain';
        const PENDING_PLAIN_TTL=600000; 
        
        function _pendingPlainState(s){
          const out={rootId:s.rootId,updatedAt:s.updatedAt,nodes:s.nodes};
          if(Array.isArray(s.trashed)&&s.trashed.length)out.trashed=s.trashed;
          if(Array.isArray(s.dbFields))out.dbFields=s.dbFields;
          if(s.tagColors&&Object.keys(s.tagColors).length)out.tagColors=s.tagColors;
          if(Array.isArray(s.contacts)&&s.contacts.length)out.contacts=s.contacts;
          if(Array.isArray(s.templates)&&s.templates.length)out.templates=s.templates;
          
          if(Array.isArray(s._localCreated)&&s._localCreated.length)out._localCreated=s._localCreated;
          
          if(s._tombstones&&typeof s._tombstones==='object'){
            const keys=Object.keys(s._tombstones);
            if(keys.length){
              const _now=Date.now();
              const fresh={};
              for(const k of keys){const _ts=+s._tombstones[k];if(_ts&&_now-_ts<=30000)fresh[k]=_ts;}
              if(Object.keys(fresh).length)out._tombstones=fresh;
            }
          }
          return out;
        }
        
        let _lastEncPayload=null;
        let _encFallbackTimer=null; 
        
        function scheduleEncFallback(){
          if(!(ui&&ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey))return;
          clearTimeout(_encFallbackTimer); 
          _encFallbackTimer=setTimeout(async()=>{
            _encFallbackTimer=null;
            try{
              if(!(ui&&ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey))return;
              
              const ua0=state.updatedAt;
              const payload=await encryptState(getState(),ui.encKey);
              if(state.updatedAt!==ua0)return;
              _lastEncPayload=payload;
              try{sessionStorage.setItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY,JSON.stringify({payload,ts:Date.now(),ttl:60000}));}catch(e){}
              
              try{sessionStorage.removeItem(PENDING_PLAIN_KEY);}catch(e){}
            }catch(e){}
          },200);
        }
        function _flushPendingSession(payload){
          if(!(ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey))return;
          const p=payload||_lastEncPayload;
          if(!p)return;
          try{
            sessionStorage.setItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY,JSON.stringify({payload:p,ts:Date.now(),ttl:60000}));
          }catch(e){}
        }
        async function recoverPendingEncrypted(){
          try{
            if(!(ui&&ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey))return;
            const merged=[];
            
            const pend=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY)||'null');
            if(pend&&pend.payload){
              if(Date.now()-(pend.ts||0)<=60000){
                const st=await decryptState(pend.payload,ui.encKey);
                if(st&&st.nodes)merged.push(st);
              }
              sessionStorage.removeItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY);
            }
            
            const plain=JSON.parse(sessionStorage.getItem(PENDING_PLAIN_KEY)||'null');
            if(plain&&plain.st&&plain.st.nodes&&Date.now()-(plain.ts||0)<=PENDING_PLAIN_TTL){
              merged.push(plain.st);
            }
            sessionStorage.removeItem(PENDING_PLAIN_KEY);
            if(!merged.length)return;
            const _strip=(k,v)=>k==='_rev'||k==='_origin'||k==='_localCreated'||k==='_tombstones'?undefined:v; 
            const before=JSON.stringify(state,_strip);
            
            merged.forEach(st=>mergeRemoteState(st));
            if(JSON.stringify(state,_strip)!==before){scheduleNotify();try{toast('宸蹭粠宕╂簝鍓嶇殑澶囦唤鎭㈠');}catch(e){}}
          }catch(e){}
        }
        const _logTimers=new Map();
        function flushPendingLogs(){
          const keys=Array.from(_logTimers.keys());
          keys.forEach(key=>{
            const entry=_logTimers.get(key);
            clearTimeout(entry.timer);
            _logTimers.delete(key);
            try{entry.commit();}catch(e){}
          });
        }
        function flushPersistNow(){
          if(ui.locked)return;
          flushPendingLogs();
          clearTimeout(persistTimer);
          
          const snapshot=getState();
          const json=JSON.stringify(snapshot,_ORIGIN_STRIP);
          if(json===_lastWrittenJson)return;
          state._rev=(state._rev|0)+1;
          const json2=JSON.stringify(getState(),_ORIGIN_STRIP);
          try{
            if(ui.encrypted && ui.encKey){
              
              _flushPendingSession();
              encryptState(snapshot,ui.encKey).then(async payload=>{
                try{
                  _lastEncPayload=payload; 
                  try{sessionStorage.setItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY,JSON.stringify({payload,ts:Date.now(),ttl:60000}));}catch(e){}
                  if(await writeEncryptedStore(payload)){
                    try{sessionStorage.removeItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY);}catch(e){}
                    _lastWrittenJson=json2;markDiskConfirmed(snapshot.nodes);_broadcastRev(snapshot);
                  }
                }catch(e){}
              }).catch(()=>{});
            }else{
              if(IS_DESKTOP){
                flushDesktopSave();
                _lastWrittenJson=json2;markDiskConfirmed(snapshot.nodes);
                _dirtySinceFull=0;_l8CheckpointPending=false; 
                try{if(_dirtyNodes.size)_dirtyNodes.clear();if(_removedNodes.size)_removedNodes.clear();}catch(e){} 
                _broadcastRev(snapshot);
                saveAutoBackup();
              }else{
                if(json2.length>4500000){try{toast('瀛樺偍绌洪棿涓嶈冻锛岃瀵煎嚭澶囦唤');}catch(_){}return;}
                localStorage.setItem(STORE_KEY,json2);
                try{localStorage.removeItem(PENDING_KEY);}catch(e){}
                _lastWrittenJson=json2;markDiskConfirmed(snapshot.nodes);
                _broadcastRev(snapshot);
                saveAutoBackup();
              }
            }
          }catch(e){
            console.error('flushPersistNow failed',e);
            try{toast('淇濆瓨澶辫触锛氬瓨鍌ㄧ┖闂翠笉瓒?);}catch(_){}
          }
        }
        function undo(){
          if(!historyPast.length)return false;
          const ae=document.activeElement;
          if(ae&&ae.blur)withSuppressedBlur(()=>ae.blur());
          
          const _patch=historyPast.pop();
          const _preState=state; 
          historyFuture.push(_patch);  
          state=applyPatch(state,_patch,'undo');
          
          if(_patch&&_patch.paths&&_patch.paths.length&&!__juInMerge){
            try{
              const _changedIds=extractChangedIds(_patch.paths,_patch.meta);
              if(_changedIds.size){
                const _res=updateIncrementalIndexes(_changedIds,_preState,state,{op:(_patch.op||'unknown')+':undo',direction:'undo'});
                if(!_res.ok){
                  reportIndexFailure({op:(_patch.op||'unknown')+':undo',index:_res.errors[0]||'unknown',changedIds:_changedIds,error:_res.errors.join('; '),ts:_patch.t});
                  _calIndexCache=null;_tagIndexCache=null;_searchInverted=null;_backlinkMap=null;
                }else{
                  resetIndexFailureStreak();
                }
              }
            }catch(_e){l6InjectionFailure('undo',_patch&&_patch.op,_e,_patch&&_patch.t);}
          }
          
          markPersistFromPatch(_patch,_preState,state,(_patch&&_patch.op||'unknown')+':undo');
          _wordCountCache.clear();
          
          _calRecurCache=null;_yearCountsCache=null;_calDayCache=null;_renderDayCache=null;
          const cur=getCurrentDoc();
          ui.activeKey=cur?'node:'+cur.id:'nav:files';
          scheduleNotify();
          return true;
        }
        function redo(){
          if(!historyFuture.length)return false;
          const ae=document.activeElement;
          if(ae&&ae.blur)withSuppressedBlur(()=>ae.blur());
          
          const _patch=historyFuture.pop();
          const _preState=state; 
          historyPast.push(_patch);
          state=applyPatch(state,_patch,'redo');
          
          if(_patch&&_patch.paths&&_patch.paths.length&&!__juInMerge){
            try{
              const _changedIds=extractChangedIds(_patch.paths,_patch.meta);
              if(_changedIds.size){
                const _res=updateIncrementalIndexes(_changedIds,_preState,state,{op:(_patch.op||'unknown')+':redo',direction:'redo'});
                if(!_res.ok){
                  reportIndexFailure({op:(_patch.op||'unknown')+':redo',index:_res.errors[0]||'unknown',changedIds:_changedIds,error:_res.errors.join('; '),ts:_patch.t});
                  _calIndexCache=null;_tagIndexCache=null;_searchInverted=null;_backlinkMap=null;
                }else{
                  resetIndexFailureStreak();
                }
              }
            }catch(_e){l6InjectionFailure('redo',_patch&&_patch.op,_e,_patch&&_patch.t);}
          }
          
          markPersistFromPatch(_patch,_preState,state,(_patch&&_patch.op||'unknown')+':redo');
          _wordCountCache.clear();
          
          _calRecurCache=null;_yearCountsCache=null;_calDayCache=null;_renderDayCache=null;
          const cur=getCurrentDoc();
          ui.activeKey=cur?'node:'+cur.id:'nav:files';
          scheduleNotify();
          return true;
        }
        
        function makeRoot(){
          return {id:'root',text:'',parent:null,children:[],collapsed:false,done:false,
            date:null,tags:[],priority:0,recur:null,habit:null,createdAt:Date.now(),updatedAt:Date.now(),showInCal:true};
        }
        let _lastNowSeq=0;
        function _nowSeq(){
          
          const t=Date.now();
          const r=t>_lastNowSeq?t:(_lastNowSeq+1);
          _lastNowSeq=r;
          return r;
        }
        function addNodeTo(st,parentId,index,init={}){
          const p=st.nodes[parentId];
          if(!p)return null;
          const _t=_nowSeq();
          const n={id:uid(),text:'',parent:parentId,children:[],collapsed:false,done:false,
            date:null,tags:[],priority:0,recur:null,recurEnd:null,habit:null,time:null,note:null,color:null,
            showInCal:true,createdAt:_t,updatedAt:_t,_origin:'local',...init};
          st.nodes[n.id]=n;
          
          try{
            if(!st._localCreated)st._localCreated=[];
            if(!st._localCreated.includes(n.id))st._localCreated.push(n.id);
          }catch(e){}
          if(index==null||index<0||index>p.children.length)p.children.push(n.id);
          else p.children.splice(index,0,n.id);
          return n;
        }

        const TAG_RE=/(?:^|[\s(锛圽[銆愩€?锛宂)(#[A-Za-z\u4e00-\u9fa5][A-Za-z0-9\u4e00-\u9fa5_-]{0,19})/g;
        function extractTags(text){
          const out=[];const seen={};
          let m;
          TAG_RE.lastIndex=0;
          while((m=TAG_RE.exec(text))){
            const t=m[1].slice(1);
            if(t&&!seen[t]){seen[t]=1;out.push(t);}
          }
          return out;
        }
        const actions={
          addNode(parentId,index,init={}){
            let id=null;
            const owner=parentId?findOwningTopDocId(parentId,state):null;
            commit(s=>{
              const n=addNodeTo(s,parentId,index,init);
              if(n)id=n.id;
            },{snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:owner||undefined});
            if(!id)return null;
            actions.logActivity('add',id);
            persistNow(); 
            return id;
          },
          updateText(id,text,{snapshot=true,noHistory=false}={}){
            const cur=state.nodes[id];
            if(!cur)return;
            
            const newTags=[...new Set([...(cur._manualTags||[]),...extractTags(stripHtml(text||''))])];
            const tagsChanged=JSON.stringify(cur.tags||[])!==JSON.stringify(newTags);
            if((cur.text||'')===(text||'')&&!tagsChanged)return;
            const owner=findOwningTopDocId(id,state);
            commit(s=>{const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;n.text=text;n.tags=newTags;n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},
              {snapshot,noHistory,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:owner||undefined,op:'updateText',opArgs:{id}});
            actions.logActivity('mod',id);
            if(tagsChanged){renderSidebar();forceRender();}
          },
          toggleDone(id){
            const owner=findOwningTopDocId(id,state);
            commit(s=>{const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;n.done=!n.done;n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},
              {snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:owner||undefined});
            actions.logActivity('mod',id);
          },
          toggleCollapse(id){commit(s=>{const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;n.collapsed=!n.collapsed;},{snapshot:true});},
          moveNode(id,toParentId,toIndex){
            if(toParentId===id)return;
            if(!state.nodes[id]||!state.nodes[toParentId])return;
            if(state.nodes[id]._isCalRoot||state.nodes[id]._isDbRoot)return; 
            if(state.nodes[toParentId]._isCalRoot||state.nodes[toParentId]._isDbRoot)return; 
            
            {
              const trashedIds=new Set();
              (state.trashed||[]).forEach(t=>{if(t&&t.rootId){trashedIds.add(t.rootId);(t.ids||[]).forEach(i=>trashedIds.add(i));}});
              if(trashedIds.has(toParentId))return;
            }
            let cur=toParentId;
            const _vis=new Set(); 
            while(cur){if(cur===id)return;if(_vis.has(cur))return;_vis.add(cur);const cn=state.nodes[cur];cur=cn?cn.parent:null;}
            commit(s=>{
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;
              const to=s.nodes[toParentId];
              if(!to)return;
              const from=s.nodes[n.parent];
              const fromIdx=from?from.children.indexOf(id):-1;
              if(from&&fromIdx>=0)from.children.splice(fromIdx,1);
              to.children.splice(Math.max(0,Math.min(toIndex,to.children.length)),0,id);
              n.parent=toParentId;n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
            },{snapshot:true});
          },
          removeNode(id){
            const n=N(id);
            if(!n||n._isCalRoot||n._isDbRoot)return; 
            const oldText=n?stripHtml(n.text):'';
            const parent=n?n.parent:null;
            const owner=parent?findOwningTopDocId(parent,state):null;
            commit(s=>{
              const nn=s.nodes[id];if(!nn)return;
              const p=s.nodes[nn.parent];
              if(p){const i=p.children.indexOf(id);if(i>=0)p.children.splice(i,1);}
              const rm=x=>{
                const node=s.nodes[x];if(!node)return;
                (node.children||[]).forEach(rm);
                delete s.nodes[x];
                purgeNodeMeta(s,x);
                delete ui.collapsedDocs[x];
                _searchHayCache.delete(x);
                
                _tombstoneSet(x,s); 
              };
              rm(id);
            },{snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:owner||undefined});
            actions.logActivity('del',id,oldText);
          },
          createDoc(name,opts={}){
            let id;
            commit(s=>{const n=addNodeTo(s,s.rootId,0,{text:name,...opts});id=n?n.id:null;},{snapshot:true});
            
            if(id){ actions.logActivity('add',id); persistNow(); }

          },

          },

            },{snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:owner||undefined,op:'updateText',opArgs:{id}});
          },
          setStar(id,level){commit(s=>{const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;n.starred=level;n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true});},
          addDbField(docId,field){
            commit(s=>{
              if(!s.dbFields)s.dbFields=[];
              if(typeof field==='string'){if(!s.dbFields.includes(field))s.dbFields.push(field);}
              else{if(s.dbFields.some(f=>(typeof f==='string'?f:f.name)===field.name))return;s.dbFields.push(field);}
            },{snapshot:true});
          },
          removeDbField(docId,name){
            commit(s=>{
              s.dbFields=(s.dbFields||[]).filter(f=>(typeof f==='string'?f:f.name)!==name);
              const sub=[];
              (function walk(x){const n=s.nodes[x];if(!n)return;sub.push(x);(n.children||[]).forEach(walk);})(docId);
              const now=Date.now(); 
              sub.forEach(id=>{const n=s.nodes[id];if(n&&n.db&&name in n.db){delete n.db[name];n.updatedAt=now;}});
              
              try{sub.forEach(id=>_searchHayCache.delete(id));}catch(e){}
            },{snapshot:true});
            
            if(ui.dbHiddenFields&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ui.dbHiddenFields,name)){
              delete ui.dbHiddenFields[name];
              saveUI();
            }
          },
          renameDbField(docId,oldName,newName){
            commit(s=>{
              const idx=(s.dbFields||[]).findIndex(f=>(typeof f==='string'?f:f.name)===oldName);
              if(idx<0)return;
              const f=s.dbFields[idx];
              if(typeof f==='string')s.dbFields[idx]=newName;else f.name=newName;
              const now=Date.now(); 
              const _renAffected=[]; 
              for(const id in s.nodes){const n=s.nodes[id];if(n.db&&oldName in n.db){n.db[newName]=n.db[oldName];delete n.db[oldName];n.updatedAt=now;_renAffected.push(id);}}
              
              if(s.dbColumnWidths){
                const oldKey=docId+'::'+oldName, newKey=docId+'::'+newName;
                if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(s.dbColumnWidths,oldKey)){
                  s.dbColumnWidths[newKey]=s.dbColumnWidths[oldKey];delete s.dbColumnWidths[oldKey];
                }
              }
              
              try{_renAffected.forEach(id=>_searchHayCache.delete(id));}catch(e){}
            },{snapshot:true});
            
            if(ui.dbHiddenFields&&Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(ui.dbHiddenFields,oldName)){
              ui.dbHiddenFields[newName]=ui.dbHiddenFields[oldName];
              delete ui.dbHiddenFields[oldName];
              saveUI();
            }
          },
          setDbCell(rowId,fieldName,value){
            const cur=state.nodes[rowId];
            if(!cur)return;
            const old=cur.db?cur.db[fieldName]:undefined;
            const isObj=(v)=>v&&typeof v==='object';
            const same=(isObj(old)||isObj(value))?JSON.stringify(old)===JSON.stringify(value):old===value;
            if(same)return;
            commit(s=>{
              const n=s.nodes[rowId];if(!n)return;
              if(!n.db)n.db={};
              n.db[fieldName]=value;
              n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
            },{snapshot:true,op:'setDbCell',opArgs:{id:rowId,field:fieldName}});
          },
          addDbRow(docId,init={}){
            let id;
            commit(s=>{
              const doc=s.nodes[docId];if(!doc)return;
              const n=addNodeTo(s,docId,null,{text:'鏂版潯鐩?,db:{},...init});
              id=n?n.id:null;
            },{snapshot:true});
            return id;
          },
          setDbColumnWidth(docId,name,w){commit(s=>{if(!s.dbColumnWidths)s.dbColumnWidths={};s.dbColumnWidths[docId+'::'+name]=w;},{silent:true,persistOnly:true,light:true,dirtyText:false,dirtyDocs:null,noRollback:true});},
          setDbRowHeight(rowId,h){commit(s=>{if(!s.dbRowHeights)s.dbRowHeights={};s.dbRowHeights[rowId]=h;},{silent:true,persistOnly:true,light:true,dirtyText:false,dirtyDocs:null,noRollback:true});},
          importTree(parentId,tree){
            commit(s=>{
              const p=s.nodes[parentId];if(!p)return;
              const insertAt=p.children.length;
              function addOne(data,pid,at){
                const n=addNodeTo(s,pid,at,{text:data.text||'',done:!!data.done,date:data.date||null,
                  time:data.time||null,tags:data.tags||[],note:data.note||null,recur:data.recur||null,showInCal:true});
                if(!n)return;
                (data.children||[]).forEach((c,i)=>addOne(c,n.id,i));
                return n;
              }
              tree.forEach((node,i)=>addOne(node,parentId,insertAt+i));
            },{snapshot:true});
          },
          addTemplate(name,tree){commit(s=>{if(!s.templates)s.templates=[];s.templates.push({id:uid(),name,createdAt:Date.now(),tree});},{snapshot:true});},
          removeTemplate(id){commit(s=>{s.templates=(s.templates||[]).filter(t=>t.id!==id);},{snapshot:true});},
          moveToTrash(id){
            const _src=state.nodes[id];
            if(!_src||_src._isCalRoot||_src._isDbRoot)return; 
            const sub=docSubtree(id);
            commit(s=>{
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;
              const ids=[];
              (function walk(x){const nd=s.nodes[x];if(!nd)return;ids.push(x);(nd.children||[]).forEach(walk);})(id);
              if(!s.trashed)s.trashed=[];
              const p=s.nodes[n.parent];
              const origIndex=p?p.children.indexOf(id):-1;
              s.trashed.unshift({rootId:id,ids,deletedAt:Date.now(),parentId:n.parent,origIndex});
              if(p){const i=p.children.indexOf(id);if(i>=0)p.children.splice(i,1);}
              
              const _trashNow=Date.now();
              ids.forEach(x=>{const nd=s.nodes[x];if(nd){nd._deleted=true;nd.updatedAt=_trashNow;}});
              
              ids.forEach(x=>_tombstoneSet(x,s)); 
            },{snapshot:true});
            if(sub.includes(ui.currentDocId)){ui.currentDocId=null;ui.activeKey=null;}
          },
          restoreFromTrash(idx){
            commit(s=>{
              const t=s.trashed[idx];if(!t)return;
              const targetParentId=(t.parentId&&s.nodes[t.parentId])?t.parentId:s.rootId;
              (function fix(id,pid){const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;n.parent=pid;n._deleted=false;(n.children||[]).forEach(c=>fix(c,id));})(t.rootId,targetParentId);
              const target=s.nodes[targetParentId];
              if(target&&!target.children.includes(t.rootId)){
                const pos=(typeof t.origIndex==='number'&&t.origIndex>=0&&t.origIndex<=target.children.length)
                  ?t.origIndex
                  :target.children.length;
                target.children.splice(pos,0,t.rootId);
              }
              
              try{(t.ids||[]).forEach(x=>{_recentlyRemoved.delete(x);if(s._tombstones)delete s._tombstones[x];});}catch(e){}
              s.trashed.splice(idx,1);
            },{snapshot:true});
          },
          purgeTrash(idx){
            commit(s=>{
              const t=s.trashed[idx];if(!t)return;
              
              (t.ids||[]).forEach(x=>{delete s.nodes[x];purgeNodeMeta(s,x);
                
                try{if(ui&&ui.collapsedDocs)delete ui.collapsedDocs[x];}catch(e){}
                
                try{_searchHayCache.delete(x);}catch(e){}
                try{if(s._localCreated){const _li=s._localCreated.indexOf(x);if(_li>=0)s._localCreated.splice(_li,1);}}catch(e){}
              });
              
              (t.ids||[]).forEach(x=>_tombstoneSet(x,s)); 
              s.trashed.splice(idx,1);
            },{snapshot:true});
          },
          logActivity(type,id,detail){
            const n=(!detail&&id)?state.nodes[id]:null;
            if(n&&isBlankNode(n))return;
            const key=type+':'+id;
            const doCommit=()=>{
              
              const ds=todayStr();
              const nn=id&&state.nodes[id];
              const entry={id,text:detail||(nn?stripHtml(nn.text):'')||'',t:Date.now()};
              commit(s=>{
                if(!s.activity)s.activity={};
                if(!s.activity[ds])s.activity[ds]={add:[],del:[],mod:[]};
                s.activity[ds][type].push(entry);
                if(s.activity[ds][type].length>200)s.activity[ds][type]=s.activity[ds][type].slice(-200);
                const keys=Object.keys(s.activity).sort();
                const MAX_DAYS=90;
                if(keys.length>MAX_DAYS){keys.slice(0,keys.length-MAX_DAYS).forEach(k=>delete s.activity[k]);}
                
                let total=0;for(const k in s.activity){const b=s.activity[k];if(b){total+=(b.add?b.add.length:0)+(b.mod?b.mod.length:0)+(b.del?b.del.length:0);}}
                if(total>5000){
                  const kk=Object.keys(s.activity).sort();
                  for(const k of kk){if(total<=5000)break;const b=s.activity[k];if(b){total-=(b.add?b.add.length:0)+(b.mod?b.mod.length:0)+(b.del?b.del.length:0);delete s.activity[k];}}
                }
              },{silent:true,light:true,dirtyText:false,dirtyDocs:null});
              schedulePersist();
              scheduleNotify();
            };
            if(type==='mod'){
              const prev=_logTimers.get(key);
              if(prev)clearTimeout(prev.timer);
              const timer=setTimeout(()=>{
                _logTimers.delete(key);
                doCommit();
              },1500);
              _logTimers.set(key,{timer,commit:doCommit});
            }else doCommit();
          }
        };

        function loadUI(){
          if(ui.desktopUiApplied)return;
          try{
            const raw=localStorage.getItem(UI_KEY);
            if(raw){const u=JSON.parse(raw);applyUIFields(u);}
          }catch(e){}
