        }
        function applyUIFields(u){
          if(u.theme)document.documentElement.dataset.theme=u.theme;
          if(typeof u.scale==='number')ui.scale=u.scale;
          if(typeof u.mmScale==='number')ui.mmScale=u.mmScale;
          if(u.currentDocId&&state&&state.nodes[u.currentDocId])ui.currentDocId=u.currentDocId;
          if(u.view==='stats')ui.view='auto';
          else if(u.view&&TOP_LEVEL_VIEWS.includes(u.view))ui.view=u.view;
          else ui.view='auto';
          if(typeof u.sidebarLeftCollapsed==='boolean')ui.sidebarLeftCollapsed=u.sidebarLeftCollapsed;
          if(typeof u.sidebarRightCollapsed==='boolean')ui.sidebarRightCollapsed=u.sidebarRightCollapsed;
          if(typeof u.sidebarLeftWidth==='number'&&u.sidebarLeftWidth>=140&&u.sidebarLeftWidth<=480)ui.sidebarLeftWidth=u.sidebarLeftWidth;
          if(typeof u.sidebarRightWidth==='number'&&u.sidebarRightWidth>=180&&u.sidebarRightWidth<=340)ui.sidebarRightWidth=u.sidebarRightWidth; 
          if(u.collapsedDocs)ui.collapsedDocs=u.collapsedDocs;
          const UI_VER=3;
          if((u.uiVersion||1)<UI_VER){ui.navExpanded={starred:false,files:false,database:false,tags:false};}
          else if(u.navExpanded){ui.navExpanded=Object.assign({starred:false,files:false,database:false,tags:false},u.navExpanded);}
          if(typeof u.floatCal==='boolean')ui.floatCal=u.floatCal;
          if(typeof u.pomoVisible==='boolean')ui.pomoVisible=u.pomoVisible;
          if(u.filesSort)ui.filesSort=u.filesSort;
          ui.collapsedPanels=Object.assign({today:false,added:false,stats:false,note:false},u.collapsedPanels||{});
          ui.calColWidths=(u.calColWidths&&typeof u.calColWidths==='object')?u.calColWidths:{note:null,today:null};
          ui.logCollapsed=Object.assign({add:false,mod:false,del:false},u.logCollapsed||{});
          if(u.dbView)ui.dbView=u.dbView;
          if(u.dbHiddenFields)ui.dbHiddenFields=Object.assign({},u.dbHiddenFields); 
          if(u.dbSortsMap)ui.dbSortsMap=u.dbSortsMap;
          if(u.dbFiltersMap)ui.dbFiltersMap=u.dbFiltersMap;
          if(u.dbSummary)ui.dbSummary=u.dbSummary;
          if(u.maxCalNodes)ui.maxCalNodes=u.maxCalNodes;
          if(u.calView)ui.calView=u.calView;
          if(typeof u.calTagFilter==='string')ui.calTagFilter=u.calTagFilter;
          if(u.selectedDate&&!isNaN(parseYmd(u.selectedDate).getTime()))ui.selectedDate=u.selectedDate;
          if(typeof u.reminderEnabled==='boolean')ui.reminderEnabled=u.reminderEnabled;
          if(typeof u.autoBackupOnClose==='boolean')ui.autoBackupOnClose=u.autoBackupOnClose;
          
          if(typeof u.virtualScrollOff==='boolean')ui.virtualScrollOff=u.virtualScrollOff;
          try{if(typeof _vsUserDisabled!=='undefined')_vsUserDisabled=(ui.virtualScrollOff===true);}catch(e){}
        }
        let _saveUITimer=null;
        function saveUI(){
          clearTimeout(_saveUITimer);
          _saveUITimer=setTimeout(_saveUINow,150);
        }
        function _saveUINow(){
          if(ui.locked)return;
          if(IS_DESKTOP&&!(ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey)){scheduleDesktopSave();return;}
          const data={
            theme:document.documentElement.dataset.theme,scale:ui.scale,
            mmScale:ui.mmScale,
            view:ui.view,
            sidebarLeftCollapsed:ui.sidebarLeftCollapsed,sidebarRightCollapsed:ui.sidebarRightCollapsed,sidebarLeftWidth:ui.sidebarLeftWidth,sidebarRightWidth:ui.sidebarRightWidth,
            collapsedDocs:ui.collapsedDocs,navExpanded:ui.navExpanded,
            floatCal:ui.floatCal,pomoVisible:ui.pomoVisible,filesSort:ui.filesSort,
            collapsedPanels:ui.collapsedPanels,logCollapsed:ui.logCollapsed,calColWidths:ui.calColWidths,
            dbView:ui.dbView,
            dbHiddenFields:ui.dbHiddenFields,
            dbSortsMap:ui.dbSortsMap,dbFiltersMap:ui.dbFiltersMap,dbSummary:ui.dbSummary,
            maxCalNodes:ui.maxCalNodes,calView:ui.calView,calTagFilter:ui.calTagFilter,
            reminderEnabled:ui.reminderEnabled,
            autoBackupOnClose:ui.autoBackupOnClose,
            virtualScrollOff:ui.virtualScrollOff,
            selectedDate:ui.selectedDate,
            currentDocId:ui.currentDocId,
            uiVersion:3
          };
          if(ui.encrypted && ui.encKey){
            
            
            const k=ui.encKey;
            try{localStorage.removeItem(UI_KEY);}catch(e){}
            encryptState(data,k).then(payload=>{
              
              if(!(ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey===k))return;
              try{localStorage.setItem(UI_ENC_KEY,JSON.stringify(payload));}catch(e){}
            }).catch(()=>{});
            return;
          }
          try{
            localStorage.setItem(UI_KEY,JSON.stringify(data));
            try{localStorage.removeItem(UI_ENC_KEY);}catch(e){}
          }catch(e){}
        }
        async function loadUIEncrypted(){
          if(!ui.encKey)return; 
          try{
            const raw=localStorage.getItem(UI_ENC_KEY);
            if(!raw)return;
            const payload=JSON.parse(raw);
            const data=await decryptState(payload,ui.encKey);
            applyUIFields(data);
            applyScale();
          }catch(e){
            try{localStorage.removeItem(UI_ENC_KEY);}catch(e2){}
          }
        }
        function applyTheme(theme){

                :'<circle cx="12" cy="12" r="8"/><path d="M12 4a8 8 0 0 1 0 16"/>';

          }
        }
        function toggleTheme(){
          const cur=document.documentElement.dataset.theme||'day';
          const next=cur==='day'?'night':'day';
          applyTheme(next);saveUI();forceRender();
          toast('涓婚锛?+(next==='day'?'鏃ラ棿鎶ょ溂':'澶滈棿鎶ょ溂'));
        }
        function N(id){return state.nodes[id];}
        
        const MB_LRU_CAP=5000;
        const MB_VIEW_CAP=200;
        let _mbDisabled=true;
        let _mbUserDisabled=false;
        let _mbShadow=false;
        let _mbPrintForceFull=false;
        const _mbPinned=new Set();
        const MB={
          ready:true,
          workset:new Map(),
          viewport:new Set(),
          dirty:new Set(),
          metrics:{hits:0,misses:0,backfills:0,evictions:0,pinnedHits:0,capBreach:0,rss:0}
        };
        function _mbEnabled(){return !_mbDisabled&&!_mbUserDisabled&&MB.ready===true;}
        function mbPinned(id){return _mbPinned.has(id)||_mbPrintForceFull;}
        function _mbRss(){try{if(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance&&performance.memory){return performance.memory.usedJSHeapSize||0;}}catch(e){}return 0;}
        function mbTouch(id){
          const v=MB.workset.get(id);
          if(v===undefined)return undefined;
          MB.workset.delete(id);MB.workset.set(id,v);
          MB.metrics.hits++;
          return v;
        }
        function mbEvict(){
          MB.metrics.rss=_mbRss();
          if(MB.workset.size<=MB_LRU_CAP)return;
          for(const id of MB.workset.keys()){
            if(MB.workset.size<=MB_LRU_CAP)break;
            if(MB.viewport.has(id))continue;
            if(MB.dirty.has(id))continue;
            if(mbPinned(id))continue;
            MB.workset.delete(id);
            MB.metrics.evictions++;
          }
          if(MB.workset.size>MB_LRU_CAP)MB.metrics.capBreach++;
          MB.metrics.rss=_mbRss();
        }
        function _mbProject(row,projection){
          if(row===undefined||row===null)return null;
          const out={};
          const keys=(projection&&projection.length)?projection:['id','text','parent','children','collapsed','done'];
          for(const k of keys){out[k]=row[k];}
          return out;
        }
        async function MB_backfill(id){
          MB.metrics.misses++;
          const node=N(id);
          if(node){MB.workset.set(id,node);MB.metrics.backfills++;mbEvict();}
          return node;
        }
        async function getNodeAsync(id){
          if(!_mbEnabled())return Promise.resolve(N(id));
          if(MB.viewport.has(id)){MB.metrics.pinnedHits++;return N(id);}
          if(MB.workset.has(id))return mbTouch(id);
          return await MB_backfill(id);
        }
        async function prefetch(ids){
          if(!_mbEnabled())return;
          const list=ids||[];
          for(const id of list){if(!MB.viewport.has(id)&&!MB.workset.has(id))await MB_backfill(id);}
        }
        async function mbResolve(desc){
          const d=desc||{};
          const kind=d.kind||'outline';
          const win=d.window||{start:0,count:200,overscan:24};
          const proj=d.projection||['id','text','parent','children','collapsed','done'];
          const rootId=d.rootId||(state&&state.rootId);
          const ids=(typeof docSubtree==='function'&&rootId)?docSubtree(rootId):[];
          const total=ids.length;
          const end=Math.min(total,win.start+win.count);
          const rows=[];
          if(!_mbEnabled()){
            for(let i=win.start;i<end;i++){rows.push(_mbProject(N(ids[i]),proj));}
            return {kind:kind,rows:rows,window:{start:win.start,count:win.count,total:total},degraded:true};
          }
          for(let i=win.start;i<end;i++){
            MB.viewport.add(ids[i]);
            rows.push(_mbProject(await getNodeAsync(ids[i]),proj));
          }
          return {kind:kind,rows:rows,window:{start:win.start,count:win.count,total:total},degraded:false};
        }
        function _mbOnCommit(success,changedIds){
          if(!_mbEnabled())return;
          try{
            if(Array.isArray(changedIds)){for(const id of changedIds){if(MB.workset.has(id))MB.workset.delete(id);}}
            
            if(typeof mbCoalesceCommit==='function')mbCoalesceCommit(changedIds);
          }catch(e){}
        }
        function _mbSetViewport(ids){
          if(!_mbEnabled())return;
          MB.viewport.clear();
          for(const id of (ids||[])){MB.viewport.add(id);if(MB.workset.has(id))MB.workset.delete(id);}
          MB.metrics.rss=_mbRss();
        }
        
        const MB_COMMIT_BATCH_MS=16;
        const MB_CONSISTENCY_RING=64;
        let _mbViewportRaf=0;
        let _mbCommitTimer=0;
        let _mbCommitBatch=null;
        const MB_W4={
          debounceFlushes:0,batchFlushes:0,batchPending:0,
          incrementalApplied:0,consistencyChecks:0,consistencyMismatch:0,crashFallbacks:0,
          ring:[]
        };
        function _mbRaf(fn){try{if(typeof requestAnimationFrame==='function')return requestAnimationFrame(fn);}catch(e){}return setTimeout(fn,16);}
        function _mbRafCancel(h){try{if(typeof cancelAnimationFrame==='function')cancelAnimationFrame(h);else clearTimeout(h);}catch(e){}}
        
        function mbDebounceViewport(ids){
          if(!_mbEnabled())return;
          if(_mbViewportRaf)_mbRafCancel(_mbViewportRaf);
          const snap=Array.isArray(ids)?ids.slice():ids;
          _mbViewportRaf=_mbRaf(function(){_mbViewportRaf=0;MB_W4.debounceFlushes++;_mbSetViewport(snap);});
        }
        
        function _mbApplyIncremental(dirtyIds){
          if(!_mbEnabled())return 0;
          let n=0;
          for(const id of (dirtyIds||[])){if(MB.workset.has(id)){MB.workset.delete(id);n++;}}
          MB_W4.incrementalApplied+=n;
          return n;
        }
        
        function mbCoalesceCommit(changedIds){
          if(!_mbEnabled())return;
          if(!(_mbCommitBatch instanceof Set))_mbCommitBatch=new Set();
          if(Array.isArray(changedIds))for(const id of changedIds)_mbCommitBatch.add(id);
          MB_W4.batchPending=_mbCommitBatch.size;
          if(_mbCommitTimer)return;
          _mbCommitTimer=setTimeout(function(){
            _mbCommitTimer=0;
            const batch=_mbCommitBatch||new Set();
            _mbCommitBatch=null;
            MB_W4.batchFlushes++;MB_W4.batchPending=0;
            _mbApplyIncremental(batch);
          },MB_COMMIT_BATCH_MS);
        }
        
        async function mbAuditConsistency(desc){
          const d=desc||{};
          const proj=d.projection||['id','text','parent','children','collapsed','done'];
          const rootId=d.rootId||(state&&state.rootId);
          const ids=(typeof docSubtree==='function'&&rootId)?docSubtree(rootId):[];
          const win=d.window||{start:0,count:200,overscan:24};
          const end=Math.min(ids.length,win.start+win.count);
          let mismatch=0;
          for(let i=win.start;i<end;i++){
            const a=_mbProject(N(ids[i]),proj);
            let b;try{b=_mbProject(await getNodeAsync(ids[i]),proj);}catch(e){b={__error:1};}
            if(JSON.stringify(a)!==JSON.stringify(b)){mismatch++;if(MB_W4.ring.length<MB_CONSISTENCY_RING)MB_W4.ring.push(ids[i]);}
          }
          MB_W4.consistencyChecks++;MB_W4.consistencyMismatch+=mismatch;
          return {ok:mismatch===0,mismatch:mismatch,rows:end-win.start};
        }
        
        function _mbCrashSafe(fn,fallback){
          try{return fn();}catch(e){_mbDisabled=true;MB.ready=false;MB_W4.crashFallbacks++;return fallback;}
        }
        
        let savedFocus=null;
        let _blurSuppressCount=0;
        function withSuppressedBlur(fn){
          _blurSuppressCount++;
          try{fn();}finally{_blurSuppressCount--;}
        }
        function isBlurSuppressed(){return _blurSuppressCount>0;}
        function getTextOffset(root,container,offset){
          if(!root.contains(container))return 0;
          const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
          let count=0,cur;
          while((cur=walker.nextNode())){
            if(cur===container)return count+Math.min(offset,cur.nodeValue.length);
            count+=cur.nodeValue.length;
          }
          return count;
        }
        
        function nodePath(root,node){
          const path=[];
          let cur=node;
          while(cur&&cur!==root&&cur.parentNode){
            const parent=cur.parentNode;
            let idx=-1;
            for(let i=0;i<parent.childNodes.length;i++){if(parent.childNodes[i]===cur){idx=i;break;}}
            if(idx<0)break;
            path.unshift(idx);
            cur=parent;
          }
          return path;
        }
        function rangeFromPath(root,path,offset){
          if(!Array.isArray(path)||!path.length)return null;
          let node=root;
          for(const idx of path){
            if(!node.childNodes||idx<0||idx>=node.childNodes.length)return null;
            node=node.childNodes[idx];
          }
          const r=document.createRange();
          if(node.nodeType===Node.TEXT_NODE){
            r.setStart(node,Math.max(0,Math.min(offset,node.nodeValue.length)));
          }else{
            
            const ci=Math.max(0,Math.min(offset,node.childNodes.length));
            
            if(ci<node.childNodes.length&&node.childNodes[ci].nodeType===Node.TEXT_NODE){
              r.setStart(node.childNodes[ci],0);
            }else{
              r.setStart(node,ci);
            }
          }
          r.collapse(true);
          return r;
        }
        function rangeFromTextOffset(root,offset){
          const walker=document.createTreeWalker(root,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
          let remaining=Math.max(0,offset),cur,last=null;
          while((cur=walker.nextNode())){
            if(remaining<=cur.nodeValue.length){
              const r=document.createRange();
              r.setStart(cur,remaining);
              r.collapse(true);
              return r;
            }
            remaining-=cur.nodeValue.length;
            last=cur;
          }
          const r=document.createRange();
          if(last)r.setStart(last,last.nodeValue.length);
          else r.selectNodeContents(root);
          r.collapse(true);
          return r;
        }
        function editorRole(el){
          if(!el||!el.classList)return null;
          if(el.classList.contains('doc-header-title'))return 'header';
          if(el.classList.contains('we-body'))return 'word';
          if(el.classList.contains('text'))return 'node';
          return null;
        }
        function editorSelector(id,role){
          const safe=CSS.escape(id);
          switch(role){
            case 'header':return `.doc-header-title[data-id="${safe}"]`;
            case 'word':return `.we-body[data-id="${safe}"]`;
            default:return `#content .text[data-id="${safe}"]`;
          }
        }
        function isEditable(el){
          if(!el)return false;
          if(el.isContentEditable)return true;
          const tag=el.tagName;
          if(tag==='TEXTAREA')return true;
          if(tag==='INPUT'){
            return !['checkbox','radio','button','submit','range','file','color','image','date','time'].includes(el.type);
          }
          return false;
        }
        function saveFocus(){
          const el=document.activeElement;
          if(!isEditable(el)||!el.dataset||!el.dataset.id){savedFocus=null;return;}
          const role=editorRole(el);
          if(!role){savedFocus=null;return;}
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(!sel||!sel.rangeCount){savedFocus=null;return;}
          const r=sel.getRangeAt(0);
          if(!el.contains(r.startContainer)){savedFocus=null;return;}
          
          const start=getTextOffset(el,r.startContainer,r.startOffset);
          const end=r.collapsed?start:getTextOffset(el,r.endContainer,r.endOffset);
          savedFocus={id:el.dataset.id,role,start,end,collapsed:r.collapsed,
            sp:nodePath(el,r.startContainer),eo:r.startOffset,
            ep:r.collapsed?null:nodePath(el,r.endContainer),ee:r.endOffset};
        }
        function restoreFocus(){
          if(!savedFocus)return;
          const{id,role,start,end,collapsed,sp,eo,ep,ee}=savedFocus;
          savedFocus=null;
          const el=document.querySelector(editorSelector(id,role));
          if(!el)return;
          el.focus({preventScroll:true});
          try{
            const sel=window.getSelection();
            
            let r=sp?rangeFromPath(el,sp,eo):null;
            if(!r)r=rangeFromTextOffset(el,start);
            if(!collapsed&&end>start){
              let rEnd=ep?rangeFromPath(el,ep,ee):null;
              if(!rEnd)rEnd=rangeFromTextOffset(el,end);
              r.setEnd(rEnd.startContainer,rEnd.startOffset);
            }
            sel.removeAllRanges();
            sel.addRange(r);
          }catch(e){}
        }
        function focusNode(id,offset,toEnd){
          if(_vsActive)_vsScrollToId(_vsActive,id); 
          const el=document.querySelector('#content .text[data-id="'+CSS.escape(id)+'"]')||
                   document.querySelector('[data-id="'+CSS.escape(id)+'"][contenteditable]');
          if(!el){ui.focusId=id;return;}
          el.focus({preventScroll:true});
          try{
            const sel=window.getSelection();
            let r;
            if(offset!=null)r=rangeFromTextOffset(el,offset);
            else{
              r=document.createRange();
              r.selectNodeContents(el);
              r.collapse(!toEnd);
            }
            sel.removeAllRanges();
            sel.addRange(r);
          }catch(e){}
          ui.focusId=id;
          $$('.row.sel,.cal-row.sel').forEach(r=>r.classList.remove('sel'));
          const row=el.closest('.row,.cal-row');if(row)row.classList.add('sel');
          const box=el.closest('.node,.cal-node');if(box)box.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'smooth'});
        }
        function focusAfterRender(id,containerSel,{toEnd=true,offset=null}={}){
          requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
            if(_vsActive)_vsScrollToId(_vsActive,id); 
            const root=containerSel?document.querySelector(containerSel):document.querySelector('#content');
            if(!root)return;
            const el=root.querySelector('.text[data-id="'+CSS.escape(id)+'"]')||
                     root.querySelector('[data-id="'+CSS.escape(id)+'"][contenteditable]');
            if(!el)return;
            el.focus({preventScroll:true});
            try{
              const sel=window.getSelection();
              let r;
              if(offset!=null)r=rangeFromTextOffset(el,offset);
              else{
                r=document.createRange();
                r.selectNodeContents(el);
                r.collapse(!toEnd);
              }
              sel.removeAllRanges();
              sel.addRange(r);
            }catch(e){}
          });
        }
        function focusTextAfterRender(id){focusAfterRender(id);}
        
        let toastTimer=null;
        function toast(msg){
          const t=$('#toast');if(!t)return;
          t.textContent=msg;t.classList.add('on');
          clearTimeout(toastTimer);
          toastTimer=setTimeout(()=>t.classList.remove('on'),1800);
        }
        let _forceRender=false;
        let _renderPending=false;
        let _renderRaf=null;
        function scheduleRender(){
          if(_renderPending)return;
          _renderPending=true;
          _renderRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
            _renderPending=false;_renderRaf=null;
            renderMain();
          });
        }
        
        function commitPendingEditBeforeRender(){
          const ae=document.activeElement;
          if(!ae||!ae.dataset||!ae.dataset.id)return;
          const role=editorRole(ae);
          if(!role)return;
          const id=ae.dataset.id;
          const cur=N(id);
          if(!cur)return;
          if(role==='node'){
            const finalHtml=sanitizeHtml(ae.innerHTML);
            
            const newTags=[...new Set([...(cur._manualTags||[]),...extractTags(stripHtml(finalHtml))])];
            if(finalHtml!==(cur.text||'')){
              const owner=findOwningTopDocId(id,state);
              
              commit(s=>{const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;n.text=finalHtml;n.tags=newTags;n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},
                {snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:owner||undefined,silent:true,persistOnly:true,op:'updateText',opArgs:{id}});
            }
          }else if(role==='header'){
            const finalText=(ae.textContent||'').trim();
            if(finalText&&finalText!==stripHtml(cur.text||'')){
              commit(s=>{const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;n.text=finalText;n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},
                {snapshot:true,silent:true,persistOnly:true,op:'updateText',opArgs:{id}});
            }
          }else if(role==='word'){
            const norm=sanitizeHtml(ae.innerHTML);
            if(norm!==(cur.content||'')){
              commit(s=>{const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;n.content=norm;n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},
                {snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,silent:true,persistOnly:true,op:'updateText',opArgs:{id}});
            }
          }
        }
        function forceRender(){
          
          try{commitPendingEditBeforeRender();}catch(e){}
          saveFocus();
          _forceRender=true;
          const ae=document.activeElement;
          if(ae&&ae.blur){
            const tag=ae.tagName;
            if(ae.isContentEditable||tag==='INPUT'||tag==='TEXTAREA'||tag==='SELECT'){
              withSuppressedBlur(()=>ae.blur());
            }
          }
          if(_renderRaf){cancelAnimationFrame(_renderRaf);_renderRaf=null;}
          _renderPending=false;
          renderMain();
        }
        function initDragClose(el,onClose){
          if(el.dataset.dragInit)return;el.dataset.dragInit='1';
          const cl=document.createElement('button');
          cl.className='pop-close';cl.textContent='鉁?;cl.title='鍏抽棴';
          cl.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>e.stopPropagation());
          cl.addEventListener('click',e=>{e.stopPropagation();window._popAnchor=null;if(onClose)onClose();else el.remove();}); 
          el.appendChild(cl);
          let drag=false,sx=0,sy=0,ox=0,oy=0;
          el.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
            if(e.button!==0)return;
            const t=e.target;
            if(t.closest('button,input,select,a,.sw,.cp-swatch,.sp-btn,.tp-item,.ctx-item,.pop-close'))return;
            drag=true;sx=e.clientX;sy=e.clientY;
            const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();ox=r.left;oy=r.top;
            el.style.position='fixed';
            el.style.left=ox+'px';el.style.top=oy+'px';
            window._popAnchor={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};
            const move=ev=>{
              if(!drag)return;
              el.style.left=Math.max(0,Math.min(window.innerWidth-90,ox+ev.clientX-sx))+'px';
              el.style.top=Math.max(0,Math.min(window.innerHeight-36,oy+ev.clientY-sy))+'px';
            };
            
            const up=()=>{drag=false;window._popAnchor=null;document.removeEventListener('pointermove',move);document.removeEventListener('pointerup',up);};
            document.addEventListener('pointermove',move);
            document.addEventListener('pointerup',up);
          });
        }
        
        function placePop(el,W,H,anchor){
          const vw=window.innerWidth,vh=window.innerHeight;
          
          const w=el.offsetWidth||W,h=el.offsetHeight||H;
          let ax=null,ay=null,ah=0;
          if(anchor&&anchor.getBoundingClientRect){const r=anchor.getBoundingClientRect();ax=r.left;ay=r.top;ah=r.height||0;}
          else if(anchor&&typeof anchor==='object'&&('left'in anchor)){ax=anchor.left;ay=anchor.top||0;ah=(anchor.bottom!=null&&anchor.top!=null)?(anchor.bottom-anchor.top):0;}
          else if(window._popAnchor){ax=window._popAnchor.x;ay=window._popAnchor.y;}
          let left,top;
          if(ax==null){left=Math.max(8,(vw-w)/2);top=Math.max(8,vh*0.2);}
          else{
            left=Math.max(8,Math.min(ax,vw-w-8));
            top=ay+ah+4;
            if(top+h>vh-8&&ay-h-4>8)top=ay-h-4;
            top=Math.max(8,Math.min(top,vh-8));
          }
          el.style.left=left+'px';el.style.top=top+'px';
        }
        function renderViewTitle(title){
          const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='doc-header';
          const name=document.createElement('div');name.className='doc-header-title';
          name.textContent=title;
          bar.appendChild(name);
          return bar;
        }
        function showContextMenu(items,e){
          const menu=$('#ctxMenu');if(!menu)return;
          menu.innerHTML='';
          items.forEach(it=>{
            if(it.sep){const s=document.createElement('div');s.className='ctx-sep';menu.appendChild(s);return;}
            const d=document.createElement('div');
            d.className='ctx-item'+(it.danger?' danger':'');
            d.innerHTML=`<span class="ic">${it.ic||''}</span><span>${esc(it.txt)}</span>`;
            d.onclick=()=>{menu.classList.remove('on');window._popAnchor=null;it.fn();}; 
            menu.appendChild(d);
          });
          menu.style.left='-9999px';menu.style.top='0px';
          window._popAnchor={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};
          menu.classList.add('on');
          const mw=menu.offsetWidth,mh=menu.offsetHeight;
          const x=Math.max(4,Math.min(e.clientX,window.innerWidth-mw-4));
          const y=Math.max(4,Math.min(e.clientY,window.innerHeight-mh-4));
          menu.style.left=x+'px';menu.style.top=y+'px';
        }
        document.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{if(!e.target.closest('#ctxMenu')){$('#ctxMenu')?.classList.remove('on');window._popAnchor=null;} });
        
        const _modalStack=[];
        function appConfirm(msg,onOk,onCancel){
          const modal=document.createElement('div');modal.className='modal-overlay';
          modal.innerHTML=`<div class="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="modal-head"><span>纭</span><button class="modal-close">鉁?/button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="padding:8px 0">${esc(msg)}</div></div><div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn" data-cancel>鍙栨秷</button><button class="btn primary" data-ok>纭畾</button></div></div>`;
          document.body.appendChild(modal);
          _modalStack.push(modal); 
          const isTop=()=>_modalStack[_modalStack.length-1]===modal; 
          const popStack=()=>{const i=_modalStack.indexOf(modal);if(i>=0)_modalStack.splice(i,1);}; 
          
          const focusables=()=>Array.from(modal.querySelectorAll('a[href],button,input,select,textarea,[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')).filter(el=>!el.disabled);
          const onKey=e=>{
            if(!isTop())return; 
            if(e.key==='Escape')close();
            else if(e.key==='Enter'){
              
              e.preventDefault();
              cleanup();modal.remove();popStack();onOk&&onOk();
            }
            else if(e.key==='Tab'){ 
              const f=focusables();if(!f.length)return;
              const first=f[0],last=f[f.length-1];
              if(e.shiftKey){if(document.activeElement===first||!modal.contains(document.activeElement)){e.preventDefault();last.focus();}}
              else{if(document.activeElement===last||!modal.contains(document.activeElement)){e.preventDefault();first.focus();}}
            }
          };
          const cleanup=()=>document.removeEventListener('keydown',onKey);
          const close=()=>{cleanup();modal.remove();popStack();if(onCancel)onCancel();};
          modal.querySelector('.modal-close').onclick=close;
          modal.querySelector('[data-cancel]').onclick=close;
          modal.querySelector('[data-ok]').onclick=()=>{cleanup();modal.remove();popStack();onOk&&onOk();};
          modal.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===modal&&isTop())close();}; 
          document.addEventListener('keydown',onKey);
          focusables()[0]?.focus(); 
        }
        function appPrompt(msg,defaultVal,onOk){
          const modal=document.createElement('div');modal.className='modal-overlay';
          modal.innerHTML=`<div class="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="modal-head"><span>杈撳叆</span><button class="modal-close">鉁?/button></div><div class="modal-body"><div style="margin-bottom:6px">${esc(msg)}</div><input type="text" style="width:100%;padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid var(--line-soft);border-radius:6px;background:var(--bg-mid);outline:none"></div><div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn" data-cancel>鍙栨秷</button><button class="btn primary" data-ok>纭畾</button></div></div>`;
          document.body.appendChild(modal);
          _modalStack.push(modal); 
          const isTop=()=>_modalStack[_modalStack.length-1]===modal; 
          const inp=modal.querySelector('input');
          inp.value=defaultVal||'';inp.focus();inp.select();
          
          const focusables=()=>Array.from(modal.querySelectorAll('a[href],button,input,select,textarea,[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')).filter(el=>!el.disabled);
          const onKey=e=>{
            if(!isTop())return;
            if(e.key!=='Tab')return;
            const f=focusables();if(!f.length)return;
            const first=f[0],last=f[f.length-1];
            if(e.shiftKey){if(document.activeElement===first||!modal.contains(document.activeElement)){e.preventDefault();last.focus();}}
            else{if(document.activeElement===last||!modal.contains(document.activeElement)){e.preventDefault();first.focus();}}
          };
          document.addEventListener('keydown',onKey);
          const close=v=>{document.removeEventListener('keydown',onKey);modal.remove();const i=_modalStack.indexOf(modal);if(i>=0)_modalStack.splice(i,1);onOk&&onOk(v);}; 
          modal.querySelector('.modal-close').onclick=()=>close(null);
          modal.querySelector('[data-cancel]').onclick=()=>close(null);
          modal.querySelector('[data-ok]').onclick=()=>close(inp.value);
          modal.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===modal&&isTop())close(null);}; 
          inp.onkeydown=e=>{if(!isTop())return;if(e.key==='Enter')close(inp.value);else if(e.key==='Escape')close(null);};
        }
        
        
        function mkCalSel(entries,val){
          const root=document.createElement('div');
          root.className='cal-sel';root.tabIndex=0;root.setAttribute('role','listbox');root.setAttribute('aria-label','鏃堕棿閫夋嫨');
          const cur=document.createElement('span');cur.className='cal-sel-val';
          const arrow=document.createElement('span');arrow.className='cal-sel-arrow';arrow.textContent='鈻?;
          root.appendChild(cur);root.appendChild(arrow);
          let panel=null;
          function renderVal(){
            const it=entries.find(e=>e[0]===val);
            cur.textContent=it?it[1]:'';
          }
          function close(){if(panel){panel.remove();panel=null;root.classList.remove('open');}}
          
          const onFocusin=e=>{if(panel&&!root.contains(e.target)&&!panel.contains(e.target))close();};
          document.addEventListener('focusin',onFocusin);
          let _blurTimer=null; 
          if(!root._cleanups)root._cleanups=[];
          root._cleanups.push(()=>{
            document.removeEventListener('focusin',onFocusin);
            clearTimeout(_blurTimer);
            if(panel){panel.remove();panel=null;root.classList.remove('open');} 
          });
          function open(){
            if(root.classList.contains('disabled'))return;
            if(panel){close();return;}
            const p=document.createElement('ul');p.className='cal-sel-panel';
            entries.forEach(([v,label])=>{
              const li=document.createElement('li');li.className='cal-sel-opt'+(v===val?' cur':'');li.textContent=label;
              li.onmousedown=e=>e.preventDefault(); 
              li.onclick=()=>{val=v;renderVal();close();root.focus();};
              p.appendChild(li);
            });
            document.body.appendChild(p);
            const r=root.getBoundingClientRect();
            const ph=p.offsetHeight,vh=window.innerHeight;
            const downSpace=vh-r.bottom-4,upSpace=r.top-4;
            const vw=window.innerWidth;
            p.style.left=Math.max(4,Math.min(r.left,vw-4-p.offsetWidth))+'px'; 
            p.style.width=Math.max(r.width,64)+'px';
            if(ph<=downSpace||downSpace>=upSpace){p.style.top=(r.bottom+2)+'px';p.style.bottom='';}
            else{p.style.top='';p.style.bottom=(vh-r.bottom+2)+'px';}
            panel=p;root.classList.add('open');
          }
          root.addEventListener('click',()=>{if(!root.classList.contains('disabled'))open();});
          root.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
            if(root.classList.contains('disabled'))return;
            if(!panel){
              if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '||e.key==='ArrowDown'){e.preventDefault();open();}
              return;
            }
            if(e.key==='Escape'){e.preventDefault();close();root.focus();}
            else if(e.key==='ArrowDown'){e.preventDefault();const i=entries.findIndex(x=>x[0]===val);const nv=entries[Math.min(i+1,entries.length-1)][0];val=nv;renderVal();}
            else if(e.key==='ArrowUp'){e.preventDefault();const i=entries.findIndex(x=>x[0]===val);const nv=entries[Math.max(i-1,0)][0];val=nv;renderVal();}
            else if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();close();root.focus();}
          });
          
          root.addEventListener('blur',()=>{clearTimeout(_blurTimer);_blurTimer=setTimeout(()=>{if(!root.contains(document.activeElement))close();},150);});
          root._val=()=>val;
          root._set=v=>{val=v;renderVal();};
          root._disable=d=>{root.classList.toggle('disabled',!!d);if(d)close();};
          renderVal();
          return root;
        }
        function openCalTimeModal(id){
          const n=state.nodes[id];if(!n)return;
          const modal=document.createElement('div');modal.className='modal-overlay';
          const freq=(n.recur&&n.recur.freq)||'';
          
          const recurOpts=[['','涓嶉噸澶?],['daily','姣忓ぉ'],['workday','姣忔硶瀹氬伐浣滄棩'],['weekly','姣忓懆'],['monthly','姣忔湀'],['yearly','姣忓勾'],['lunar','姣忓勾锛堝啘鍘嗘棩锛?]];
          const optHtml=recurOpts.map(o=>`<option value="${o[0]}"${o[0]===freq?' selected':''}>${o[1]}</option>`).join('');
          
          const _pt=s=>{if(!s)return null;const m=/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(s);return m?{h:+m[1],m:+m[2]}:null;};
          const t0=_pt(n.time),t1=_pt(n.timeEnd);
          const hEntries=Array.from({length:24},(_,i)=>[i,pad(i)]);
          const mEntries=[0,5,10,15,20,25,30,35,40,45,50,55,60].map(v=>[v,pad(v)]);
          const hEntriesWithNone=[['','鏃?],...hEntries];
          modal.innerHTML=`<div class="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" style="width:380px;max-width:92vw">
            <div class="modal-head"><span>璁剧疆鏃堕棿</span><button class="modal-close">鉁?/button></div>
            <div class="modal-body">
              <div style="margin-bottom:6px">寮€濮嬫椂闂?/div>
              <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">
                <div class="m-sh-wrap" style="flex:1;min-width:0"></div>
                <span style="color:var(--fg-dim);flex:none">:</span>
                <div class="m-sm-wrap" style="flex:1;min-width:0"></div>
              </div>
              <div style="margin:12px 0 6px"><label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;cursor:pointer"><input type="checkbox" class="m-end" ${t1?'checked':''}> 缁撴潫鏃堕棿</label></div>
              <div class="m-endrow" style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;${t1?'':'opacity:.5'}">
                <div class="m-eh-wrap" style="flex:1;min-width:0"></div>
                <span style="color:var(--fg-dim);flex:none">:</span>
                <div class="m-em-wrap" style="flex:1;min-width:0"></div>
              </div>
              <div style="margin:12px 0 6px">閲嶅鏂瑰紡</div>
              <select class="m-sel">${optHtml}</select>
              <div style="margin:12px 0 6px">閲嶅缁撴潫鏃ユ湡锛堢暀绌轰负鏃犻檺鍒讹級</div>
              <div style="display:flex;gap:8px">
                <input type="date" class="m-date" value="${esc(n.recurEnd||'')}" style="flex:1;min-width:0">
                <button type="button" class="btn" data-none>鏃犻檺鍒?/button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="modal-footer"><button class="btn" data-cancel>鍙栨秷</button><button class="btn primary" data-ok>淇濆瓨</button></div>
          </div>`;
          document.body.appendChild(modal);
          _modalStack.push(modal); 
          const isTop=()=>_modalStack[_modalStack.length-1]===modal;
          const sH=mkCalSel(hEntriesWithNone,t0?t0.h:'');
          const sM=mkCalSel(mEntries,t0?Math.min(Math.round(t0.m/5)*5,55):0);
          const eH=mkCalSel(hEntries,t1?t1.h:0);
          const eM=mkCalSel(mEntries,t1?Math.min(Math.round(t1.m/5)*5,55):0);
          modal.querySelector('.m-sh-wrap').replaceWith(sH);
          modal.querySelector('.m-sm-wrap').replaceWith(sM);
          modal.querySelector('.m-eh-wrap').replaceWith(eH);
          modal.querySelector('.m-em-wrap').replaceWith(eM);
          const endCb=modal.querySelector('.m-end');
          const endRow=modal.querySelector('.m-endrow');
          const sel=modal.querySelector('.m-sel');
          const dateInp=modal.querySelector('.m-date');
          sH.focus();
          endCb.onchange=()=>{
            endRow.style.opacity=endCb.checked?'1':'.5';
            eH._disable(!endCb.checked);eM._disable(!endCb.checked);
            if(endCb.checked&&!t1){
              const hv=sH._val();if(hv!==''&&!isNaN(+hv)){eH._set(Math.min(+hv+1,23));eM._set(0);}
            }
          };
          const focusables=()=>Array.from(modal.querySelectorAll('a[href],button,input,select,textarea,.cal-sel,[tabindex]:not([tabindex="-1"])')).filter(el=>!el.disabled);
          const onKey=e=>{
            if(!isTop()||e.key!=='Tab')return;
            const f=focusables();if(!f.length)return;
            const first=f[0],last=f[f.length-1];
            if(e.shiftKey){if(document.activeElement===first||!modal.contains(document.activeElement)){e.preventDefault();last.focus();}}
            else{if(document.activeElement===last||!modal.contains(document.activeElement)){e.preventDefault();first.focus();}}
          };
          document.addEventListener('keydown',onKey);
          const close=()=>{document.removeEventListener('keydown',onKey);modal.remove();const i=_modalStack.indexOf(modal);if(i>=0)_modalStack.splice(i,1);

          function save(){

            if(end&&!/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(end)){toast('缁撴潫鏃ユ湡鏍煎紡鏃犳晥');return;}

        function getCurrentDoc(){
          let id=ui.currentDocId;
          if(!id||!state.nodes[id]||state.nodes[id]._deleted){
            const root=state.nodes[state.rootId];
            const first=root.children.map(cid=>state.nodes[cid]).find(n=>n&&!n._isCalRoot&&!n._isDbRoot&&!n._deleted);
            
            id=first?first.id:null;
            if(id)ui.currentDocId=id;
          }
          return id?state.nodes[id]:null;
        }
        function docSubtree(id){
          const a=[];
          
          (function w(x){const n=state.nodes[x];if(!n||n._deleted)return;a.push(x);(n.children||[]).forEach(w);})(id);
          return a;
        }
        function docNodeCount(id){return docSubtree(id).length;}
        function docWordCount(id){
          const hit=_wordCountCache.get(id); 
          if(hit!==undefined)return hit;
          let c=0;
          
          (function w(x){
            const n=state.nodes[x];
            if(!n||n._deleted)return;
            c+=stripHtml(n.text).length;
            if(n._isWord&&n.content)c+=stripHtml(n.content).length;
            if(n._wordFlat)return; 
            (n.children||[]).forEach(w);
          })(id);
          _wordCountCache.set(id,c); 
          return c;
        }
        function isFolder(id){const n=N(id);return !!(n&&n._isFolder&&!n._isDbRoot);}
        function isWordDoc(id){const n=N(id);return !!(n&&n._isWord);}
        function nodesOnDate(ds){
          const r=[];
          for(const id in state.nodes){
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(n.id===state.rootId||n._deleted)continue;
            if(isFolder(n.id))continue;
            if(n.date===ds)r.push(n);
          }
          return r.sort((a,b)=>(a.createdAt-b.createdAt)||(a.id<b.id?-1:a.id>b.id?1:0));
        }
        function fmtDateShort(ts){
          if(!ts)return '';
          const d=new Date(ts);
          return d.getFullYear()+'.'+(d.getMonth()+1)+'.'+d.getDate();
        }
        function fmtRelative(ts,short){
          if(!ts)return '';
          const d=new Date(ts);const diff=Date.now()-ts;
          if(diff<60000)return '鍒氬垰';
          if(diff<3600000)return Math.floor(diff/60000)+(short?'鍒嗗墠':'鍒嗛挓鍓?);
          if(diff<86400000)return short?(pad(d.getHours())+':'+pad(d.getMinutes())):(Math.floor(diff/3600000)+'灏忔椂鍓?);
          if(diff<604800000)return Math.floor(diff/86400000)+(short?'澶?:'澶╁墠');
          return `${d.getFullYear()}/${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()}`;
        }
        function getNodeSvg(id){
          if(id===state.rootId)return`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 7a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4l2 2h8a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v8a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V7z"/></svg>`;
          if(isFolder(id))return`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M3 7a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4l2 2h8a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v8a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H5a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V7z"/></svg>`;
          if(isWordDoc(id))return`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 3h8l5 5v12a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H6a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V4a1 1 0 0 1 1-1z"/><path d="M14 3v5h5"/><path d="M8 12h8M8 16h8"/></svg>`;
          return`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M6 3h8l5 5v12a1 1 0 0 1-1 1H6a1 1 0 0 1-1-1V4a1 1 0 0 1 1-1z"/><path d="M14 3v5h5"/></svg>`;
        }

        function countVisibleNodes(doc){
          let count=0;
          (function walk(n){
            if(!n)return;
            count++;
            if(!n.collapsed&&n.children)n.children.forEach(cid=>{const cn=N(cid);if(cn)walk(cn);});
          })(doc);
          return count;
        }

        function _vsFlatten(doc){ 
          const list=[];
          function walk(n,depth){
            if(!n)return;
            list.push({id:n.id,depth:depth});
            if(!n.collapsed&&n.children)n.children.forEach(cid=>{const cn=N(cid);if(cn)walk(cn,depth+1);});
          }
          doc.children.forEach(cid=>{const cn=N(cid);if(cn)walk(cn,0);});
          return list;
        }
        function _vsWindow(vs){
          const c=vs.content;const total=vs.list.length;const rowH=VS_ROW_H;
          if(!c)return {first:0,last:0};
          const connected=vs.layer&&vs.layer.isConnected;
          const layerTop=connected?(vs.layer.getBoundingClientRect().top-c.getBoundingClientRect().top):0;
          const vh=connected?(c.clientHeight||0):(window.innerHeight||720);
          if(vs.htable){ 
            const r=vs.htable.range(c.scrollTop||(-layerTop)||0,vh,VS_OVERSCAN);
            let first=r.first;let last=r.last;
            if(last<first)last=first;
            if(last-first>VS_MAX_ROWS)last=Math.min(total,first+VS_MAX_ROWS);
            return {first:first,last:last};
          }
          let first=Math.max(0,Math.floor((-layerTop)/rowH)-VS_OVERSCAN);
          let last=Math.min(total,Math.ceil((-layerTop+vh)/rowH)+VS_OVERSCAN);
          if(last<first)last=first;
          if(last-first>VS_MAX_ROWS)last=Math.min(total,first+VS_MAX_ROWS);
          return {first:first,last:last};
        }
        function _vsUpdate(vs){
          if(!vs||!vs.wrap)return;

          while(pool.length<want){

          }
          for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){
            const el=pool[i];
            if(i<want){
              const idx=w.first+i;const item=vs.list[idx];const n=N(item.id);
              if(n){vs.fill(el,n,item.depth,idx);el.style.display='';el.style.transform='translateY('+(vs.htable?vs.htable.offset(idx):idx*VS_ROW_H)+'px)';}
              else{el.style.display='none';}
            }else{el.style.display='none';}
          }
          vs.first=w.first;vs.last=w.last;
          vs.wrap.dataset.vsFirst=String(w.first);vs.wrap.dataset.vsLast=String(w.last);vs.wrap.dataset.vsTotal=String(total);
        }
        function _vsBindScroll(){

        }

        }

        const VS_METRIC_CAP=256;
        const _vsMetric={commit:[],viewSwitch:[],longtask:[],commitN:0,viewSwitchN:0,longtaskN:0};
        function _vsMetricPush(kind,ms){
          const v=Number(ms);if(!Number.isFinite(v)||v<0)return;
          const a=_vsMetric[kind];if(!a)return;
          a.push(v);if(a.length>VS_METRIC_CAP)a.splice(0,a.length-VS_METRIC_CAP);
          _vsMetric[kind+'N']++;
        }
        function _vsP95Of(a){
          if(!a||!a.length)return NaN;
          const s=a.slice().sort(function(x,y){return x-y;});
          return s[Math.max(0,Math.ceil(0.95*s.length)-1)];
        }

        }

        function __l14On(){try{return _l14Enabled===true;}catch(e){return false;}}
        
        function __vsHeightTable(list,measure){
          const n=list?list.length:0;
          const heights=new Array(n);
          const prefix=new Array(n+1);
          prefix[0]=0;
          for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
            let v=measure?measure(list[i],i):0;
            v=Number(v);
            if(!Number.isFinite(v)||v<=0)v=VS_ROW_H;
            heights[i]=v;prefix[i+1]=prefix[i]+v;
          }
          return {
            size:n,heights:heights,prefix:prefix,total:prefix[n],
            heightAt:function(i){return (i>=0&&i<n)?heights[i]:VS_ROW_H;},
            offset:function(i){if(!(i>0))return 0;if(i>=n)return prefix[n];return prefix[i];},
            indexAt:function(y){ 
              if(n===0)return 0;
              const yy=Math.max(0,Math.min(Number(y)||0,prefix[n]));
              let lo=0,hi=n;
              while(lo<hi){const mid=(lo+hi)>>1;if(prefix[mid]<=yy)lo=mid+1;else hi=mid;}
              let i=lo-1;if(i<0)i=0;if(i>n-1)i=n-1;return i;
            },
            range:function(scrollTop,vh,overscan){
              if(n===0)return {first:0,last:0,offset:0};
              const os=Number.isFinite(overscan)?overscan:VS_OVERSCAN;
              const v=Number(vh)||0;const st=Number(scrollTop)||0;
              let first=Math.max(0,this.indexAt(st)-os);
              let last=Math.min(n,this.indexAt(st+v)+os+1);
              if(last<first)last=first;
              return {first:first,last:last,offset:this.offset(first)};
            },
          };
        }
        function __vsHTFor(doc,list){
          const gen=(typeof state==='object'&&state)?(state.updatedAt||0):0;
          const key=(doc&&doc.id)?doc.id:'__l14__';
          let e=__vsHTable.get(key);
          if(!e||e.gen!==gen){e={gen:gen,table:__vsHeightTable(list)};__vsHTable.set(key,e);}
          return e.table;
        }
        function __vsHTCalibrate(vs){ 
          if(!vs||!vs.htable||!vs.pool||!vs.layer||!vs.layer.isConnected)return false;
          const h=vs.htable;const base=vs.first>=0?vs.first:0;
          let changed=false;
          for(let i=0;i<vs.pool.length;i++){
            const el=vs.pool[i];if(!el||el.style.display==='none')continue;
            const idx=base+i;if(idx<0||idx>=h.size)continue;
            const m=el.offsetHeight;
            if(Number.isFinite(m)&&m>0&&Math.abs(m-h.heights[idx])>1){changed=true;break;}
          }
          if(!changed)return false;
          const measure=function(item,idx){
            const el=vs.pool[idx-base];
            const m=(el&&el.style.display!=='none')?el.offsetHeight:0;
            return (Number.isFinite(m)&&m>0)?m:h.heights[idx];
          };
          vs.htable=__vsHeightTable(vs.list,measure);
          return true;
        }

        function __vsGenTick(){const s=(typeof state==='object'&&state)?state:null;return s?(s.updatedAt||0):0;}
        function __vsGenReset(){__vsGenCache={gen:null,doc:null,list:null,count:-1};}

        }
        function __vsPatchEquals(prevList,nextList){ 
          if(!prevList||!nextList)return false;
          if(prevList.length!==nextList.length)return false;
          for(let i=0;i<prevList.length;i++){
            const a=prevList[i],b=nextList[i];
            if(!a||!b||a.id!==b.id||a.depth!==b.depth)return false;
          }
          return true;
        }
        function __vsIncremental(vs,doc){ 
          if(!vs||!doc)return null;
          const prev=vs.list;
          const next=__vsGenFlatten(doc);
          const reused=__vsPatchEquals(prev,next);
          vs.list=next;
          return {reused:reused,count:next.length};
        }
        
        function __dbGalleryCard(n){
          const card=document.createElement('div');
          card.className='db-gallery-card';
          const html=[];
          const titleName=dbPrimaryField();
          if(dbFieldVisible(titleName)){
            html.push('<div class="title">'+esc(stripHtml(n.text)||'(绌?')+'</div>');
          }
          (state.dbFields||[]).forEach(f=>{
            const def=dbFieldDef(f);
            const name=def.name;
            if(name===titleName||!dbFieldVisible(name))return;
            if(def.type==='formula'||def.type==='link'||def.type==='multi_select')return;
            if(isStarField(name,def)){
              const st=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,parseInt((n.db&&n.db[name])||0)||0));
              html.push('<div class="meta"><span style="display:inline-flex;gap:1px">'+mkStarsHtml(st)+'</span></div>');
            }else if(def.type==='number'){
              const v=dbTextValue(n,f);
              if(v)html.push('<div class="meta">'+esc(name+'锛?+v)+'</div>');
            }else{
              const v=dbTextValue(n,f);
              if(v)html.push('<div class="meta"><span>'+esc(v)+'</span></div>');
            }
          });
          card.innerHTML=html.join('')||'<div class="title">(鏃犳樉绀哄唴瀹?</div>';
          card.onclick=()=>openNode(n.id);
          return card;
        }
        function __renderDbGalleryVirtual(rows){
          const n=rows?rows.length:0;
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');
          wrap.className='db-gallery db-gallery-vs';
          const CARD_H=104;
          const canvas=document.createElement('div');
          canvas.className='db-gallery-canvas';
          canvas.style.position='relative';
          canvas.style.height=(n*CARD_H)+'px';
          wrap.appendChild(canvas);
          const cols=Math.max(1,Math.floor(((wrap.clientWidth||900)+12)/212));
          const rowCount=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(n/cols));
          const capRows=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(L14_VIEWPORT_CAP/cols));
          const content=document.getElementById('content');
          const pool=[];
          const windowRows=function(){
            if(!content)return {first:0,last:Math.min(rowCount,capRows)};
            const base=canvas.getBoundingClientRect().top-content.getBoundingClientRect().top;
            const vh=content.clientHeight||720;const overscan=2;
            let first=Math.max(0,Math.floor((-base)/CARD_H)-overscan);
            let last=Math.min(rowCount,Math.ceil((-base+vh)/CARD_H)+overscan);
            if(last<first)last=first;
            if(last-first>capRows)last=Math.min(rowCount,first+capRows);
            return {first:first,last:last};
          };
          const update=function(){
            const w=windowRows();
            const want=(w.last-w.first)*cols;
            while(pool.length<want){
              const cell=document.createElement('div');
              cell.className='db-gallery-cell';
              canvas.appendChild(cell);
              pool.push(cell);
            }
            for(let i=0;i<pool.length;i++){
              const cell=pool[i];
              if(i<want){
                const linear=w.first*cols+i;
                const row=rows[linear];
                if(row){
                  cell.style.display='';
                  cell.style.position='absolute';
                  cell.style.left=((i%cols)/cols*100)+'%';
                  cell.style.width=(100/cols)+'%';
                  cell.style.top=((w.first+Math.floor(i/cols))*CARD_H)+'px';
                  if(!cell._l14card||cell._l14id!==row.id){
                    cell.innerHTML='';
                    cell.appendChild(__dbGalleryCard(row));
                    cell._l14card=true;cell._l14id=row.id;
                  }
                }else{cell.style.display='none';}
              }else{cell.style.display='none';}
            }
            wrap.dataset.vsFirst=String(w.first*cols);
            wrap.dataset.vsLast=String(w.last*cols);
            wrap.dataset.vsTotal=String(n);
          };
          wrap._vsUpdate=update;
          if(content&&!wrap._l14Bound){
            wrap._l14Bound=true;
            content.addEventListener('scroll',function(){if(wrap.isConnected)update();},{passive:true});
          }
          update();
          return wrap;
        }

        function __l14SearchAll(query,opts){
          const q=String(query==null?'':query);
          const hits=[];
          if(!q)return hits;
          const s=(typeof state==='object'&&state)?state:null;
          if(!s||!s.nodes)return hits;
          const needle=q.toLowerCase();
          const limit=(opts&&Number.isFinite(opts.limit))?opts.limit:100000;
          const ids=Object.keys(s.nodes);
          for(let i=0;i<ids.length;i++){
            const n=s.nodes[ids[i]];
            if(!n||n._deleted)continue;
            const text=stripHtml(n.text||'');
            if(text&&text.toLowerCase().indexOf(needle)>=0){
              hits.push({id:n.id,text:text});
              if(hits.length>=limit)break;
            }
          }
          return hits;
        }
        function __l14SearchLocate(id){
          if(_vsActive)_vsScrollToId(_vsActive,id);
          return !!N(id);
        }
        function __l14SearchOpen(query){
          const prev=document.getElementById('l14-search-panel');
          if(prev)prev.remove();
          const panel=document.createElement('div');
          panel.id='l14-search-panel';panel.className='l14-search-panel';
          const input=document.createElement('input');
          input.type='text';input.className='l14-search-input';
          input.placeholder='搴旂敤鍐呮悳绱紙鍚睆澶?/ 鎶樺彔锛?;
          input.value=String(query==null?'':query);
          const result=document.createElement('div');
          result.className='l14-search-result';
          const run=function(){
            const hits=__l14SearchAll(input.value,{limit:200});
            result.textContent=hits.length?('鍛戒腑 '+hits.length+' 椤?):'鏃犲懡涓?;
            result.dataset.hits=String(hits.length);
            if(hits.length&&hits[0])__l14SearchLocate(hits[0].id);
          };
          input.addEventListener('input',function(){__vsInteractGuard.coalesce(run);});
          panel.appendChild(input);panel.appendChild(result);
          document.body.appendChild(panel);input.focus();run();
          return panel;
        }
        
        const __vsInteractGuard={
          enabled:false,renders:0,notes:{},geometryReads:0,geometryHits:0,_raf:false,
          note:function(kind){this.renders++;if(kind)this.notes[kind]=(this.notes[kind]||0)+1;},
          geom:function(hit){this.geometryReads++;if(hit)this.geometryHits++;},
          coalesce:function(fn){
            if(typeof fn!=='function')return;
            if(this._raf)return;
            const self=this;this._raf=true;
            const run=function(){self._raf=false;try{fn();}catch(e){}};
            if(typeof requestAnimationFrame==='function')requestAnimationFrame(run);else setTimeout(run,16);
          },
          stats:function(){return {renders:this.renders,geometryReads:this.geometryReads,geometryHits:this.geometryHits,notes:this.notes};},
        };
        function __vsGeomWindow(vs){ 
          const c=vs&&vs.content;
          if(!c)return null;
          const key=(c.scrollTop||0)+'|'+(c.clientHeight||0);
          const cache=__vsGeomCache.cache;
          if(__vsGeomCache.gen===__vsGenTick()&&cache&&cache.key===key&&cache.last){
            __vsInteractGuard.geom(true);return cache.last;
          }
          __vsInteractGuard.geom(false);
          return null;
        }
        function __l14ShadowSample(){
          const s=(typeof state==='object'&&state)?state:null;
          const doc=(s&&s.nodes)?s.nodes[s.rootId]:null;
          const total=doc?(__l14On()?__vsGenCount(doc):countVisibleNodes(doc)):0;
          _l14Shadow={ok:!!doc,total:total,cap:L14_VIEWPORT_CAP,calls:((_l14Shadow&&_l14Shadow.calls)||0)+1};
          return _l14Shadow;
        }
        
        const L14_LONGTASK_BUDGET=50;       
        const L14_RSS_CAP_MB=200;           
        const L14_P95_BUDGET=16;            
        const __l14LongtaskGate={
          enabled:false,longtasks:0,maxDuration:0,budget:L14_LONGTASK_BUDGET,
          rssCapMB:L14_RSS_CAP_MB,p95Budget:L14_P95_BUDGET,
          switches:[],rssMB:-1,observer:null,_raf:false,
          _throttle:{wait:0,last:0,timer:null,pending:false},
          note:function(duration){ 
            const d=Number(duration)||0;
            if(d>this.budget){this.longtasks++;if(d>this.maxDuration)this.maxDuration=d;}
            return this.longtasks;
          },
          debounce:function(fn){ 
            if(typeof fn!=='function')return;
            if(this._raf)return;
            this._raf=true;const self=this;
            const run=function(){self._raf=false;try{fn();}catch(e){}};
            if(typeof requestAnimationFrame==='function')requestAnimationFrame(run);else setTimeout(run,16);
          },
          throttle:function(fn,wait){ 
            if(typeof fn!=='function')return;
            const t=this._throttle,w=Number.isFinite(wait)?wait:16,self=this;
            const now=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();
            const invoke=function(){t.last=(typeof performance!=='undefined'&&performance.now)?performance.now():Date.now();t.pending=false;try{fn();}catch(e){}};
            if(!t.last||(now-t.last)>=w){invoke();return;}
            if(t.pending)return;
            t.pending=true;t.timer=setTimeout(invoke,Math.max(0,w-(now-t.last)));
          },
          bindObserver:function(){ 
            if(this.observer)return true;
            if(typeof window==='undefined'||typeof PerformanceObserver==='undefined')return false;
            try{const self=this;
              this.observer=new PerformanceObserver(function(list){list.getEntries().forEach(function(en){self.note(en.duration);});});
              this.observer.observe({entryTypes:['longtask']});
              return true;
            }catch(e){this.observer=null;return false;}
          },
          markSwitch:function(ms){ 
            const v=Number(ms);
            if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v>=0){this.switches.push(v);if(this.switches.length>256)this.switches.splice(0,this.switches.length-256);}
            return this.switches.length;
          },
          p95:function(){ 
            const s=this.switches.slice().sort(function(a,b){return a-b;});
            if(!s.length)return NaN;
            return s[Math.max(0,Math.ceil(0.95*s.length)-1)];
          },
          sampleRss:function(){ 
            const m=(typeof performance!=='undefined')?performance.memory:null;
            this.rssMB=(m&&Number.isFinite(m.usedJSHeapSize))?(m.usedJSHeapSize/1048576):-1;
            return this.rssMB;
          },
          rssGate:function(rssMB){ 
            const v=Number(rssMB);
            if(!Number.isFinite(v)||v<0)return {ok:true,measured:false,cap:this.rssCapMB};
            return {ok:v<=this.rssCapMB,measured:true,rssMB:v,cap:this.rssCapMB};
          },
          longtaskGate:function(){ 
            return {ok:this.longtasks===0,longtasks:this.longtasks,maxDuration:this.maxDuration,budget:this.budget};
          },
          p95Gate:function(){ 
            const p=this.p95();const ok=!Number.isFinite(p)||p<=this.p95Budget;
            return {ok:ok,p95:Number.isFinite(p)?p:null,budget:this.p95Budget};
          },
          degradeParity:function(offRows,fullRows){ 
            if(!Array.isArray(offRows)||!Array.isArray(fullRows))return {ok:false,reason:"no-rows"};
            if(offRows.length!==fullRows.length)return {ok:false,reason:"len"};
            for(let i=0;i<offRows.length;i++){if(offRows[i]!==fullRows[i])return {ok:false,reason:"row@"+i};}
            return {ok:true,rows:offRows.length};
          },
          measure:function(){ 
            const p=this.p95();
            return {version:'l14-longtask-gate/v1',longtasks:this.longtasks,maxDuration:this.maxDuration,budget:this.budget,switches:this.switches.length,p95:Number.isFinite(p)?p:null,p95Budget:this.p95Budget,rssMB:this.rssMB,rssCapMB:this.rssCapMB,enabled:this.enabled};
          },
          reset:function(){this.longtasks=0;this.maxDuration=0;this.switches=[];this.rssMB=-1;this._throttle={wait:0,last:0,timer:null,pending:false};return true;},
        };

        function renderOutlinePage(doc){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='outline';

              if(vc>VIRTUAL_THRESHOLD){wrap.classList.add('virtual');useVirtual=true;}
            }
          }catch(e){_virtualDisabled=true;try{reportError('virtual-scroll-fallback',{msg:String(e&&e.message||e)});}catch(_){}}
          try{
            wrap.appendChild(renderDocHeaderInline(doc));
            if(_outlineSel.docId&&_outlineSel.docId!==doc.id){_outlineSel={docId:doc.id,anchorId:null,ids:new Set()};}
            else if(!_outlineSel.docId){_outlineSel.docId=doc.id;}

              const _live=new Set();

              if(_live.size!==_outlineSel.ids.size)_outlineSel.ids=_live;
            }
            if(_outlineSel.ids.size>0){
              const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='outline-selbar';
              bar.innerHTML=`宸查€?<b>${_outlineSel.ids.size}</b> 涓妭鐐?路 鎷栧姩鎵嬫焺鍙壒閲忕Щ鍔?/ 鏀瑰彉灞傜骇`;
              const cls=document.createElement('button');cls.className='nt-btn';cls.textContent='鍙栨秷閫夋嫨 (Esc)';
              cls.onclick=()=>{_outlineSel.ids=new Set();_outlineSel.anchorId=null;forceRender();};
              bar.appendChild(cls);
              wrap.appendChild(bar);

            else{doc.children.forEach(cid=>{const n=N(cid);if(n)wrap.appendChild(renderOutlineRow(n,0));});}
          }catch(e){
            _virtualDisabled=true;
            try{reportError('virtual-scroll-fallback',{msg:String(e&&e.message||e)});}catch(_){}
            
            wrap.innerHTML='';
            if(wrap._vs)wrap._vs=null;
            if(_vsActive&&_vsActive.wrap===wrap)_vsActive=null;

        function _syncOutlineHeaderH(wrap){

        function renderDocHeaderInline(doc){
          const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='doc-header';
          const name=document.createElement('div');name.className='doc-header-title';
          name.contentEditable='true';name.spellcheck=false;name.dataset.id=doc.id;name.dataset.ph='鏈懡鍚嶆枃妗?;
          name.textContent=stripHtml(doc.text)||(doc.id===state.rootId?'鎴戠殑鏂囨。':'鏈懡鍚?);
          let composing=false;
          name.addEventListener('compositionstart',()=>composing=true);
          name.addEventListener('compositionend',()=>composing=false);
          name.addEventListener('input',e=>{
            if(composing||e.isComposing)return;
            syncEmptyState(name,'鏈懡鍚嶆枃妗?);
          });
          name.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
            if(isBlurSuppressed())return;
            if(!name.isConnected)return;
            const finalText=name.textContent.trim();
            const cur=N(doc.id);if(!cur)return;
            if(finalText&&finalText!==stripHtml(cur.text||'')){
              commit(s=>{s.nodes[doc.id].text=finalText;s.nodes[doc.id].updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true,op:'updateText',opArgs:{id:doc.id}});
              actions.logActivity('mod',doc.id);
              renderSidebar();
            }
          });
          name.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
            if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();name.blur();}
            if(e.key==='Escape'){name.textContent=stripHtml(N(doc.id).text||'')||'鏈懡鍚?;name.blur();}
            if(!(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey))e.stopPropagation();
          });
          
          name.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{
            e.preventDefault();
            const cur=N(doc.id);
            if(!cur)return;
            const items=[];
            if(doc.id!==state.rootId){
              items.push({ic:'鉁忥笍',txt:'閲嶅懡鍚嶏紙閫変腑锛?,fn:()=>{
                name.focus();
                const r=document.createRange();r.selectNodeContents(name);
                const sel=window.getSelection();sel.removeAllRanges();sel.addRange(r);
              }});
            }
            items.push({ic:'猸?,txt:'鏄熸爣 +1',fn:()=>{
              const lv=cur.starred||0;
              actions.setStar(doc.id,lv>=5?0:lv+1);
              renderSidebar();updateRightSidebar();
            }});
            if(doc.id!==state.rootId){
              items.push({sep:true});
              items.push({ic:'馃棏',txt:'绉诲叆鍥炴敹绔?,danger:true,fn:()=>{
                actions.moveToTrash(doc.id);
                if(ui.currentDocId===doc.id)ui.currentDocId=null;
                renderSidebar();forceRender();
              }});
            }
            showContextMenu(items,e);
          });
          bar.appendChild(name);
          if(doc.starred>0){const stars=document.createElement('span');stars.className='doc-header-stars';stars.textContent='鈽?.repeat(doc.starred);bar.appendChild(stars);}
          
          return bar;
        }
        function _buildOutlineBox(box,n,depth,opts){
          opts=opts||{};
          while(box.firstChild)box.removeChild(box.firstChild);
          box.className='node';box.dataset.id=n.id;

          if(n.children&&n.children.length)box.setAttribute('aria-expanded',n.collapsed?'false':'true');

          box.setAttribute('aria-selected',(ui.focusId===n.id)?'true':'false');
          const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='row';row.dataset.depth=depth;
          if(ui.focusId===n.id)row.classList.add('sel');

          const handle=document.createElement('span');
          handle.className='drag-handle';handle.textContent='鈰嫯';handle.title='鎷栧姩绉诲姩锛堝彲鍏堝閫夛紝鍐嶆嫋鍔ㄦ墜鏌勬壒閲忕Щ鍔級';
          handle.onmousedown=e=>outlineDragStart(e,n.id);
          row.appendChild(handle);

          arrow.className='arrow'+(n.children.length?'':' leaf');
          if(n.children.length)arrow.textContent=n.collapsed?'鈻?:'鈻?;
          arrow.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();actions.toggleCollapse(n.id);forceRender();};
          row.appendChild(arrow);
          const text=document.createElement('div');

          text.dataset.id=n.id;text.dataset.ph='杈撳叆鍐呭';

          if(n.priority&&n.priority>0){const pb=document.createElement('span');pb.className='prio-badge p'+n.priority;pb.textContent=({1:'P0',2:'P1',3:'P2',4:'P3',5:'P4'})[n.priority]||'';actions2.appendChild(pb);}

          if(tagDots.length>2){
            for(let i=2;i<tagDots.length;i++){tagDots[i].style.display='none';}
            const more=document.createElement('span');more.className='tag-more';
            more.textContent='+'+(tagDots.length-2);
            more.title='杩樻湁 '+(tagDots.length-2)+' 涓爣绛?;
            tagDots[1].insertAdjacentElement('afterend',more);
          }
          row.appendChild(actions2);
          row.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
            if(e.button!==0)return;
            if(e.target.closest('.drag-handle')||e.target.closest('.arrow')||e.target.closest('.row-actions'))return;
            if(e.target.closest('.text')&&!(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey||e.shiftKey))return;
            if(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey){
              e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
              toggleOutlineSel(n.id);forceRender();return;
            }
            if(e.shiftKey){
              e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
