              if(doc&&_outlineSel.anchorId)outlineSelectRange(doc,_outlineSel.anchorId,n.id,false);
              else if(doc){_outlineSel.ids=new Set([n.id]);_outlineSel.anchorId=n.id;_outlineSel.docId=doc.id;forceRender();}
              return;
            }
            e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
            _outlineSel.ids=new Set([n.id]);_outlineSel.anchorId=n.id;_outlineSel.docId=(getCurrentDoc()||{}).id||null;
            forceRender();
          });
          box.appendChild(row);
          if(n.block==='table')box.appendChild(renderInlineTable(n));
          
          if(!opts.flat&&n.children.length){
            if(n.collapsed){
              const hint=document.createElement('div');hint.className='collapsed-hint';hint.textContent='鈻?'+n.children.length+' 涓瓙鑺傜偣';
              hint.onclick=()=>{actions.toggleCollapse(n.id);forceRender();};
              box.appendChild(hint);
            }else{
              const kids=document.createElement('div');kids.className='children';
              n.children.forEach(cid=>{const cn=N(cid);if(cn)kids.appendChild(renderOutlineRow(cn,depth+1));});
              box.appendChild(kids);
            }
          }
        }
        function renderOutlineRow(n,depth){
          depth=depth||0;
          const box=document.createElement('div');
          _buildOutlineBox(box,n,depth,{});
          return box;
        }
        
        let _outlineSel={docId:null,anchorId:null,ids:new Set()};
        let _outlineEscBound=false;
        let _dragState=null;
        function outlineVisibleSeq(doc){
          const seq=[];
          (function walk(n){
            if(!n)return;
            seq.push(n.id);
            if(!n.collapsed&&n.children)n.children.forEach(cid=>{const cn=N(cid);if(cn)walk(cn);});
          })(doc);
          return seq;
        }
        function toggleOutlineSel(id){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();
          const ids=new Set(_outlineSel.ids);
          if(ids.has(id))ids.delete(id);else ids.add(id);
          _outlineSel.ids=ids;_outlineSel.anchorId=id;_outlineSel.docId=doc?doc.id:null;
        }
        function outlineSelectRange(doc,anchorId,targetId,additive){
          const seq=outlineVisibleSeq(doc);
          const ai=seq.indexOf(anchorId),ti=seq.indexOf(targetId);
          if(ai<0||ti<0)return;
          const lo=Math.min(ai,ti),hi=Math.max(ai,ti);
          const range=new Set(seq.slice(lo,hi+1));
          if(additive){
            const ids=new Set(_outlineSel.ids);
            range.forEach(id=>ids.add(id));
            _outlineSel.ids=ids;
          }else{
            _outlineSel.ids=range;
          }
          _outlineSel.anchorId=anchorId;
          forceRender();
        }
        function topSelected(doc){
          const ids=[];
          _outlineSel.ids.forEach(id=>{
            const n=N(id);if(!n)return;
            const p=N(n.parent);
            if(!p||p.id===state.rootId||!_outlineSel.ids.has(n.parent))ids.push(id);
          });
          const seq=outlineVisibleSeq(doc);
          return ids.sort((a,b)=>seq.indexOf(a)-seq.indexOf(b));
        }
        function isDescendantOf(id,ancId){
          let cur=id;
          while(cur){
            const n=N(cur);if(!n)return false;
            if(cur===ancId)return true;
            cur=n.parent;
          }
          return false;
        }
        function outlineBatchMove(tops,toParentId,toIndex){
          if(!tops.length)return;
          if(tops.some(tid=>isDescendantOf(toParentId,tid))){toast('鏃犳硶绉诲姩鍒拌嚜韬垨鍏跺瓙鑺傜偣涓?);return;}
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();
          const toRef=N(toParentId);
          if(!toRef)return;
          commit(s=>{
            const to=s.nodes[toParentId];
            if(!to)return;
            
            let removedBefore=0;
            for(const id of tops){
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)continue;
              const p=s.nodes[n.parent];
              if(p===to){const i=p.children.indexOf(id);if(i>=0&&i<toIndex)removedBefore++;}
            }
            tops.forEach(id=>{
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;
              const p=s.nodes[n.parent];
              if(p){const i=p.children.indexOf(id);if(i>=0)p.children.splice(i,1);}
            });
            let idx=Math.max(0,Math.min(toIndex-removedBefore,to.children.length));
            tops.forEach(id=>{
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;
              n.parent=toParentId;
              to.children.splice(idx,0,id);
              idx++;
            });
          },{snapshot:true});
          forceRender();
          _outlineSel.ids=new Set(tops);
          toast('宸茬Щ鍔?'+tops.length+' 涓妭鐐?);
        }
        function outlineBatchIndent(){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();if(!doc)return;
          const tops=topSelected(doc);
          if(!tops.length)return;
          commit(s=>{
            tops.forEach(id=>{
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;
              const p=s.nodes[n.parent];if(!p)return;
              const idx=p.children.indexOf(id);
              if(idx<=0)return;
              const prev=p.children[idx-1];
              const pv=s.nodes[prev];if(!pv)return;
              const fi=p.children.indexOf(id);
              p.children.splice(fi,1);
              pv.children.push(id);
              n.parent=prev;
            });
          },{snapshot:true});
          forceRender();toast('宸茬缉杩?'+tops.length+' 涓妭鐐?);
        }
        function outlineBatchOutdent(){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();if(!doc)return;
          const tops=topSelected(doc);
          if(!tops.length)return;
          commit(s=>{
            tops.forEach(id=>{
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n)return;
              const p=s.nodes[n.parent];
              if(!p||p.id===state.rootId)return;
              const gp=s.nodes[p.parent];
              if(!gp||gp.id===state.rootId)return;
              const pi=gp.children.indexOf(p.id);
              if(pi<0)return;
              const fi=p.children.indexOf(id);
              p.children.splice(fi,1);
              gp.children.splice(pi+1,0,id);
              n.parent=gp.id;
            });
          },{snapshot:true});
          forceRender();toast('宸插弽缂╄繘 '+tops.length+' 涓妭鐐?);
        }
        function outlineBatchDelete(){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();if(!doc)return;
          const tops=topSelected(doc);
          if(!tops.length)return;
          appConfirm('纭鍒犻櫎鎵€閫?'+tops.length+' 涓妭鐐癸紙鍚叾瀛愯妭鐐癸級锛?,()=>{
            tops.forEach(id=>actions.removeNode(id));
            _outlineSel.ids=new Set();_outlineSel.anchorId=null;
            forceRender();
          });
        }
        function outlineDragStart(e,id){
          if(e.button!==0)return;
          e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();
          if(!doc)return;
          if(!_outlineSel.ids.has(id)){
            _outlineSel.ids=new Set([id]);_outlineSel.anchorId=id;_outlineSel.docId=doc.id;
            forceRender();
          }
          const tops=topSelected(doc);
          if(!tops.length)return;
          _dragState={docId:doc.id,startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,active:false,tops,ghost:null,line:null,cur:null};
          document.addEventListener('mousemove',outlineDragMove);
          document.addEventListener('mouseup',outlineDragEnd);
        }
        function outlineDropTarget(x,y){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();if(!doc)return null;
          const rows=[...document.querySelectorAll('.outline .node > .row')];
          let row=null;
          for(const r of rows){
            const rc=r.getBoundingClientRect();
            if(y>=rc.top&&y<=rc.bottom){row=r;break;}
          }
          if(!row)return null;
          const id=row.parentElement.dataset.id;
          if(!id||_outlineSel.ids.has(id))return null;
          const n=N(id);if(!n)return null;
          const rc=row.getBoundingClientRect();
          const after=y>rc.top+rc.height/2;
          const depth=Number(row.dataset.depth||0);
          const oc=document.querySelector('.outline').getBoundingClientRect();
          const baseX=oc.left+24;
          const L=Math.max(0,depth+Math.round((x-baseX)/22));
          let parent=null,index=0,lineTop=0,lineLeft=baseX+L*22;
          if(L>=depth+1){
            parent=id;index=after?n.children.length:0;
            lineTop=after?rc.bottom:rc.top;
            lineLeft=baseX+(depth+1)*22;
          }else if(L<=depth-1){
            let anc=n,ancLevel=depth;
            while(ancLevel>L&&anc.parent&&anc.parent!==state.rootId){
              const ancNode=N(anc.parent);if(!ancNode)return null;
              anc=ancNode;ancLevel--;
              if(_outlineSel.ids.has(anc.id))return null;
            }
            if(anc.id===n.id)return null;
            let branch=n;
            let curN=n,curP=N(curN.parent);
            while(curP&&curP.id!==anc.id){
              branch=curN;curN=curP;curP=N(curN.parent);
            }
            parent=anc.id;
            index=anc.children.indexOf(branch.id)+(after?1:0);
            lineTop=after?rc.bottom:rc.top;
          }else{
            const par=N(n.parent);
            if(!par)return null;
            parent=par.id;
            index=par.children.indexOf(id)+(after?1:0);
            lineTop=after?rc.bottom:rc.top;
            lineLeft=baseX+depth*22;
          }
          if(parent==null)return null;
          if(_dragState&&_dragState.tops.some(tid=>isDescendantOf(parent,tid)))return null;
          return {parent,index,lineTop,lineLeft,lineWidth:Math.max(140,rc.right-x)};
        }
        function outlineDragMove(e){
          if(!_dragState)return;
          const dx=e.clientX-_dragState.startX,dy=e.clientY-_dragState.startY;
          if(!_dragState.active){
            if(Math.abs(dx)<5&&Math.abs(dy)<5)return;
            _dragState.active=true;
            const ghost=document.createElement('div');ghost.className='outline-ghost';
            ghost.textContent='绉诲姩 '+_dragState.tops.length+' 涓妭鐐?;
            document.body.appendChild(ghost);
            const line=document.createElement('div');line.className='outline-drop-line';
            document.body.appendChild(line);
            _dragState.ghost=ghost;_dragState.line=line;
            document.body.classList.add('drag-outline');
            _dragState.tops.forEach(id=>{
              const el=document.querySelector('.outline .node[data-id="'+CSS.escape(id)+'"] > .row');
              if(el)el.classList.add('dragging');
            });
          }
          if(_dragState.ghost){
            _dragState.ghost.style.left=(e.clientX+12)+'px';
            _dragState.ghost.style.top=(e.clientY+12)+'px';
          }
          const t=outlineDropTarget(e.clientX,e.clientY);
          _dragState.cur=t;
          if(t&&_dragState.line){
            _dragState.line.style.display='block';
            _dragState.line.style.left=(t.lineLeft)+'px';
            _dragState.line.style.top=(t.lineTop-1)+'px';
            _dragState.line.style.width=(t.lineWidth)+'px';
          }else if(_dragState.line){
            _dragState.line.style.display='none';
          }
          const sc=document.getElementById('content');
          if(sc){
            const r=sc.getBoundingClientRect();
            if(e.clientY<r.top+46)sc.scrollTop-=14;
            else if(e.clientY>r.bottom-46)sc.scrollTop+=14;
          }
        }
        function outlineDragEnd(e){
          const ds=_dragState;
          if(!ds)return;
          _dragState=null;
          document.removeEventListener('mousemove',outlineDragMove);
          document.removeEventListener('mouseup',outlineDragEnd);
          if(ds.ghost)ds.ghost.remove();
          if(ds.line)ds.line.remove();
          document.body.classList.remove('drag-outline');
          ds.tops.forEach(id=>{
            const el=document.querySelector('.outline .node[data-id="'+CSS.escape(id)+'"] > .row');
            if(el)el.classList.remove('dragging');
          });
          if(ds.active&&ds.cur){
            const t=ds.cur;
            outlineBatchMove(ds.tops,t.parent,t.index);
          }
        }
        
        function renderInlineTable(n){
          const data=n.tableData||{cols:['椤圭洰','鏁伴噺','閲戦'],rows:[['','','']]};
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='inline-table-wrap';
          const caption=document.createElement('div');caption.className='it-caption';
          caption.textContent='鍐呰仈琛ㄦ牸 路 鍙抽敭琛ㄥご绠＄悊鍒?;
          wrap.appendChild(caption);
          const table=document.createElement('table');table.className='inline-table';
          const colmenu=(ci,e)=>{
            showContextMenu([
              {ic:'鈫?,txt:'宸︾Щ',fn:()=>{
                if(ci<=0)return;
                commit(s=>{
                  const d=s.nodes[n.id];if(!d||!d.tableData)return;
                  [d.tableData.cols[ci-1],d.tableData.cols[ci]]=[d.tableData.cols[ci],d.tableData.cols[ci-1]];
                  d.tableData.rows.forEach(r=>{[r[ci-1],r[ci]]=[r[ci],r[ci-1]];});
                },{snapshot:true});
                forceRender();
              }},
              {ic:'鈫?,txt:'鍙崇Щ',fn:()=>{
                if(ci>=data.cols.length-1)return;
                commit(s=>{
                  const d=s.nodes[n.id];if(!d||!d.tableData)return;
                  [d.tableData.cols[ci+1],d.tableData.cols[ci]]=[d.tableData.cols[ci],d.tableData.cols[ci+1]];
                  d.tableData.rows.forEach(r=>{[r[ci+1],r[ci]]=[r[ci],r[ci+1]];});
                },{snapshot:true});
                forceRender();
              }},
              {sep:true},
              {ic:'鉁忥笍',txt:'閲嶅懡鍚嶅垪',fn:()=>{
                appPrompt('鍒楀悕绉帮細',data.cols[ci],(v)=>{
                  if(!v||!v.trim())return;
                  commit(s=>{
                    const d=s.nodes[n.id];if(!d||!d.tableData)return;
                    d.tableData.cols[ci]=v.trim();
                  },{snapshot:true});
                  forceRender();
                });
              }},
              {ic:'馃棏',txt:'鍒犻櫎鍒?,danger:true,fn:()=>{
                if(data.cols.length<=1){toast('鑷冲皯淇濈暀涓€鍒?);return;}
                commit(s=>{
                  const d=s.nodes[n.id];if(!d||!d.tableData)return;
                  d.tableData.cols.splice(ci,1);
                  d.tableData.rows.forEach(r=>r.splice(ci,1));
                },{snapshot:true});
                forceRender();
              }}
            ],e);
          };
          const thead=document.createElement('thead');
          const hr=document.createElement('tr');
          data.cols.forEach((c,ci)=>{
            const th=document.createElement('th');th.className='it-col-head';
            const inp=document.createElement('input');inp.value=c;
            inp.title='閲嶅懡鍚嶏細鐩存帴淇敼鍚庡洖杞?;
            inp.onchange=()=>{
              commit(s=>{
                const d=s.nodes[n.id];if(!d||!d.tableData)return;
                d.tableData.cols[ci]=inp.value.trim()||('鍒?+(ci+1));
              },{snapshot:true});
            };
            const menu=document.createElement('span');menu.className='it-col-menu';menu.textContent='鈰?;
            menu.title='鍒楁搷浣?;
            menu.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();colmenu(ci,e);};
            th.appendChild(inp);th.appendChild(menu);
            hr.appendChild(th);
          });
          const thDel=document.createElement('th');thDel.style.cssText='width:24px;min-width:24px;padding:0';
          hr.appendChild(thDel);
          thead.appendChild(hr);table.appendChild(thead);
          const tbody=document.createElement('tbody');tbody.className='it-body';
          const commitCell=(ri,ci,val)=>{
            commit(s=>{
              const d=s.nodes[n.id];
              if(!d)return;
              if(!d.tableData)d.tableData={cols:['A'],rows:[['']]};
              if(!d.tableData.rows[ri])d.tableData.rows[ri]=[];
              while(d.tableData.rows[ri].length<=ci)d.tableData.rows[ri].push('');
              d.tableData.rows[ri][ci]=val;
              d.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
            },{snapshot:true});
          };
          data.rows.forEach((r,ri)=>{
            const tr=document.createElement('tr');
            for(let ci=0;ci<data.cols.length;ci++){
              const td=document.createElement('td');
              const inp=document.createElement('input');
              inp.value=(r&&r[ci])!=null?r[ci]:'';
              inp.oninput=()=>{_dbCellSchedule(n.id+'::it::'+ri+':'+ci,()=>commitCell(ri,ci,inp.value),300);};
              inp.onblur=()=>{_dbCellCancel(n.id+'::it::'+ri+':'+ci);commitCell(ri,ci,inp.value);};
              td.appendChild(inp);
              tr.appendChild(td);
            }
            const tdDel=document.createElement('td');tdDel.style.cssText='width:24px;min-width:24px;padding:0;position:relative';
            const del=document.createElement('span');del.className='it-row-del';del.textContent='鉁?;
            del.title='鍒犻櫎琛?;
            del.onclick=()=>{
              commit(s=>{const d=s.nodes[n.id];if(!d||!d.tableData)return;d.tableData.rows.splice(ri,1);d.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true});
              forceRender();
            };
            tdDel.appendChild(del);
            tr.appendChild(tdDel);
            tbody.appendChild(tr);
          });
          const addTr=document.createElement('tr');
          const addTd=document.createElement('td');addTd.colSpan=data.cols.length;
          addTd.className='it-add-cell';addTd.textContent='锛?娣诲姞琛?;
          addTd.onclick=()=>{
            commit(s=>{
              const d=s.nodes[n.id];
              if(!d)return;
              if(!d.tableData)d.tableData={cols:['A'],rows:[['']]};
              d.tableData.rows.push(new Array(d.tableData.cols.length).fill(''));
              d.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
            },{snapshot:true});
            forceRender();
          };
          const addTd2=document.createElement('td');addTd2.className='it-add-cell';addTd2.textContent='锛嬪垪';
          addTd2.title='娣诲姞鍒?;
          addTd2.onclick=()=>{
            commit(s=>{
              const d=s.nodes[n.id];
              if(!d)return;
              if(!d.tableData)d.tableData={cols:['A'],rows:[['']]};
              d.tableData.cols.push('鍒?+(d.tableData.cols.length+1));
              d.tableData.rows.forEach(r=>r.push(''));
              d.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
            },{snapshot:true});
            forceRender();
          };
          addTr.appendChild(addTd);addTr.appendChild(addTd2);
          tbody.appendChild(addTr);
          table.appendChild(tbody);
          
          const sums=data.cols.map((c,ci)=>{
            
            const nums=data.rows.map(r=>{if(!r)return null;return r[ci];}).filter(v=>v!==''&&v!=null).map(Number).filter(x=>!isNaN(x));
            return nums.length?nums.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0):null;
          });
          if(sums.slice(1).some(v=>v!==null)){
            const tf=document.createElement('tfoot');
            const tr=document.createElement('tr');tr.className='it-footer';
            const td0=document.createElement('td');td0.textContent='姹囨€?;
            td0.style.cssText='font-weight:700;color:var(--fg-dim)';
            tr.appendChild(td0);
            for(let ci=1;ci<data.cols.length;ci++){
              const td=document.createElement('td');
              td.textContent=sums[ci]==null?'':String(Math.round(sums[ci]*100)/100);
              tr.appendChild(td);
            }
            const sp=document.createElement('td');tr.appendChild(sp);
            tf.appendChild(tr);
            table.appendChild(tf);
          }
          wrap.appendChild(table);
          return wrap;
        }
        
        function syncEmptyState(el,ph){
          if(ph!==undefined&&el.dataset.ph===undefined)el.dataset.ph=ph;
          if(!el.textContent)el.dataset.empty='1';
          else delete el.dataset.empty;
        }
        function bindOutlineText(text,id){
          let composing=false;
          let _lastDoneTapAt=0;
          text.addEventListener('compositionstart',()=>composing=true);
          text.addEventListener('compositionend',()=>{composing=false;syncEmptyState(text,'杈撳叆鍐呭');});
          text.addEventListener('input',e=>{
            if(composing||e.isComposing)return;
            syncEmptyState(text,'杈撳叆鍐呭');
          });
          text.addEventListener('focus',()=>{
            savedFocus=null;
            ui.focusId=id;
            $$('.row.sel').forEach(r=>r.classList.remove('sel'));
            text.closest('.row')?.classList.add('sel');
          });
          text.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
            if(isBlurSuppressed())return;
            if(!text.isConnected)return;
            const finalHtml=sanitizeHtml(text.innerHTML);
            const cur=N(id);
            if(cur&&finalHtml!==(cur.text||''))actions.updateText(id,finalHtml,{noHistory:true}); 
          });
          
          text.addEventListener('paste',e=>{
            try{
              const dt=e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData;
              if(!dt)return;
              e.preventDefault();
              const html=dt.getData('text/html');
              const plain=dt.getData('text/plain');
              if(html){
                const clean=sanitizeHtml(html);
                if(clean){
                  document.execCommand('insertHTML',false,clean);
                  return;
                }
              }
              if(plain!=null&&plain!=='')document.execCommand('insertText',false,plain);
            }catch(err){}
          });
          text.addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{
            if(e.target!==text)return;
            if(e.altKey)return;
            if(Date.now()-_lastTouchDoneToggleAt<600)return;
            const sel=window.getSelection();
            if(sel&&!sel.isCollapsed)return;
            e.preventDefault();
            actions.toggleDone(id);
            forceRender();
          });
          text.addEventListener('click',e=>{
            if(e.target!==text)return;
            
            if(_lastPointerType==='touch'&&!e.altKey&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey){
              const tsel=window.getSelection();
              if(tsel&&tsel.isCollapsed){
                const now=Date.now();
                if(now-_lastDoneTapAt<300){
                  _lastDoneTapAt=0;
                  _lastTouchDoneToggleAt=Date.now();
                  e.preventDefault();
                  actions.toggleDone(id);forceRender();
                  return;
                }
                _lastDoneTapAt=now;
              }
            }
            if(e.altKey){e.preventDefault();actions.toggleDone(id);forceRender();return;}
            
            if(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey){
              const lt=linkTitleAtPoint(text,e);
              if(!lt)return;
              e.preventDefault();
              navigateToWikiLink(lt,text);
            }
          });
          
          text.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{
            
            if(_lastPointerType==='touch')return;
            e.preventDefault();
            const cur=N(id);
            if(!cur)return;
            const items=[];
            if(_outlineSel.ids.size>1&&_outlineSel.ids.has(id)){
              const tops=topSelected(getCurrentDoc());
              items.push({sep:true});
              items.push({ic:'馃棏',txt:'鍒犻櫎鎵€閫?('+tops.length+' 涓?',danger:true,fn:()=>outlineBatchDelete()});
              items.push({ic:'猡?,txt:'缂╄繘鎵€閫?,fn:()=>outlineBatchIndent()});
              items.push({ic:'猡?,txt:'鍙嶇缉杩涙墍閫?,fn:()=>outlineBatchOutdent()});
              items.push({sep:true});
            }
            items.push(
              {ic:cur.done?'鈼?:'鈼?,txt:cur.done?'鏍囪鏈畬鎴?:'鏍囪瀹屾垚',fn:()=>{actions.toggleDone(id);forceRender();}},
              {ic:'猡?,txt:'缂╄繘',fn:()=>indentNode(id)},
              {ic:'猡?,txt:'鍙嶇缉杩?,fn:()=>outdentNode(id)},
              {sep:true},
              {ic:'馃搵',txt:'澶嶅埗鑺傜偣',fn:()=>{
                const p=N(cur.parent);if(!p)return;
                const idx=p.children.indexOf(id);
                const newId=actions.addNode(cur.parent,idx+1,
                  clone({text:cur.text,done:cur.done,date:cur.date,time:cur.time,timeEnd:cur.timeEnd,tags:cur.tags,priority:cur.priority}));
                forceRender();
                if(newId)focusAfterRender(newId,'.outline');
              }},
              {ic:'馃搮',txt:'娣诲姞鍒版棩鍘?,fn:()=>{actions.setNodeDate(id,todayStr());toast('宸叉坊鍔犲埌鏃ュ巻');}},
              cur.block==='table'
                ?{ic:'馃摑',txt:'杞负鏂囨湰鑺傜偣',fn:()=>{
                  commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[id];if(!nn)return;delete nn.block;delete nn.tableData;nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true,op:'updateText',opArgs:{id}});
                  forceRender();
                }}
                :{ic:'馃搳',txt:'鎻掑叆鍐呰仈琛ㄦ牸',fn:()=>{
                  commit(s=>{
                    const nn=s.nodes[id];
                    if(!nn)return;
                    nn.block='table';
                    nn.tableData={cols:['椤圭洰','鏁伴噺','閲戦'],rows:[['','','']]};
                    nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
                  },{snapshot:true});
                  forceRender();
                }},
              {sep:true},
              {ic:'馃棏',txt:'鍒犻櫎鑺傜偣',danger:true,fn:()=>{
                const prevId=previousVisible(id);
                actions.removeNode(id);
                forceRender();
                if(prevId)focusAfterRender(prevId,'.outline');
              }}
            );
            showContextMenu(items,e);
          });
          text.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
            const n=N(id);if(!n)return;
            
            if(e.isComposing||e.keyCode===229)return;
            const sel=window.getSelection();
            if(sel && !sel.isCollapsed && e.key==='Backspace' && !e.altKey) return;
            let atStart=false;
            if(sel&&sel.isCollapsed&&sel.anchorNode&&text.contains(sel.anchorNode)){
              try{
                const r=document.createRange();
                r.selectNodeContents(text);
                r.setEnd(sel.anchorNode,sel.anchorOffset);
                atStart=r.toString().length===0;
                
                if(!atStart&&sel.anchorOffset===0&&(!text.textContent||text.textContent.replace(/\s/g,'')===''))atStart=true;
              }catch(err){atStart=false;}
            }
            if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&e.key.toLowerCase()==='z'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();if(undo()){forceRender();toast('宸叉挙閿€');}return;}
            if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&(e.key.toLowerCase()==='y'||(e.shiftKey&&e.key.toLowerCase()==='z'))){e.preventDefault();if(redo()){forceRender();toast('宸查噸鍋?);}return;}
            if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey){
              e.preventDefault();
              const s2=window.getSelection();
              if(!s2||!s2.rangeCount)return;
              const range=s2.getRangeAt(0);
              const afterRange=range.cloneRange();
              afterRange.selectNodeContents(text);
              afterRange.setStart(range.endContainer,range.endOffset);
              const afterFrag=afterRange.extractContents();
              const tmp=document.createElement('div');
              tmp.appendChild(afterFrag);
              const afterHtml=sanitizeHtml(tmp.innerHTML).trim();
              const beforeHtml=sanitizeHtml(text.innerHTML).trim();
              const p=N(n.parent);const idx=p?p.children.indexOf(id):0;
              let newId=null;
              commit(s=>{
                const nn=s.nodes[id];
                if(nn&&beforeHtml!==(nn.text||'')){nn.text=beforeHtml;nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();}
                const added=addNodeTo(s,n.parent,idx+1,{text:afterHtml});
                if(added)newId=added.id;
              },{snapshot:true});
              if(!newId)return;
              actions.logActivity('add',newId);
              forceRender();
              focusAfterRender(newId,'.outline');
              return;
            }
            if(e.key==='Tab'){e.preventDefault();if(e.shiftKey)outdentNode(id);else indentNode(id);return;}
            if(e.key==='Backspace'&&atStart&&!e.altKey){
              e.preventDefault();
              
              const liveHtml=sanitizeHtml(text.innerHTML);
              const livePlain=stripHtml(liveHtml).trim();
              if(livePlain.length>0){
                const prevId=previousVisible(id);
                if(prevId){
                  const prevNode=N(prevId);
                  
                  if(prevNode&&prevNode.parent===state.rootId)return;
                  
                  const caretOffset=stripHtml((prevNode&&prevNode.text)||'').length;
                  commit(s=>{
                    const pn=s.nodes[prevId],cn=s.nodes[id];
                    if(!pn||!cn)return;
                    cn.text=liveHtml;                 
                    
                    try{
                      const docP=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<div>'+(pn.text||'')+'</div>','text/html');
                      const docC=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<div>'+cn.text+'</div>','text/html');
                      const bodyP=docP.body.firstChild;
                      Array.from(docC.body.firstChild.childNodes).forEach(child=>{
                        bodyP.appendChild(docP.importNode(child,true));
                      });
                      pn.text=bodyP.innerHTML;
                    }catch(e){
                      pn.text=(pn.text||'')+cn.text; 
                    }
                    pn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();          
                    const movedKids=(cn.children||[]).slice();
                    movedKids.forEach(cid=>{
                      const cc=s.nodes[cid];
                      if(cc){cc.parent=prevId;pn.children.push(cid);}
                    });
                    const p=s.nodes[cn.parent];
                    if(p){const i=p.children.indexOf(id);if(i>=0)p.children.splice(i,1);}
                    delete s.nodes[id];
                    pn.collapsed=false;
                  },{snapshot:true});
                  actions.logActivity('mod',prevId);  
                  forceRender();focusAfterRender(prevId,'.outline',{offset:caretOffset});
                }
                return;
              }
              
              if((n.children||[]).length){
                const p=N(n.parent);
                const gp=p?N(p.parent):null;
                if(!gp||gp.id===state.rootId){toast('璇ヨ妭鐐瑰凡澶勪簬椤跺眰锛屾棤娉曞啀鍙嶇缉杩?);return;}
                outdentNode(id);
                return;
              }
              
              const prevId=previousVisible(id);
              if(prevId){
                actions.removeNode(id);
                forceRender();focusAfterRender(prevId,'.outline');
              }else{
                actions.removeNode(id);
                forceRender();
              }
              return;
            }
            if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&(e.key==='ArrowUp'||e.key==='ArrowDown')){e.preventDefault();moveSibling(id,e.key==='ArrowUp'?-1:1);return;}
            if(!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey&&!e.altKey&&e.key==='ArrowUp'&&sel&&sel.isCollapsed){
              let atFirstLine=false;
              try{
                const r=document.createRange();
                r.selectNodeContents(text);
                r.setEnd(sel.anchorNode,sel.anchorOffset);
                atFirstLine=!r.toString().includes('\n');
              }catch(err){}
              if(atFirstLine){
                const prevId=previousVisible(id);
                if(prevId){e.preventDefault();focusNode(prevId,null,true);}
              }
              return;
            }
            if(!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey&&!e.altKey&&e.key==='ArrowDown'&&sel&&sel.isCollapsed){
              const nextId=nextVisible(id);
              if(nextId){e.preventDefault();focusNode(nextId,null,true);}
              return;
            }
            if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&e.key.toLowerCase()==='d'){
              e.preventDefault();
              const p=N(n.parent);if(!p)return;
              const idx=p.children.indexOf(id);
              const newId=actions.addNode(n.parent,idx+1,clone({text:n.text,done:n.done,date:n.date,time:n.time,timeEnd:n.timeEnd,tags:n.tags,priority:n.priority}));
              forceRender();focusAfterRender(newId,'.outline');toast('宸插鍒?);return;
            }
            if(!(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey))e.stopPropagation();
          });
        }
        function previousVisible(id){
          const n=N(id);if(!n)return null;
          const p=N(n.parent);if(!p)return null;
          const i=p.children.indexOf(id);
          if(i>0){
            let prev=p.children[i-1];
            
            const _vis=new Set();
            while(N(prev)&&N(prev).children.length&&!N(prev).collapsed&&!_vis.has(prev)){_vis.add(prev);const c=N(prev).children;prev=c[c.length-1];}
            return N(prev)?prev:null;
          }
          return p.id===state.rootId?null:p.id;
        }
        function nextVisible(id){
          const n=N(id);
          if(!n)return null;
          if(n.children.length&&!n.collapsed)return n.children[0];
          let cur=id;
          const _vis=new Set(); 
          while(cur&&!_vis.has(cur)){
            _vis.add(cur);
            const parentId=N(cur)?.parent;
            if(!parentId)return null;
            const p=N(parentId);
            if(!p)return null;
            const i=p.children.indexOf(cur);
            if(i<p.children.length-1)return p.children[i+1];
            cur=p.id;
            if(cur===state.rootId)return null;
          }
          return null;
        }
        function indentNode(id){
          const n=N(id);if(!n)return;
          const p=N(n.parent);if(!p)return;
          const idx=p.children.indexOf(id);
          if(idx<=0)return;
          const prevId=p.children[idx-1];
          const prev=N(prevId);if(!prev)return;
          actions.moveNode(id,prevId,prev.children.length);
          forceRender();focusAfterRender(id,'.outline');
        }
        function outdentNode(id){
          const n=N(id);if(!n)return;
          const p=N(n.parent);if(!p||p.id===state.rootId)return;
          const gp=N(p.parent);
          if(!gp||gp.id===state.rootId)return;   
          const pi=gp.children.indexOf(p.id);
          actions.moveNode(id,gp.id,pi+1);
          forceRender();focusAfterRender(id,'.outline');
        }
        function moveSibling(id,dir){
          const n=N(id);if(!n)return;
          const p=N(n.parent);if(!p)return;
          const i=p.children.indexOf(id);
          if(i<0)return;
          const j=i+dir;
          if(j<0||j>=p.children.length)return;
          actions.moveNode(id,p.id,j);
          forceRender();focusAfterRender(id,'.outline');
        }
        
        function ensureCalendarRoot(){
          let id=null;
          for(const nid in state.nodes){if(state.nodes[nid]._isCalRoot){id=nid;break;}}
          if(id)return id;
          id=uid();
          commit(s=>{
            const root=s.nodes[s.rootId];
            if(!root)return;
            s.nodes[id]={id,text:'鏃ュ巻寰呭姙',parent:root.id,children:[],collapsed:false,
              done:false,date:null,tags:[],priority:0,recur:null,habit:null,time:null,
              note:null,color:null,showInCal:false,_isCalRoot:true,_isFolder:true,
              createdAt:Date.now(),updatedAt:Date.now()};
            root.children.push(id);
          },{silent:true,persistOnly:true});
          
          dedupCalRoots();
          return getCalendarRootId();
        }
        function getCalendarRootId(){
          for(const nid in state.nodes){if(state.nodes[nid]._isCalRoot)return nid;}
          return ensureCalendarRoot();
        }
        
        function dedupCalRoots(){
          try{
            const all=Object.values(state.nodes).filter(n=>n&&n._isCalRoot&&!n._deleted);
            if(all.length<=1)return;
            all.sort((a,b)=>((a.createdAt||0)-(b.createdAt||0))||(a.id<b.id?-1:a.id>b.id?1:0));
            const keep=all[0];
            all.slice(1).forEach(d=>{
              commit(s=>{
                const k=s.nodes[keep.id],dd=s.nodes[d.id];
                if(!k||!dd)return;
                (dd.children||[]).forEach(c=>{
                  if(s.nodes[c]){
                    s.nodes[c].parent=keep.id;
                    if(!k.children.includes(c))k.children.push(c);
                  }
                });
                const p=s.nodes[dd.parent];
                if(p&&Array.isArray(p.children)){const i=p.children.indexOf(d.id);if(i>=0)p.children.splice(i,1);}
                delete s.nodes[d.id];
              },{snapshot:true,noHistory:true});
            });
            forceRender();
          }catch(e){}
        }
        function expandRecurring(n,targetDs){
          if(!n.recur||!n.date)return false;
          if(n.recurEnd&&targetDs>n.recurEnd)return false; 
          const base=parseYmd(n.date);
          const target=parseYmd(targetDs);
          if(isNaN(base.getTime())||isNaN(target.getTime()))return false;
          if(target<base)return false;
          const{freq,interval=1,weekday}=n.recur;
          if(freq==='daily'){const diff=Math.round((target-base)/86400000);return diff%interval===0;}
          if(freq==='weekly'){
            if(weekday!=null){
              if(target.getDay()!==weekday)return false;
              const bs=new Date(base);bs.setDate(bs.getDate()-bs.getDay());
              const ts=new Date(target);ts.setDate(ts.getDate()-ts.getDay());
              const weeks=Math.round((ts-bs)/86400000/7);
              return weeks>=0&&weeks%interval===0;
            }
            const diff=Math.round((target-base)/86400000);
            return diff%(7*interval)===0;
          }
          if(freq==='monthly'){
            return target.getDate()===base.getDate()&&
              ((target.getFullYear()-base.getFullYear())*12+(target.getMonth()-base.getMonth()))%interval===0;
          }
          if(freq==='yearly'){
            return target.getMonth()===base.getMonth()&&target.getDate()===base.getDate()&&
              (target.getFullYear()-base.getFullYear())%interval===0;
          }
          if(freq==='workday'){
            
            const dow=target.getDay();
            if(dow===0||dow===6)return false;
            if(isRestDay(target.getFullYear(),target.getMonth()+1,target.getDate()))return false;
            let wc=0;const cur=new Date(base);
            while(cur<target){
              const d=cur.getDay();
              if(d!==0&&d!==6&&!isRestDay(cur.getFullYear(),cur.getMonth()+1,cur.getDate()))wc++;
              cur.setDate(cur.getDate()+1);
            }
            return wc%interval===0;
          }
          if(freq==='lunar'){
            
            if(target.getFullYear()===base.getFullYear())return false; 
            if(target.getFullYear()-base.getFullYear()<0)return false;
            if((target.getFullYear()-base.getFullYear())%interval!==0)return false;
            const lu=solarToLunar(base.getFullYear(),base.getMonth()+1,base.getDate());
            if(!lu)return false;
            const hit=lunarToSolarDate(target.getFullYear(),lu.month,lu.day);
            if(!hit)return false;
            return ymd(target)===ymd(new Date(hit.y,hit.m-1,hit.d));
          }
          return false;
        }
        
        function lunarToSolarDate(y,lM,lD){
          for(let m=1;m<=12;m++){
            const dim=new Date(y,m,0).getDate();
            for(let d=1;d<=dim;d++){
              const lu=solarToLunar(y,m,d);
              if(lu&&lu.month===lM&&lu.day===lD)return{y,m,d};
            }
          }
          return null;
        }
        
        function _calSubtreeStep(stack,visited,idx,deadline){
          while(stack.length){
            const pid=stack.pop();
            if(visited.has(pid))continue;
            visited.add(pid);
            const parent=N(pid);if(!parent)continue;
            (parent.children||[]).forEach(cid=>{
              const n=N(cid);
              if(!n||n._deleted)return;
              if(n.date){if(!idx.has(n.date))idx.set(n.date,new Set());idx.get(n.date).add(cid);}
              stack.push(cid);
            });
            if(_l7Now()>=deadline)return true;
          }
          return false;
        }
        
        function _calSweepStep(ids,i,deadline,visited,idx){
          while(i<ids.length){
            const id=ids[i];i++;
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(n&&!n._deleted&&n.date&&!visited.has(id)){ 
              visited.add(id);
              if(!idx.has(n.date))idx.set(n.date,new Set());
              idx.get(n.date).add(id);
            }
            if(_l7Now()>=deadline)break;
          }
          return i;
        }
        function buildCalIndex(){
          if(_calIndexCache)return _calIndexCache;
          const idx=new Map();
          const calRootId=getCalendarRootId();
          const stack=[calRootId];
          const visited=new Set();
          _calSubtreeStep(stack,visited,idx,Infinity);
          const ids=Object.keys(state.nodes);
          let i=0;
          while(i<ids.length)i=_calSweepStep(ids,i,Infinity,visited,idx);
          _calIndexCache=idx;
          return idx;
        }
        function buildRecurList(){
          if(_calRecurCache)return _calRecurCache;
          const list=[];
          const calRootId=getCalendarRootId();
          const stack=[calRootId];const visited=new Set();
          while(stack.length){
            const pid=stack.pop();
            if(visited.has(pid))continue;
            visited.add(pid);
            const parent=N(pid);if(!parent)continue;
            (parent.children||[]).forEach(cid=>{
              const n=N(cid);
              if(!n||n._deleted)return;
              if(n.recur&&n.date)list.push(n);
              stack.push(cid);
            });
          }
          _calRecurCache=list;
          return list;
        }
        function calendarTodosOnDate(ds){
          if(_calDayCache){
            const hit=_calDayCache.get(ds); 
            if(hit!==undefined)return hit;
          }
          if(!_calDayCache)_calDayCache=makeLRU(2000);
          const idx=buildCalIndex();
          
          const result=[];
          const idSet=idx.get(ds);
          if(idSet){
            for(const id of idSet){
              const n=N(id);
              if(n)result.push(n);
            }
          }
          const recurs=buildRecurList();
          for(let i=0;i<recurs.length;i++){
            const n=recurs[i];
            if(n.date!==ds&&expandRecurring(n,ds))result.push(n);
          }
          result.sort((a,b)=>(a.createdAt-b.createdAt)||(a.id<b.id?-1:a.id>b.id?1:0));
          _calDayCache.set(ds,result); 
          return result;
        }
        function buildYearCounts(year){
          if(_yearCountsCache&&_yearCountsCache.year===year)return _yearCountsCache.map;
          const counts=new Map();
          const calRootId=getCalendarRootId();
          const stack=[calRootId];const visited=new Set();
          while(stack.length){
            const pid=stack.pop();
            if(visited.has(pid))continue;
            visited.add(pid);
            const parent=N(pid);if(!parent)continue;
            (parent.children||[]).forEach(cid=>{
              const n=N(cid);
              if(!n||n._deleted)return;
              if(n.showInCal!==false&&!isBlankNode(n)){
                if(n.date&&n.date.startsWith(year+'-'))counts.set(n.date,(counts.get(n.date)||0)+1);
                if(n.recur&&n.date){
                  const base=parseYmd(n.date);
                  if(!isNaN(base.getTime())){
                    const{freq,interval=1,weekday}=n.recur;
                    const yearStart=new Date(year,0,1);
                    const yearEnd=new Date(year,11,31);
                    const step=(d)=>{const ds2=ymd(d);if(n.recurEnd&&ds2>n.recurEnd)return;if(ds2!==n.date)counts.set(ds2,(counts.get(ds2)||0)+1);};
                    if(freq==='daily'){
                      const cur=new Date(base);
                      while(cur<yearStart)cur.setDate(cur.getDate()+interval);
                      while(cur<=yearEnd){step(cur);cur.setDate(cur.getDate()+interval);}
                    }else if(freq==='weekly'){
                      const wd=weekday!=null?weekday:base.getDay();
                      const first=new Date(year,0,1);
                      while(first.getDay()!==wd)first.setDate(first.getDate()+1);
                      const bs=new Date(base);bs.setDate(bs.getDate()-bs.getDay());
                      const d=new Date(first);
                      while(d<=yearEnd){
                        if(d>=base){
                          const ts=new Date(d);ts.setDate(ts.getDate()-ts.getDay());
                          const weeks=Math.round((ts-bs)/86400000/7);
                          if(weeks>=0&&weeks%interval===0)step(d);
                        }
                        d.setDate(d.getDate()+7);
                      }
                    }else if(freq==='monthly'){
                      for(let mm=0;mm<12;mm++){
                        const d=new Date(year,mm,base.getDate());
                        if(d.getMonth()!==mm)continue;
                        if(d<base||d>yearEnd)continue;
                        const mdiff=(year-base.getFullYear())*12+(mm-base.getMonth());
                        if(mdiff>=0&&mdiff%interval===0)step(d);
                      }
                    }else if(freq==='yearly'){
                      const d=new Date(year,base.getMonth(),base.getDate());
                      if(d.getFullYear()!==year)return;
                      if(d<base||d>yearEnd)return;
                      if((year-base.getFullYear())%interval===0)step(d);
                    }else if(freq==='workday'){
                      const cur=new Date(base);
                      while(cur<yearStart)cur.setDate(cur.getDate()+1);
                      let wc=0;
                      while(cur<=yearEnd){
                        const d=cur.getDay();
                        if(d!==0&&d!==6&&!isRestDay(cur.getFullYear(),cur.getMonth()+1,cur.getDate())){wc++;if(wc%interval===0)step(cur);}
                        cur.setDate(cur.getDate()+1);
                      }
                    }else if(freq==='lunar'){
                      const lu=solarToLunar(base.getFullYear(),base.getMonth()+1,base.getDate());
                      if(lu){
                        const hit=lunarToSolarDate(year,lu.month,lu.day);
                        if(hit){
                          const d=new Date(hit.y,hit.m-1,hit.d);
                          if(d>=base&&d<=yearEnd&&(year-base.getFullYear())%interval===0)step(d);
                        }
                      }
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
              stack.push(cid);
            });
          }
          _yearCountsCache={year,map:counts};
          return counts;
        }
        
        const LUNAR_INFO=[0x04bd8,0x04ae0,0x0a570,0x054d5,0x0d260,0x0d950,0x16554,0x056a0,0x09ad0,0x055d2,0x04ae0,0x0a5b6,0x0a4d0,0x0d250,0x1d255,0x0b540,0x0d6a0,0x0ada2,0x095b0,0x14977,0x04970,0x0a4b0,0x0b4b5,0x06a50,0x06d40,0x1ab54,0x02b60,0x09570,0x052f2,0x04970,0x06566,0x0d4a0,0x0ea50,0x06e95,0x05ad0,0x02b60,0x186e3,0x092e0,0x1c8d7,0x0c950,0x0d4a0,0x1d8a6,0x0b550,0x056a0,0x1a5b4,0x025d0,0x092d0,0x0d2b2,0x0a950,0x0b557,0x06ca0,0x0b550,0x15355,0x04da0,0x0a5b0,0x14573,0x052b0,0x0a9a8,0x0e950,0x06aa0,0x0aea6,0x0ab50,0x04b60,0x0aae4,0x0a570,0x05260,0x0f263,0x0d950,0x05b57,0x056a0,0x096d0,0x04dd5,0x04ad0,0x0a4d0,0x0d4d4,0x0d250,0x0d558,0x0b540,0x0b6a0,0x195a6,0x095b0,0x049b0,0x0a974,0x0a4b0,0x0b27a,0x06a50,0x06d40,0x0af46,0x0ab60,0x09570,0x04af5,0x04970,0x064b0,0x074a3,0x0ea50,0x06b58,0x055c0,0x0ab60,0x096d5,0x092e0,0x0c960,0x0d954,0x0d4a0,0x0da50,0x07552,0x056a0,0x0abb7,0x025d0,0x092d0,0x0cab5,0x0a950,0x0b4a0,0x0baa4,0x0ad50,0x055d9,0x04ba0,0x0a5b0,0x15176,0x052b0,0x0a930,0x07954,0x06aa0,0x0ad50,0x05b52,0x04b60,0x0a6e6,0x0a4e0,0x0d260,0x0ea65,0x0d530,0x05aa0,0x076a3,0x096d0,0x04afb,0x04ad0,0x0a4d0,0x1d0b6,0x0d250,0x0d520,0x0dd45,0x0b5a0,0x056d0,0x055b2,0x049b0,0x0a577,0x0a4b0,0x0aa50,0x1b255,0x06d20,0x0ada0];
        const LUNAR_MIN=1900,LUNAR_MAX=1900+LUNAR_INFO.length-1;
        function leapMonth(y){return LUNAR_INFO[y-1900]&0xf;}
        function leapDays(y){if(!leapMonth(y))return 0;return (LUNAR_INFO[y-1900]&0x10000)?30:29;}
        function monthDays(y,m){return (LUNAR_INFO[y-1900]&(0x10000>>m))?30:29;}
        function lYearDays(y){let s=348;for(let i=0x8000;i>0x8;i>>=1)s+=(LUNAR_INFO[y-1900]&i)?1:0;return s+leapDays(y);}
        function solarToLunar(y,m,d){
          if(y<LUNAR_MIN||y>LUNAR_MAX)return null;
          let offset=Math.floor((Date.UTC(y,m-1,d)-Date.UTC(1900,0,31))/86400000);
          if(offset<0)return null;
          let i,temp=0;
          for(i=1900;i<=LUNAR_MAX&&offset>0;i++){temp=lYearDays(i);offset-=temp;}
          if(offset<0){offset+=temp;i--;}
          const year=i,leap=leapMonth(year);
          let isLeap=false,j;
          for(j=1;j<13&&offset>0;j++){
            if(leap>0&&j===leap+1&&!isLeap){--j;isLeap=true;temp=leapDays(year);}
            else temp=monthDays(year,j);
            if(isLeap&&j===leap+1)isLeap=false;
            offset-=temp;
          }
          if(offset===0&&leap>0&&j===leap+1){if(isLeap){isLeap=false;}else{isLeap=true;--j;}}
          if(offset<0){offset+=temp;--j;}
          const month=j,day=offset+1;
          if(month<1||month>12||day<1||day>30)return null;
          return{year,month,day,isLeap};
        }
        const CN_NUM=['','涓€','浜?,'涓?,'鍥?,'浜?,'鍏?,'涓?,'鍏?,'涔?,'鍗?];
        const LUNAR_MONTH_CN=['','姝?,'浜?,'涓?,'鍥?,'浜?,'鍏?,'涓?,'鍏?,'涔?,'鍗?,'鍐?,'鑵?];
        const TIANGAN=['','鐢?,'涔?,'涓?,'涓?,'鎴?,'宸?,'搴?,'杈?,'澹?,'鐧?];
        const DIZHI=['','瀛?,'涓?,'瀵?,'鍗?,'杈?,'宸?,'鍗?,'鏈?,'鐢?,'閰?,'鎴?,'浜?];
        function lunarDayStr(d){if(d===10)return'鍒濆崄';if(d===20)return'浜屽崄';if(d===30)return'涓夊崄';const p=['鍒?,'鍗?,'寤?,'鍗?][Math.floor(d/10)];return p+CN_NUM[d%10];}
        function lunarMonthStr(m){return LUNAR_MONTH_CN[m]+'鏈?;}
        function ganzhiYear(y){const i=(y-4)%60;return TIANGAN[i%10+1]+DIZHI[i%12+1];}
        const SOLAR_FEST={'01-01':'鍏冩棪','02-14':'鎯呬汉鑺?,'03-08':'濡囧コ鑺?,'03-12':'妞嶆爲鑺?,'04-01':'鎰氫汉鑺?,'05-01':'鍔冲姩鑺?,'05-04':'闈掑勾鑺?,'06-01':'鍎跨鑺?,'07-01':'寤哄厷鑺?,'08-01':'寤哄啗鑺?,'09-10':'鏁欏笀鑺?,'10-01':'鍥藉簡鑺?,'12-24':'骞冲畨澶?,'12-25':'鍦ｈ癁鑺?};
        const LUNAR_FEST={'1-1':'鏄ヨ妭','1-15':'鍏冨','2-2':'榫欐姮澶?,'5-5':'绔崍','7-7':'涓冨','7-15':'涓厓','8-15':'涓','9-9':'閲嶉槼','12-8':'鑵婂叓'};
        
        const HOLIDAY_REST=[
          
          [2024,1,1,2024,1,1],[2024,2,10,2024,2,17],[2024,4,4,2024,4,6],[2024,5,1,2024,5,5],[2024,6,10,2024,6,10],[2024,9,15,2024,9,17],[2024,10,1,2024,10,7],
          
          [2025,1,1,2025,1,1],[2025,1,28,2025,2,4],[2025,4,4,2025,4,6],[2025,5,1,2025,5,5],[2025,5,31,2025,6,2],[2025,10,1,2025,10,8],
          
          [2026,1,1,2026,1,3],[2026,2,15,2026,2,23],[2026,4,4,2026,4,6],[2026,5,1,2026,5,5],[2026,6,19,2026,6,21],[2026,9,25,2026,9,27],[2026,10,1,2026,10,7]
