        function isRestDay(y,m,d){
          const t=y*10000+m*100+d;
          for(let i=0;i<HOLIDAY_REST.length;i++){
            const a=HOLIDAY_REST[i];
            const s=a[0]*10000+a[1]*100+a[2],e=a[3]*10000+a[4]*100+a[5];
            if(t>=s&&t<=e)return true;
            if(a[0]>y)return false;
          }
          
          return false;
        }
        function dayLabel(date){
          const y=date.getFullYear(),m=date.getMonth()+1,d=date.getDate();
          const key=pad(m)+'-'+pad(d);
          const rest=isRestDay(y,m,d);
          if(SOLAR_FEST[key])return{text:SOLAR_FEST[key],fest:true,rest};
          const lu=solarToLunar(y,m,d);
          if(lu){
            const lk=lu.month+'-'+lu.day;
            if(!lu.isLeap&&LUNAR_FEST[lk])return{text:LUNAR_FEST[lk],fest:true,rest};
            if(lu.day===1)return{text:(lu.isLeap?'闂?:'')+lunarMonthStr(lu.month),fest:false,rest};
            return{text:lunarDayStr(lu.day),fest:false,rest};
          }
          return{text:'',fest:false,rest};
        }
        
        function parseNaturalDate(str){
          str=str.trim().toLowerCase();
          const now=new Date();
          const result={date:null,time:null,recur:null};
          const recurMap={
            '姣忓懆涓€':{freq:'weekly',interval:1,weekday:1},
            '姣忓懆浜?:{freq:'weekly',interval:1,weekday:2},
            '姣忓懆涓?:{freq:'weekly',interval:1,weekday:3},
            '姣忓懆鍥?:{freq:'weekly',interval:1,weekday:4},
            '姣忓懆浜?:{freq:'weekly',interval:1,weekday:5},
            '姣忓懆鍏?:{freq:'weekly',interval:1,weekday:6},
            '姣忓懆鏃?:{freq:'weekly',interval:1,weekday:0},
            '姣忓懆澶?:{freq:'weekly',interval:1,weekday:0},
            '姣忓ぉ':{freq:'daily',interval:1},'姣忔棩':{freq:'daily',interval:1},
            '姣忓懆':{freq:'weekly',interval:1},
            '姣忔湀':{freq:'monthly',interval:1},'姣忓勾':{freq:'yearly',interval:1},
            '姣忔硶瀹氬伐浣滄棩':{freq:'workday',interval:1},'娉曞畾宸ヤ綔鏃?:{freq:'workday',interval:1},'宸ヤ綔鏃?:{freq:'workday',interval:1},
            '鍐滃巻骞撮噸澶?:{freq:'lunar',interval:1},'鍐滃巻骞?:{freq:'lunar',interval:1},'鍐滃巻':{freq:'lunar',interval:1}
          };
          const _recurKeys=Object.keys(recurMap).sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length);
          for(const key of _recurKeys){
            if(str.includes(key)){result.recur=recurMap[key];break;}
          }
          if(str.includes('鏄庡ぉ'))result.date=ymd(new Date(now.getTime()+86400000));
          else if(str.includes('鍚庡ぉ'))result.date=ymd(new Date(now.getTime()+2*86400000));
          else if(str.includes('浠婂ぉ')||str.includes('浠婃棩'))result.date=ymd(now);
          else if(str.includes('涓嬪懆')){
            const dayMap={'涓€':1,'浜?:2,'涓?:3,'鍥?:4,'浜?:5,'鍏?:6,'鏃?:0,'澶?:0};
            let found=false;
            for(const d in dayMap){
              if(str.includes('涓嬪懆'+d)){
                const target=dayMap[d];
                const cur=now.getDay();
                const daysToNextMonday=cur===0?1:(8-cur);
                const nextMonday=new Date(now.getFullYear(),now.getMonth(),now.getDate()+daysToNextMonday);
                const offset=(target===0?6:target-1);
                const targetDate=new Date(nextMonday.getFullYear(),nextMonday.getMonth(),nextMonday.getDate()+offset);
                result.date=ymd(targetDate);found=true;break;
              }
            }
            if(!found)result.date=ymd(new Date(now.getTime()+7*86400000));
          }else{
            const m2=str.match(/(\d{1,2})鏈?\d{1,2})[鍙锋棩]?/);
            if(m2){
              const mo=parseInt(m2[1])-1;
              const da=parseInt(m2[2]);
              const yr=now.getFullYear();
              let dt=new Date(yr,mo,da);
              if(dt<now)dt=new Date(yr+1,mo,da);
              result.date=ymd(dt);
            }
          }
          let _period=null;
          if(/涓嬪崍|鏅氫笂|鍌嶆櫄/.test(str))_period='pm';
          else if(/涓婂崍|鏃╀笂|鍑屾櫒/.test(str))_period='am';
          const tm=str.match(/(\d{1,2})[:鐐癸細](\d{1,2})?/);
          if(tm){
            let h=parseInt(tm[1],10);
            let mi=tm[2]?parseInt(tm[2],10):0;
            if(!isNaN(h)&&h>=0&&h<=23){
              if(isNaN(mi)||mi<0||mi>59)mi=0;
              if(_period==='pm'&&h<12)h+=12;
              if(_period==='am'&&h===12)h=0;
              result.time=pad(h)+':'+pad(mi);
            }
          }else if(str.includes('鏃╀笂')||str.includes('涓婂崍')){result.time='09:00';}
          else if(str.includes('涓嬪崍')){result.time='14:00';}
          else if(str.includes('鏅氫笂')){result.time='19:00';}
          if(result.recur&&result.recur.weekday!=null&&!result.date){
            const _now=new Date();
            const _cur=_now.getDay();
            let _diff=result.recur.weekday-_cur;
            if(_diff<0)_diff+=7;
            result.date=ymd(new Date(_now.getFullYear(),_now.getMonth(),_now.getDate()+_diff));
          }
          return (result.date||result.time||result.recur)?result:null;
        }
        
        
        
        let _noteEnsured=false;
        function getNoteDoc(){
          const r=Object.values(state.nodes).find(n=>n&&n._isNoteRoot);
          return r?r.id:null;
        }
        
        function dedupNoteRoots(){
          try{
            const all=Object.values(state.nodes).filter(n=>n&&n._isNoteRoot&&!n._deleted);
            if(all.length<=1)return;
            all.sort((a,b)=>((a.createdAt||0)-(b.createdAt||0))||(a.id<b.id?-1:a.id>b.id?1:0));
            const keep=all[0];
            all.slice(1).forEach(d=>{
              commit(s=>{
                const k=s.nodes[keep.id],dd=s.nodes[d.id];
                if(!k||!dd)return;
                (dd.children||[]).forEach(c=>{
                  if(s.nodes[c]){
                    s.nodes[c].parent=keep.id;
                    if(!k.children.includes(c))k.children.push(c);
                  }
                });
                const p=s.nodes[dd.parent];
                if(p&&Array.isArray(p.children)){const i=p.children.indexOf(d.id);if(i>=0)p.children.splice(i,1);}
                delete s.nodes[d.id];
              },{snapshot:true,noHistory:true}); 
            });
            forceRender();
          }catch(e){}
        }
        function ensureNoteDoc(){
          if(_noteEnsured){const id=getNoteDoc();return id;}
          _noteEnsured=true;
          const id=getNoteDoc();
          if(id)return id;
          const nid=actions.addNode(state.rootId,null,{text:'璁颁簨鏈?,_isNoteRoot:true,_isFolder:true});
          actions.logActivity('add',nid);
          
          dedupNoteRoots();
          return nid;
        }
        function renderNoteRow(n,depth){
          depth=depth||0;
          const box=document.createElement('div');box.className='cal-note-node';box.dataset.id=n.id;
          const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='cal-note-row';row.dataset.depth=depth;
          if(ui.focusId===n.id)row.classList.add('sel');
          const arrow=document.createElement('span');
          arrow.className='arrow'+(n.children.length?'':' leaf');
          if(n.children.length)arrow.textContent=n.collapsed?'鈻?:'鈻?;
          arrow.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();actions.toggleCollapse(n.id);forceRender();};
          row.appendChild(arrow);
          const text=document.createElement('div');
          text.className='text'+(n.done?' done':'');text.contentEditable='true';text.spellcheck=false;
          text.dataset.id=n.id;text.dataset.ph='杈撳叆鍐呭';
          text.innerHTML=sanitizeHtml(n.text||'');
          syncEmptyState(text,'杈撳叆鍐呭');
          bindNoteText(text,n.id);
          row.appendChild(text);
          box.appendChild(row);
          if(n.children.length){
            if(n.collapsed){
              const hint=document.createElement('div');hint.className='collapsed-hint';hint.textContent='鈻?'+n.children.length+' 涓瓙鑺傜偣';
              hint.onclick=()=>{actions.toggleCollapse(n.id);forceRender();};
              box.appendChild(hint);
            }else{
              const kids=document.createElement('div');kids.className='cal-note-children';
              n.children.forEach(cid=>{const cn=N(cid);if(cn)kids.appendChild(renderNoteRow(cn,depth+1));});
              box.appendChild(kids);
            }
          }
          return box;
        }
        function bindNoteText(text,id){
          let composing=false;
          let _lastDoneTapAt=0;
          text.addEventListener('compositionstart',()=>composing=true);
          text.addEventListener('compositionend',()=>{composing=false;syncEmptyState(text,'杈撳叆鍐呭');});
          text.addEventListener('input',e=>{
            if(composing||e.isComposing)return;
            syncEmptyState(text,'杈撳叆鍐呭');
          });
          text.addEventListener('focus',()=>{
            ui.focusId=id;
            $$('.cal-note-row.sel').forEach(r=>r.classList.remove('sel'));
            text.closest('.cal-note-row')?.classList.add('sel');
          });
          text.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
            if(isBlurSuppressed())return;
            if(!text.isConnected)return;
            const finalHtml=sanitizeHtml(text.innerHTML);
            const cur=N(id);
            if(cur&&finalHtml!==(cur.text||''))actions.updateText(id,finalHtml,{noHistory:true}); 
          });
          
          text.addEventListener('paste',e=>{
            try{
              const dt=e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData;
              if(!dt)return;
              e.preventDefault();
              const html=dt.getData('text/html');
              const plain=dt.getData('text/plain');
              if(html){
                const clean=sanitizeHtml(html);
                if(clean){document.execCommand('insertHTML',false,clean);return;}
              }
              if(plain!=null&&plain!=='')document.execCommand('insertText',false,plain);
            }catch(err){}
          });
          text.addEventListener('dblclick',e=>{
            if(e.target!==text)return;
            if(Date.now()-_lastTouchDoneToggleAt<600)return;
            const sel=window.getSelection();
            if(sel&&!sel.isCollapsed)return;
            e.preventDefault();
            actions.toggleDone(id);
            forceRender();
          });
          
          text.addEventListener('click',e=>{
            if(e.target!==text)return;
            if(_lastPointerType!=='touch')return;
            const tsel=window.getSelection();
            if(!tsel||!tsel.isCollapsed)return;
            const now=Date.now();
            if(now-_lastDoneTapAt<300){
              _lastDoneTapAt=0;
              _lastTouchDoneToggleAt=Date.now();
              e.preventDefault();
              actions.toggleDone(id);forceRender();
            }else{
              _lastDoneTapAt=now;
            }
          });
          text.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
            const n=N(id);if(!n)return;
            
            if(e.isComposing||e.keyCode===229)return;
            const sel=window.getSelection();
            if(sel && !sel.isCollapsed && e.key==='Backspace' && !e.altKey) return;
            let atStart=false;
            if(sel&&sel.isCollapsed&&sel.anchorNode&&text.contains(sel.anchorNode)){
              try{
                const r=document.createRange();
                r.selectNodeContents(text);
                r.setEnd(sel.anchorNode,sel.anchorOffset);
                atStart=r.toString().length===0;
                
                if(!atStart&&sel.anchorOffset===0&&(!text.textContent||text.textContent.replace(/\s/g,'')===''))atStart=true;
              }catch(err){atStart=false;}
            }
            if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&e.key.toLowerCase()==='z'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();if(undo()){forceRender();toast('宸叉挙閿€');}return;}
            if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&(e.key.toLowerCase()==='y'||(e.shiftKey&&e.key.toLowerCase()==='z'))){e.preventDefault();if(redo()){forceRender();toast('宸查噸鍋?);}return;}
            if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey&&!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey){
              e.preventDefault();
              const s2=window.getSelection();
              if(!s2||!s2.rangeCount)return;
              const range=s2.getRangeAt(0);
              const afterRange=range.cloneRange();
              afterRange.selectNodeContents(text);
              afterRange.setStart(range.endContainer,range.endOffset);
              const afterFrag=afterRange.extractContents();
              const tmp=document.createElement('div');
              tmp.appendChild(afterFrag);
              const afterHtml=sanitizeHtml(tmp.innerHTML).trim();
              const beforeHtml=sanitizeHtml(text.innerHTML).trim();
              const p=N(n.parent);const idx=p?p.children.indexOf(id):0;
              let newId=null;
              commit(s=>{
                const nn=s.nodes[id];
                if(nn&&beforeHtml!==(nn.text||'')){nn.text=beforeHtml;nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();}
                const added=addNodeTo(s,n.parent,idx+1,{text:afterHtml});
                if(added)newId=added.id;
              },{snapshot:true});
              if(!newId)return;
              actions.logActivity('add',newId);
              forceRender();
              focusAfterRender(newId,'.cal-note');
              return;
            }
            if(e.key==='Tab'){e.preventDefault();if(e.shiftKey)outdentNode(id);else indentNode(id);forceRender();focusAfterRender(id,'.cal-note');return;}
            if(e.key==='Backspace'&&atStart&&!e.altKey){
              e.preventDefault();
              const liveHtml=sanitizeHtml(text.innerHTML);
              const livePlain=stripHtml(liveHtml).trim();
              if(livePlain.length>0){
                
                const prevId=previousVisible(id);
                if(prevId){
                  const prevNode=N(prevId);
                  if(prevNode&&prevNode.parent===state.rootId)return;
                  if(prevNode&&prevNode.children&&prevNode.children.length)return;
                  if(n.children&&n.children.length)return;
                  const caretOffset=stripHtml((prevNode&&prevNode.text)||'').length;
                  commit(s=>{
                    const pn=s.nodes[prevId],cn=s.nodes[id];
                    if(!pn||!cn)return;
                    cn.text=liveHtml;
                    try{
                      const docP=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<div>'+(pn.text||'')+'</div>','text/html');
                      const docC=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<div>'+cn.text+'</div>','text/html');
                      const bodyP=docP.body.firstChild;
                      Array.from(docC.body.firstChild.childNodes).forEach(child=>{bodyP.appendChild(docP.importNode(child,true));});
                      pn.text=bodyP.innerHTML;
                    }catch(e){pn.text=(pn.text||'')+cn.text;}
                    pn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
                    const p=s.nodes[cn.parent];
                    if(p){const i=p.children.indexOf(id);if(i>=0)p.children.splice(i,1);}
                    delete s.nodes[id];
                  },{snapshot:true});
                  actions.logActivity('mod',prevId);
                  forceRender();
                  focusAfterRender(prevId,'.cal-note',{offset:caretOffset});
                }
                return;
              }
              
              const prevId=previousVisible(id);
              actions.removeNode(id);
              forceRender();
              if(prevId)focusAfterRender(prevId,'.cal-note');
              return;
            }
          });
        }
        
        function makeCalDivider(side){
          const d=document.createElement('div');
          d.className='cal-divider'+(side==='stats'?' h':'');
          d.title=side==='stats'?'鎷栧姩璋冩暣楂樺害':'鎷栧姩璋冩暣瀹藉害';
          let _drag=null;
          const onMove=ev=>{
            if(!_drag)return;
            if(!d.isConnected){onUp();return;}          
            const{target,key,mode,startX,startY,base,min,max}=_drag;
            if(mode==='h'){
              
              const dy=ev.clientY-startY;
              const h=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,base-dy));
              ui.calColWidths[key]=Math.round(h);
              if(target)target.style.height=Math.round(h)+'px';
            }else{
              const dx=ev.clientX-startX;
              const w=Math.max(min,Math.min(max,base+dx));
              ui.calColWidths[key]=Math.round(w);
              if(target)target.style.width=Math.round(w)+'px';
            }
            ev.preventDefault();
          };
          const onUp=()=>{
            if(!_drag)return;
            _drag=null;
            d.classList.remove('dragging');
            document.body.classList.remove('sb-resizing');
            document.body.classList.remove('cal-resizing');
            document.removeEventListener('pointermove',onMove);
            document.removeEventListener('pointerup',onUp);
            document.removeEventListener('pointercancel',onUp);
            saveUI();
          };
          d.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
            if(e.button!==0)return;
            e.preventDefault();
            e.stopPropagation();
            if(_drag)return;
            const isH=(side==='stats');
            const target=isH?d.nextElementSibling:d.previousElementSibling;   
            const key=side==='note'?'note':(side==='today'?'today':'statsH');
            const defBase=side==='note'?260:(side==='today'?320:360);
            const min=isH?120:(side==='note'?140:160);
            
            
            const max=isH?Math.max(120,Math.min(900,Math.round((d.parentElement?d.parentElement.clientHeight:0)-96))):Math.max(min,Math.min(Math.round((d.parentElement?d.parentElement.clientWidth:0)-340),Math.round((d.parentElement?d.parentElement.clientWidth:0)*0.45))); 
            const cur=(ui.calColWidths&&ui.calColWidths[key])||0;
            const curSize=cur||(target?(isH?target.offsetHeight:target.offsetWidth):defBase);
            _drag={target,key,mode:isH?'h':'v',startX:e.clientX,startY:e.clientY,base:curSize,min,max};
            d.classList.add('dragging');
            if(isH)document.body.classList.add('cal-resizing');
            else document.body.classList.add('sb-resizing');
            try{d.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(err){}
            document.addEventListener('pointermove',onMove);
            document.addEventListener('pointerup',onUp);
            document.addEventListener('pointercancel',onUp);
          });
          return d;
        }
        
        function buildCalLogBlock(){
          const panel2=document.createElement('div');
          panel2.className='cal-panel'+(ui.collapsedPanels.added?' collapsed':'');
          const p2head=document.createElement('div');p2head.className='cal-panel-head';
          const act=(state.activity&&state.activity[ui.selectedDate])||{add:[],del:[],mod:[]};
          
          const validFilter=function(list){return (list||[]).filter(function(it){
            if(!it||!it.id)return false;
            const n=N(it.id);
            if(n&&isBlankNode(n))return false;
            if(!n&&(!it.text||!String(it.text).trim()))return false;
            return true;
          });};
          const latestByNode=function(list){
            const m=new Map();
            (list||[]).forEach(function(it){const prev=m.get(it.id);if(!prev||(it.t||0)>(prev.t||0))m.set(it.id,it);});
            return Array.from(m.values());
          };
          const vAdd=validFilter(act.add),vMod=validFilter(act.mod),vDel=validFilter(act.del);
          const delList=latestByNode(vDel);
          const delIds=new Set(delList.map(function(it){return it.id;}));
          const modList=latestByNode(vMod.filter(function(it){return !delIds.has(it.id);}));
          const modIds=new Set(modList.map(function(it){return it.id;}));
          const addList=latestByNode(vAdd.filter(function(it){return !delIds.has(it.id)&&!modIds.has(it.id);}));
          const total=delList.length+modList.length+addList.length;
          p2head.innerHTML=`<span class="chev">鈻?/span>绯荤粺鏃ュ織 <span class="badge">${total}</span>`;
          p2head.onclick=()=>{ui.collapsedPanels.added=!ui.collapsedPanels.added;saveUI();forceRender();};
          panel2.appendChild(p2head);
          const p2body=document.createElement('div');p2body.className='cal-panel-body';
          const mkLogGroup=(label,list,strike,key)=>{
            
            const arr=validFilter(list);
            const group=document.createElement('div');group.className='cal-node cal-log-group';
            const head=document.createElement('div');head.className='cal-row';head.style.cursor=arr.length?'pointer':'default';
            const chev=document.createElement('span');chev.className='arrow'+(arr.length?'':' leaf');
            const collapsed=!!ui.logCollapsed[key];chev.textContent=arr.length?(collapsed?'鈻?:'鈻?):'';
            head.appendChild(chev);
            const t=document.createElement('div');t.className='text';t.style.fontWeight='700';
            t.textContent=label+' ';
            
            const gb=document.createElement('span');gb.className='badge';gb.textContent=arr.length;
            t.appendChild(gb);
            head.appendChild(t);
            const bodyWrap=document.createElement('div');bodyWrap.className='cal-children';
            bodyWrap.style.display=(!arr.length||collapsed)?'none':'';
            arr.slice(-50).reverse().forEach(it=>{
              const r=document.createElement('div');r.className='cal-row cal-log-row';
              const b=document.createElement('span');b.className='bullet';
              const txt=document.createElement('div');txt.className='text';
              txt.style.cursor='default';txt.style.opacity='.75';
              if(strike)txt.style.textDecoration='line-through';
              
              const nn=it.id?N(it.id):null;
              txt.textContent=(nn?stripHtml(nn.text):it.text)||'(绌?';
              r.appendChild(b);r.appendChild(txt);
              bodyWrap.appendChild(r);
            });
            
            if(arr.length>50){const tip=document.createElement('div');tip.className='cal-log-row';tip.style.cssText='opacity:.6;font-size:.85em;padding:2px 0';tip.textContent='浠呮樉绀烘渶杩?50 鏉★紙鍏?'+arr.length+' 鏉★級';bodyWrap.appendChild(tip);}
            if(arr.length)head.onclick=()=>{
              const nowCollapsed=bodyWrap.style.display!=='none';
              bodyWrap.style.display=nowCollapsed?'none':'';
              chev.textContent=nowCollapsed?'鈻?:'鈻?;
              ui.logCollapsed[key]=nowCollapsed;saveUI();
            };
            group.appendChild(head);group.appendChild(bodyWrap);
            p2body.appendChild(group);
          };
          mkLogGroup('鏂板',addList,false,'add');
          mkLogGroup('淇敼',modList,false,'mod');
          mkLogGroup('鍒犻櫎',delList,true,'del');
          if(!total){const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='cal-no-activity';e.textContent='浠婃棩鏆傛棤绯荤粺鏃ュ織';p2body.appendChild(e);}
          panel2.appendChild(p2body);
          return panel2;
        }
        
        function tgHourH(){return Math.round(40*currentBaseFontPx()/14);} 
        function tgTotalH(){return 24*tgHourH();} 
        let _calTgTimer=null,_calTgAutoKey='',_calTgPrevScroll=null,_calTgLastView='';
        function clearCalTgTimer(){if(_calTgTimer){clearInterval(_calTgTimer);_calTgTimer=null;}}
        function cnNow(){
          const now=new Date();
          return new Date(now.getTime()+now.getTimezoneOffset()*60000+8*3600000); 
        }
        function cnYmd(d){return d.getFullYear()+'-'+pad(d.getMonth()+1)+'-'+pad(d.getDate());}
        function tgTimeY(t){
          const m=/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/.exec(String(t||''));
          if(!m)return null;
          const h=+m[1],mi=+m[2];
          if(h>23||mi>59)return null;
          return (h+mi/60)*tgHourH();
        }
        function tgNowY(){const d=cnNow();return(d.getHours()+d.getMinutes()/60)*tgHourH();}
        function tgDayNodes(ds){
          let list=calendarTodosOnDate(ds).filter(n=>n.showInCal!==false&&(!isBlankNode(n)||ui.focusId===n.id));
          if(ui.calTagFilter)list=list.filter(n=>nodeHasTag(n,ui.calTagFilter));
          return list;
        }
        function buildTimeGrid(days){
          
          const nDays=days.length;
          const cnToday=cnYmd(cnNow());
          const headCols=document.createElement('div');
          headCols.className='cal-tg-cols';
          headCols.style.gridTemplateColumns='repeat('+nDays+',1fr)';
          const bodyCols=document.createElement('div');
          bodyCols.className='cal-tg-cols';
          bodyCols.style.gridTemplateColumns='repeat('+nDays+',1fr)';
          const todayIdx=days.findIndex(d=>ymd(d)===cnToday);
          const openNodeFromMap=(id,ds)=>{
            const ae=document.activeElement;if(ae&&ae.blur)ae.blur();
            ui.selectedDate=ds;ui.focusId=id;saveUI();forceRender();
            setTimeout(()=>{const t=document.querySelector('.cal-node .text[data-id="'+CSS.escape(id)+'"]');if(t)t.focus();},30);
          };
          days.forEach((d,di)=>{
            const ds=ymd(d);
            const isToday=ds===cnToday;
            const head=document.createElement('div');
            head.className='cal-tg-colhead'+(isToday?' today':'');
            const dow=document.createElement('span');dow.className='dow';dow.textContent='鍛?+'鏃ヤ竴浜屼笁鍥涗簲鍏?[d.getDay()];
            const dnum=document.createElement('span');dnum.className='dnum';dnum.textContent=(d.getMonth()+1)+'/'+d.getDate();
            head.appendChild(dow);
            
            const drow=document.createElement('span');drow.className='drow';
            drow.appendChild(dnum);
            const lab=dayLabel(d);
            if(lab.text){const lu=document.createElement('span');lu.className='dlunar';lu.textContent=lab.text;drow.appendChild(lu);}
            head.appendChild(drow);
            const list=tgDayNodes(ds);
            const allDay=list.filter(n=>!tgTimeY(n.time));
            allDay.slice(0,3).forEach(n=>{
              const c=document.createElement('span');c.className='cal-tg-adj'+(n.done?' done':'');
              c.textContent=tagStripText(n);c.title='#'+(n.tags||[]).join(' #')+' 路 '+stripHtml(n.text);
              c.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();openNodeFromMap(n.id,ds);};
              head.appendChild(c);
            });
            if(allDay.length>3){const m=document.createElement('span');m.className='cal-tg-more';m.textContent='+'+(allDay.length-3);m.title='杩樻湁 '+(allDay.length-3)+' 鏉″叏澶╀簨椤?;m.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();ui.selectedDate=ds;saveUI();forceRender();};head.appendChild(m);}
            headCols.appendChild(head);
            const col=document.createElement('div');
            col.className='cal-tg-col'+(isToday?' today':'');
            col.dataset.ds=ds;
            col.onclick=(e)=>{
              const ae=document.activeElement;if(ae&&ae.blur)ae.blur();
              
              const sc=col.closest('.cal-tg-scroll');
              const rect=col.getBoundingClientRect();
              
              const yPx=e.clientY-rect.top;
              let hh=Math.floor(yPx/tgHourH()),mi=Math.round((yPx%tgHourH())/tgHourH()*60);
              if(mi>=60){mi-=60;hh++;}
              if(hh<0||hh>23)return;
              
              const m0=snapHalfMins(hh*60+mi);
              hh=Math.floor(m0/60);mi=m0%60;
              const t=pad(hh)+':'+pad(mi);
              const endMin=m0+60; 
              const te=(endMin<=23*60+59)?pad(Math.floor(endMin/60))+':'+pad(endMin%60):null;
              ui.selectedDate=ds;saveUI();
              const calRootId=getCalendarRootId();
              const id=actions.addNode(calRootId,null,{date:ds,time:t,timeEnd:te,showInCal:true,text:''});
              ui.focusId=id;forceRender();
              focusAfterRender(id,'.cal-page');
            };
            list.filter(n=>tgTimeY(n.time)).forEach(n=>{
              const ev=document.createElement('div');
              ev.className='cal-tg-ev'+(n.done?' done':'');
              const y0=tgTimeY(n.time);
              ev.style.top=y0+'px';
              const y1=tgTimeY(n.timeEnd);
              if(y1!=null&&y1>y0)ev.style.height=(y1-y0)+'px'; 
              const _ts=fmtHm(n.time),_te=fmtHm(n.timeEnd);
              ev.title=((_ts&&_te&&_te>_ts)?_ts+'-'+_te+' ':(_ts?_ts+' ':'')+'#'+(n.tags||[]).join(' #')+' 路 '+stripHtml(n.text));
              
              const txt=document.createElement('span');txt.className='evtx';txt.textContent=tagStripText(n);
              txt.contentEditable='true';txt.spellcheck=false;
              ev.appendChild(txt);
              
              if(n.tags&&n.tags.length){
                ev.classList.add('tagged');
                ev.style.setProperty('--evtag',getTagColor(n.tags[0]));
              }
              const _display=tagStripText(n);
              let _editing=false;
              txt.addEventListener('focus',()=>{_editing=true;});
              txt.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
                if(!_editing)return;_editing=false;
                const v=(txt.textContent||'').replace(/\n/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
                if(v===_display)return; 
                
                let suffix='';
                if(n.tags&&n.tags.length)suffix=' '+n.tags.filter(Boolean).map(t=>'#'+t).join(' ');
                actions.updateText(n.id,v+suffix,{noHistory:true});
                invalidateCalIndex();forceRender();
              });
              txt.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
                if(e.isComposing||e.keyCode===229)return; 
                if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();txt.blur();}
                else if(e.key==='Escape'){txt.textContent=_display;txt.blur();}
              });
              ev.onclick=e=>{
                if(ev._calDragged){ev._calDragged=false;return;} 
                e.stopPropagation();
                if(document.activeElement!==txt){ 
                  txt.focus();
                  const sel=window.getSelection();if(sel){const r=document.createRange();r.selectNodeContents(txt);sel.removeAllRanges();sel.addRange(r);}
                } 
              };
              
              ev.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{
                e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
                const cur=state.nodes[n.id];
                const items=[
                  
                  {ic:'鈴?,txt:'璁剧疆鏃堕棿',fn:()=>openCalTimeModal(n.id)},
                  {sep:true},
                  {ic:'馃搮',txt:'娓呴櫎鏃ユ湡',fn:()=>{actions.clearNodeSchedule(n.id,{date:true,time:false,recur:false});forceRender();toast('宸叉竻闄ゆ棩鏈?);}},
                  {ic:'鈴?,txt:'娓呴櫎鏃堕棿',fn:()=>{actions.clearNodeSchedule(n.id,{date:false,time:true,recur:false});forceRender();toast('宸叉竻闄ゆ椂闂?);}},
                  {ic:'馃棏',txt:'娓呴櫎鍏ㄩ儴鏃ョ▼',danger:true,fn:()=>{actions.clearNodeSchedule(n.id,{date:true,time:true,recur:true});forceRender();toast('宸叉竻闄ゅ叏閮ㄦ棩绋?);}}
                ];
                showContextMenu(items,e);
              });
              bindCalEventDrag(ev,n); 
              col.appendChild(ev);
            });
            bodyCols.appendChild(col);
          });
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='cal-tg';
          const scroll=document.createElement('div');scroll.className='cal-tg-scroll';
          scroll.setAttribute('role','region');scroll.setAttribute('aria-label','鏃ュ巻鏃堕棿缃戞牸锛堜笢鍏尯锛?);
          const head=document.createElement('div');head.className='cal-tg-head';
          const corner=document.createElement('div');corner.className='cal-tg-corner';
          head.appendChild(corner);head.appendChild(headCols);
          const body=document.createElement('div');body.className='cal-tg-body';
          const gutter=document.createElement('div');gutter.className='cal-tg-gutter';
          for(let h=0;h<=24;h++){ 
            const hl=document.createElement('div');hl.className='cal-tg-hour';
            hl.textContent=pad(h)+':00';hl.style.top=(h*tgHourH())+'px';
            gutter.appendChild(hl);
          }
          body.appendChild(gutter);body.appendChild(bodyCols);
          scroll.appendChild(head);scroll.appendChild(body);
          const sr=document.createElement('div');sr.className='cal-tg-sr';
          sr.setAttribute('aria-live','polite');sr.textContent='褰撳墠鏃堕棿 '+pad(cnNow().getHours())+':'+pad(cnNow().getMinutes());
          scroll.appendChild(sr);
          if(todayIdx>=0){
            const y=tgNowY();
            const nowline=document.createElement('div');nowline.className='cal-tg-nowline';
            nowline.style.left=(todayIdx/nDays*100)+'%';nowline.style.right=((nDays-1-todayIdx)/nDays*100)+'%';
            nowline.style.transform='translateY('+y+'px)';
            nowline.setAttribute('role','img');nowline.setAttribute('aria-label','褰撳墠鏃堕棿 '+pad(cnNow().getHours())+':'+pad(cnNow().getMinutes()));
            const dot=document.createElement('div');dot.className='cal-tg-nowdot';dot.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
            nowline.appendChild(dot);
            const label=document.createElement('div');label.className='cal-tg-nowlabel';label.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); 
            label.textContent=pad(cnNow().getHours())+':'+pad(cnNow().getMinutes());
            nowline.appendChild(label);
            bodyCols.appendChild(nowline);
            wrap._tgScroll=scroll;wrap._tgLine=nowline;wrap._tgLabel=label;
            wrap._tgTodayIdx=todayIdx;wrap._tgDays=days.map(ymd);
            
            const key=ui.calView+':'+ymd(days[0]);
            const enteringWeek=(_calTgLastView!=='week');
            _calTgLastView='week';
            if(_calTgAutoKey!==key||enteringWeek){
              _calTgAutoKey=key;
              _calTgPrevScroll=null; 
              requestAnimationFrame(()=>{const hh=scroll.clientHeight;scroll.scrollTop=Math.max(0,y-hh*0.33);});
            }else if(_calTgPrevScroll!=null){
              
              const ps=_calTgPrevScroll;
              requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
                if(!scroll.isConnected)return;
                scroll.scrollTop=Math.max(0,Math.min(ps,scroll.scrollHeight-scroll.clientHeight));
                _calTgPrevScroll=null; 
              });
            }
            startCalTgTimer(wrap);
          }
          wrap.appendChild(scroll);
          return wrap;
        }
        function startCalTgTimer(wrap){
          clearCalTgTimer();
          const scroll=wrap._tgScroll,line=wrap._tgLine,label=wrap._tgLabel,days=wrap._tgDays;
          if(!scroll||!line)return;
          let lastDate=cnYmd(cnNow()),lastUserScroll=0;
          const onUserScroll=()=>{lastUserScroll=Date.now();};
          scroll.addEventListener('wheel',onUserScroll,{passive:true});
          scroll.addEventListener('touchstart',onUserScroll,{passive:true});
          scroll.addEventListener('pointerdown',onUserScroll);
          _calTgTimer=setInterval(()=>{
            if(!line.isConnected){clearCalTgTimer();return;} 
            const now=cnNow(),ds=cnYmd(now);
            const y=(now.getHours()+now.getMinutes()/60)*tgHourH();
            const t=pad(now.getHours())+':'+pad(now.getMinutes());
            line.style.transform='translateY('+y+'px)'; 
            line.setAttribute('aria-label','褰撳墠鏃堕棿 '+t);
            if(label.isConnected)label.textContent=t;
            const sr=document.querySelector('.cal-tg-sr');if(sr)sr.textContent='褰撳墠鏃堕棿 '+t;
            if(ds!==lastDate){ 
              lastDate=ds;
              ui.selectedDate=ds;ui.calMonth={y:now.getFullYear(),m:now.getMonth()};saveUI();
              _calTgAutoKey=''; 
              forceRender();
              return;
            }
            
            if(Date.now()-lastUserScroll>45000){
              const r=scroll.getBoundingClientRect(),ly=line.getBoundingClientRect().top;
              if(ly<r.top+10||ly>r.bottom-10){
                scroll.scrollTo({top:Math.max(0,scroll.scrollTop+(y-r.height*0.33)),behavior:'smooth'});
              }
            }
          },60000);
        }
        if(typeof window!=='undefined'){
          window.addEventListener('pagehide',clearCalTgTimer);
          window.addEventListener('beforeunload',clearCalTgTimer);
        }
        function renderCalendarPage(){
          
          if(!_calIndexCache){
            const _pageSk=document.createElement('div');_pageSk.className='cal-page';
            _pageSk.appendChild(l7Skeleton('calendar',{mode:'rows',count:10,hint:'姝ｅ湪鏋勫缓鏃ュ巻绱㈠紩鈥?,label:'鏃ュ巻绱㈠紩'}));
            l7CalIndexWhenReady(function(){if(_l7IsCalendarView())forceRender();});
            return _pageSk;
          }
          clearCalTgTimer(); 
          document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--tg-hour-h',tgHourH()+'px'); 
          const page=document.createElement('div');page.className='cal-page';
          const now=new Date();
          if(!ui.calMonth)ui.calMonth={y:now.getFullYear(),m:now.getMonth()};
          if(!ui.calView)ui.calView='month';
          if(ui.calView==='day'){ui.calView='week';saveUI();} 
          const calView=ui.calView;
          
          if(calView!=='week')_calTgLastView=calView;
          const{y,m}=ui.calMonth;
          const top=document.createElement('div');top.className='cal-top';
          const header=document.createElement('div');header.className='cal-header';
          const title=document.createElement('div');title.className='cal-date-title';
          if(calView==='year')title.textContent=y+' 骞?;
          else if(calView==='week'){
            const selD=parseYmd(ui.selectedDate||todayStr());
            const dow=selD.getDay();
            const sun=new Date(selD.getFullYear(),selD.getMonth(),selD.getDate()-dow);
            const sat=new Date(sun.getFullYear(),sun.getMonth(),sun.getDate()+6);
            title.textContent=(sun.getMonth()+1)+'/'+sun.getDate()+' - '+(sat.getMonth()+1)+'/'+sat.getDate();
          }else title.textContent=y+' 骞?'+(m+1)+' 鏈?;
          header.appendChild(title);
          const actions2=document.createElement('div');actions2.className='cal-actions';
          const mkBtn=(txt,fn,cls)=>{const b=document.createElement('button');b.className='cal-tool'+(cls?' '+cls:'');b.textContent=txt;b.onclick=fn;return b;};
          const viewSwitch=document.createElement('div');viewSwitch.className='cal-view-switch';
          ['year','month','week'].forEach(v=>{
            const b=document.createElement('button');
            b.className='cal-view-btn'+(calView===v?' active':'');
            b.textContent={year:'骞?,month:'鏈?,week:'鍛?}[v];
            b.onclick=()=>{ui.calView=v;saveUI();forceRender();};
            viewSwitch.appendChild(b);
          });
          actions2.appendChild(viewSwitch);
          const tagSel=document.createElement('select');
          tagSel.className='cal-tool cal-tag-filter';
          tagSel.title='鎸夋爣绛剧瓫閫夊緟鍔?;
          const allOpt=document.createElement('option');allOpt.value='';allOpt.textContent='鏍囩锛氬叏閮?;
          tagSel.appendChild(allOpt);
          const ctagMap=calendarTagIndex();
          Array.from(ctagMap.entries()).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).forEach(([t,cnt])=>{
            const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t;o.textContent='鈼?#'+t+' ('+cnt+')';
            tagSel.appendChild(o);
          });
          if(ui.calTagFilter&&ctagMap.has(ui.calTagFilter)){tagSel.value=ui.calTagFilter;tagSel.classList.add('active');}
          else if(ui.calTagFilter){ui.calTagFilter='';saveUI();}
          tagSel.onchange=()=>{ui.calTagFilter=tagSel.value;saveUI();forceRender();};
          if(calView==='month'){

        function fmtHm(s){
          const m=/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})$/.exec(String(s||''));
          if(!m)return null;
          if(+m[1]>23||+m[2]>59)return null; 
          
          return pad(+m[1])+':'+pad(+m[2]);
        }
        
        function parseCalTimeInput(raw){
          const s=String(raw||'').replace(/\s/g,'');
          const m=/^(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2})(?:\s*-\s*(\d{1,2}):(\d{1,2}))?$/.exec(s);
          if(!m)return null;
          const h0=+m[1],mi0=+m[2];
          if(h0>23||mi0>59)return null;
          const t0=pad(h0)+':'+pad(mi0);
          if(m[3]==null)return{t0:t0,t1:null};
          const h1=+m[3],mi1=+m[4];
          if(h1>23||mi1>59)return null;
          const t1=pad(h1)+':'+pad(mi1);
          if(!(t1>t0))return null; 
          return{t0:t0,t1:t1};
        }
        
        function snapHalfMins(mins){
          let m=Math.round(mins/30)*30;
          if(m>=1440)m=1439;
          if(m<0)m=0;
          return m;
        }
        function calMinToHm(m){
          const mm=clampM(Math.round(m),0,1439);
          return pad(Math.floor(mm/60))+':'+pad(mm%60);
        }
        function clampM(v,lo,hi){return Math.max(lo,Math.min(hi,v));}
        function bindCalEventDrag(ev,n){
          ev.style.touchAction='none';
          let mode=null,startY=0,origTop=0,origH=0,dragged=false;
          ev.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
            if(e.button!==0)return;
            
            if(e.target.closest('.evtx')){
              startY=e.clientY;
              dragged=false;
              const move=ev2=>{
                const dy=ev2.clientY-startY;
                if(!dragged&&Math.abs(dy)>8)dragged=true;
                ev.classList.add('cal-tg-dragging');
              };
              const up=()=>{
                ev.classList.remove('cal-tg-dragging');
                ev.removeEventListener('pointermove',move);
                ev.removeEventListener('pointerup',up);
                if(!dragged)return;
                ev._calDragged=true;
                setTimeout(()=>{ev._calDragged=false;},100); 
                const top=clampM(parseFloat(ev.style.top)||0,0,tgTotalH()-15); 
                const h=clampM(parseFloat(ev.style.height)||tgHourH(),15,tgTotalH()-top);
                const snap30=m=>Math.round(m/30)*30;
                const m0=clampM(snap30(top/tgHourH()*60),0,1439);
                const m1=clampM(snap30((top+h)/tgHourH()*60),m0+30,1439);
                const t0=calMinToHm(m0);
                const t1=calMinToHm(m1);
                commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[n.id];if(!nn)return;nn.time=t0;nn.timeEnd=(t1>t0)?t1:null;nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true,dirtyDocs:getCalendarRootId()});
                invalidateCalIndex();
                forceRender();
              };
              ev.addEventListener('pointermove',move);
              ev.addEventListener('pointerup',up);
              return;
            }
            const r=ev.getBoundingClientRect();
            mode=(e.clientY-r.bottom)>-6?'end':'move'; 
            startY=e.clientY;
            origTop=parseFloat(ev.style.top)||0;
            origH=parseFloat(ev.style.height)||tgHourH();
            dragged=false;
            e.preventDefault();
            const move=ev2=>{
              const dy=ev2.clientY-startY;
              if(!dragged&&Math.abs(dy)>4)dragged=true;
              if(mode==='move'){
                ev.style.top=clampM(origTop+dy,0,tgTotalH()-origH)+'px'; 
              }else{
                ev.style.height=clampM(origH+dy,15,tgTotalH()-origTop)+'px';
              }
              ev.classList.add('cal-tg-dragging');
            };
            const up=()=>{
              ev.classList.remove('cal-tg-dragging');
              ev.removeEventListener('pointermove',move);
              ev.removeEventListener('pointerup',up);
              if(!dragged)return; 
              ev._calDragged=true;
              setTimeout(()=>{ev._calDragged=false;},100); 
              const top=clampM(parseFloat(ev.style.top)||0,0,tgTotalH()-15); 
              const h=clampM(parseFloat(ev.style.height)||tgHourH(),15,tgTotalH()-top);
              
              const snap30=m=>Math.round(m/30)*30;
              const m0=clampM(snap30(top/tgHourH()*60),0,1439);
              const m1=clampM(snap30((top+h)/tgHourH()*60),m0+30,1439);
              const t0=calMinToHm(m0);
              const t1=calMinToHm(m1);
              commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[n.id];if(!nn)return;nn.time=t0;nn.timeEnd=(t1>t0)?t1:null;nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true,dirtyDocs:getCalendarRootId()});
              invalidateCalIndex();
              forceRender();
            };
            ev.addEventListener('pointermove',move);
            ev.addEventListener('pointerup',up);
          });
        }
        function fmtCalTime(n){
          const t=fmtHm(n&&n.time);
          const t2=fmtHm(n&&n.timeEnd);
          if(t&&t2&&t2>t)return t+'-'+t2;
          return t||'';
        }
        function renderCalNode(n){
          const box=document.createElement('div');
          box.className='cal-node';
          box.dataset.id=n.id;
          
          const cb=document.createElement('input');
          cb.type='checkbox';
          cb.className='cal-checkbox';
          cb.checked=!!n.done;
          box.appendChild(cb);
          if(n.tags&&n.tags.length){
            const td=document.createElement('span');td.className='cal-tag-dot';td.style.background=getTagColor(n.tags[0]);
            td.title='#'+n.tags.join(' #');
            box.appendChild(td);
          }
          
          if(n.time){
            const t=document.createElement('span');
            t.className='cal-node-time';
            const _ts=fmtHm(n.time),_te=fmtHm(n.timeEnd);
            t.textContent=(_ts&&_te&&_te>_ts)?_ts+'-'+_te:(_ts||'');
            t.title='鐐瑰嚮璁剧疆鏃堕棿锛堜笌鏃ュ巻浜嬩欢鍧楀悓姝ワ級';
            t.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();openCalTimeModal(n.id);};
            box.appendChild(t);
          }
          const text=document.createElement('div');
          text.className='text'+(n.done?' done':'');
          text.contentEditable='true';
          text.spellcheck=false;
          text.dataset.id=n.id;
          text.dataset.ph='杈撳叆寰呭姙鈥?;
          text.innerHTML=sanitizeHtml(n.text||''); 
          syncEmptyState(text,'杈撳叆寰呭姙鈥?);
          cb.addEventListener('pointerdown',()=>{
            if(document.activeElement===text)text.blur();
          });
          cb.addEventListener('change',()=>{
            const cur=N(n.id);
            if(!cur)return;
            if(!!cur.done!==cb.checked){actions.toggleDone(n.id);}
          });
          let composing=false;
          text.addEventListener('compositionstart',()=>composing=true);
          text.addEventListener('compositionend',()=>{composing=false;syncEmptyState(text,'杈撳叆寰呭姙鈥?);});
          text.addEventListener('input',e=>{
            if(composing||e.isComposing)return;
            syncEmptyState(text,'杈撳叆寰呭姙鈥?);
          });
          text.addEventListener('focus',()=>{savedFocus=null;ui.focusId=n.id;});
          
          text.addEventListener('click',e=>{
            if(!(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey))return;
            const lt=linkTitleAtPoint(text,e);
            if(!lt)return;
            e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
            navigateToWikiLink(lt,text);
          });
          text.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
            if(isBlurSuppressed())return;
            if(!text.isConnected)return;
            
            const finalHtml=sanitizeHtml(text.innerHTML);
            const cur=N(n.id);
            if(!cur)return;
            const parsed=parseNaturalDate(text.textContent);
            let newDate=cur.date,newTime=cur.time,newRecur=cur.recur;
            if(parsed){
              if(parsed.date)  newDate  = parsed.date;
              if(parsed.time)  newTime  = parsed.time;
              if(parsed.recur) newRecur = parsed.recur;
            }
            const dateChanged=newDate!==cur.date||newTime!==cur.time||newRecur!==cur.recur;
            
            if(finalHtml!==(cur.text||''))actions.updateText(n.id,finalHtml,{noHistory:true}); 
            if(dateChanged){
              commit(s=>{
                const nn=s.nodes[n.id];
                if(!nn)return;
                nn.date=newDate;
                nn.time=newTime;
                nn.recur=newRecur;
                nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
              },{snapshot:true,dirtyDocs:getCalendarRootId()});

        const _dbCellTimers=new Map();
        function _dbCellSchedule(key,fn,ms){
          const prev=_dbCellTimers.get(key);
          if(prev)clearTimeout(prev);
          _dbCellTimers.set(key,setTimeout(()=>{
            _dbCellTimers.delete(key);
            try{fn();}catch(e){console.error(e);}
          },ms));
        }
        function _dbCellCancel(key){
          const t=_dbCellTimers.get(key);
          if(t){clearTimeout(t);_dbCellTimers.delete(key);}
        }
        const DB_VIEWS=[
          {k:'table',n:'琛ㄦ牸'},{k:'board',n:'鐪嬫澘'},{k:'gallery',n:'鐢诲粖'},

        function getDbRows(docId){
          
          const doc=docId?N(docId):null;
          if(!doc)return[];
          return doc.children.map(id=>N(id)).filter(n=>n&&!isFolder(n.id)&&!n._deleted);
        }
        
        const DB_SF_OPS={
          text:  [['contains','鍖呭惈'],['not_contains','涓嶅寘鍚?],['is','绛変簬'],['is_not','涓嶇瓑浜?],['empty','涓虹┖'],['not_empty','涓嶄负绌?]],
          select:[['is','绛変簬'],['is_not','涓嶇瓑浜?],['empty','涓虹┖'],['not_empty','涓嶄负绌?]],
          number:[['is','绛変簬'],['is_not','涓嶇瓑浜?],['gt','澶т簬'],['gte','澶т簬绛変簬'],['lt','灏忎簬'],['lte','灏忎簬绛変簬'],['empty','涓虹┖'],['not_empty','涓嶄负绌?]],

          link:  [['contains','鍖呭惈'],['not_contains','涓嶅寘鍚?],['is','绛変簬'],['is_not','涓嶇瓑浜?],['empty','涓虹┖'],['not_empty','涓嶄负绌?]],
          multi_select:[['contains','鍖呭惈'],['not_contains','涓嶅寘鍚?],['is','绛変簬'],['is_not','涓嶇瓑浜?],['empty','涓虹┖'],['not_empty','涓嶄负绌?]]
        };
        function dbFieldName(f){
          return typeof f==='string'?f:(f&&f.name)||'';
        }
        
        function dbFieldVisible(name){
          return !(ui.dbHiddenFields&&ui.dbHiddenFields[name]);
        }
        
        function dbResolveField(name,types){
          const fields=(state&&state.dbFields)||[];
          if(name){
            const hit=fields.find(f=>dbFieldName(f)===name);
            if(hit)return hit;
          }
          if(types){
            const t=Array.isArray(types)?types:[types];
            for(const f of fields){
              const def=dbFieldDef(f);
              if(t.includes(def.type))return f;
            }
          }
          return null;
        }
        
        function dbPrimaryField(){
          const a=state&&state.dbFields;
          return (a&&a.length)?dbFieldName(a[0]):'鏍囬';
        }
        function dbFieldDef(f,fields){
          
          if(typeof f==='string'){
            const arr=fields!==undefined?fields:(state&&state.dbFields);
            const hit=Array.isArray(arr)?arr.find(x=>x&&typeof x==='object'&&x.name===f):null;
            if(hit)return hit;
            return {name:f,type:dbFieldType(f)};
          }
          return (f||{name:'',type:'text'});
        }
        function dbFieldType(f){
          if(f&&typeof f==='object'&&f.type)return f.type;
          if(f==='浼樺厛绾?||f==='鐘舵€?)return 'select';
          if(f==='鏄熺骇')return 'number';
          if(f==='鏃堕棿')return 'date';
          return 'text';
        }
        
        function dbRawValue(n,f){
          const def=dbFieldDef(f);
          if(def.type==='formula'){
            try{return dbFormulaEval(n,def);}catch(e){return null;}
          }
          if(f===null||f===undefined)return stripHtml(n.text||'');
          if(def.name===dbPrimaryField())return stripHtml(n.text||'');
          return n.db&&n.db[def.name];
        }
        function dbTextValue(n,f){
          const def=dbFieldDef(f);
          const v=dbRawValue(n,f);
          if(def.type==='link'){
            const ids=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];
            if(!ids.length)return '';
            return ids.map(id=>{const m=N(id);return m?stripHtml(m.text||''):'';}).filter(Boolean).join('銆?);
          }
          if(def.type==='multi_select'){
            const arr=Array.isArray(v)?v:[];
            return arr.filter(x=>x!==''&&x!=null).join('銆?);
          }
          if(v==null||v==='')return '';
          if(typeof v==='object')return formatTimeValue(v);
          return String(v);
        }
        function dbNumValue(n,f){
          const v=dbRawValue(n,f);
          if(v==null||v==='')return null;
          const num=Number(v);
          return isNaN(num)?null:num;
        }
        
        let _formulaDepth=0;
        function dbDateStr(v){
          if(v==null||v==='')return '';
          if(typeof v==='number'){
            if(v>1e12)return ymd(new Date(v));
            if(v>1e9)return ymd(new Date(v*1000));
            return '';
          }
          if(typeof v==='object'){
            const s=v.start||v.end||'';
            return String(s).split(' ')[0]||'';
          }
          return String(v).split(' ')[0]||'';
        }
        function dbFormulaToNum(v){
          if(v==null||v==='')return NaN;
          if(typeof v==='number')return v;
          if(typeof v==='boolean')return v?1:0;
          if(typeof v==='object'){const ds=dbDateStr(v);return ds?Date.parse(ds):NaN;}
          const s=String(v).trim().replace(/,/g,'');
          if(!s)return NaN;
          if(/^-?\d+(\.\d+)?$/.test(s))return parseFloat(s);
          return NaN;
        }
        function dbFormulaToStr(v){
          if(v==null)return '';
          if(typeof v==='object')return formatTimeValue(v);
          return String(v);
        }
        function dbFormulaTruthy(v){
          if(v==null)return false;
          if(typeof v==='boolean')return v;
          if(typeof v==='number')return v!==0&&!isNaN(v);
          const s=String(v).trim();
          if(s==='')return false;
          if(s.toLowerCase()==='false')return false;
          return true;
        }
        function _dbFormulaDiff(a,b,unit){
          const d1=Date.parse(dbDateStr(a)),d2=Date.parse(dbDateStr(b));
          if(isNaN(d1)||isNaN(d2))return null;
          const A=new Date(d1),B=new Date(d2);
          const y1=A.getFullYear(),m1=A.getMonth(),dA=A.getDate();
          const y2=B.getFullYear(),m2=B.getMonth(),dB=B.getDate();
          const u=String(unit||'D').toUpperCase();
          if(u==='Y')return y2-y1;
          if(u==='M')return (y2-y1)*12+(m2-m1);
          if(u==='D')return Math.round((d2-d1)/86400000);
          if(u==='YM'){let r=((y2-y1)*12+(m2-m1))%12;if(r<0)r+=12;return r;}
          if(u==='YD'){let last=new Date(y2,m2,dA);if(last>B)last=new Date(y2-1,m2,dA);return Math.round((B-last)/86400000);}
          if(u==='MD'){let r=dB-dA;if(r<0){const pm=new Date(y2,m2,0).getDate();r=pm-dA+dB;}return r;}
          return Math.round((d2-d1)/86400000);
        }
        function dbFormulaAgg(arg,mode,docId){
          if(typeof arg==='string'){
            const rows=getDbRows(docId); 
            const vals=[];
            rows.forEach(r=>{
              const v=dbRawValue(r,arg);
              if(mode==='count'){vals.push(dbFormulaTruthy(v)?1:0);}
              else{const n=dbFormulaToNum(v);if(!isNaN(n))vals.push(n);}
            });
            if(mode==='count')return vals.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);
            if(!vals.length)return null;
            const sum=vals.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);
            return mode==='avg'?sum/vals.length:sum;
          }
          const arr=Array.isArray(arg)?arg:[arg];
          if(mode==='count')return arr.filter(x=>dbFormulaTruthy(x)).length;
          const vals=arr.map(dbFormulaToNum).filter(n=>!isNaN(n));
          if(!vals.length)return null;
          const sum=vals.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);
          return mode==='avg'?sum/vals.length:sum;
        }
        function dbFormulaCall(name,args,docId){ 
          name=String(name||'').toUpperCase();
          const num=a=>dbFormulaToNum(a);
          const numArgs=args.map(num);
          switch(name){
            case 'ABS':return Math.abs(num(args[0]));
            case 'ROUND':{const p=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(args[1])||0));const k=Math.pow(10,p);return Math.round(num(args[0])*k)/k;}
            case 'ROUNDUP':{const p=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(args[1])||0));const k=Math.pow(10,p);return Math.ceil(num(args[0])*k)/k;}
            case 'ROUNDDOWN':{const p=Math.max(0,Math.floor(num(args[1])||0));const k=Math.pow(10,p);return Math.floor(num(args[0])*k)/k;}
            case 'INT':return Math.floor(num(args[0]));
            case 'FLOOR':return Math.floor(num(args[0]));
            case 'CEIL':case 'CEILING':return Math.ceil(num(args[0]));
            case 'SQRT':return Math.sqrt(num(args[0]));
            case 'POWER':case 'POW':return Math.pow(num(args[0]),num(args[1]));
            case 'MOD':return num(args[0])%num(args[1]);
            case 'MAX':return Math.max(...numArgs);
            case 'MIN':return Math.min(...numArgs);
            case 'LEN':return dbFormulaToStr(args[0]).length;
            case 'LEFT':return dbFormulaToStr(args[0]).slice(0,Math.max(0,num(args[1])||0));
            case 'RIGHT':{const s=dbFormulaToStr(args[0]);const n=Math.max(0,num(args[1])||0);return n===0?'':s.slice(-n);}
            case 'UPPER':return dbFormulaToStr(args[0]).toUpperCase();
            case 'LOWER':return dbFormulaToStr(args[0]).toLowerCase();
            case 'TRIM':return dbFormulaToStr(args[0]).trim();
            case 'CONCAT':return args.map(a=>dbFormulaToStr(a)).join('');
            case 'TEXT':return dbFormulaToStr(args[0]);
            case 'VALUE':return dbFormulaToNum(args[0]);
            case 'IF':return dbFormulaTruthy(args[0])?args[1]:args[2];
            case 'AND':return args.every(a=>dbFormulaTruthy(a));
            case 'OR':return args.some(a=>dbFormulaTruthy(a));
            case 'NOT':return !dbFormulaTruthy(args[0]);
            case 'ISBLANK':return args[0]==null||args[0]===''||(Array.isArray(args[0])&&!args[0].length);
            case 'TODAY':return todayStr();
            case 'NOW':{const d=new Date();return ymd(d)+' '+pad(d.getHours())+':'+pad(d.getMinutes())+':'+pad(d.getSeconds());}
            case 'YEAR':{const ds=dbDateStr(args[0]);return ds?new Date(ds).getFullYear():null;}
            case 'MONTH':{const ds=dbDateStr(args[0]);const d=ds?new Date(ds):null;return d?(d.getMonth()+1):null;}
            case 'DAY':{const ds=dbDateStr(args[0]);const d=ds?new Date(ds):null;return d?d.getDate():null;}
            case 'HOUR':{const s=dbFormulaToStr(args[0]);const m=s.match(/(\d{1,2}):/);return m?parseInt(m[1],10):null;}
            case 'MINUTE':{const s=dbFormulaToStr(args[0]);const m=s.match(/:(\d{1,2})/);return m?parseInt(m[1],10):null;}
            case 'WEEKDAY':{const ds=dbDateStr(args[0]);const d=ds?new Date(ds):null;if(!d)return null;const mode=num(args[1]);const w=d.getDay();if(mode===2)return w===0?7:w;if(mode===3)return w===0?6:w-1;return w+1;}
            case 'DATE':{const y=num(args[0]),m=num(args[1]),d=num(args[2]);if(isNaN(y)||isNaN(m)||isNaN(d))return null;return ymd(new Date(y,m-1,d));}
            case 'DATEDIF':return _dbFormulaDiff(args[0],args[1],args[2]);
            case 'SUM':return dbFormulaAgg(args[0],'sum',docId); 
            case 'AVG':return dbFormulaAgg(args[0],'avg',docId);
            case 'COUNT':return dbFormulaAgg(args[0],'count',docId);
            default:return null;
          }
        }
        const DB_AGG_FNS=['SUM','AVG','COUNT'];
        class _FormulaParser{
          constructor(src,fieldValue,docId){
            this.src=src;this.pos=0;this.fieldValue=fieldValue;this.docId=docId; 
          }
          skipWs(){while(this.pos<this.src.length&&/\s/.test(this.src[this.pos]))this.pos++;}
          peek(){return this.src[this.pos]||'';}
          matchWord(w){
            this.skipWs();
            const re=new RegExp('^'+w+'(?![A-Za-z0-9_\\u4e00-\\u9fa5])');
            const m=this.src.slice(this.pos).match(re);
            if(m){this.pos+=m[0].length;return true;}
            return false;
          }
          parse(){this.skipWs();const v=this.orExpr();this.skipWs();return v;}
          orExpr(){
            let v=this.andExpr();
            for(;;){
              this.skipWs();
              if(this.matchWord('OR')){const r=this.andExpr();v=dbFormulaTruthy(v)||dbFormulaTruthy(r);}
              else if(this.peek()==='|'&&this.src[this.pos+1]==='|'){this.pos+=2;const r=this.andExpr();v=dbFormulaTruthy(v)||dbFormulaTruthy(r);}
              else break;
            }
            return v;
          }
          andExpr(){
            let v=this.cmpExpr();
            for(;;){
              this.skipWs();
              if(this.matchWord('AND')){const r=this.cmpExpr();v=dbFormulaTruthy(v)&&dbFormulaTruthy(r);}
              else if(this.peek()==='&'&&this.src[this.pos+1]==='&'){this.pos+=2;const r=this.cmpExpr();v=dbFormulaTruthy(v)&&dbFormulaTruthy(r);}
              else break;
            }
            return v;
          }
          cmpExpr(){
            const l=this.addExpr();
            this.skipWs();
            const p=this.peek();
            let op=null;
            if(p==='='&&this.src[this.pos+1]==='='){op='==';this.pos+=2;}
            else if(p==='='){op='==';this.pos+=1;}
            else if(p==='!'&&this.src[this.pos+1]==='='){op='!=';this.pos+=2;}
            else if(p==='<'&&this.src[this.pos+1]==='>'){op='!=';this.pos+=2;}
            else if(p==='>'&&this.src[this.pos+1]==='='){op='>=';this.pos+=2;}
            else if(p==='<'&&this.src[this.pos+1]==='='){op='<=';this.pos+=2;}
            else if(p==='>'){op='>';this.pos+=1;}
            else if(p==='<'){op='<';this.pos+=1;}
            if(!op)return l;
            const r=this.addExpr();
            const ln=dbFormulaToNum(l),rn=dbFormulaToNum(r);
            const numeric=!isNaN(ln)&&!isNaN(rn);
            switch(op){
              case '==':return numeric?ln===rn:dbFormulaToStr(l)===dbFormulaToStr(r);
              case '!=':return numeric?ln!==rn:dbFormulaToStr(l)!==dbFormulaToStr(r);
              case '>':return numeric?ln>rn:dbFormulaToStr(l)>dbFormulaToStr(r);
              case '>=':return numeric?ln>=rn:dbFormulaToStr(l)>=dbFormulaToStr(r);
              case '<':return numeric?ln<rn:dbFormulaToStr(l)<dbFormulaToStr(r);
              case '<=':return numeric?ln<=rn:dbFormulaToStr(l)<=dbFormulaToStr(r);
            }
            return false;
          }
          addExpr(){
            let v=this.mulExpr();
            for(;;){
              this.skipWs();
              const p=this.peek();
              if(p==='+'){this.pos++;const r=this.mulExpr();v=dbFormulaToNum(v)+dbFormulaToNum(r);}
              else if(p==='-'){this.pos++;const r=this.mulExpr();v=dbFormulaToNum(v)-dbFormulaToNum(r);}
              else if(p==='&'&&this.src[this.pos+1]!=='&'){this.pos++;const r=this.mulExpr();v=dbFormulaToStr(v)+dbFormulaToStr(r);}
              else break;
            }
            return v;
          }
          mulExpr(){
            let v=this.unary();
            for(;;){
              this.skipWs();
              const p=this.peek();
              if(p==='*'){this.pos++;const r=this.unary();v=dbFormulaToNum(v)*dbFormulaToNum(r);}
              else if(p==='/'){this.pos++;const rn=dbFormulaToNum(this.unary());v=rn===0?null:dbFormulaToNum(v)/rn;}
              else if(p==='%'){this.pos++;const r=this.unary();v=dbFormulaToNum(v)%dbFormulaToNum(r);}
              else if(p==='^'){this.pos++;const r=this.unary();v=Math.pow(dbFormulaToNum(v),dbFormulaToNum(r));}
              else break;
            }
            return v;
          }
          unary(){
            this.skipWs();
            const p=this.peek();
            if(p==='-'){this.pos++;return -dbFormulaToNum(this.unary());}
            if(p==='+'){this.pos++;return dbFormulaToNum(this.unary());}
            if(p==='!'){this.pos++;return !dbFormulaTruthy(this.unary());}
            if(this.matchWord('NOT')){return !dbFormulaTruthy(this.unary());}
            return this.atom();
          }
          atom(){
            this.skipWs();
            const p=this.peek();
            if(p==='('){this.pos++;const v=this.orExpr();this.skipWs();if(this.peek()===')')this.pos++;return v;}
            if(p==='"'||p==="'"){
              const q=p;this.pos++;let s='';
              while(this.pos<this.src.length&&this.src[this.pos]!==q){s+=this.src[this.pos];this.pos++;}
              if(this.peek()===q)this.pos++;
              return s;
            }
            if(p==='['){
              this.pos++;let s='';
              while(this.pos<this.src.length&&this.src[this.pos]!==']'){s+=this.src[this.pos];this.pos++;}
              if(this.peek()===']')this.pos++;
              if(this.aggMode)return s; 
              return this.fieldValue(s);
            }
            const nm=this.src.slice(this.pos).match(/^(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)/);
            if(nm){this.pos+=nm[0].length;return parseFloat(nm[0]);}
            const id=this.src.slice(this.pos).match(/^[A-Za-z_\u4e00-\u9fa5][A-Za-z0-9_\u4e00-\u9fa5]*/);
            if(id){
              this.pos+=id[0].length;
              this.skipWs();
              const name=id[0];
              if(this.peek()==='('){
                this.pos++;const args=[];
                this.skipWs();
                const isAgg=DB_AGG_FNS.includes(name.toUpperCase());
                const prevAgg=this.aggMode;
                if(isAgg)this.aggMode=true;
                if(this.peek()!==')'){
                  for(;;){
                    args.push(this.orExpr());
                    this.skipWs();
                    if(this.peek()===','){this.pos++;continue;}
                    break;
                  }
                }
                if(this.peek()===')')this.pos++;
                this.aggMode=prevAgg;
                return dbFormulaCall(name,args,this.docId); 
              }
              if(this.aggMode)return name; 
              return this.fieldValue(name);
            }
            return '';
          }
        }
        function dbFormulaEval(row,field){
          const src=String(field&&field.expr||'').trim();
          if(!src)return '';
          if(_formulaDepth>8)return null;
          _formulaDepth++;
          try{
            const cache=new Map();
            const fieldValue=(name)=>{
              if(cache.has(name))return cache.get(name);
              cache.set(name,null);
              let v;
              try{
                const def=(state.dbFields||[]).find(f=>dbFieldName(f)===name)||{name,type:'text'};
                if(dbFieldType(def)==='formula'){
                  v=dbFormulaEval(row,def);
                }else if(name===dbPrimaryField()){ 
                  v=stripHtml(row.text||'');
                }else{
                  v=row.db&&row.db[name];
                }
              }catch(e){v=null;}
              cache.set(name,v);
              return v;
            };
            const P=new _FormulaParser(src,fieldValue,row&&row.parent); 
            return P.parse();
          }catch(e){
            return null;
          }finally{
            _formulaDepth--;
          }
        }
        function dbFormulaDisplay(v){
          if(v==null||v==='')return '';
          if(typeof v==='boolean')return v?'TRUE':'FALSE';
          if(typeof v==='number'){
            if(!isFinite(v))return String(v);
            if(Number.isInteger(v))return String(v);
            return String(Math.round(v*10000)/10000);
          }
          return String(v);
        }
        function dbFilterRuleActive(f){
          if(!f||!f.field)return false;
          if(f.op==='empty'||f.op==='not_empty')return true;
          return String(f.value==null?'':f.value)!=='';
        }
        function dbRowMatches(n,f){
          const t=dbFieldType(f.field);
          const raw=dbRawValue(n,f.field);
          const s=dbTextValue(n,f.field);
          const empty=(raw==null||raw===''||s===''||(Array.isArray(raw)&&!raw.length));
          const op=f.op;
          if(op==='empty')return empty;
          if(op==='not_empty')return !empty;
          if(empty)return false;
          if(t==='number'||t==='formula'){
            const nv=dbNumValue(n,f.field);
            const fv=Number(f.value);
            if(op==='contains'||op==='not_contains'){
              const hit=s.includes(String(f.value));
              return op==='contains'?hit:!hit;
            }
            if(nv==null||isNaN(fv))return false;
            switch(op){
              case 'is': return nv===fv;
              case 'is_not': return nv!==fv;
              case 'gt': return nv>fv;
              case 'gte': return nv>=fv;
              case 'lt': return nv<fv;
              case 'lte': return nv<=fv;
            }
          }else if(t==='date'){
            const d1=s.split(' ')[0],d2=String(f.value).split(' ')[0];
            switch(op){
              case 'is': return d1===d2;
              case 'is_not': return d1!==d2;
              case 'before': return d1<d2;
              case 'after': return d1>d2;
            }
          }else if(t==='multi_select'){
            const arr=Array.isArray(raw)?raw:[];
            switch(op){
              case 'is': return arr.includes(String(f.value));
              case 'is_not': return !arr.includes(String(f.value));
              case 'contains': return arr.some(v=>String(v).includes(String(f.value)));
              case 'not_contains': return !arr.some(v=>String(v).includes(String(f.value)));
            }
          }else{
            switch(op){
              case 'is': return s===String(f.value);
              case 'is_not': return s!==String(f.value);
              case 'contains': return s.includes(String(f.value));
              case 'not_contains': return !s.includes(String(f.value));
            }
          }
          return true;
        }
        function dbFilterItemActive(item){
          if(!item)return false;
          if(item.or)return (item.rules||[]).some(r=>dbFilterItemActive(r));
          return dbFilterRuleActive(item);
        }
        function dbRowMatchesItem(n,item){
          if(!item)return true;
          if(item.or){
            const rules=item.rules||[];
            if(!rules.some(r=>dbFilterItemActive(r)))return true; 
            return rules.some(r=>dbFilterItemActive(r)&&dbRowMatches(n,r));
          }
          return dbRowMatches(n,item);
        }
        function dbCompareRows(a,b,field){
          const t=dbFieldType(field);
          let va,vb;
          if(t==='number'||t==='formula'){
            va=dbNumValue(a,field);vb=dbNumValue(b,field);
            const ea=va==null,eb=vb==null;
            if(ea&&eb)return 0;
            if(ea)return 1;
            if(eb)return -1;
            return va-vb;
          }
          if(t==='date'){
            va=dbTextValue(a,field).split(' ')[0];vb=dbTextValue(b,field).split(' ')[0];
            const ea=!va,eb=!vb;
            if(ea&&eb)return 0;
            if(ea)return 1;
            if(eb)return -1;
            return va<vb?-1:va>vb?1:0;
          }
          if(field==='浼樺厛绾?){
            const rank={'楂?:0,'涓?:1,'浣?:2};
            va=rank[dbTextValue(a,field)];vb=rank[dbTextValue(b,field)];
            const ea=va==null,eb=vb==null;
            if(ea&&eb)return 0;
            if(ea)return 1;
            if(eb)return -1;
            return va-vb;
          }
          va=dbTextValue(a,field);vb=dbTextValue(b,field);
          const ea=!va,eb=!vb;
          if(ea&&eb)return 0;
          if(ea)return 1;
          if(eb)return -1;
          return String(va).localeCompare(String(vb),'zh-Hans-CN');
        }
        function dbRowsApply(docId,rows){
          
          const tableId=docId||null;
          const filters=(tableId&&ui.dbFiltersMap&&ui.dbFiltersMap[tableId])||[];
          const sorts=(tableId&&ui.dbSortsMap&&ui.dbSortsMap[tableId])||[];
          let out=rows;
          
          if(filters.length)out=out.filter(n=>filters.every(it=>!dbFilterItemActive(it)||dbRowMatchesItem(n,it)));
          if(sorts.length){
            out=out.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
              for(const s of sorts){
                const c=dbCompareRows(a,b,s.field);
                if(c!==0)return s.dir==='desc'?-c:c;
              }
              
              return a.id<b.id?-1:(a.id>b.id?1:0);
            });
          }
          return out;
        }
        function dbSfAutoClose(panel,ids){
          
          if(panel._dbSfAutoClosed)return;
          panel._dbSfAutoClosed=true;
          setTimeout(()=>{
            
            if(!panel.isConnected)return;
            const onDoc=(e)=>{
              if(e.target.closest('.db-sf-panel')||(ids&&ids.some(id=>e.target.closest('#'+id))))return;
              closePicker(panel);
            };
            document.addEventListener('mousedown',onDoc);
            if(!panel._cleanups)panel._cleanups=[];
            panel._cleanups.push(()=>document.removeEventListener('mousedown',onDoc));
          },0);
        }
        function dbSfCloseAll(){
          document.querySelectorAll('.db-sf-panel').forEach(p=>closePicker(p));
        }

        function dbTableList(){
          const root=N(state.rootId);
          if(!root)return[];
          const dbRoot=root.children.map(id=>N(id)).find(n=>n&&n._isDbRoot);
          if(!dbRoot)return[];
          return dbRoot.children.map(id=>N(id)).filter(n=>n&&!n._deleted&&n.dbRoot);
        }
        function dbFieldSummable(f){
          const t=dbFieldType(f);
          return t==='number'||t==='formula';
        }
        function dbSummaryMode(){
          return (ui.dbSummary&&ui.dbSummary.mode)||'sum';
        }
        function dbSummaryOf(rows,field){
          const mode=dbSummaryMode();
          const vals=[];
          rows.forEach(n=>{
            const v=dbNumValue(n,field);
            if(v!=null&&!isNaN(v))vals.push(v);
          });
          if(mode==='count')return vals.length;
          if(!vals.length)return null;
          const sum=vals.reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);
          return mode==='avg'?sum/vals.length:sum;
        }
        function dbFmtSummary(v){
          if(v==null)return '鈥?;
          if(typeof v!=='number')return String(v);
          if(Number.isInteger(v))return String(v);
          return String(Math.round(v*100)/100);
        }

        const OPT_COLORS=['#E15554','#3BB273','#E1BC29','#4D9DE0','#7768AE','#E8871E','#20B2AA','#D9534F'];
