        function openFieldTypeMenu(anchor,cb){
          dbSfCloseAll();
          const TYPES=[
            {k:'text',ic:'T',n:'鏂囨湰',d:'鍗曡鏂囨湰'},
            {k:'number',ic:'#',n:'鏁板瓧',d:'鏁板€硷紝鍙弬涓庢眹鎬?},
            {k:'date',ic:'鈼?,n:'鏃ユ湡',d:'鏃ユ湡 / 鏃堕棿 / 鑼冨洿'},
            {k:'select',ic:'鈼?,n:'鍗曢€?,d:'浠庨€夐」涓€夋嫨涓€椤?},
            {k:'multi_select',ic:'鈻?,n:'澶氶€?,d:'浠庨€夐」涓€夋嫨澶氶」'},
            {k:'formula',ic:'茠',n:'鍏紡',d:'鐢卞叾浠栧瓧娈佃嚜鍔ㄨ绠?},
            {k:'link',ic:'鈬?,n:'鍏宠仈',d:'鍏宠仈鍙︿竴寮犺〃鐨勮褰?}
          ];
          const pop=document.createElement('div');pop.className='nt-pop';pop.dataset.pop='ftype';
          pop.style.width='276px';
          const title=document.createElement('div');title.className='nt-pop-title';title.textContent='瀛楁绫诲瀷';
          pop.appendChild(title);
          TYPES.forEach(t=>{
            const item=document.createElement('div');item.className='nt-item';
            item.innerHTML=`<span class="ic">${t.ic}</span><span style="flex:1"><span style="display:block">${t.n}</span><span class="desc">${t.d}</span></span>`;
            item.onclick=()=>{
              const rect=pop.getBoundingClientRect();
              
              closePicker(pop);
              cb(t.k,{left:rect.left,top:rect.top,bottom:rect.bottom});
            };
            pop.appendChild(item);
          });
          document.body.appendChild(pop);
          placePop(pop,300,400,anchor);
        
          initDragClose(pop,()=>closePicker(pop));
          ntAutoClose(pop);
        }
        function openFieldConfigPop(type,docId,onAdd,anchor){
          const pop=document.createElement('div');pop.className='nt-pop';pop.dataset.pop='fcfg';
          pop.style.width='304px';
          const title=document.createElement('div');title.className='nt-pop-title';
          title.textContent=({text:'鏂囨湰鍒?,number:'鏁板瓧鍒?,date:'鏃ユ湡鍒?,select:'鍗曢€夊垪',multi_select:'澶氶€夊垪',formula:'鍏紡鍒?,link:'鍏宠仈鍒?})[type]||'鏂板垪';
          pop.appendChild(title);
          const body=document.createElement('div');
          body.style.cssText='display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px';
          const nameLbl=document.createElement('label');nameLbl.className='nt-field-label';nameLbl.textContent='鍒楀悕绉?;
          const nameInp=document.createElement('input');nameInp.className='nt-input';nameInp.placeholder='渚嬪锛氶噾棰?;
          body.appendChild(nameLbl);body.appendChild(nameInp);
          let extra=null;
          const getExtra=()=>extra?extra.get():'';
          if(type==='date'){
            const lbl=document.createElement('label');lbl.className='nt-field-label';
            lbl.textContent='鏃ユ湡绮掑害锛堝悓鏃堕棿鍒楁诞绐楋級';
            const sel=document.createElement('select');sel.className='nt-input';
            [['date','鍗曟棩鏈?],['datetime','鏃ユ湡鏃堕棿'],['range','鏃ユ湡鑼冨洿'],['datetimerange','鏃ユ湡鏃堕棿鑼冨洿']].forEach(([k,txt])=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=k;o.textContent=txt;sel.appendChild(o);});
            const wrap=document.createElement('div');
            wrap.appendChild(lbl);wrap.appendChild(sel);
            body.appendChild(wrap);
            extra={get:()=>sel.value};
          }else if(type==='select'||type==='multi_select'){
            const lbl=document.createElement('label');lbl.className='nt-field-label';lbl.textContent='閫夐」锛堣緭鍏ュ悗鍥炶溅蹇€熸柊澧烇級';
            const list=document.createElement('div');list.className='nt-opt-list';
            const opts=[];
            const renderOpts=()=>{
              list.innerHTML='';
              opts.forEach((o,i)=>{
                const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='nt-opt-row';
                const dot=document.createElement('span');dot.className='dot';dot.style.background=OPT_COLORS[i%OPT_COLORS.length];
                const inp=document.createElement('input');inp.className='nt-input';inp.value=o;
                inp.oninput=()=>{opts[i]=inp.value;};
                const del=document.createElement('button');del.className='db-sf-del';del.textContent='鉁?;del.title='鍒犻櫎閫夐」';
                del.onclick=()=>{opts.splice(i,1);renderOpts();};
                row.appendChild(dot);row.appendChild(inp);row.appendChild(del);
                list.appendChild(row);
              });
            };
            const addBtn=document.createElement('div');addBtn.className='nt-opt-add';
            const addInp=document.createElement('input');addInp.className='nt-input';addInp.placeholder='杈撳叆閫夐」锛屽洖杞︽坊鍔?;
            addBtn.appendChild(addInp);
            const doAdd=()=>{
              const v=addInp.value.trim();
              if(!v)return;
              opts.push(v);addInp.value='';renderOpts();addInp.focus();
            };
            addInp.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();doAdd();}};
            renderOpts();
            const wrap=document.createElement('div');
            wrap.appendChild(lbl);wrap.appendChild(list);wrap.appendChild(addBtn);
            body.appendChild(wrap);
            extra={get:()=>opts.map(s=>String(s).trim()).filter(Boolean)};
          }else if(type==='formula'){
            const lbl=document.createElement('label');lbl.className='nt-field-label';lbl.textContent='鍏紡琛ㄨ揪寮?;
            const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.className='nt-input';ta.rows=3;ta.placeholder='渚嬪锛氭槦绾?* 2';
            const help=document.createElement('div');help.className='db-formula-box';
            help.innerHTML='鏁板瓧锛欰BS ROUND INT MAX MIN SUM AVG COUNT 路 鏂囨湰锛歀EN LEFT UPPER TRIM CONCAT IF 路 鏃ユ湡锛?code>TODAY()</code> <code>YEAR(瀛楁)</code> <code>MONTH()</code> <code>DAY()</code> <code>DATEDIF(寮€濮?缁撴潫,"Y/M/D")</code> <code>DATE(骞?鏈?鏃?</code>銆傚瓧娈靛悕鐩存帴寮曠敤锛屽惈绌烘牸鐨勭敤 <code>[鏂规嫭鍙穄</code>銆?;
            const wrap=document.createElement('div');
            wrap.appendChild(lbl);wrap.appendChild(ta);wrap.appendChild(help);
            body.appendChild(wrap);
            extra={get:()=>ta.value};
          }else if(type==='link'){
            const lbl=document.createElement('label');lbl.className='nt-field-label';lbl.textContent='鍏宠仈鐩爣琛?;
            const sel=document.createElement('select');sel.className='nt-input';
            const tables=dbTableList().filter(t=>t.id!==docId);
            if(tables.length){
              tables.forEach(t=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t.id;o.textContent=stripHtml(t.text)||'鏈懡鍚?;sel.appendChild(o);});
            }else{
              const o=document.createElement('option');o.value='';o.textContent='锛堟殏鏃犲彲鍏宠仈鐨勮〃锛?;sel.appendChild(o);
            }
            const wrap=document.createElement('div');
            wrap.appendChild(lbl);wrap.appendChild(sel);
            body.appendChild(wrap);
            extra={get:()=>sel.value};
          }
          const actions=document.createElement('div');actions.className='nt-actions';
          const cancel=document.createElement('button');cancel.className='nt-btn';cancel.textContent='鍙栨秷';
          cancel.onclick=()=>closePicker(pop);
          const ok=document.createElement('button');ok.className='nt-btn primary';ok.textContent='纭畾';
          ok.onclick=()=>{
            const name=nameInp.value.trim();
            if(!name){toast('璇疯緭鍏ュ垪鍚嶇О');return;}
            if((state.dbFields||[]).some(f=>dbFieldName(f)===name)){toast('宸插瓨鍦ㄥ悓鍚嶅垪');return;}
            let def={name,type};
            if(type==='date')def.dateType=getExtra()||'date';
            else if(type==='select'||type==='multi_select'){
              const os=getExtra();
              if(!os.length){toast('璇疯緭鍏ヨ嚦灏戜竴涓€夐」');return;}
              def.options=os;
            }else if(type==='formula'){
              const expr=String(getExtra()||'').trim();
              if(!expr){toast('璇疯緭鍏ュ叕寮忚〃杈惧紡');return;}
              def.expr=expr;
            }else if(type==='link'){
              const t=getExtra();
              if(!t){toast('璇烽€夋嫨鍏宠仈鐩爣琛?);return;}
              def.target=t;
            }
            closePicker(pop);
            onAdd(def);
          };
          actions.appendChild(cancel);actions.appendChild(ok);
          body.appendChild(actions);
          pop.appendChild(body);
          document.body.appendChild(pop);
          placePop(pop,320,440,anchor||null); 
          initDragClose(pop,()=>closePicker(pop));
          ntAutoClose(pop);
          nameInp.focus();
        }
        
        function openDbFieldConfigDialog(docId,fieldName,mode){
          const pop=document.createElement('div');pop.className='nt-pop';pop.dataset.pop='fcfg';
          pop.style.width='304px';
          const title=document.createElement('div');title.className='nt-pop-title';
          title.textContent=mode==='formula'?'鍏紡鍒楅厤缃?:mode==='link'?'鍏宠仈鍒楅厤缃?:'鍒楃被鍨?;
          pop.appendChild(title);
          const body=document.createElement('div');
          body.style.cssText='display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px';
          if(mode==='formula'){
            const def=(state.dbFields||[]).find(f=>dbFieldName(f)===fieldName);
            const cur=dbFieldType(def)==='formula'?(def.expr||''):'';
            const lbl=document.createElement('label');lbl.className='nt-field-label';
            lbl.textContent='鍏紡琛ㄨ揪寮忥紙鍒楋細'+fieldName+'锛?;
            const ta=document.createElement('textarea');ta.className='nt-input';ta.rows=4;ta.value=cur;
            const help=document.createElement('div');help.className='db-formula-box';
            help.innerHTML='鏁板瓧锛欰BS ROUND INT MAX MIN SUM AVG COUNT 路 鏂囨湰锛歀EN LEFT UPPER TRIM CONCAT IF 路 鏃ユ湡锛?code>TODAY()</code> <code>YEAR(瀛楁)</code> <code>MONTH()</code> <code>DAY()</code> <code>DATEDIF(寮€濮?缁撴潫,"Y/M/D")</code> <code>DATE(骞?鏈?鏃?</code>銆傚瓧娈靛悕鐩存帴寮曠敤锛屽惈绌烘牸鐨勭敤 <code>[鏂规嫭鍙穄</code>銆?;
            body.appendChild(lbl);body.appendChild(ta);body.appendChild(help);
            const actions=document.createElement('div');actions.className='nt-actions';
            const cancel=document.createElement('button');cancel.className='nt-btn';cancel.textContent='鍙栨秷';
            cancel.onclick=()=>closePicker(pop);
            const ok=document.createElement('button');ok.className='nt-btn primary';ok.textContent='淇濆瓨';
            ok.onclick=()=>{
              const expr=ta.value.trim();
              if(!expr){toast('璇疯緭鍏ュ叕寮忚〃杈惧紡');return;}
              commit(s=>{
                const idx=(s.dbFields||[]).findIndex(f=>dbFieldName(f)===fieldName);
                if(idx<0)return;
                s.dbFields[idx]={name:fieldName,type:'formula',expr};
              },{snapshot:true});
              closePicker(pop);
              forceRender();toast('鍏紡宸蹭繚瀛橈細'+expr);
            };
            actions.appendChild(cancel);actions.appendChild(ok);
            body.appendChild(actions);
          }else if(mode==='link'){
            const def=(state.dbFields||[]).find(f=>dbFieldName(f)===fieldName);
            const curTarget=def&&def.target||'';
            const tables=dbTableList().filter(t=>t.id!==docId);
            const lbl=document.createElement('label');lbl.className='nt-field-label';
            lbl.textContent='鍏宠仈鐩爣琛紙鍒楋細'+fieldName+'锛?;
            const sel=document.createElement('select');sel.className='nt-input';
            if(tables.length){
              tables.forEach(t=>{
                const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=t.id;o.textContent=stripHtml(t.text)||'鏈懡鍚?;
                if(t.id===curTarget)o.selected=true;
                sel.appendChild(o);
              });
            }else{
              const o=document.createElement('option');o.value='';o.textContent='锛堟殏鏃犲彲鍏宠仈鐨勮〃锛?;sel.appendChild(o);
            }
            body.appendChild(lbl);body.appendChild(sel);
            const actions=document.createElement('div');actions.className='nt-actions';
            const cancel=document.createElement('button');cancel.className='nt-btn';cancel.textContent='鍙栨秷';
            cancel.onclick=()=>closePicker(pop);
            const ok=document.createElement('button');ok.className='nt-btn primary';ok.textContent='淇濆瓨';
            ok.onclick=()=>{
              const target=sel.value;
              if(!target){toast('璇烽€夋嫨鍏宠仈鐩爣琛?);return;}
              commit(s=>{
                const idx=(s.dbFields||[]).findIndex(f=>dbFieldName(f)===fieldName);
                if(idx<0)return;
                s.dbFields[idx]={name:fieldName,type:'link',target};
              },{snapshot:true});
              closePicker(pop);
              forceRender();toast('鍏宠仈琛ㄥ凡鏇存柊');
            };
            actions.appendChild(cancel);actions.appendChild(ok);
            body.appendChild(actions);
          }else{
            const cap=document.createElement('div');cap.className='nt-caption';
            cap.textContent='灏嗚鍒楄浆涓烘櫘閫氭枃鏈垪锛熷師鏁版嵁淇濈暀锛涜嫢鍘熶负鍏紡鍒楋紝灏嗘妸褰撳墠璁＄畻缁撴灉鍥哄寲涓烘枃鏈€?;
            body.appendChild(cap);
            const actions=document.createElement('div');actions.className='nt-actions';
            const cancel=document.createElement('button');cancel.className='nt-btn';cancel.textContent='鍙栨秷';
            cancel.onclick=()=>closePicker(pop);
            const ok=document.createElement('button');ok.className='nt-btn primary';ok.textContent='杞崲';
            ok.onclick=()=>{
              commit(s=>{
                const idx=(s.dbFields||[]).findIndex(f=>dbFieldName(f)===fieldName);
                if(idx<0)return;
                const def=s.dbFields[idx];
                if(dbFieldType(def)==='formula'){
                  getDbRows(docId).forEach(n=>{ 
                    if(!n.db)n.db={};
                    n.db[fieldName]=dbFormulaDisplay(dbRawValue(n,def));
                  });
                }
                s.dbFields[idx]=fieldName;
              },{snapshot:true});
              closePicker(pop);
              forceRender();toast('宸茶浆涓烘枃鏈垪');
            };
            actions.appendChild(cancel);actions.appendChild(ok);
            body.appendChild(actions);
          }
          pop.appendChild(body);
          document.body.appendChild(pop);
          placePop(pop,320,420,null);
          initDragClose(pop,()=>closePicker(pop));
          ntAutoClose(pop);
        }
        function dbLinkTitle(id){
          const m=N(id);
          return m?stripHtml(m.text||'鏈懡鍚?):'宸插垹闄よ褰?;
        }
        function openLinkPicker(anchorEl,rowId,fieldName,targetId){
          const existing=document.querySelector('.link-picker');
          if(existing)closePicker(existing);
          if(!targetId||!N(targetId)){toast('璇峰厛鍦ㄥ垪鑿滃崟涓缃叧鑱旂洰鏍囪〃');return;}
          const target=N(targetId);
          const rows=target.children.map(id=>N(id)).filter(n=>n&&!isFolder(n.id)&&!n._deleted);
          if(!rows.length){toast('鐩爣琛ㄦ殏鏃犺褰?);return;}
          const curRow=N(rowId);
          const cur=Array.isArray(curRow&&curRow.db&&curRow.db[fieldName])?(curRow.db[fieldName]):[];
          const picker=document.createElement('div');picker.className='link-picker';
          picker.innerHTML=`<h4>鍏宠仈锛?{esc(stripHtml(target.text)||'鏈懡鍚?)}</h4><div class="link-picker-list"></div><div class="link-picker-actions"><button class="btn" data-cancel>鍙栨秷</button><button class="btn primary" data-ok>纭畾</button></div>`;
          const list=picker.querySelector('.link-picker-list');
          const cbMap={};
          rows.forEach(r=>{
            const item=document.createElement('label');item.className='link-picker-item';
            const cb=document.createElement('input');cb.type='checkbox';
            cb.checked=cur.includes(r.id);
            item.appendChild(cb);
            const span=document.createElement('span');span.textContent=stripHtml(r.text)||'(绌?';
            item.appendChild(span);
            
            list.appendChild(item);
            cbMap[r.id]=cb;
          });
          document.body.appendChild(picker);
          placePop(picker,340,440,anchorEl);
        
          initDragClose(picker,()=>closePicker(picker));
          picker.querySelector('[data-cancel]').onclick=()=>closePicker(picker);
          picker.querySelector('[data-ok]').onclick=()=>{
            const ids=Object.keys(cbMap).filter(id=>cbMap[id].checked);
            actions.setDbCell(rowId,fieldName,ids);
            closePicker(picker);
            forceRender();
          };
          bindPickerAutoClose(picker);
        }
        function openMultiSelectPicker(anchorEl,rowId,fieldName,opts){
          const existing=document.querySelector('.nt-pop[data-pop="msel"]');
          if(existing)closePicker(existing);
          const rw=N(rowId);
          const pop=document.createElement('div');pop.className='nt-pop';pop.dataset.pop='msel';
          pop.style.width='272px';
          const title=document.createElement('div');title.className='nt-pop-title';
          title.textContent='澶氶€夛細'+fieldName;
          pop.appendChild(title);
          const list=document.createElement('div');list.className='nt-opt-list';
          const render=()=>{
            const r2=N(rowId);
            const cur=Array.isArray(r2&&r2.db&&r2.db[fieldName])?(r2.db[fieldName]):[];
            list.innerHTML='';
            if(!opts.length){
              const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='nt-caption';empty.style.padding='4px 8px';
              empty.textContent='鏆傛棤閫夐」锛屽湪涓嬫柟杈撳叆骞跺洖杞︽柊澧炪€?;
              list.appendChild(empty);
            }
            opts.forEach(o=>{
              const checked=cur.includes(o);
              const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='nt-item';
              const box=document.createElement('span');box.className='nt-opt-check'+(checked?' on':'');
              box.textContent=checked?'鉁?:'';
              const name=document.createElement('span');name.textContent=o;name.style.flex='1';
              row.appendChild(box);row.appendChild(name);
              row.onclick=()=>{
                const r3=N(rowId);
                const c=Array.isArray(r3&&r3.db&&r3.db[fieldName])?(r3.db[fieldName]):[];
                const next=c.includes(o)?c.filter(x=>x!==o):[...c,o];
                actions.setDbCell(rowId,fieldName,next);
                forceRender();
                render();
              };
              list.appendChild(row);
            });
          };
          render();
          pop.appendChild(list);
          const addWrap=document.createElement('div');addWrap.className='nt-opt-add';
          const addInp=document.createElement('input');addInp.className='nt-input';addInp.placeholder='杈撳叆鍚庡洖杞︽坊鍔犻€夐」鈥?;
          addWrap.appendChild(addInp);
          addInp.onkeydown=e=>{
            if(e.key==='Enter'&&addInp.value.trim()){
              const v=addInp.value.trim();
              if(!opts.includes(v)){
                opts.push(v);
                commit(s=>{
                  const idx=(s.dbFields||[]).findIndex(f=>dbFieldName(f)===fieldName);
                  if(idx>=0&&s.dbFields[idx]&&typeof s.dbFields[idx]==='object'){
                    if(!Array.isArray(s.dbFields[idx].options))s.dbFields[idx].options=[];
                    if(!s.dbFields[idx].options.includes(v))s.dbFields[idx].options.push(v);
                  }
                },{snapshot:true});
              }
              const r2=N(rowId);
              const c=Array.isArray(r2&&r2.db&&r2.db[fieldName])?(r2.db[fieldName]):[];
              actions.setDbCell(rowId,fieldName,c.includes(v)?c:[...c,v]);
              addInp.value='';
              forceRender();
              render();
            }
          };
          pop.appendChild(addWrap);
          document.body.appendChild(pop);
          placePop(pop,300,380,anchorEl);
        
          initDragClose(pop,()=>closePicker(pop));
          ntAutoClose(pop);
          addInp.focus();
        }
        function renderDatabasePage(doc){
          const container=document.createElement('div');
          if(!doc){container.innerHTML='<div class="empty-state">璇峰厛閫夋嫨鏁版嵁搴?/div>';return container;}
          const page=document.createElement('div');page.className='db-page';
          const toolbar=document.createElement('div');toolbar.className='db-toolbar';
          const views=document.createElement('div');views.className='db-views';
          DB_VIEWS.forEach(v=>{
            const b=document.createElement('button');
            b.className='db-view-btn'+(ui.dbView===v.k?' active':'');
            b.textContent=v.n;
            b.onclick=()=>{ui.dbView=v.k;saveUI();forceRender();};
            views.appendChild(b);
          });
          toolbar.appendChild(views);
          const sfGroup=document.createElement('div');sfGroup.className='db-sf-group';
          const filters=ui.dbFiltersMap&&ui.dbFiltersMap[doc.id]||[];
          const sorts=ui.dbSortsMap&&ui.dbSortsMap[doc.id]||[];
          const btnFilter=document.createElement('button');
          btnFilter.className='db-sf-btn'+(filters.length?' active':'');
          btnFilter.id='btnDbFilter';
          btnFilter.title='鎸夋潯浠剁瓫閫夎';
          btnFilter.innerHTML='<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width:13px;height:13px;stroke:currentColor;fill:none;stroke-width:2;stroke-linecap:round"><path d="M4 5h16M7 12h10M10 19h4"/></svg>绛涢€?+(filters.length?'<span class="cnt">'+filters.length+'</span>':'');
          btnFilter.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();openDbFilterPanel(btnFilter);};
          sfGroup.appendChild(btnFilter);
          const btnSort=document.createElement('button');
          btnSort.className='db-sf-btn'+(sorts.length?' active':'');
          btnSort.id='btnDbSort';
          btnSort.title='璁剧疆鎺掑簭瑙勫垯';

        }
        function renderDbTable(rows,doc){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');
          wrap.className='db-table-wrap';
          if(!rows.length){wrap.innerHTML='<div class="db-empty">鏆傛棤鏁版嵁锛岀偣鍑汇€? 琛屻€嶆坊鍔?/div>';return wrap;}
          const allFields=state.dbFields||['鏍囬'];
          const hidden=ui.dbHiddenFields||{};
          const fields=allFields.filter(f=>!hidden[dbFieldName(f)]); 
          if(!fields.length){wrap.innerHTML='<div class="db-empty">鎵€鏈夊垪宸查殣钘忥紝鐐瑰嚮銆屾樉闅愩€嶉噸鏂版樉绀?/div>';return wrap;}
          const table=document.createElement('table');
          table.className='db-table';
          const fieldName=f=>typeof f==='string'?f:f.name;
          
          const titleFieldName=dbPrimaryField();
          
          const _fieldDefCache=new Map();
          fields.forEach(_f=>_fieldDefCache.set(_f,dbFieldDef(_f)));
          const allStatuses=new Set(['杩涜','寰呭姙','瀹屾垚','鏀惧純']);
          
          getDbRows(doc.id).forEach(n=>{const sv=n.db&&n.db['鐘舵€?];if(sv)allStatuses.add(sv);}); 
          const thead=document.createElement('thead');
          const tr=document.createElement('tr');
          fields.forEach((f,i)=>{
            const fn=fieldName(f);
            const th=document.createElement('th');
            th.textContent=fn;
            const _ft=dbFieldType(f);
            if(_ft==='formula'){
              const tag=document.createElement('span');tag.className='db-field-tag formula';tag.textContent='茠';
              tag.title='鍏紡鍒楋細'+(f&&f.expr||'');
              th.appendChild(tag);
            }else if(_ft==='link'){
              const tag=document.createElement('span');tag.className='db-field-tag link';tag.textContent='鈬?;
              tag.title='鍏宠仈鍒?;
              th.appendChild(tag);
            }else if(_ft==='multi_select'){
              const tag=document.createElement('span');tag.className='db-field-tag';tag.textContent='鈻?;
              tag.title='澶氶€夊垪';
              th.appendChild(tag);
            }
            const _ck=doc.id+'::'+fn;
            if(state.dbColumnWidths&&state.dbColumnWidths[_ck]){
              th.style.width=state.dbColumnWidths[_ck]+'px';
              th.style.minWidth=state.dbColumnWidths[_ck]+'px';
            }
            const resize=document.createElement('div');
            resize.className='db-col-resize';
            resize.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
              e.preventDefault();
              e.stopPropagation();
              try{resize.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(err){}
              ui._resizingCol=true;
              ui._resizingColStart=Date.now();
              const startX=e.clientX;const startW=th.offsetWidth;
              const move=(ev)=>{
                const fontSize=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
                const minW=fontSize;
                const w=Math.max(minW,startW+(ev.clientX-startX));
                th.style.width=w+'px';th.style.minWidth=w+'px';
              };
              const up=()=>{
                try{
                  resize.removeEventListener('pointermove',move);
                  resize.removeEventListener('pointerup',up);
                  resize.removeEventListener('pointercancel',up);
                }finally{
                  
                  ui._resizingCol=false;
                  ui._resizingColStart=0;
                  try{resize.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(err){}
                  try{actions.setDbColumnWidth(doc.id,fn,th.offsetWidth);}
                  catch(err){console.error('[JU] col resize commit failed',err);}
                }
              };
              resize.addEventListener('pointermove',move);
              resize.addEventListener('pointerup',up);
              resize.addEventListener('pointercancel',up);
            });
            th.appendChild(resize);
            th.oncontextmenu=(e)=>{
              e.preventDefault();
              const items=[];
              items.push({ic:'茠',txt:'璁句负鍏紡鍒椻€?,fn:()=>openDbFieldConfigDialog(doc.id,fn,'formula')});
              if(dbFieldType(f)==='formula')items.push({ic:'茠',txt:'缂栬緫鍏紡鈥?,fn:()=>openDbFieldConfigDialog(doc.id,fn,'formula')});
              if(dbFieldType(f)==='formula')items.push({ic:'茠',txt:'杞负鏂囨湰鍒?,fn:()=>openDbFieldConfigDialog(doc.id,fn,'text')});
              if(dbFieldType(f)==='link')items.push({ic:'鈬?,txt:'閫夋嫨鍏宠仈琛ㄢ€?,fn:()=>openDbFieldConfigDialog(doc.id,fn,'link')});
              items.push({sep:true});
              items.push({ic:'鉁忥笍',txt:'閲嶅懡鍚嶅垪',fn:()=>{appPrompt('鍒楀悕绉帮細',fn,(nf)=>{
                  if(!nf||!nf.trim()||nf.trim()===fn)return;
                  actions.renameDbField(doc.id,fn,nf.trim());
                  forceRender();
                });}});
              items.push({ic:'鈫?,txt:'鍦ㄥ乏渚ф彃鍏ュ垪',fn:()=>{appPrompt('鏂板垪鍚嶇О锛?,'',(nf)=>{
                  if(!nf||!nf.trim())return;
                  commit(s=>{
                    const idx=(s.dbFields||[]).findIndex(f=>(typeof f==='string'?f:f.name)===fn);
                    if(idx<0)return;
                    s.dbFields.splice(idx,0,nf.trim());
                  },{snapshot:true});
                  forceRender();
                });}});
              items.push({ic:'鈫?,txt:'宸︾Щ',fn:()=>{if(i<=0)return;commit(s=>{[s.dbFields[i-1],s.dbFields[i]]=[s.dbFields[i],s.dbFields[i-1]];},{snapshot:true});forceRender();}});
              items.push({ic:'鈫?,txt:'鍙崇Щ',fn:()=>{if(i>=state.dbFields.length-1)return;commit(s=>{[s.dbFields[i+1],s.dbFields[i]]=[s.dbFields[i],s.dbFields[i+1]];},{snapshot:true});forceRender();}});
              items.push({ic:'馃棏',txt:'鍒犻櫎鍒?,danger:true,fn:()=>{
                  if(state.dbFields.length<=1){toast('鑷冲皯淇濈暀涓€鍒?);return;}
                  appConfirm('鍒犻櫎鍒椼€?+fn+'銆嶏紵璇ュ垪鎵€鏈夋暟鎹皢涓€骞跺垹闄ゃ€?,()=>{
                    actions.removeDbField(doc.id,fn);
                    forceRender();toast('宸插垹闄ゅ垪');
                  });
                }});
              showContextMenu(items,e);
            };
            tr.appendChild(th);
          });
          thead.appendChild(tr);table.appendChild(thead);
          const tbody=document.createElement('tbody');
          rows.forEach((n)=>{
            const r=document.createElement('tr');

              r.style.height=state.dbRowHeights[n.id]+'px';
            }
            fields.forEach((f,i)=>{
              const fn=fieldName(f);

              rowResize.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
                e.preventDefault();
                e.stopPropagation();
                try{rowResize.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(err){}
                const startY=e.clientY;const startH=r.offsetHeight;
                const move=(ev)=>{
                  const fontSize=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).fontSize);
                  const minH=fontSize*1.5;
                  const h=Math.max(minH,startH+(ev.clientY-startY));
                  r.style.height=h+'px';
                  Array.from(r.children).forEach(c=>c.style.height=h+'px');
                };
                const up=()=>{
                  try{
                    rowResize.removeEventListener('pointermove',move);
                    rowResize.removeEventListener('pointerup',up);
                    rowResize.removeEventListener('pointercancel',up);
                  }finally{
                    
                    try{rowResize.releasePointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(err){}
                    try{actions.setDbRowHeight(n.id,r.offsetHeight);}
                    catch(err){console.error('[JU] row resize commit failed',err);}
                  }
                };
                rowResize.addEventListener('pointermove',move);
                rowResize.addEventListener('pointerup',up);
                rowResize.addEventListener('pointercancel',up);
              });
              if(i===0)td.appendChild(rowResize);
              const _fdef=_fieldDefCache.get(f); 
              const _hasType=typeof f==='object'&&!!f.type;
              if(_hasType&&_fdef.type==='formula'){
                const span=document.createElement('span');span.className='db-cell-formula';
                const v=dbFormulaDisplay(dbRawValue(n,f));

                span.title='鍏紡锛?+(_fdef.expr||'');

                td.appendChild(span);
              }else if(_hasType&&_fdef.type==='link'){
                const box=document.createElement('div');box.className='link-chips';

          table.appendChild(tbody);
          if(ui.dbSummary&&ui.dbSummary.on){
            const tfoot=document.createElement('tfoot');
            const fr=document.createElement('tr');fr.className='db-summary-row';
            fields.forEach((f,i)=>{
              const fn=fieldName(f);
              const td=document.createElement('td');
              if(i===0){
                td.className='db-summary-label';
                td.textContent='姹囨€?路 '+rows.length+' 琛?;
              }else{
                const mode=dbSummaryMode();
                if(mode==='count'){
                  const cnt=rows.filter(n=>{
                    const v=dbRawValue(n,f);
                    return !(v==null||v===''||(Array.isArray(v)&&!v.length));
                  }).length;
                  td.textContent=String(cnt);
                  td.className='db-summary-num';
                }else if(dbFieldSummable(f)){
                  td.textContent=dbFmtSummary(dbSummaryOf(rows,f));
                  td.className='db-summary-num';
                  td.title=mode==='sum'?'姹傚拰':'姹傚钩鍧?;
                }else{
                  td.textContent='';
                }
              }
              fr.appendChild(td);
            });
            tfoot.appendChild(fr);
            table.appendChild(tfoot);
          }
          wrap.appendChild(table);
          attachPanScroll(wrap);
          return wrap;
        }
        
        const _panCleanups=new Set();
        function attachPanScroll(wrap){
          
          if(wrap._panCleanup){try{wrap._panCleanup();}catch(err){}wrap._panCleanup=null;}
          
          _panCleanups.forEach(c=>{try{const w=c._wrap;if(w&&!w.isConnected)c();}catch(e){}});
          let dragging=false,sx=0,sy=0,sl=0,st=0;
          const onMove=(e)=>{
            if(!dragging)return;
            wrap.scrollLeft=sl-(e.clientX-sx);
            wrap.scrollTop=st-(e.clientY-sy);
          };
          const onUp=()=>{
            if(!dragging)return;
            dragging=false;
            wrap.style.cursor='';
            document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onMove);
            document.removeEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
          };
          const onDown=e=>{
            if(e.shiftKey)return;
            if(e.target.closest('input,select,button,textarea,[contenteditable],.db-col-resize,.db-row-resize,.db-cell-date'))return;
            dragging=true;sx=e.clientX;sy=e.clientY;sl=wrap.scrollLeft;st=wrap.scrollTop;
            wrap.style.cursor='grabbing';
            document.addEventListener('mousemove',onMove);
            document.addEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
            e.preventDefault();
          };
          wrap.addEventListener('mousedown',onDown);
          
          wrap._panCleanup=()=>{
            wrap.removeEventListener('mousedown',onDown);
            document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onMove);
            document.removeEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
            if(wrap.style.cursor==='grabbing')wrap.style.cursor='';
            _panCleanups.delete(wrap._panCleanup);
            wrap._panCleanup=null;
          };
          wrap._panCleanup._wrap=wrap;
          _panCleanups.add(wrap._panCleanup);
        }
        function formatTimeValue(v){
          if(!v && v!==0)return '閫夋嫨鈥?;
          if(typeof v==='number'){
            const ms=v>1e12?v:(v>1e9?v*1000:0);
            if(ms){
              const d=new Date(ms);
              return `${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(d.getDate())}`;
            }
            return String(v);
          }
          if(typeof v==='string')return v;
          if(v.type==='date')return v.start||'閫夋嫨鈥?;
          if(v.type==='datetime')return (v.start||'閫夋嫨鈥?).trim();
          if(v.type==='range')return (v.start||'')+' - '+(v.end||'');
          if(v.type==='datetimerange')return (v.start||'')+' - '+(v.end||'');
          return String(v);
        }
        function closePicker(el){
          if(!el)return;
          
          if(el._cleanups&&el._cleanups.length){
            el._cleanups.forEach(fn=>{try{fn();}catch(e){}});
            el._cleanups.length=0;
          }
          
          if(el._cleanup){try{el._cleanup();}catch(e){}el._cleanup=null;}
          if(el.parentNode)el.remove();
          window._popAnchor=null; 
        }
        function bindPickerAutoClose(el){
          const _shouldClose=e=>{if(!e.target.closest('.date-picker, .region-picker'))closePicker(el);};
          const onDocDown=e=>_shouldClose(e);
          const onDocTouch=e=>_shouldClose(e);
          if(!el._cleanups)el._cleanups=[];
          el._cleanups.push(()=>{
            document.removeEventListener('mousedown',onDocDown);
            document.removeEventListener('touchstart',onDocTouch);
          });
          setTimeout(()=>{
            
            if(!el.isConnected)return;
            document.addEventListener('mousedown',onDocDown);
            
            document.addEventListener('touchstart',onDocTouch,{passive:true});
          },0);
        }
        function openTimePicker(anchorEl,callback,current){
          const existing=document.querySelector('.date-picker');
          if(existing)closePicker(existing);
          const picker=document.createElement('div');
          picker.className='date-picker';
          picker.innerHTML=`
            <div class="dp-row">
              <select id="dpType">
                <option value="date">鍗曟棩鏈?/option>
                <option value="datetime">鏃ユ湡鏃堕棿</option>
                <option value="range">鏃ユ湡鑼冨洿</option>
                <option value="datetimerange">鏃ユ湡鏃堕棿鑼冨洿</option>
              </select>
            </div>
            <div class="dp-row">
              <input type="date" id="dpStart">
              <input type="time" id="dpStartTime" style="display:none">
            </div>
            <div class="dp-row" id="dpEndRow" style="display:none">
              <input type="date" id="dpEnd">
              <input type="time" id="dpEndTime" style="display:none">
            </div>
            <div class="dp-actions">
              <button class="btn" id="dpCancel">鍙栨秷</button>
              <button class="btn primary" id="dpOk">纭畾</button>
            </div>`;
          const typeSel=picker.querySelector('#dpType');
          const dpStart=picker.querySelector('#dpStart');
          const dpStartTime=picker.querySelector('#dpStartTime');
          const dpEndRow=picker.querySelector('#dpEndRow');
          const dpEnd=picker.querySelector('#dpEnd');
          const dpEndTime=picker.querySelector('#dpEndTime');
          document.body.appendChild(picker);
          placePop(picker,280,300,anchorEl);
        
          initDragClose(picker,()=>closePicker(picker));
          if(current&&typeof current==='object'){
            typeSel.value=current.type||'date';
            if(current.start){const parts=current.start.split(/[ T]/);dpStart.value=parts[0]||'';if(parts[1])dpStartTime.value=parts[1];}
            if(current.end){const parts=current.end.split(/[ T]/);dpEnd.value=parts[0]||'';if(parts[1])dpEndTime.value=parts[1];}
          }else{
            let sd='',st='';
            if(typeof current==='string'&&current){
              const parts=current.split(/[ T]/);
              sd=parts[0]||'';
              if(parts[1])st=parts[1];
            }
            dpStart.value=sd||todayStr();
            if(st)dpStartTime.value=st;
          }
          const updateVis=()=>{
            const t=typeSel.value;
            dpStartTime.style.display=(t==='datetime'||t==='datetimerange')?'block':'none';
            dpEndTime.style.display=(t==='datetimerange')?'block':'none';
            dpEndRow.style.display=(t==='range'||t==='datetimerange')?'flex':'none';
            if(!dpStart.value)dpStart.value=todayStr();
            if((t==='range'||t==='datetimerange')&&!dpEnd.value)dpEnd.value=dpStart.value;
          };
          typeSel.onchange=updateVis;updateVis();
          picker.querySelector('#dpCancel').onclick=()=>closePicker(picker);
          picker.querySelector('#dpOk').onclick=()=>{
            const t=typeSel.value;
            let val;
            const sd=dpStart.value,st=dpStartTime.value,ed=dpEnd.value,et=dpEndTime.value;
            if(t==='date')val={type:'date',start:sd};
            else if(t==='datetime')val={type:'datetime',start:sd+(st?' '+st:'')};
            else if(t==='range')val={type:'range',start:sd,end:ed};
            else val={type:'datetimerange',start:sd+(st?' '+st:''),end:ed+(et?' '+et:'')};
            callback(val);closePicker(picker);
          };
          bindPickerAutoClose(picker);
        }

        function mkStarsHtml(level){
          let html='';
          for(let i=1;i<=5;i++){
            const on=i<=level;
            html+='<span style="color:'+(on?'var(--icon)':'transparent')+';-webkit-text-stroke:1px var(--star-edge);font-size:.95em">鈽?/span>';
          }
          return html;
        }
        function isStarField(name,def){
          return def.type==='number'&&/鏄焲star/i.test(name);
        }
        function dbBoardCardHtml(n){
          
          const parts=[];
          const titleName=dbPrimaryField(); 
          if(dbFieldVisible(titleName)){
            parts.push(`<div>${esc(stripHtml(n.text)||'(绌?')}</div>`);
          }
          (state.dbFields||[]).forEach(f=>{
            const def=dbFieldDef(f);
            const name=def.name;
            if(name===titleName)return;
            if(!dbFieldVisible(name))return;
            if(def.type==='formula'||def.type==='link'||def.type==='multi_select')return;
            
            if(isStarField(name,def)){
              const st=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,parseInt((n.db&&n.db[name])||0)||0));
              parts.push(`<div class="meta"><span style="display:inline-flex;gap:1px">${mkStarsHtml(st)}</span></div>`);
              return;
            }
            const v=dbTextValue(n,f);
            if(v)parts.push(`<div class="meta">${esc(v)}</div>`);
          });
          return parts.join('')||'<div>(鏃犳樉绀哄唴瀹?</div>';
        }
        
        function dbInferSelectField(rows,excludeSet){
          const rowArr=rows||[];
          return (state.dbFields||[]).find(f=>{
            const def=dbFieldDef(f);
            const nm=def.name;
            if(excludeSet&&excludeSet.has(nm))return false;
            if(!dbFieldVisible(nm))return false;
            if(def.type==='select'||def.type==='multi_select')return true;
            if(def.type!=='text')return false;
            const vals=new Set();
            rowArr.forEach(n=>{const v=n.db&&n.db[nm];if(v!=null&&v!=='')vals.add(String(v));});
            return vals.size>=2&&vals.size<=12;
          });
        }
        
        function dbBoardGroupDef(rows){
          const byName=dbResolveField('鐘舵€?,null);
          if(byName&&dbFieldVisible(dbFieldName(byName)))return byName;
          const t=dbPrimaryField(); 
          return dbInferSelectField(rows,new Set([t,'浼樺厛绾?]))||null;
        }
        function renderDbBoard(rows){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');
          wrap.className='db-board';
          
          const groupDef=dbBoardGroupDef(rows);
          const groupName=groupDef?dbFieldName(groupDef):null;
          const groupVisible=groupName?dbFieldVisible(groupName):false;
          
          if(!groupVisible){
            const c=document.createElement('div');
            c.className='db-board-col';
            c.dataset.status='';
            c.innerHTML='<h4>鈼?鍏ㄩ儴</h4>';
            rows.forEach(n=>{
              const card=document.createElement('div');
              card.className='db-board-card';
              card.dataset.id=n.id;
              card.innerHTML=dbBoardCardHtml(n);
              card.onclick=()=>openNode(n.id);
              c.appendChild(card);
            });
            wrap.appendChild(c);
            return wrap;
          }
          const statuses=new Set(['杩涜','寰呭姙','瀹屾垚','鏀惧純']);
          rows.forEach(n=>{const s=n.db&&n.db[groupName];if(s)statuses.add(s);});
          const cols=[...statuses];
          const hasUnset=rows.some(n=>!(n.db&&n.db[groupName]));
          if(hasUnset)cols.unshift('鏈缃?);
          cols.forEach(col=>{
            const c=document.createElement('div');
            c.className='db-board-col';
            c.dataset.status=col;
            c.dataset.field=groupName; 
            c.innerHTML=`<h4>鈼?${esc(col)}</h4>`;
            c.ondragover=e=>{e.preventDefault();c.style.background='var(--accent-soft)';};
            c.ondragleave=()=>{c.style.background='';};
            c.ondrop=e=>{
              e.preventDefault();c.style.background='';
              const id=e.dataTransfer.getData('text/plain');
              if(id){
                actions.setDbCell(id,groupName,col==='鏈缃??'':col);
                forceRender();
              }
            };
            rows.filter(n=>{
              const s=n.db&&n.db[groupName];
              if(col==='鏈缃?)return !s;
              return s===col;
            }).forEach(n=>{
              const card=document.createElement('div');
              card.className='db-board-card';
              card.draggable=true;
              card.dataset.id=n.id;
              card.ondragstart=e=>{e.dataTransfer.setData('text/plain',n.id);};
              card.ondragend=()=>{
                card._suppressClick=true;
                setTimeout(()=>{card._suppressClick=false;},50);
              };
              card.innerHTML=dbBoardCardHtml(n);
              card.onclick=()=>{
                if(card._suppressClick)return;
                openNode(n.id);
              };
              
              (function(){
                let touchTimer=null,touchDragging=false,activePointerId=null,touchStartX=0,touchStartY=0;
                card.addEventListener('pointerdown',e=>{
                  if(e.pointerType==='mouse')return;
                  card._suppressClick=false;
                  activePointerId=e.pointerId;
                  touchStartX=e.clientX;touchStartY=e.clientY;
                  try{card.setPointerCapture(activePointerId);}catch(err){}
                  touchTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
                    touchDragging=true;
                    card.style.opacity='.5';
                  },250);
                });
                card.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
                  if(!touchDragging){
                    if(touchTimer&&Math.hypot(e.clientX-touchStartX,e.clientY-touchStartY)>8){
                      clearTimeout(touchTimer);touchTimer=null;
                      touchDragging=true;
                      card.style.opacity='.5';
                    }
                    if(!touchDragging)return;
                  }
                  e.preventDefault();
                  document.querySelectorAll('.db-board-col').forEach(cc=>cc.style.background='');
                  const el=document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX,e.clientY);
                  const colEl=el&&el.closest('.db-board-col');
                  if(colEl)colEl.style.background='var(--accent-soft)';
                });
                const end=e=>{
                  clearTimeout(touchTimer);
                  try{card.releasePointerCapture(activePointerId);}catch(err){}
                  activePointerId=null;
                  if(!touchDragging)return;
                  touchDragging=false;
                  card._suppressClick=true;
                  card.style.opacity='';
                  const el=document.elementFromPoint(e.clientX,e.clientY);
                  const colEl=el&&el.closest('.db-board-col');
                  document.querySelectorAll('.db-board-col').forEach(cc=>cc.style.background='');
                  if(colEl){
                    const ns=colEl.dataset.status==='鏈缃??'':colEl.dataset.status;
                    actions.setDbCell(n.id,groupName,ns);
                    forceRender();
                  }
                };
                card.addEventListener('pointerup',end);
                card.addEventListener('pointercancel',end);
              })();
              c.appendChild(card);
            });
            wrap.appendChild(c);
          });
          return wrap;
        }
        function renderDbGallery(rows){
          if(__l14On()&&rows&&rows.length>VIRTUAL_THRESHOLD){try{return __renderDbGalleryVirtual(rows);}catch(e){}} 
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');
          wrap.className='db-gallery';
          
          const titleName=dbPrimaryField(); 
          rows.forEach(n=>{
            const card=document.createElement('div');
            card.className='db-gallery-card';
            const html=[];
            if(dbFieldVisible(titleName)){
              html.push('<div class="title">'+esc(stripHtml(n.text)||'(绌?')+'</div>');
            }
            (state.dbFields||[]).forEach(f=>{
              const def=dbFieldDef(f);
              const name=def.name;
              if(name===titleName||!dbFieldVisible(name))return;
              if(def.type==='formula'||def.type==='link'||def.type==='multi_select')return;
              
              if(isStarField(name,def)){
                const st=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,parseInt((n.db&&n.db[name])||0)||0));
                html.push('<div class="meta"><span style="display:inline-flex;gap:1px">'+mkStarsHtml(st)+'</span></div>');
              }else if(def.type==='number'){
                const v=dbTextValue(n,f);
                if(v)html.push('<div class="meta">'+esc(name+'锛?+v)+'</div>');
              }else{
                const v=dbTextValue(n,f);
                if(v)html.push('<div class="meta"><span>'+esc(v)+'</span></div>');
              }
            });
            card.innerHTML=html.join('')||'<div class="title">(鏃犳樉绀哄唴瀹?</div>';
            card.onclick=()=>openNode(n.id);
            wrap.appendChild(card);
          });
          return wrap;
        }
        function renderDbTimelineList(rows,mapRow,emptyMsg){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');
          wrap.className='db-timeline';
          if(!rows.length){wrap.innerHTML=`<div class="db-empty">${emptyMsg}</div>`;return wrap;}
          rows.forEach(n=>{

            item.className='db-tl-item';

            item.onclick=()=>openNode(n.id);
            wrap.appendChild(item);
          });
          return wrap;
        }
        function dbUserSortActive(){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();
          return !!(doc&&ui.dbSortsMap&&ui.dbSortsMap[doc.id]&&ui.dbSortsMap[doc.id].length);
        }
        function renderDbTimeline(rows){
          
          const dateDef=dbResolveField('鏃堕棿',['date']);
          const dateName=dateDef?dbFieldName(dateDef):null;
          const dateVisible=dateName?dbFieldVisible(dateName):false;
          const sorted=dbUserSortActive()
            ?rows
            :rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
              const ta=(dateVisible&&a.db&&a.db[dateName])?String(formatTimeValue(a.db[dateName])):'';
              const tb=(dateVisible&&b.db&&b.db[dateName])?String(formatTimeValue(b.db[dateName])):'';
              if(ta&&tb)return ta.localeCompare(tb);
              if(ta)return -1;
              if(tb)return 1;
              return (b.updatedAt||0)-(a.updatedAt||0);
            });
          return renderDbTimelineList(sorted,n=>{
            const hasTime=dateVisible&&!!(n.db&&n.db[dateName]);
            const dateLabel=hasTime
              ?String(formatTimeValue(n.db[dateName])).split(' ')[0]||''
              :'';
            const parts=[];
            const titleName=dbPrimaryField(); 
            if(dbFieldVisible(titleName)){
              parts.push(`<div class="t">${esc(stripHtml(n.text)||'(绌?')}</div>`);
            }
            const subParts=[];
            if(hasTime){
              
              (state.dbFields||[]).forEach(f=>{
                const def=dbFieldDef(f);
                const name=def.name;
                if(name===titleName||name===dateName)return;
                if(!dbFieldVisible(name))return;
                if(def.type==='formula'||def.type==='link'||def.type==='multi_select')return;
                if(isStarField(name,def)){
                  const st=Math.max(0,Math.min(5,parseInt((n.db&&n.db[name])||0)||0));
                  subParts.push('<span style="display:inline-flex;gap:1px">'+mkStarsHtml(st)+'</span>');
                  return;
                }
                const v=dbTextValue(n,f);
                if(v)subParts.push(esc(v));
              });
            }else{
              subParts.push('鍔ㄦ€佹洿鏂?);
            }
            const subHtml=subParts.length?`<div class="d">${subParts.join(' ')}</div>`:'';
            return `<div class="db-tl-date">${dateLabel}</div><div class="db-tl-content">${parts.join('')}${subHtml}</div>`;
          },'鏆傛棤鏃堕棿绾?鍔ㄦ€佹暟鎹?);
        }
        function renderDbChart(rows){

        const MM_NW=170,MM_NH=30,MM_GX=220,MM_GY=38;
        
        function mmLayoutConsts(){
          const k=Math.max(0.5,Math.min(2.5,currentBaseFontPx()/14));
          return{NW:Math.round(MM_NW*k),NH:Math.round(MM_NH*k),GX:Math.round(MM_GX*k),GY:Math.round(MM_GY*k)};
        }
        
        function mmNodeText(n){
          return stripHtml(n&&n.text||'').trim() || (n&&n.id===state.rootId?'鏂囨。':'');
        }
        
        function mmNodeHeight(n){
          const{NH}=mmLayoutConsts();
          const text=mmNodeText(n);
          const charsPerLine=16;
          const lines=Math.max(1,Math.ceil(text.length/charsPerLine));
          const lineH=Math.round(18*Math.max(0.8,currentBaseFontPx()/14));
          const padV=10;
          return Math.max(NH,lines*lineH+padV);
        }
        function layoutMindmap(docId){
          const{NW,NH,GX,GY}=mmLayoutConsts();
          const doc=N(docId);
          if(!doc)return{nodes:[],width:0,height:0,NW,NH};
          let cursorY=0;
          function layout(id,depth){
            const n=N(id);if(!n)return null;
            const x=depth*GX;
            const h=mmNodeHeight(n);
            const kidsRaw=n.collapsed?[]:n.children;
            const kids=kidsRaw.map(k=>N(k)).filter(cn=>cn&&!isBlankNode(cn));
            if(!kids.length){
              const y=cursorY+h/2;
              cursorY+=h+GY;
              return{id,x,y,h,kids:[]};
            }
            const sub=kids.map(k=>layout(k.id,depth+1)).filter(Boolean);
            if(!sub.length){
              const y=cursorY+h/2;
              cursorY+=h+GY;
              return{id,x,y,h,kids:[]};
            }
            const y=(sub[0].y+sub[sub.length-1].y)/2;
            return{id,x,y,h,kids:sub};
          }
          const trees=doc.children.map(cid=>layout(cid,1)).filter(Boolean);
          const rootNode={id:doc.id,x:0,y:trees.length?(trees[0].y+trees[trees.length-1].y)/2:0,h:mmNodeHeight(doc),kids:trees};
          const all=[];
          (function c(nd){all.push(nd);nd.kids.forEach(c);})(rootNode);
          const minX=all.reduce((a,n)=>Math.min(a,n.x),0);
          const maxX=all.reduce((a,n)=>Math.max(a,n.x),0)+NW;
          const maxY=all.reduce((a,n)=>Math.max(a,n.y+n.h/2),0);
          return{nodes:all,width:maxX-minX+80,height:maxY+80,NW,NH,offsetX:40-minX,offsetY:40,rootNode};
        }
        function renderMindmap(doc){
          if(__l14On()&&doc&&countVisibleNodes(doc)>VIRTUAL_THRESHOLD){try{return __renderMindmapVirtual(doc);}catch(e){}} 
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='mm-wrap';
          if(doc)wrap.appendChild(renderDocHeaderInline(doc));

          if(!doc){wrap.innerHTML='<div class="empty-state">璇峰厛閫夋嫨鏂囨。</div>';return wrap;}
          const layout=layoutMindmap(doc.id);
          const{nodes,width,height,NW,NH,offsetX,offsetY,rootNode}=layout;
          canvas.style.width=width+'px';canvas.style.height=height+'px';
          canvas.style.transformOrigin='0 0';
          let _mmScale=Math.max(0.4,Math.min(2.5,ui.mmScale||1));
          canvas.dataset.scale=_mmScale;
          canvas.style.transform='scale('+_mmScale+')';
          spacer.style.cssText='position:relative;width:'+(width*_mmScale)+'px;height:'+(height*_mmScale)+'px;';
          const NS='http:
          const svg=document.createElementNS(NS,'svg');
          svg.setAttribute('width',width);svg.setAttribute('height',height);
          const line=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--line').trim();
          
          function _pos(nd){
            const n=N(nd.id);
            
            const x=(n&&typeof n.mmX==='number')?n.mmX:(nd.x+offsetX);
            const y=(n&&typeof n.mmY==='number')?n.mmY:(nd.y+offsetY);
            return [x,y];
          }
          function edges(nd){
            nd.kids.forEach(k=>{
              const[p1x,p1y]=_pos(nd),[p2x,p2y]=_pos(k),mx=(p1x+p2x)/2;
              const path=document.createElementNS(NS,'path');
              path.setAttribute('d',`M${p1x},${p1y} C${mx},${p1y} ${mx},${p2y} ${p2x},${p2y}`);
              path.setAttribute('fill','none');path.setAttribute('stroke',line);path.setAttribute('stroke-width','1.5');
              svg.appendChild(path);edges(k);
            });
          }
          edges(rootNode);canvas.appendChild(svg);
          nodes.forEach(nd=>{
            const n=N(nd.id);
            
            if(nd.id!==doc.id){
              const txt=n?stripHtml(n.text||'').trim():'';
              if(!txt)return;
            }
            const d=document.createElement('div');
            let cls='mm-node';
            if(nd.id===doc.id)cls+=' root';
            else if(nd.x===MM_GX)cls+=' lv1';
            if(n&&n.done)cls+=' done';
            d.className=cls;
            
            const _px=(n&&typeof n.mmX==='number')?n.mmX:(nd.x+offsetX);
            const _py=(n&&typeof n.mmY==='number')?n.mmY:(nd.y+offsetY);
            d.style.left=_px+'px';d.style.top=_py+'px';
            
            const _h=nd.h||NH;
            d.style.minHeight=_h+'px';
            d.style.height='auto';
            d.style.marginTop=(-_h/2)+'px';
            d.style.whiteSpace='normal';
            d.style.wordBreak='break-word';
            d.dataset.autoX=nd.x+offsetX;d.dataset.autoY=nd.y+offsetY;
            if(nd.id===doc.id)d.textContent=stripHtml(doc.text)||'鏂囨。';
            else d.textContent=n?stripHtml(n.text||''):'';
            if(nd.id!==doc.id){
              d.style.cursor='text';
              d.onclick=e=>{
                e.stopPropagation();
                
                if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&!d.classList.contains('editing')){
                  const lt=linkTitleAtPoint(d,e);
                  if(lt){e.preventDefault();navigateToWikiLink(lt,d);return;}
                }
                if(d.classList.contains('editing'))return;
                d.classList.add('editing');d.contentEditable='true';d.focus();
                const range=document.createRange();range.selectNodeContents(d);
                const sel=window.getSelection();sel.removeAllRanges();sel.addRange(range);
              };
              d.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
                d.classList.remove('editing');d.contentEditable='false';
                d.style.cursor='text';
                const target=N(nd.id);
                if(target){const newText=d.textContent;if(newText!==stripHtml(target.text||'')){commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[nd.id];if(!nn)return;nn.text=newText;nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true});}}
              });
              d.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
                if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();d.blur();}
                if(e.key==='Escape'){d.textContent=stripHtml(N(nd.id).text||'');d.blur();}
                e.stopPropagation();
              });
              
              d.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
                if(d.classList.contains('editing')){e.stopPropagation();return;}
                e.stopPropagation();
                const startX=e.clientX,startY=e.clientY;
                const origLeft=parseFloat(d.style.left),origTop=parseFloat(d.style.top);
                let dragged=false;
                const onMM=ev=>{
                  if(!dragged){
                    if(Math.abs(ev.clientX-startX)<5&&Math.abs(ev.clientY-startY)<5)return;
                    dragged=true;d.style.cursor='grabbing';
                  }
                  ev.preventDefault();
                  const dx=(ev.clientX-startX)/_mmScale,dy=(ev.clientY-startY)/_mmScale;
                  d.style.left=(origLeft+dx)+'px';
                  d.style.top=(origTop+dy)+'px';
                };
                const onMU=()=>{
                  document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onMM);
                  document.removeEventListener('mouseup',onMU);
                  if(!dragged)return;
                  d.style.cursor='text';
                  
                  const nx=parseFloat(d.style.left);
                  const ny=parseFloat(d.style.top);
                  
                  commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[nd.id];if(nn){nn.mmX=Math.round(nx);nn.mmY=Math.round(ny);nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();}},{snapshot:false,persistOnly:true});
                };
                document.addEventListener('mousemove',onMM);
                document.addEventListener('mouseup',onMU);
              });
            }else d.style.cursor='default';
            canvas.appendChild(d);
          });
          let dragging=false,sx=0,sy=0,sl=0,st=0;
          const onMove=e=>{if(!dragging)return;wrap.scrollLeft=sl-(e.clientX-sx);wrap.scrollTop=st-(e.clientY-sy);};
          const onUp=()=>{if(!dragging)return;dragging=false;wrap.style.cursor='grab';document.body.style.userSelect='';document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onMove);document.removeEventListener('mouseup',onUp);};
          wrap.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{if(e.target.closest('.mm-node')||e.target.closest('.doc-header'))return;dragging=true;sx=e.clientX;sy=e.clientY;sl=wrap.scrollLeft;st=wrap.scrollTop;wrap.style.cursor='grabbing';document.body.style.userSelect='none';document.addEventListener('mousemove',onMove);document.addEventListener('mouseup',onUp);}); 
          wrap.addEventListener('wheel',e=>{
            if(!e.ctrlKey&&!e.metaKey)return;
            e.preventDefault();
            const scale=_mmScale;
            const ns=Math.max(0.4,Math.min(2.5,scale*(e.deltaY<0?1.1:0.9)));
            const rect=wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
            const mx=e.clientX-rect.left,my=e.clientY-rect.top;
            const cx=(wrap.scrollLeft+mx)/scale;
            const cy=(wrap.scrollTop+my)/scale;
            _mmScale=ns;
            ui.mmScale=ns;
            canvas.dataset.scale=ns;
            canvas.style.transform='scale('+ns+')';
            spacer.style.width=(width*ns)+'px';
            spacer.style.height=(height*ns)+'px';
            wrap.scrollLeft=cx*ns-mx;
            wrap.scrollTop=cy*ns-my;
            saveUI();
          },{passive:false});
          
          const resetBtn=document.createElement('button');
          resetBtn.className='mm-reset-btn';resetBtn.textContent='閲嶇疆甯冨眬';
          resetBtn.onclick=()=>{
            commit(s=>{for(const id in s.nodes){const n=s.nodes[id];if(n){delete n.mmX;delete n.mmY;}}},{snapshot:true});
            forceRender();
          };
          wrap.appendChild(resetBtn);
          requestAnimationFrame(()=>{wrap.scrollLeft=(width*_mmScale-wrap.clientWidth)/2;wrap.scrollTop=(height*_mmScale-wrap.clientHeight)/2;});
          return wrap;
        }
