        <script>
        "use strict";
        
        const GRAPH_WORKER_SRC = `
        'use strict';
        let S = null;
        let _running = false;
        let _paused = false;
        let _resumeResolver = null;
        let _yieldChannel = null;
        let _yieldQueue = [];
        function setupYieldChannel() {
          if (_yieldChannel) return;
          _yieldChannel = new MessageChannel();
          _yieldChannel.port1.onmessage = () => {
            const cb = _yieldQueue.shift();
            if (cb) cb();
          };
          try { _yieldChannel.port1.start(); } catch (e) {}
        }
        function yieldNow() {
          setupYieldChannel();
          return new Promise((resolve) => {
            _yieldQueue.push(resolve);
            _yieldChannel.port2.postMessage(null);
          });
        }
        function waitForResume() {
          return new Promise((resolve) => { _resumeResolver = resolve; });
        }
        function doResume() {
          _paused = false;
          if (_resumeResolver) { const r = _resumeResolver; _resumeResolver = null; r(); }
        }
        self.onmessage = (e) => {
          const msg = e.data || {};
          switch (msg.type) {
            case 'init':         return handleInit(msg);
            case 'updatePinned': return handleUpdatePinned(msg);
            case 'cancel':       return handleCancel();
            case 'pause':        return (_paused = true);
            case 'resume':       doResume(); startIfNeeded(); return;
            case 'dispose':      S = null; _running = false; return;
          }
        };
        function startIfNeeded() {
          if (!S || _running) return;
          _running = true;
          const gen = S.currentClientGen;
          run(gen).then((positions) => {
            _running = false;
            if (!S) return;
            const changed = S.currentClientGen !== gen;
            self.postMessage({ type: 'done', clientGen: gen, positions }, [positions.buffer]);
            if (changed) startIfNeeded();
          }).catch((err) => {
            _running = false;
            console.warn('[worker] run failed', err);
          });
        }
        async function run(myGen) {
          const maxIter = S.maxIter;
          let lastYield = performance.now();
          for (let iter = 0; iter < maxIter; iter++) {
            if (_paused) await waitForResume();
            if (myGen !== S.currentClientGen) return S.positions.slice();
            computeForces();
            integrate();
            resolveCollisions(); 
            const now = performance.now();
            if (now - lastYield > 8) { await yieldNow(); lastYield = performance.now(); }
            if (iter > 10 && totalKinetic() < 1e-3) break;
          }
          return S.positions.slice();
        }
        function computeForces() {
          const n = S.n, pos = S.positions, vel = S.velocities;
          for (let i = 0; i < vel.length; i++) vel[i] = 0;
          const tree = buildQuadTree();
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            const x = pos[i * 2], y = pos[i * 2 + 1];
            const f = bhForce(tree, x, y, S.theta, S.eps2, i);
            vel[i * 2] += f.fx; vel[i * 2 + 1] += f.fy;
          }
          const edges = S.edges;
          for (let e = 0; e < edges.length; e += 2) {
            const a = edges[e], b = edges[e + 1];
            const ax = pos[a * 2], ay = pos[a * 2 + 1];
            const bx = pos[b * 2], by = pos[b * 2 + 1];
            const dx = bx - ax, dy = by - ay;
            const d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
            const f = (d - S.springLen) * S.springK;
            const fx = (dx / d) * f, fy = (dy / d) * f;
            vel[a * 2] += fx; vel[a * 2 + 1] += fy;
            vel[b * 2] -= fx; vel[b * 2 + 1] -= fy;
          }
          const cx = S.W / 2, cy = S.H / 2;
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (S.pinned[i]) continue;
            vel[i * 2] += (cx - pos[i * 2]) * S.centerK;
            vel[i * 2 + 1] += (cy - pos[i * 2 + 1]) * S.centerK;
          }
        }
        function integrate() {
          const n = S.n, pos = S.positions, vel = S.velocities;
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            if (S.pinned[i]) {
              pos[i * 2] = S.pinnedPos[i * 2];
              pos[i * 2 + 1] = S.pinnedPos[i * 2 + 1];
              vel[i * 2] = 0; vel[i * 2 + 1] = 0;
              continue;
            }
            vel[i * 2] *= 0.85; vel[i * 2 + 1] *= 0.85;
            pos[i * 2] += vel[i * 2];
            pos[i * 2 + 1] += vel[i * 2 + 1];
            if (pos[i * 2] < 40) pos[i * 2] = 40;
            if (pos[i * 2] > S.W - 40) pos[i * 2] = S.W - 40;
            if (pos[i * 2 + 1] < 40) pos[i * 2 + 1] = 40;
            if (pos[i * 2 + 1] > S.H - 40) pos[i * 2 + 1] = S.H - 40;
          }
        }
        
        function resolveCollisions() {
          const pos = S.positions, n = S.n, radii = S.radii;
          if (!radii || !radii.length) return;
          const pad = 18;
          for (let iter = 0; iter < 3; iter++) {
            for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
              for (let j = i + 1; j < n; j++) {
                let dx = pos[j * 2] - pos[i * 2];
                let dy = pos[j * 2 + 1] - pos[i * 2 + 1];
                let d = Math.sqrt(dx * dx + dy * dy) || 1;
                const min = radii[i] + radii[j] + pad;
                if (d < min) {
                  const push = (min - d) / 2;
                  const ux = dx / d, uy = dy / d;
                  if (!S.pinned[i]) { pos[i * 2] -= ux * push; pos[i * 2 + 1] -= uy * push; }
                  if (!S.pinned[j]) { pos[j * 2] += ux * push; pos[j * 2 + 1] += uy * push; }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
        function totalKinetic() {
          let sum = 0;
          for (let i = 0; i < S.velocities.length; i++) sum += S.velocities[i] * S.velocities[i];
          return sum;
        }
        function buildQuadTree() {
          const n = S.n, pos = S.positions;
          let minX = Infinity, minY = Infinity, maxX = -Infinity, maxY = -Infinity;
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            const x = pos[i * 2], y = pos[i * 2 + 1];
            if (x < minX) minX = x; if (x > maxX) maxX = x;
            if (y < minY) minY = y; if (y > maxY) maxY = y;
          }
          const size = Math.max(maxX - minX, maxY - minY, 1) * 1.1;
          const cx = (minX + maxX) / 2, cy = (minY + maxY) / 2;
          const nodes = [];
          function createNode(cx, cy, size) {
            nodes.push({ cx, cy, size, mass: 0, gx: 0, gy: 0, body: -1, children: null });
            return nodes.length - 1;
          }
          const root = createNode(cx, cy, size);
          const MAX_DEPTH = 20;
          function insert(nodeIdx, bodyIdx, depth) {
            if (depth > MAX_DEPTH) {
              const node = nodes[nodeIdx];
              if (node.body === -1) { node.body = bodyIdx; }
              return;
            }
            const node = nodes[nodeIdx];
            const m = node.mass;
            node.mass += 1;
            node.gx = (node.gx * m + pos[bodyIdx * 2]) / node.mass;
            node.gy = (node.gy * m + pos[bodyIdx * 2 + 1]) / node.mass;
            if (node.body === -1 && node.children === null) { node.body = bodyIdx; return; }
            if (node.children === null) {
              const half = node.size / 2;
              const q = node.size / 4;
              node.children = [
                createNode(node.cx - q, node.cy - q, half),
                createNode(node.cx + q, node.cy - q, half),
                createNode(node.cx - q, node.cy + q, half),
                createNode(node.cx + q, node.cy + q, half),
              ];
              const old = node.body;
              node.body = -1;
              insert(quadOf(node, old), old, depth + 1);
            }
            insert(quadOf(node, bodyIdx), bodyIdx, depth + 1);
          }
          function quadOf(node, bodyIdx) {
            const x = pos[bodyIdx * 2], y = pos[bodyIdx * 2 + 1];
            const right = x >= node.cx ? 1 : 0;
            const bottom = y >= node.cy ? 2 : 0;
            return node.children[right + bottom];
          }
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) insert(root, i, 0);
          return { nodes, root };
        }
        function bhForce(tree, x, y, theta, eps2, selfIdx) {
          let fx = 0, fy = 0;
          const stack = [tree.root];
          const nodes = tree.nodes;
          while (stack.length) {
            const node = nodes[stack.pop()];
            if (node.mass === 0) continue;
            const dx = x - node.gx, dy = y - node.gy;
            const d2 = dx * dx + dy * dy;
            if (d2 < eps2) {
              fx += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
              fy += (Math.random() - 0.5) * 0.5;
              continue;
            }
            if (node.body !== -1) {
              if (node.body === selfIdx) continue;
              const f = S.repulsion / d2;
              const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
              fx += f * dx / d; fy += f * dy / d;
              continue;
            }
            if (node.size / Math.sqrt(d2) < theta) {
              const f = S.repulsion * node.mass / d2;
              const d = Math.sqrt(d2);
              fx += f * dx / d; fy += f * dy / d;
              continue;
            }
            for (let i = 0; i < node.children.length; i++) stack.push(node.children[i]);
          }
          return { fx, fy };
        }
        function handleInit(msg) {
          S = {
            n: msg.n, W: msg.W, H: msg.H,
            positions: new Float32Array(msg.positions),
            velocities: new Float32Array(msg.n * 2),
            edges: new Uint32Array(msg.edges),
            pinned: new Uint8Array(msg.pinned),
            pinnedPos: new Float32Array(msg.pinnedPos),
            theta: 0.7, repulsion: 20000, eps2: 100, 
            springLen: 180, springK: 0.015, centerK: 0.008, 
            maxIter: msg.iters || 80,
            radii: new Float32Array(msg.radii || []), 
            currentClientGen: msg.clientGen,
          };
          startIfNeeded();
        }
        function handleUpdatePinned(msg) {
          if (!S) return;
          if (msg.clientGen >= S.currentClientGen) S.currentClientGen = msg.clientGen;
          const n = S.n;
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            S.pinned[i] = msg.pinned[i];
            if (msg.pinned[i]) {
              const x = msg.pinnedPos[i * 2];
              const y = msg.pinnedPos[i * 2 + 1];
              S.pinnedPos[i * 2] = x; S.pinnedPos[i * 2 + 1] = y;
              S.positions[i * 2] = x; S.positions[i * 2 + 1] = y;
              S.velocities[i * 2] = 0; S.velocities[i * 2 + 1] = 0;
            }
          }
          if (msg.iters) S.maxIter = msg.iters;
          startIfNeeded();
        }
        function handleCancel() { if (S) S.currentClientGen = -1; }
        `;
        let _graphWorkerUrl=null;
        function getGraphWorkerUrl(){
          if(_graphWorkerUrl)return _graphWorkerUrl;
          const blob=new Blob([GRAPH_WORKER_SRC],{type:'application/javascript'});
          _graphWorkerUrl=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          return _graphWorkerUrl;
        }
        class GraphLayoutWorker{
          constructor(){
            this.worker=null;
            this.url=null;
            this.ready=false;
            this.clientGen=0;
            this.pending=null;
            this.timeoutStrikes=0; 
            this._init();
          }
          _init(){
            try{
              if(!window.Worker)throw new Error('no Worker');
              this.url=getGraphWorkerUrl();
              this.worker=new Worker(this.url);
              this.worker.onmessage=(e)=>this._onMessage(e.data);
              this.worker.onerror=(e)=>{
                console.warn('[graph-worker] error',e.message||e);
                this.ready=false;
                this._rejectPending(new Error('worker error'));
              };
              this.ready=true;
            }catch(e){
              console.warn('[graph-worker] init failed',e);
              this.ready=false;
            }
          }
          _onMessage(msg){
            if(!msg||msg.type!=='done')return;
            if(msg.clientGen!==this.clientGen)return;
            if(!this.pending)return;
            const p=this.pending;
            this.pending=null;
            clearTimeout(p.timer);
            this.timeoutStrikes=0; 
            p.resolve(msg.positions);
          }
          _rejectPending(err){
            if(!this.pending)return;
            const p=this.pending;
            this.pending=null;
            clearTimeout(p.timer);
            p.reject(err);
          }
          
          _timeoutHit(gen){
            if(!this.pending||this.pending.gen!==gen)return;
            this.timeoutStrikes++;
            if(this.timeoutStrikes>=2){
              console.warn('[graph-worker] 杩炵画瓒呮椂锛宼erminate 閲嶅缓');
              this._rejectPending(new Error('timeout'));
              if(this.worker){try{this.worker.terminate();}catch(e){}this.worker=null;}
              if(this.url){try{URL.revokeObjectURL(this.url);}catch(e){}this.url=null;}
              this.ready=false;
            }else{
              this._rejectPending(new Error('timeout'));
            }
          }
          _send(payload,transfer){
            try{this.worker.postMessage(payload,transfer||[]);}catch(e){console.warn('[JU] graph worker postMessage failed',e);} 
          }
          initLayout({n,positions,edges,pinned,pinnedPos,W,H,radii,iters}){
            if(!this.ready)return Promise.reject(new Error('not ready'));
            if(this.pending)this._rejectPending(new Error('superseded'));
            this.clientGen++;
            const gen=this.clientGen;
            const posCopy=new Float32Array(positions);
            const edgesCopy=new Uint32Array(edges);
            const pinnedCopy=new Uint8Array(pinned);
            const pinnedPosCopy=new Float32Array(pinnedPos);
            const radiiCopy=radii?new Float32Array(radii):new Float32Array(0); 
            this._send({
              type:'init',clientGen:gen,n,W,H,iters,
              positions:posCopy.buffer,edges:edgesCopy.buffer,
              pinned:pinnedCopy.buffer,pinnedPos:pinnedPosCopy.buffer,
              radii:radiiCopy.buffer,
            },[posCopy.buffer,edgesCopy.buffer,pinnedCopy.buffer,pinnedPosCopy.buffer,radiiCopy.buffer]);
            return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
              const timer=setTimeout(()=>this._timeoutHit(gen),8000); 
              this.pending={resolve,reject,gen,timer};
            });
          }
          updatePinned({pinned,pinnedPos,iters=20}){
            if(!this.ready)return null;
            if(this.pending)this._rejectPending(new Error('superseded'));
            this.clientGen++;
            const gen=this.clientGen;
            const pinnedCopy=new Uint8Array(pinned);
            const pinnedPosCopy=new Float32Array(pinnedPos);
            this._send({
              type:'updatePinned',clientGen:gen,iters,
              pinned:pinnedCopy.buffer,pinnedPos:pinnedPosCopy.buffer,
            },[pinnedCopy.buffer,pinnedPosCopy.buffer]);
            return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
              const timer=setTimeout(()=>this._timeoutHit(gen),4000); 
              this.pending={resolve,reject,gen,timer};
            });
          }
          cancel(){
            if(this.ready){this.clientGen++;this._send({type:'cancel'});}
            this._rejectPending(new Error('cancelled'));
          }
          pause(){if(this.ready)this._send({type:'pause'});}
          resume(){if(this.ready)this._send({type:'resume'});}
          dispose(){
            if(this.worker){
              try{this._send({type:'dispose'});}catch(e){}
              setTimeout(()=>{
                try{this.worker.terminate();}catch(e){}
                this.worker=null;
              },0);
            }
            if(this.url){URL.revokeObjectURL(this.url);this.url=null;}
            this.ready=false;
            this._rejectPending(new Error('disposed'));
          }
        }
        let _graphWorker=null;
        function getGraphWorker(){
          if(!_graphWorker)_graphWorker=new GraphLayoutWorker();
          return _graphWorker;
        }
        class GraphRenderer{
          constructor(wrap,docs,nodeEls){
            this.wrap=wrap;this.docs=docs;this.nodeEls=nodeEls;this.n=docs.length;
            this.lastX=new Float32Array(this.n).fill(NaN);
            this.lastY=new Float32Array(this.n).fill(NaN);
            this.current=null;this.dirty=false;this.rafId=null;
            this.draggingIds=new Set();
            this.visible={minX:0,maxX:0,minY:0,maxY:0};
            this._visibleValid=false;
          }
          setPositions(buf){
            this.current=new Float32Array(buf);
            this.dirty=true;
            if(!this.rafId)this.rafId=requestAnimationFrame(()=>this.flush());
          }
          setDraggingPosition(index,x,y){
            this.lastX[index]=x;this.lastY[index]=y;
            const el=this.nodeEls.get(this.docs[index].id);
            if(el)el.style.transform='translate3d('+x.toFixed(1)+'px, '+y.toFixed(1)+'px, 0) translate(-50%, -50%)';
          }
          invalidateVisible(){this._visibleValid=false;}
          computeVisible(){
            if(this._visibleValid)return this.visible;
            const wrap=this.wrap;
            const sl=wrap.scrollLeft,st=wrap.scrollTop;
            const M=60;
            this.visible={minX:sl-M,maxX:sl+wrap.clientWidth+M,minY:st-M,maxY:st+wrap.clientHeight+M};
            this._visibleValid=true;
            return this.visible;
          }
          flush(){
            this.rafId=null;
            if(!this.dirty||!this.current)return;
            this.dirty=false;
            const n=this.n,cur=this.current,lastX=this.lastX,lastY=this.lastY;
            const vis=this.computeVisible();
            const dragging=this.draggingIds;
            for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
              const x=cur[i*2],y=cur[i*2+1];
              const id=this.docs[i].id;
              const isDragging=dragging.has(id);
              if(!isDragging&&(x<vis.minX||x>vis.maxX||y<vis.minY||y>vis.maxY))continue;
              const lx=lastX[i],ly=lastY[i];
              if(!isDragging&&Math.abs(x-lx)<0.5&&Math.abs(y-ly)<0.5)continue;
              lastX[i]=x;lastY[i]=y;
              const el=this.nodeEls.get(id);
              if(!el)continue;
              el.style.transform='translate3d('+x.toFixed(1)+'px, '+y.toFixed(1)+'px, 0) translate(-50%, -50%)';
            }
          }
          beginDrag(id){this.draggingIds.add(id);const el=this.nodeEls.get(id);if(el)el.classList.add('dragging');}
          endDrag(id){this.draggingIds.delete(id);const el=this.nodeEls.get(id);if(el)el.classList.remove('dragging');}
          dispose(){
            if(this.rafId){cancelAnimationFrame(this.rafId);this.rafId=null;}
            this.dirty=false;this.current=null;this.draggingIds.clear();
          }
        }
        function renderGraph(){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');
          wrap.className='graph-canvas';
          const gTitle=renderViewTitle('鐭ヨ瘑鍥捐氨');
          gTitle.style.cssText='position:sticky;top:0;left:0;right:0;z-index:12;background:var(--bg);margin:0;border-radius:0;max-width:none;padding:10px 14px 8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-soft)'; 
          wrap.appendChild(gTitle);
          const docs=[];
          for(const id in state.nodes){
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(id===state.rootId||isFolder(id)||isBlankNode(n)||n._deleted)continue;
            let p=n.parent,inCal=false;
            while(p&&p!==state.rootId){
              const pn=N(p);if(!pn)break;
              if(pn._isCalRoot){inCal=true;break;}
              p=pn.parent;
            }
            if(inCal)continue;
            docs.push(n);
          }
          if(!docs.length){
            wrap.innerHTML='<div class="empty-state">鏆傛棤鍙鍖栬妭鐐?/div>';
            return wrap;
          }
          const MAX_NODES=300;
          if(docs.length>MAX_NODES){
            docs.sort((a,b)=>(b.updatedAt||0)-(a.updatedAt||0)); 
            docs.length=MAX_NODES;
            const tip=document.createElement('div');
            tip.style.cssText='position:absolute;top:10px;left:10px;z-index:10;padding:4px 10px;border-radius:6px;background:var(--bg-panel);border:1px solid var(--line-soft);font-size:.78em;color:var(--fg-dim)';
            tip.textContent='浠呮樉绀烘渶杩戞洿鏂扮殑 '+MAX_NODES+' 涓妭鐐?;
            wrap.appendChild(tip);
          }
          const W=Math.max(800,window.innerWidth-400);
          const H=Math.max(500,window.innerHeight-200);
          const cx=W/2,cy=H/2,R=Math.min(W,H)*0.35;
          const n=docs.length;
          const positions=new Float32Array(n*2);
          docs.forEach((doc,i)=>{
            const a=(i/n)*Math.PI*2;
            positions[i*2]=cx+Math.cos(a)*R;
            positions[i*2+1]=cy+Math.sin(a)*R;
          });
          const idIndex=new Map(docs.map((d,i)=>[d.id,i]));
          const byTitle={};
          docs.forEach(d=>{const t=stripHtml(d.text||'').trim();if(!t)return;const up=d.updatedAt||0;const cur=byTitle[t];if(!cur||up>cur.up)byTitle[t]={id:d.id,up:up};}); 
          const edges=[];
          docs.forEach(d=>{
            const raw=stripHtml(d.text||'');
            const re=/\[\[([^\]]+)\]\]/g;let m;
            while((m=re.exec(raw))!==null){
              const hit=byTitle[m[1].trim()];const tid=hit?hit.id:null;
              if(tid&&tid!==d.id)edges.push({from:d.id,to:tid});
            }
          });
          const edgeArr=new Uint32Array(edges.length*2);
          edges.forEach((e,i)=>{
            edgeArr[i*2]=idIndex.get(e.from);
            edgeArr[i*2+1]=idIndex.get(e.to);
          });
          const pinned=new Uint8Array(n);
          const pinnedPos=new Float32Array(n*2);
          docs.forEach((doc,i)=>{
            const p=_graphPositions[doc.id];
            if(p){pinned[i]=1;pinnedPos[i*2]=p.x;pinnedPos[i*2+1]=p.y;}
          });
          
          const radii=new Float32Array(n);
          docs.forEach((doc,i)=>{
            const text=stripHtml(doc.text)||'鏈懡鍚?;
            const w=Math.min(180,Math.max(70,text.length*8+24));
            radii[i]=w/2+8;
          });
          const nodeEls=new Map();
          docs.forEach((doc,i)=>{
            const el=document.createElement('div');
            el.className='graph-node';
            el.dataset.id=doc.id;
            el.textContent=stripHtml(doc.text)||'鏈懡鍚?; 
            el.title=stripHtml(doc.text);
            const x=positions[i*2],y=positions[i*2+1];
            el.style.transform='translate3d('+x.toFixed(1)+'px, '+y.toFixed(1)+'px, 0) translate(-50%, -50%)';
            wrap.appendChild(el);
            nodeEls.set(doc.id,el);
          });
          const renderer=new GraphRenderer(wrap,docs,nodeEls);
          const NS='http:
          const svg=document.createElementNS(NS,'svg');
          svg.setAttribute('class','graph-svg');
          svg.setAttribute('width',W);
          svg.setAttribute('height',H);
          svg.style.cssText='position:absolute;top:0;left:0;pointer-events:none';
          const lineColor=getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--line').trim();
          edges.forEach(e=>{
            const ia=idIndex.get(e.from),ib=idIndex.get(e.to);
            const line=document.createElementNS(NS,'line');
            line.setAttribute('x1',positions[ia*2]);line.setAttribute('y1',positions[ia*2+1]);
            line.setAttribute('x2',positions[ib*2]);line.setAttribute('y2',positions[ib*2+1]);
            line.setAttribute('stroke',lineColor);line.setAttribute('stroke-width','1.5');
            line.setAttribute('opacity','0.6');
            line.dataset.a=String(ia);line.dataset.b=String(ib);
            svg.appendChild(line);
          });
          wrap.appendChild(svg);
          const updateLines=()=>{
            svg.querySelectorAll('line').forEach(line=>{
              const ia=+line.dataset.a,ib=+line.dataset.b;
              line.setAttribute('x1',isNaN(renderer.lastX[ia])?positions[ia*2]:renderer.lastX[ia]);
              line.setAttribute('y1',isNaN(renderer.lastY[ia])?positions[ia*2+1]:renderer.lastY[ia]);
              line.setAttribute('x2',isNaN(renderer.lastX[ib])?positions[ib*2]:renderer.lastX[ib]);
              line.setAttribute('y2',isNaN(renderer.lastY[ib])?positions[ib*2+1]:renderer.lastY[ib]);
            });
          };
          const onScroll=()=>{
            renderer.invalidateVisible();
            if(renderer.current){
              renderer.dirty=true;
              if(!renderer.rafId)renderer.rafId=requestAnimationFrame(()=>renderer.flush());
            }
          };
          wrap.addEventListener('scroll',onScroll,{passive:true});
          const onResize=()=>renderer.invalidateVisible();
          window.addEventListener('resize',onResize);
          docs.forEach((doc,i)=>{
            const el=nodeEls.get(doc.id);
            el.addEventListener('click',()=>{
              if(el._dragging)return;
              ui.currentDocId=doc.id;
              ui.view='auto';
              ui.focusId=doc.id;
              ui.activeKey='node:'+doc.id;
              saveUI();forceRender();
            });
          });
          let dragId=null,dragIdx=-1;
          wrap.addEventListener('pointerdown',(e)=>{
            const el=e.target.closest('.graph-node');
            if(!el||e.button!==0)return;
            dragId=el.dataset.id;
            dragIdx=idIndex.get(dragId);
            if(dragIdx==null)return;
            renderer.beginDrag(dragId);
            try{el.setPointerCapture(e.pointerId);}catch(err){}
            e.preventDefault();
          });
          const onMove=(e)=>{
            if(!dragId)return;
            const wrapR=wrap.getBoundingClientRect();
            const x=e.clientX-wrapR.left+wrap.scrollLeft;
            const y=e.clientY-wrapR.top+wrap.scrollTop;
            renderer.setDraggingPosition(dragIdx,x,y);
            const el=nodeEls.get(dragId);
            if(el)el._dragging=true;
            updateLines();
          };
          const onUp=()=>{
            if(!dragId)return;
            const el=nodeEls.get(dragId);
            renderer.endDrag(dragId);
            const x=renderer.lastX[dragIdx],y=renderer.lastY[dragIdx];
            renderer.lastX[dragIdx]=NaN;
            renderer.lastY[dragIdx]=NaN;
            const curDoc=(dragIdx>=0&&dragIdx<docs.length)?docs[dragIdx]:null;
            dragId=null;dragIdx=-1;
            if(el)setTimeout(()=>{el._dragging=false;},50);
            if(!curDoc||isNaN(x)||isNaN(y))return;
            _graphPositions[curDoc.id]={x,y};
            saveGraphPositions();
            const worker=getGraphWorker();
            if(worker.ready){
              const np=new Uint8Array(n);
              const npp=new Float32Array(n*2);
              docs.forEach((doc,i)=>{
                const p=_graphPositions[doc.id];
                if(p){np[i]=1;npp[i*2]=p.x;npp[i*2+1]=p.y;}
              });
              const pr=worker.updatePinned({pinned:np,pinnedPos:npp,iters:20});
              if(pr)pr.then(buf=>{if(buf){renderer.setPositions(buf);setTimeout(updateLines,20);}}).catch(()=>{});
            }
          };
          wrap.addEventListener('pointermove',onMove);
          wrap.addEventListener('pointerup',onUp);
          wrap.addEventListener('pointercancel',onUp);
          const worker=getGraphWorker();
          if(worker.ready){
            worker.initLayout({
              n,positions,edges:edgeArr,pinned,pinnedPos,W,H,radii, 
              iters:n<=80?150:n<=300?80:40,
            }).then(buf=>{
              renderer.setPositions(buf);
              setTimeout(updateLines,20);
            }).catch((err)=>{
              if(err&&err.message==='superseded')return;
              runSyncGraphLayout({n,positions,edgeArr,pinned,pinnedPos,W,H,radii});
              renderer.setPositions(positions.buffer);
              updateLines();
            });
          }else{
            runSyncGraphLayout({n,positions,edgeArr,pinned,pinnedPos,W,H,radii});
            renderer.setPositions(positions.buffer);
            updateLines();
          }
          wrap._renderer=renderer;
          wrap._cleanup=()=>{
            wrap.removeEventListener('scroll',onScroll);
            wrap.removeEventListener('pointermove',onMove);
            wrap.removeEventListener('pointerup',onUp);
            wrap.removeEventListener('pointercancel',onUp);
            window.removeEventListener('resize',onResize);
            renderer.dispose();
            const w=getGraphWorker();
            if(w.ready)w.cancel();
          };
          return wrap;
        }
        function runSyncGraphLayout({n,positions,edgeArr,pinned,pinnedPos,W,H,radii}){
          const MAX_ITER=n>300?40:(n>80?80:150);
          const vel=new Float32Array(n*2);
          const REP=20000,EPS2=100,SPR=0.015,SPRLEN=180,CEN=0.008; 
          const PAD=18;
          for(let iter=0;iter<MAX_ITER;iter++){
            for(let i=0;i<vel.length;i++)vel[i]=0;
            
            if(n<=150){
              for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
                for(let j=i+1;j<n;j++){
                  let dx=positions[i*2]-positions[j*2];
                  let dy=positions[i*2+1]-positions[j*2+1];
                  let d2=dx*dx+dy*dy;
                  if(d2<EPS2){dx=Math.random()-0.5;dy=Math.random()-0.5;d2=EPS2;}
                  const d=Math.sqrt(d2);
                  const f=REP/d2;
                  vel[i*2]+=(dx/d)*f;vel[i*2+1]+=(dy/d)*f;
                  vel[j*2]-=(dx/d)*f;vel[j*2+1]-=(dy/d)*f;
                }
              }
            }
            for(let e=0;e<edgeArr.length;e+=2){
              const a=edgeArr[e],b=edgeArr[e+1];
              const dx=positions[b*2]-positions[a*2];
              const dy=positions[b*2+1]-positions[a*2+1];
              const d=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)||1;
              const f=(d-SPRLEN)*SPR;
              vel[a*2]+=(dx/d)*f;vel[a*2+1]+=(dy/d)*f;
              vel[b*2]-=(dx/d)*f;vel[b*2+1]-=(dy/d)*f;
            }
            for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
              if(pinned[i]){
                positions[i*2]=pinnedPos[i*2];
                positions[i*2+1]=pinnedPos[i*2+1];
                vel[i*2]=0;vel[i*2+1]=0;
                continue;
              }
              vel[i*2]+=(W/2-positions[i*2])*CEN;
              vel[i*2+1]+=(H/2-positions[i*2+1])*CEN;
              vel[i*2]*=0.85;vel[i*2+1]*=0.85;
              positions[i*2]+=vel[i*2];
              positions[i*2+1]+=vel[i*2+1];
              if(positions[i*2]<40)positions[i*2]=40;
              if(positions[i*2]>W-40)positions[i*2]=W-40;
              if(positions[i*2+1]<40)positions[i*2+1]=40;
              if(positions[i*2+1]>H-40)positions[i*2+1]=H-40;
            }
            
            if(radii&&radii.length){
              for(let i=0;i<n;i++){
                for(let j=i+1;j<n;j++){
                  let dx=positions[j*2]-positions[i*2];
                  let dy=positions[j*2+1]-positions[i*2+1];
                  let d=Math.sqrt(dx*dx+dy*dy)||1;
                  const min=radii[i]+radii[j]+PAD;
                  if(d<min){
                    const push=(min-d)/2;
                    const ux=dx/d,uy=dy/d;
                    if(!pinned[i]){positions[i*2]-=ux*push;positions[i*2+1]-=uy*push;}
                    if(!pinned[j]){positions[j*2]+=ux*push;positions[j*2+1]+=uy*push;}
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
        }
        
        
        function treeRowVisible(n){return !!n&&!n._deleted;}
        function renderPresentation(doc){
          if(!doc){const w=document.createElement('div');w.className='empty-state';w.textContent='璇峰厛閫夋嫨鏂囨。';return w;}
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='pres-page';
          wrap.appendChild(renderDocHeaderInline(doc));
          const list=document.createElement('div');list.className='pres-list';
          function render(id,depth){
            const n=N(id);if(!n)return;
            if(id!==doc.id&&n.collapsed)return;
            if(id!==doc.id&&treeRowVisible(n)){ 
              const lvl=Math.min(depth,3);
              const item=document.createElement('div');
              item.className='pres-item pres-l'+lvl;
              if(depth>3)item.style.paddingLeft=(48+(depth-3)*16)+'px';
              item.innerHTML=(n.done?'鉁?':'')+esc(stripHtml(n.text||''));
              
              item.onclick=e=>{
                const lt=linkTitleAtPoint(item,e);
                if(lt){e.preventDefault();navigateToWikiLink(lt,item);}
              };
              list.appendChild(item);
            }
            const nd=id===doc.id?1:depth+1;
            n.children.forEach(c=>render(c,nd));
          }
          if(!doc.children.length){
            const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='empty-state';
            e.textContent='璇ユ枃妗ｆ殏鏃犲唴瀹?;
            wrap.appendChild(e);return wrap;
          }
          if(_vsShouldUse(doc)){ 
            wrap.appendChild(list);
            _renderPresentationVirtual(wrap,doc);
            return wrap;
          }
          doc.children.forEach(c=>render(c,1));
          wrap.appendChild(list);
          return wrap;
        }
        
        
        let _zhCollator=null;
        function _getZhCollator(){
          if(!_zhCollator){
            const loc=(typeof APP_LOCALE!=='undefined'&&APP_LOCALE)||'zh-Hans';
            _zhCollator=new Intl.Collator(loc,{numeric:true,sensitivity:'base'});
          }
          return _zhCollator;
        }
        function sortDocs(ids,sorter){
          return[...ids].sort((aid,bid)=>{
            const a=N(aid),b=N(bid);
            const aF=isFolder(aid),bF=isFolder(bid);
            if(sorter==='type'&&aF!==bF)return aF?-1:1;
            if(sorter==='created')return(b.createdAt||0)-(a.createdAt||0);
            if(sorter==='updated')return(b.updatedAt||0)-(a.updatedAt||0);
            if(sorter==='star')return(b.starred||0)-(a.starred||0);
            if(aF!==bF)return aF?-1:1;
            return _getZhCollator().compare(stripHtml(a.text||''),stripHtml(b.text||''));
          });
        }
        function renderFolderGrid(folder){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='files-wrap';
          if(!folder){wrap.innerHTML='<div class="empty-state">鏂囦欢澶逛笉瀛樺湪</div>';return wrap;}
          wrap.appendChild(renderFolderHeader(folder));
          const toolbar=document.createElement('div');toolbar.className='files-toolbar';
          const sortSel=document.createElement('select');sortSel.className='files-sort';
          sortSel.innerHTML=`<option value="name">鎸夊悕绉?/option><option value="type">鎸夌被鍨?/option><option value="created">鎸夊垱寤烘椂闂?/option><option value="updated">鎸変慨鏀规椂闂?/option><option value="star">鎸夋槦鏍囩瓑绾?/option>`;
          sortSel.value=ui.filesSort||'name';
          sortSel.onchange=()=>{ui.filesSort=sortSel.value;saveUI();forceRender();};
          toolbar.appendChild(sortSel);
          wrap.appendChild(toolbar);
          const ids=sortDocs(folder.children.filter(id=>{
            const n=N(id);
            if(!n||n._deleted)return false;
            return !n._isCalRoot&&!n._isDbRoot;
          }),ui.filesSort||'name');
          if(!ids.length){const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='empty-state';e.textContent='杩欎釜鏂囦欢澶规槸绌虹殑';wrap.appendChild(e);return wrap;}
          const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='files-grid';
          ids.forEach(id=>grid.appendChild(renderFileCard(id,N(id),isFolder(id))));
          wrap.appendChild(grid);
          return wrap;
        }
        function renderFolderHeader(folder){
          const bar=document.createElement('div');bar.className='doc-header';
          const name=document.createElement('div');name.className='doc-header-title';
          if(folder.id===state.rootId){
            name.textContent='鎴戠殑鏂囨。';
            name.contentEditable=false;
          }else{
            name.contentEditable=true;name.spellcheck=false;name.dataset.id=folder.id;name.dataset.ph='鏈懡鍚嶆枃浠跺す';
            name.textContent=stripHtml(folder.text)||'鏈懡鍚?;
            name.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
              if(isBlurSuppressed())return;
              if(!name.isConnected)return;
              const finalText=name.textContent.trim();
              const cur=N(folder.id);if(!cur)return;
              if(finalText&&finalText!==stripHtml(cur.text||'')){
                commit(s=>{s.nodes[folder.id].text=finalText;s.nodes[folder.id].updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true});
                actions.logActivity('mod',folder.id);
                renderSidebar();
                if(folder.id!==state.rootId)forceRender();
              }
            });
            name.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
              if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){e.preventDefault();name.blur();}
              if(e.key==='Escape'){name.textContent=stripHtml(N(folder.id).text||'')||'鏈懡鍚?;name.blur();}
            });
          }
          bar.appendChild(name);
          
          return bar;
        }
        function renderFileCard(id,n,isFolderFlag){
          const card=document.createElement('div');card.className='file-card';
          if(ui.currentDocId===id)card.classList.add('sel');
          const icon=document.createElement('div');icon.className='file-icon';icon.innerHTML=getNodeSvg(id);card.appendChild(icon);
          const info=document.createElement('div');info.className='file-info';
          const nameEl=document.createElement('div');nameEl.className='file-name';nameEl.textContent=stripHtml(n.text)||'鏈懡鍚?;info.appendChild(nameEl);
          const meta=document.createElement('div');meta.className='file-meta';
          if(isFolderFlag)meta.textContent=(n.children.length)+' 椤?;
          else meta.textContent=docWordCount(id)+' 瀛?路 '+fmtRelative(n.updatedAt,false);
          info.appendChild(meta);
          if(n.starred>0){const stars=document.createElement('div');stars.className='file-stars';stars.textContent='鈽?.repeat(n.starred);info.appendChild(stars);}
          card.appendChild(info);
          card.onclick=()=>openNode(id);
          card.oncontextmenu=e=>{e.preventDefault();showTreeContext(e,id);};
          return card;
        }
        function ensureWordInit(docId){
          const n=N(docId);
          if(!n)return;
          if(n._isWord && n.content!==undefined) return;
          commit(s=>{
            const nn=s.nodes[docId];
            if(!nn)return;
            if(!nn._isWord) nn._isWord=true;
            if(nn.content===undefined){
              nn.content=(nn.children.map(cid=>'<p>'+esc(stripHtml(s.nodes[cid]?.text||''))+'</p>').join(''))||'';
              nn._wordFlat=true; 
            }
          },{snapshot:false,silent:true,persistOnly:true,dirtyText:true});
        }
        function renderWordEditor(doc){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='word-editor';
          const cur=N(doc.id);
          if(cur._isWord && cur.content===undefined) ensureWordInit(doc.id);
          const c2=N(doc.id);
          const body=document.createElement('div');body.className='we-body';body.contentEditable='true';
          body.dataset.id=doc.id;
          body.innerHTML=sanitizeHtml(c2.content||'');
          body.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
            if(isBlurSuppressed())return;
            if(!body.isConnected)return;
            const norm=sanitizeHtml(body.innerHTML);
            const c3=N(cur.id);
            if(c3&&norm!==c3.content)commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[cur.id];if(!nn)return;nn.content=norm;nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true,dirtyText:true});
          });
          body.addEventListener('paste',e=>{
            e.preventDefault();
            const text=(e.clipboardData||window.clipboardData).getData('text/plain');
            safeExec('insertText',text);
          });
          
          body.addEventListener('click',e=>{
            if(!(e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey))return;
            const lt=linkTitleAtPoint(body,e);
            if(!lt)return;
            e.preventDefault();
            navigateToWikiLink(lt,body);
          });
          wrap.appendChild(body);
          return wrap;
        }
        function safeNavigate(fn){
          const ae=document.activeElement;
          if(ae&&ae.blur)ae.blur();
          _forceRender=true;
          fn();
          scheduleRender();
        }
        function openNode(id){
          if(!id||!N(id))return;
          const n=N(id);
          if(n._isWord)ensureWordInit(id);
          safeNavigate(()=>{
            ui.currentDocId=id;
            ui.view='auto';
            const n2=N(id);
            if(n2._isCalRoot){
              ui.activeKey='nav:calendar';
              
              ui.selectedDate=todayStr();
              const _d=new Date();ui.calMonth={y:_d.getFullYear(),m:_d.getMonth()};
            }
            else if(n2._isDbRoot)ui.activeKey='nav:database';
            else if(n2.dbRoot){ 
              ui.activeKey='node:'+id;
              ui.navExpanded.database=true;
            }
            else ui.activeKey='node:'+id;
            saveUI();
          });
        }
        function syncTopbar(){
          const curDoc=getCurrentDoc();
          const curView=ui.view==='auto'?viewOf(curDoc):ui.view;
          $$('.tb-nav-btn').forEach(b=>{
            const v=b.dataset.view;
            let active=false;
            if(v==='document'){
              active=ui.view==='auto'&&[VIEW_KIND.LIBRARY_ROOT,VIEW_KIND.FOLDER,VIEW_KIND.DATABASE,VIEW_KIND.WORD].includes(curView);
            }else if(v==='outline'){
              active=ui.view==='auto'&&curView===VIEW_KIND.OUTLINE;
            }else{
              active=ui.view===v;
            }
            b.classList.toggle('active',active);
            
            const _cv0=curDoc?viewOf(curDoc):null;
            const _isContainer=_cv0===VIEW_KIND.LIBRARY_ROOT||_cv0===VIEW_KIND.FOLDER||_cv0===VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR;
            const _lock=(v==='outline'||v==='mindmap'||v==='presentation'||v==='graph'||v==='history')&&_isContainer;
            b.classList.toggle('disabled',_lock);
          });
        }
        function renderMain(){
          const content=$('#content');
          
          const _oldTg=content&&content.querySelector('.cal-tg-scroll');
          _calTgPrevScroll=_oldTg?_oldTg.scrollTop:null;
          const oldGraph=content&&content.querySelector('.graph-canvas');
          if(oldGraph&&oldGraph._cleanup)oldGraph._cleanup();
          if(ui._resizingCol){
            if(ui._resizingColStart && Date.now()-ui._resizingColStart<3000) return;
            ui._resizingCol=false;
            ui._resizingColStart=0;
          }
          const c=$('#content');
          if(!c)return;
          const ae=document.activeElement;
          const isTextEditing=ae&&ae.closest&&ae.closest('#content')&&(
            ae.isContentEditable||
            ae.tagName==='TEXTAREA'||
            (ae.tagName==='INPUT'&&!['checkbox','radio','button','submit','range'].includes(ae.type))
          );
          if(isTextEditing&&!_forceRender){
            syncTopbar();
            updateRightSidebar();
            renderIntro(); 
            
            {
              const _ekCurId=ui.currentDocId&&state.nodes[ui.currentDocId]?ui.currentDocId:state.rootId;
              const _ekDoc=N(_ekCurId);
              let _ekView=ui.view==='auto'?viewOf(_ekDoc):ui.view;
              const _ekNav=viewOf(_ekDoc);
              if((_ekView==='graph'||_ekView==='history')&&(_ekNav===VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR||_ekNav===VIEW_KIND.FOLDER||_ekNav===VIEW_KIND.LIBRARY_ROOT))_ekView=_ekNav;
              ui._lastViewKey=_ekView+':'+(_ekCurId||'');
            }
            
            const _earlyView=ui.view==='auto'?viewOf(getCurrentDoc()):ui.view;
            if(_earlyView===VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR||_earlyView===VIEW_KIND.DATABASE){
              updateRemindBadge();
              const _fc=$('#floatCal');
              if(_fc&&!_fc.hidden)renderFloatCal();
              
              if(_earlyView===VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR){
                const _sb=c&&c.querySelector('.cal-panel-stats .cal-panel-body');
                if(_sb){try{_sb.innerHTML='';_sb.appendChild(buildStatsBlock());}catch(e){}}
                let _lb=null;
                if(c){c.querySelectorAll('.cal-panel>.cal-panel-head').forEach(function(h){if(!_lb&&/绯荤粺鏃ュ織/.test(h.textContent||''))_lb=h.parentElement.querySelector('.cal-panel-body');});}
                if(_lb&&typeof buildCalLogBlock==='function'){try{_lb.innerHTML='';_lb.appendChild(buildCalLogBlock());}catch(e){}}
              }
            }
            return;
          }
          _forceRender=false;
          const curId=ui.currentDocId&&state.nodes[ui.currentDocId]?ui.currentDocId:state.rootId;
          ui.currentDocId=curId;
          const curDoc=N(curId);
          let view=ui.view==='auto'?viewOf(curDoc):ui.view;
          
          {
            const _cvNav=viewOf(curDoc);
            if((view==='graph'||view==='history')&&(_cvNav===VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR||_cvNav===VIEW_KIND.FOLDER||_cvNav===VIEW_KIND.LIBRARY_ROOT)){
              view=_cvNav;ui.view='auto';
            }
          }
          const viewKey=view+':'+(curId||'');
          const viewChanged=(ui._lastViewKey||'')!==viewKey;
          const _oldViewKey=ui._lastViewKey||''; 
          ui._lastViewKey=viewKey;
          const scroll=viewChanged?0:(c.scrollTop??ui._lastScroll??0);
          
          const _wasOutline=_oldViewKey.startsWith('outline');
          if(_wasOutline)_savedOutlineScroll=c.scrollTop||0;
          const _wasMindmap=_oldViewKey.startsWith('mindmap');
          if(_wasMindmap){const _mm=document.querySelector('.mm-wrap');if(_mm){_savedMindmapScroll.x=_mm.scrollLeft||0;_savedMindmapScroll.y=_mm.scrollTop||0;}}
          c.innerHTML='';
          _vsActive=null; 
          c.classList.toggle('graph-mode',view==='graph'); 
          document.body.classList.toggle('graph-mode',view==='graph'); 
          document.body.classList.toggle('mm-mode',view==='mindmap'); 
          let el;
          switch(view){
            case VIEW_KIND.LIBRARY_ROOT: el=renderFolderGrid(N(state.rootId)); break;
            case VIEW_KIND.FOLDER:       el=renderFolderGrid(curDoc); break;
            case VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR:     el=renderCalendarPage(); break;
            case VIEW_KIND.DATABASE:     el=renderDatabasePage(curDoc); break;
            case VIEW_KIND.WORD:         el=renderWordEditor(curDoc); break;
            case VIEW_KIND.OUTLINE:      el=renderOutlinePage(curDoc); break;
            case 'starred':              el=renderStarredView(); break;
            case 'templates':            el=renderTemplatesView(); break;
            case 'trash':                el=renderTrashView(); break;
            case 'mindmap':              el=renderMindmap(curDoc); break;
            case 'presentation':         el=renderPresentation(curDoc); break;
            case 'graph':                el=renderGraph(); break;
            case 'stats':                el=renderStatsView(); break;
            case 'tags':                 el=ui.tagActive?renderTagNodes(ui.tagActive):renderTagsView(); break;
            case 'history':              el=renderHistoryView(); break;
            default:                     el=renderOutlinePage(curDoc);
          }
          if(el)c.appendChild(el);
          
          if(view===VIEW_KIND.OUTLINE)requestAnimationFrame(()=>{_syncOutlineHeaderH(el);if(el&&el._vs)_vsUpdate(el._vs);});
          renderSidebar();
          syncTopbar();
          updateRightSidebar();
          updateRemindBadge();
          restoreFocus();
          const fc=$('#floatCal');
          if(fc){
            fc.hidden=!ui.floatCal;
            if(ui.floatCal){ensureElInViewport(fc);renderFloatCal();}
          }
          requestAnimationFrame(()=>{
            if(!viewChanged)c.scrollTop=scroll;
            
            if(view===VIEW_KIND.OUTLINE&&_savedOutlineScroll>0){
              c.scrollTop=_savedOutlineScroll;
              requestAnimationFrame(()=>{c.scrollTop=_savedOutlineScroll;});
            }
            if(view==='mindmap'){
              const _mm=document.querySelector('.mm-wrap');
              if(_mm){_mm.scrollLeft=_savedMindmapScroll.x;_mm.scrollTop=_savedMindmapScroll.y;
                requestAnimationFrame(()=>{_mm.scrollLeft=_savedMindmapScroll.x;_mm.scrollTop=_savedMindmapScroll.y;});
              }
            }
            if(ui.focusId){
              if(_vsActive)_vsScrollToId(_vsActive,ui.focusId); 
              const focusEl=c.querySelector('.text[data-id="'+CSS.escape(ui.focusId)+'"]')||
                            c.querySelector('[data-id="'+CSS.escape(ui.focusId)+'"][contenteditable]');
              if(focusEl)focusEl.scrollIntoView({block:'nearest',behavior:'auto'});
            }
            ui.focusId=null;
          });
        }

        <script>
        "use strict";
        
        
        let _sidebarRaf=null;
        
        function scheduleSidebar(){
          if(_sidebarRaf)return;
          _sidebarRaf=requestAnimationFrame(()=>{_sidebarRaf=null;renderSidebar();});
        }
        
        let _sidebarFp=null;
        function _sidebarFingerprint(){
          return [state.updatedAt,
            ui.navExpanded?JSON.stringify(ui.navExpanded):'{}',
            ui.filesSort||'',
            state.tagColors?JSON.stringify(state.tagColors):'{}',
            Object.keys(ui.collapsedDocs||{}).filter(k=>ui.collapsedDocs[k]).sort().join(',') 
          ].join('|');
        }
        function renderSidebar(){
          const box=$('#sidebarLeft');
          const scrollTop=box?box.scrollTop:0;
          
          ['database','starred','files','tags'].forEach(k=>{
            const btn=document.querySelector('.sb-nav-item[data-nav="'+k+'"]');
            if(btn)btn.classList.toggle('open',!!(ui.navExpanded&&ui.navExpanded[k]));
          });
          const root=N(state.rootId);
          let starred=0,docCount=0,dbRoot=null;
          const rootChildSet=new Set(root?root.children:[]);
          for(const id in state.nodes){
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(!n||n._deleted||n.id===state.rootId)continue;
            if(n.starred>0&&!n._isFolder&&!n._isDbRoot&&!n._isCalRoot)starred++;
            if(rootChildSet.has(id)){
              if(n._isDbRoot)dbRoot=n;
              else if(!n._isCalRoot)docCount++;
            }
          }
          const elStar=$('#cntStarred');if(elStar)elStar.textContent=starred;
          const dbCount=dbRoot?dbRoot.children.length:0;
          const elDb=$('#cntDb');if(elDb)elDb.textContent=dbCount;
          const elDocs=$('#cntDocs');if(elDocs)elDocs.textContent=docCount;
          const elTags=$('#cntTags');if(elTags)elTags.textContent=_l7TagCountText(); 
          
          const fp=_sidebarFingerprint();
          if(fp!==_sidebarFp){
            _sidebarFp=fp;
            renderSubDb(dbRoot);
            renderSubStarred();
            renderSubDocs();
            renderSubTags();
          }
          renderIntro();
          updateSidebarActiveState();
          if(box)box.scrollTop=scrollTop;
        }
        function renderSubDb(dbRoot){
          const box=$('#subDb');if(!box)return;
          const open=!!ui.navExpanded.database;
          box.classList.toggle('open',open);
          box.innerHTML='';
          if(!open)return;
          const items=dbRoot?dbRoot.children.map(id=>N(id)).filter(n=>n&&!n._deleted):[];
          if(!items.length){const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='sb-row sb-empty';empty.innerHTML='<span class="sb-arrow leaf"></span><span class="sb-icon"></span><span class="sb-label">鏆傛棤澶氱淮琛ㄦ牸</span>';box.appendChild(empty);return;}
          items.forEach(n=>{
            const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='sb-row';row.dataset.id=n.id;
            row.innerHTML=`<span class="sb-arrow leaf"></span><span class="sb-icon">${getNodeSvg(n.id)}</span><span class="sb-label">${esc(stripHtml(n.text)||'鏈懡鍚?)}</span>`;
            row.onclick=()=>openNode(n.id);
            row.oncontextmenu=e=>{e.preventDefault();showTreeContext(e,n.id);};
            box.appendChild(row);
          });
        }
        function renderSubStarred(){
          const box=$('#subStarred');if(!box)return;
          const open=!!ui.navExpanded.starred;
          box.classList.toggle('open',open);
          box.innerHTML='';
          if(!open)return;
          
          const items=[];
          for(const id in state.nodes){const n=state.nodes[id];if(n.starred>0&&!isFolder(id)&&!n._deleted)items.push(n);}
          items.sort((a,b)=>(b.starred||0)-(a.starred||0));
          if(!items.length){const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='sb-row sb-empty';empty.innerHTML='<span class="sb-arrow leaf"></span><span class="sb-icon"></span><span class="sb-label">鏆傛棤鏄熸爣</span>';box.appendChild(empty);return;}
          items.slice(0,30).forEach(n=>{
            const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='sb-row';row.dataset.id=n.id;
            row.innerHTML=`<span class="sb-arrow leaf"></span><span class="sb-icon">${getNodeSvg(n.id)}</span><span class="sb-label">${esc(stripHtml(n.text)||'鏈懡鍚?)}</span>`;
            row.onclick=()=>openNode(n.id);
            box.appendChild(row);
          });
        }
        function renderSubDocs(){
          const box=$('#subDocs');if(!box)return;
          
          box.setAttribute('role','tree');
          const open=!!ui.navExpanded.files;
          box.classList.toggle('open',open);
          box.innerHTML='';
          if(!open)return;
          const root=N(state.rootId);
          const ids=sortDocs(root.children.filter(id=>{const n=N(id);return n&&!n._isDbRoot&&!n._isCalRoot&&!n._deleted;}),ui.filesSort||'name');
          if(!ids.length){const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.className='sb-row sb-empty';empty.innerHTML='<span class="sb-arrow leaf"></span><span class="sb-icon"></span><span class="sb-label">鏆傛棤鏂囨。</span>';box.appendChild(empty);return;}
          ids.forEach(id=>box.appendChild(makeSidebarRow(id)));
        }
        function promptNewTag(){
          appPrompt('鏂版爣绛惧悕锛?,'',(name)=>{
            if(name===null)return;
            const t=(name||'').trim().replace(/^#/,'');
            if(!t){toast('鏍囩鍚嶄笉鑳戒负绌?);return;}
            
            let cur=getCurrentDoc();
            if(cur&&(cur._isCalRoot||cur._isDbRoot||cur._isNoteRoot||cur._isFolder)){
              const root=state.nodes[state.rootId];
              cur=(root&&root.children)?.map(cid=>state.nodes[cid]).find(n=>n&&!n._isCalRoot&&!n._isDbRoot&&!n._isNoteRoot&&!n._isFolder&&!n._deleted)||null;
            }
            const docId=cur&&!cur._isCalRoot&&!cur._isDbRoot&&!cur._isNoteRoot&&!cur._isFolder?cur.id:null;
            if(!docId){toast('璇峰厛鍦ㄥ乏渚ч€夋嫨涓€涓枃妗ｏ紝鏍囩灏嗛檮鍔犲埌璇ユ枃妗?);return;}
            
            commit(s=>{const n=s.nodes[docId];if(!n)return;
              const arr=Array.isArray(n.tags)?n.tags.slice():[];
              if(!arr.includes(t))arr.push(t);
              n.tags=arr;
              
              const man=Array.isArray(n._manualTags)?n._manualTags.slice():[];
              if(!man.includes(t))man.push(t);
              n._manualTags=man;
              n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:docId});
            _tagIndexCache=null;renderSidebar();forceRender();toast('宸叉坊鍔犳爣绛?#'+t);
          });
        }
        function renderSubTags(){
          const box=$('#subTags');if(!box)return;
          const open=!!ui.navExpanded.tags;
          box.classList.toggle('open',open);
          box.innerHTML='';
          
          if(!open){
            const add=document.createElement('div');add.className='sb-row sb-add';
            add.innerHTML='<span class="sb-arrow leaf"></span><span class="sb-icon" style="font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-fg)">锛?/span><span class="sb-label" style="color:var(--accent-fg)">鏂板缓鏍囩</span>';
            add.onclick=()=>{ui.navExpanded.tags=true;renderSidebar();setTimeout(promptNewTag,0);};
            box.appendChild(add);
            return;
          }
          
          if(!_tagIndexCache){
            _l7SkeletonEnsureCss();
            const addSk=document.createElement('div');addSk.className='sb-row sb-add';
            addSk.innerHTML='<span class="sb-arrow leaf"></span><span class="sb-icon" style="font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-fg)">锛?/span><span class="sb-label" style="color:var(--accent-fg)">鏂板缓鏍囩</span>';
            addSk.onclick=()=>{ui.navExpanded.tags=true;renderSidebar();setTimeout(promptNewTag,0);};
            box.appendChild(addSk);
            for(let _si=0;_si<4;_si++){
              const _sr=document.createElement('div');_sr.className='ju-skel-row';
              const _sl=document.createElement('div');_sl.className='ju-skel-line';_sl.style.width=(118-_si*16)+'px';
              _sr.appendChild(_sl);box.appendChild(_sr);
            }
            l7TagIndexWhenReady(function(){if(ui.navExpanded.tags)renderSubTags();});
            return;
          }
          const tagIndex=buildTagIndex();
          tags.forEach(([t,count])=>{
            const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='sb-row';row.dataset.tag=t;
            const arr=document.createElement('span');arr.className='sb-arrow leaf';row.appendChild(arr);
            const ic=document.createElement('span');ic.className='sb-icon';ic.style.cssText='color:var(--icon);font-weight:700';ic.textContent='#';
            const dot=document.createElement('span');dot.className='sb-tag-dot';dot.style.background=getTagColor(t);
            dot.title='鐐瑰嚮閫夋嫨鏍囩棰滆壊';
            dot.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();openTagPalette(dot,t);};
            row.addEventListener('contextmenu',e=>{
              e.preventDefault();
              showContextMenu([
                {ic:'鉁忥笍',txt:'閲嶅懡鍚嶆爣绛?,fn:()=>{appPrompt('閲嶅懡鍚嶄负锛?,t,(nt)=>{if(nt===null)return;const nt2=(nt||'').trim().replace(/^#/,'');if(!nt2||nt2===t){toast('鏍囩鍚嶆棤鏁堟垨鏈彉鍖?);return;}renameTag(t,nt2);});}},
                {ic:'馃棏',txt:'鍒犻櫎鏍囩',danger:true,fn:()=>{appConfirm('鍒犻櫎鏍囩 #'+t+'锛堜粠鎵€鏈夎妭鐐规枃鏈Щ闄わ級锛?,()=>removeTag(t));}},
                {sep:true},
                {ic:'馃帹',txt:'鏇存敼棰滆壊',fn:()=>{openTagPalette(row.querySelector('.sb-tag-dot'),t);}},
              ],e);
            });
            const lab=document.createElement('span');lab.className='sb-label';lab.textContent=t;
            const cnt=document.createElement('span');cnt.className='count';cnt.textContent=count;
            row.appendChild(ic);row.appendChild(dot);row.appendChild(lab);row.appendChild(cnt);
            row.onclick=()=>openTag(t);
            box.appendChild(row);
          });
          const add=document.createElement('div');add.className='sb-row sb-add';
          add.innerHTML='<span class="sb-arrow leaf"></span><span class="sb-icon" style="font-weight:700;color:var(--accent-fg)">锛?/span><span class="sb-label" style="color:var(--accent-fg)">鏂板缓鏍囩</span>';
          add.onclick=promptNewTag;
          box.appendChild(add);
        }
        
        
        function replaceTagInHtmlTag(html,oldT,newT){
          if(!html)return html||'';
          const re=new RegExp('#'+oldT.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+'(?=\\s|$)','g');
          const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString('<body>'+html+'</body>','text/html');
          const walker=doc.createTreeWalker(doc.body,NodeFilter.SHOW_TEXT);
          const texts=[];let tn;
          while((tn=walker.nextNode()))texts.push(tn);
          texts.forEach(node=>{
            const v=node.nodeValue;
            if(v.indexOf('#'+oldT)<0)return;
            node.nodeValue=v.replace(re,newT==null?'':'#'+newT);
          });
          return doc.body.innerHTML;
        }
        function renameTag(oldT,newT){
          commit(s=>{
            for(const id in s.nodes){
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n||n._deleted||n._isFolder)continue;
              const raw=stripHtml(n.text||'');
              if(raw.indexOf('#'+oldT)<0&&!(Array.isArray(n.tags)&&n.tags.includes(oldT)))continue;
              n.text=replaceTagInHtmlTag(n.text||'',oldT,newT);
              if(Array.isArray(n.tags)&&n.tags.includes(oldT))n.tags=n.tags.map(x=>x===oldT?newT:x);
              
              if(Array.isArray(n._manualTags)&&n._manualTags.includes(oldT))n._manualTags=n._manualTags.map(x=>x===oldT?newT:x);
              n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
            }
          },{snapshot:true});
          _tagIndexCache=null;renderSidebar();forceRender();toast('宸查噸鍛藉悕涓?#'+newT);
        }
        
        function removeTag(t){
          commit(s=>{
            for(const id in s.nodes){
              const n=s.nodes[id];if(!n||n._deleted||n._isFolder)continue;
              const raw=stripHtml(n.text||'');
              if(raw.indexOf('#'+t)<0&&!(Array.isArray(n.tags)&&n.tags.includes(t)))continue;
              n.text=replaceTagInHtmlTag(n.text||'',t,null);
              if(Array.isArray(n.tags)&&n.tags.includes(t))n.tags=n.tags.filter(x=>x!==t);
              
              if(Array.isArray(n._manualTags)&&n._manualTags.includes(t))n._manualTags=n._manualTags.filter(x=>x!==t);
              n.updatedAt=_nowSeq();
            }
          },{snapshot:true});
          _tagIndexCache=null;renderSidebar();forceRender();toast('宸插垹闄ゆ爣绛?#'+t);
        }
        function makeSidebarRow(id){
          const n=N(id);
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='sb-node';
          const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='sb-row';row.dataset.id=id;
          
          row.setAttribute('role','treeitem');
          const isFolderNode=isFolder(id);
          const hasKids=isFolderNode&&n.children.length>0;
          const arrow=document.createElement('span');
          arrow.className='sb-arrow'+(hasKids?'':' leaf');
          if(hasKids){
            const collapsed=!!ui.collapsedDocs[id];
            arrow.textContent=collapsed?'鈻?:'鈻?;
            
            row.setAttribute('aria-expanded',collapsed?'false':'true');
            arrow.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();ui.collapsedDocs[id]=!ui.collapsedDocs[id];saveUI();renderSidebar();};
          }
          row.appendChild(arrow);
          const icon=document.createElement('span');icon.className='sb-icon';icon.innerHTML=getNodeSvg(id);row.appendChild(icon);
          const label=document.createElement('span');label.className='sb-label';label.textContent=stripHtml(n.text)||'鏈懡鍚?;row.appendChild(label);
          row.onclick=()=>openNode(id);
          row.oncontextmenu=e=>{e.preventDefault();showTreeContext(e,id);};
          
          row.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
            if(e.button!==0)return;
            if(e.target.closest('.sb-arrow'))return; 
            if(id===state.rootId)return; 
            const dn=N(id);if(!dn||dn._deleted||dn._isDbRoot||dn._isCalRoot)return;
            const sx=e.clientX,sy=e.clientY;
            let dragging=false,dropMode=null,dropTargetId=null;
            let hoverTimer=null,hoverTid=null; 
            const clearHoverTimer=()=>{if(hoverTimer){clearTimeout(hoverTimer);hoverTimer=null;hoverTid=null;}};
            const onMM=ev=>{
              if(!dragging){
                if(Math.abs(ev.clientX-sx)<5&&Math.abs(ev.clientY-sy)<5)return;
                dragging=true;row.classList.add('dragging');e.preventDefault();
              }
              
              document.querySelectorAll('.sb-row.drop-before,.sb-row.drop-after,.sb-row.drop-child').forEach(r=>{r.classList.remove('drop-before','drop-after','drop-child');});
              const targetEl=document.elementFromPoint(ev.clientX,ev.clientY);
              const targetRow=targetEl?targetEl.closest('.sb-row[data-id]'):null;
              dropTargetId=null;dropMode=null;
              if(targetRow){
                const tid=targetRow.dataset.id;
                if(tid&&tid!==id){
                  const rect=targetRow.getBoundingClientRect();
                  const p=(ev.clientY-rect.top)/rect.height;
                  const tn=N(tid);
                  if(p<0.33){dropMode='before';dropTargetId=tid;targetRow.classList.add('drop-before');}
                  else if(p>0.67){dropMode='after';dropTargetId=tid;targetRow.classList.add('drop-after');}
                  else if(tn&&tn.children){dropMode='child';dropTargetId=tid;targetRow.classList.add('drop-child');}
                  
                  if(tn&&tn.children&&tn.children.length&&ui.collapsedDocs[tid]){
                    if(hoverTid!==tid){clearHoverTimer();hoverTid=tid;
                      hoverTimer=setTimeout(()=>{ui.collapsedDocs[tid]=false;saveUI();renderSidebar();},500);
                    }
                  }else{clearHoverTimer();}
                }else{clearHoverTimer();}
              }else{clearHoverTimer();}
            };
            const onMU=()=>{
              document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onMM);
              document.removeEventListener('mouseup',onMU);
              clearHoverTimer();
              document.querySelectorAll('.sb-row.drop-before,.sb-row.drop-after,.sb-row.drop-child').forEach(r=>{r.classList.remove('drop-before','drop-after','drop-child');});
              row.classList.remove('dragging');
              if(!dragging||!dropTargetId||!dropMode)return;
              const targetNode=N(dropTargetId);if(!targetNode)return;
              try{
                if(dropMode==='child'){
                  actions.moveNode(id,dropTargetId,targetNode.children.length);
                }else{
                  const parent=N(targetNode.parent)||N(state.rootId);
                  if(!parent)return;
                  let idx=parent.children.indexOf(dropTargetId);
                  if(idx<0)return;
                  if(dropMode==='after')idx++;
                  actions.moveNode(id,parent.id,idx);
                }
                renderSidebar();forceRender();
              }catch(err){try{toast('涓嶈兘绉诲姩鍒拌嚜韬垨瀛愯妭鐐?);}catch(_){}}
            };
            document.addEventListener('mousemove',onMM);
            document.addEventListener('mouseup',onMU);
          });
          wrap.appendChild(row);
          if(hasKids&&!ui.collapsedDocs[id]){
            const kids=document.createElement('div');kids.className='sb-children';
            n.children.forEach(cid=>kids.appendChild(makeSidebarRow(cid)));
            wrap.appendChild(kids);
          }
          return wrap;
        }
        function renderIntro(){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();
          const intro=$('#sbIntro');if(!intro)return;
          if(!doc){intro.innerHTML=`<div class="sb-intro-row"><span>鏍囬</span><span class="v">鏈€夋嫨</span></div>`;return;}
          const fmtTs=ts=>{const d=new Date(ts);return`${d.getFullYear()}/${d.getMonth()+1}/${d.getDate()} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;};
          const isRoot=doc.id===state.rootId;
          intro.innerHTML=`
            <div class="sb-intro-row"><span>鏍囬</span><span class="v">${esc(isRoot?'鎴戠殑鏂囨。':(stripHtml(doc.text)||'鏈懡鍚?))}</span></div>
            <div class="sb-intro-row"><span>鍒涘缓</span><span class="v">${fmtTs(doc.createdAt)}</span></div>
            <div class="sb-intro-row"><span>缂栬緫</span><span class="v">${fmtTs(doc.updatedAt)}</span></div>
            <div class="sb-intro-row"><span>瀛楁暟</span><span class="v">${docWordCount(doc.id)}</span></div>
            <div class="sb-intro-row"><span>鑺傜偣</span><span class="v">${docNodeCount(doc.id)}</span></div>`;
        }
        function setActiveKey(k){
          ui.activeKey=k||null;
          updateSidebarActiveState();
        }
        function updateSidebarActiveState(){
          $$('.sb-nav-item, .sb-row').forEach(el=>el.classList.remove('active'));
          const k=ui.activeKey;
          
          $$('.sb-row[role="treeitem"]').forEach(el=>{
            const on=!!(k&&k.startsWith('node:')&&el.dataset.id===k.slice(5));
            el.setAttribute('aria-selected',on?'true':'false');
          });
          if(!k)return;
          if(k.startsWith('nav:')){
            const btn=$(`.sb-nav-item[data-nav="${k.slice(4)}"]`);
            if(btn)btn.classList.add('active');
          }else if(k.startsWith('node:')){
            const id=k.slice(5);
            $$(`.sb-row[data-id="${id}"]`).forEach(el=>el.classList.add('active'));
          }else if(k.startsWith('tag:')){
            const t=k.slice(4);
            $$(`.sb-row[data-tag="${CSS.escape(t)}"]`).forEach(el=>el.classList.add('active'));
          }
        }
        function showTreeContext(e,id){
          const n=N(id);if(!n)return;
          const items=[
            {ic:'鉁忥笍',txt:'閲嶅懡鍚?,fn:()=>{appPrompt('閲嶅懡鍚嶏細',stripHtml(n.text),(t)=>{if(t===null)return;commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[id];if(!nn)return;nn.text=t.trim();nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},{snapshot:true,op:'updateText',opArgs:{id}});renderSidebar();if(ui.currentDocId===id)forceRender();});}},
            {ic:'猸?,txt:'鏄熸爣',fn:()=>{const cur=n.starred||0;const level=cur>=5?0:cur+1;actions.setStar(id,level);renderSidebar();forceRender();}}
          ];
          if(!isFolder(id)){
            items.push({ic:n._isWord?'鈻?:'馃厗',txt:n._isWord?'杞负澶х翰鏂囨。':'杞负瀵屾枃鏈枃妗?,fn:()=>{
              
              commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[id];if(!nn)return;nn._isWord=!nn._isWord;
                if(nn._isWord){if(nn.content!==undefined)nn._wordFlat=true;}
                else{delete nn._wordFlat;delete nn.content;} },{snapshot:true});
              if(N(id)._isWord)ensureWordInit(id);
              renderSidebar();forceRender();
              toast(N(id)._isWord?'宸茶浆涓哄瘜鏂囨湰':'宸茶浆涓哄ぇ绾?);
            }});
          }
          items.push({sep:true});
          items.push({ic:'馃棏',txt:'绉诲叆鍥炴敹绔?,danger:true,fn:()=>{actions.moveToTrash(id);if(ui.currentDocId===id)ui.currentDocId=null;renderSidebar();forceRender();toast('宸茬Щ鍏ュ洖鏀剁珯');}});
          showContextMenu(items,e);
        }
        function navTo(nav){
          if(nav==='starred'){
            ui.navExpanded.starred=true;
            ui.view='starred';
            saveUI();
            safeNavigate(()=>{setActiveKey('nav:starred');});
            return;
          }
          if(nav==='files'){
            
            ui.navExpanded.files=true;
            saveUI();renderSidebar();setActiveKey('nav:files');
            ui.view='auto';
            ui.currentDocId=state.rootId;
            safeNavigate(()=>{});
            return;
          }
          if(nav==='database'){
            
            ui.navExpanded.database=true;saveUI();renderSidebar();setActiveKey('nav:database');
            let dbRootId=null;
            for(const id in state.nodes){if(state.nodes[id]._isDbRoot){dbRootId=id;break;}}
            if(dbRootId&&state.nodes[dbRootId].children.length){
              ui.currentDocId=state.nodes[dbRootId].children[0];ui.view='auto';
              
              setActiveKey('node:'+ui.currentDocId);
              safeNavigate(()=>{});
            }
            return;
          }
          if(nav==='tags'){
            
            ui.navExpanded.tags=true;saveUI();renderSidebar();setActiveKey('nav:tags');
            return;
          }
          if(nav==='calendar'){
            const calRootId=getCalendarRootId();
            const _now=new Date();
            safeNavigate(()=>{
              ui.currentDocId=calRootId;ui.view='auto';
              
              ui.selectedDate=todayStr();ui.calMonth={y:_now.getFullYear(),m:_now.getMonth()};
              saveUI();setActiveKey('nav:calendar');
            });
            return;
          }
          safeNavigate(()=>{ui.view=nav;saveUI();});
        }
        function bindSidebar(){
          $$('.sb-nav-item').forEach(b=>{
            const nav=b.dataset.nav;
            b.onclick=()=>navTo(nav);
            const chev=b.querySelector('.chev');
            if(chev){
              chev.onclick=e=>{
                e.stopPropagation();
                if(nav==='starred'||nav==='files'||nav==='database'||nav==='tags'){
                  ui.navExpanded[nav]=!ui.navExpanded[nav];saveUI();renderSidebar();
                } else navTo(nav);
              };
            }
          });
          $('#btnNewDoc').onclick=e=>{
            e.stopPropagation();
            appPrompt('鏂版枃妗ｅ悕绉帮細','鏂板缓鏂囨。',(name)=>{
              if(name===null)return;
              const id=actions.createDoc((name||'鏂板缓鏂囨。').trim()||'鏂板缓鏂囨。');
              ui.currentDocId=id;ui.view='auto';saveUI();
              renderSidebar();forceRender();
              toast('宸插垱寤烘枃妗?);
            });
          };
          $('#btnNewFolder').onclick=e=>{
            e.stopPropagation();

              if(name===null)return;
              const id=actions.createDoc((name||'鏂板缓鏂囦欢澶?).trim()||'鏂板缓鏂囦欢澶?,{_isFolder:true});
              renderSidebar();forceRender();
              toast('宸插垱寤烘枃浠跺す');
            });
          };
          $('#btnNewDb').onclick=e=>{
            e.stopPropagation();
            appPrompt('鏂板缁磋〃鏍煎悕绉帮細','鏂板缓琛ㄦ牸',(name)=>{
              if(name===null)return;
              let dbRootId=null;
              for(const id in state.nodes){if(state.nodes[id]._isDbRoot){dbRootId=id;break;}}
              if(!dbRootId)dbRootId=actions.createDoc('鏁版嵁搴?,{_isDbRoot:true,_isFolder:true,dbRoot:true});
              if(!dbRootId){toast('鍒涘缓澶辫触');return;}
              const id=actions.addNode(dbRootId,null,{text:(name||'鏂板缓琛ㄦ牸').trim()||'鏂板缓琛ㄦ牸',dbRoot:true});
              if(!id){toast('鍒涘缓澶辫触');return;}
              actions.addDbRow(id,{text:'鏂版潯鐩?});
              ui.currentDocId=id;ui.view='auto';saveUI();
              ui.navExpanded.database=true;saveUI();
              renderSidebar();forceRender();
              toast('宸插垱寤哄缁磋〃鏍?);
            });
          };
          $('#btnTemplates').onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();ui.view='templates';ui.activeKey='nav:templates';saveUI();renderMain();};
          $('#btnTrash').onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();ui.view='trash';ui.activeKey='nav:trash';saveUI();renderMain();};
          $('#sortBtn').onclick=e=>{
            e.stopPropagation();
            const order=['name','type','created','updated','star'];
            const cur=ui.filesSort||'name';
            const next=order[(order.indexOf(cur)+1)%order.length];
            ui.filesSort=next;saveUI();renderSidebar();forceRender();
            const names={name:'鎸夊悕绉?,type:'鎸夌被鍨?,created:'鎸夊垱寤烘椂闂?,updated:'鎸変慨鏀规椂闂?,star:'鎸夋槦鏍囩瓑绾?};
            toast('鎺掑簭锛?+names[next]);
          };
          let searchTimer=null;
          $('#searchInput').oninput=e=>{
            clearTimeout(searchTimer);
            const q=e.target.value.trim().toLowerCase();
            searchTimer=setTimeout(()=>doSearch(q),150);
          };
        }
        function doSearch(q){
          const content=$('#content');if(!content)return;
          if(!q){forceRender();return;}
          const terms=q.split(/\s+/).filter(Boolean).map(t=>t.toLowerCase());
          content.innerHTML='';
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='files-wrap';
          wrap.innerHTML=`<div style="font-size:1.05em;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:14px">鎼滅储缁撴灉锛?{esc(q)}</div>`;
          const list=document.createElement('div');list.style.cssText='display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px';
          let found=0;
          const LIMIT=200;
          for(const id in state.nodes){
            if(found>=LIMIT)break;
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(n._deleted||id===state.rootId)continue;
            if(n._isDbRoot)continue;
            
            if(n._isCalRoot||n._isNoteRoot||n._isFolder)continue;
            const hay=nodeSearchHay(n);
            if(terms.every(t=>hay.includes(t))){
              found++;
              const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='cal-row';
              row.innerHTML=`<span class="bullet"></span><span class="text">${highlight(stripHtml(n.text),terms)}</span>`;
              row.onclick=()=>openNode(n.id);
              list.appendChild(row);
            }
          }
          wrap.appendChild(list);
          if(!found){const e2=document.createElement('div');e2.className='empty-state';e2.textContent='鏈壘鍒扮粨鏋?;wrap.appendChild(e2);}
          else if(found>=LIMIT){const m=document.createElement('div');m.style.cssText='text-align:center;color:var(--fg-dim);font-size:.78em;padding:6px';m.textContent='浠呮樉绀哄墠 '+LIMIT+' 鏉?;wrap.appendChild(m);}
          content.appendChild(wrap);
        }
        function highlight(text,terms){
          if(!text)return '';
          if(!terms||!terms.length)return esc(text);
          const lower=text.toLowerCase();
          const ranges=[];
          terms.forEach(t=>{
            if(!t)return;
            const lt=t.toLowerCase();
            if(!lt)return;
            let i=0;
            while((i=lower.indexOf(lt,i))>=0){
              ranges.push([i,i+lt.length]);
              i+=lt.length;
            }
          });
          if(!ranges.length)return esc(text);
          ranges.sort((a,b)=>a[0]-b[0]||a[1]-b[1]);
          const merged=[ranges[0]];
          for(let i=1;i<ranges.length;i++){
            const last=merged[merged.length-1];
            if(ranges[i][0]<=last[1])last[1]=Math.max(last[1],ranges[i][1]);
            else merged.push(ranges[i]);
          }
          let out='',cur=0;
          merged.forEach(([s,e])=>{
            if(s>cur)out+=esc(text.slice(cur,s));
            out+='<mark style="background:var(--accent-soft);color:var(--accent-fg)">'+esc(text.slice(s,e))+'</mark>';
            cur=e;
          });
          if(cur<text.length)out+=esc(text.slice(cur));
          return out;
        }
        
        function bindRightSidebar(){
          try{safeExec('styleWithCSS',true);}catch(e){}
          $$('[data-toggle]').forEach(el=>{el.onclick=()=>{

                safeExec('copy');toast('宸插鍒?);
              }
              else if(cmd==='cut')safeExec('cut');
              else if(cmd==='paste'){

                  navigator.clipboard.readText().then(text=>{
                    if(text){safeExec('insertText',text);}
                  }).catch(()=>toast('鏃犳硶璇诲彇鍓创鏉?));

              const doc=getCurrentDoc();if(!doc)return;
              const mode=b.dataset.expand;
              commit(s=>{
                docSubtree(doc.id).forEach(id=>{
                  if(id===doc.id)return;
                  const n=s.nodes[id];
                  if(!n)return;
                  if(mode==='all')n.collapsed=false;
                  else if(mode==='none')n.collapsed=n.children.length>0;

                  else if(mode==='l2'){const depth=nodeDepth(id)-nodeDepth(doc.id);n.collapsed=depth>=2&&n.children.length>0;}
                });
              },{snapshot:true});

        }
        function updateRightSidebar(){
