        const WIKI_RE=/\[\[([^\[\]\n]{1,80}?)\]\]/g;
        function normTitle(t){return String(t||'').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();}
        function findNodeByTitle(title){
          const t=normTitle(title);if(!t)return null;
          let fallback=null;
          for(const id in state.nodes){
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(!n||n._deleted||n.id===state.rootId)continue;
            if(normTitle(stripHtml(n.text||''))!==t)continue;
            if(isFolder(id)){if(!fallback)fallback=id;continue;}
            if(n._isCalRoot)continue;
            return id;
          }
          return fallback;
        }
        function navigateToWikiLink(title,fromEl){
          const t=normTitle(title);if(!t){toast('鏃犳晥閾炬帴');return;}
          const id=findNodeByTitle(t);
          if(id){openNode(id);return;}
          appConfirm('鑺傜偣銆?+t.slice(0,30)+'銆嶄笉瀛樺湪锛屽垱寤轰负鏂版枃妗ｅ苟璺宠浆锛?,()=>{
            const newId=actions.addNode(state.rootId,null,{text:t});
            if(newId)openNode(newId);
            else toast('鍒涘缓澶辫触');
          },()=>{});
        }
        function caretTextInfo(x,y){
          let info=null;
          try{
            if(document.caretPositionFromPoint){
              const p=document.caretPositionFromPoint(x,y);
              if(p&&p.offsetNode)info={node:p.offsetNode,offset:p.offset};
            }
            if(!info&&document.caretRangeFromPoint){
              const r=document.caretRangeFromPoint(x,y);
              if(r&&r.startContainer)info={node:r.startContainer,offset:r.startOffset};
            }
          }catch(e){}
          return info;
        }
        function linkTitleAtPoint(el,e){
          const info=caretTextInfo(e.clientX,e.clientY);
          if(!info||!info.node)return null;
          let node=info.node;
          if(node.nodeType===1){
            let found=null;let acc=0;
            Array.from(node.childNodes).forEach(ch=>{
              if(found)return;
              if(ch.nodeType===3){
                const len=(ch.textContent||'').length;
                if(info.offset>=acc&&info.offset<=acc+len)found=ch;
                acc+=len;
              }
            });
            node=found;
          }
          if(!node||node.nodeType!==3)return null;
          const text=node.textContent||'';
          const off=Math.max(0,Math.min(text.length,info.offset));
          WIKI_RE.lastIndex=0;
          let m;
          while((m=WIKI_RE.exec(text))!==null){
            const s=m.index,en=m.index+m[0].length;
            if(off>=s&&off<=en)return m[1].trim();
          }
          return null;
        }
        function outgoingLinksOf(docId){
          const set=new Map();
          docSubtree(docId).forEach(id=>{
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(!n||n._deleted)return;
            const txt=stripHtml(n.text||'');if(!txt)return;
            WIKI_RE.lastIndex=0;let m;
            while((m=WIKI_RE.exec(txt))!==null){
              const t=m[1].trim();if(t&&!set.has(t))set.set(t,id);
            }
          });
          return set;
        }
        function backlinksOf(docId){
          const doc=N(docId);if(!doc)return [];
          const docTitles=new Set();
          docSubtree(docId).forEach(id=>{
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(!n||isBlankNode(n))return;
            const t=normTitle(stripHtml(n.text||''));
            if(t)docTitles.add(t);
          });
          const groups=new Map();
          for(const id in state.nodes){
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(!n||n._deleted||id===state.rootId)continue;
            if(findOwningTopDocId(id,state)===docId)continue;
            const txt=stripHtml(n.text||'');if(!txt)continue;
            WIKI_RE.lastIndex=0;let m;let hit=false;
            while((m=WIKI_RE.exec(txt))!==null){
              if(docTitles.has(normTitle(m[1]))){hit=true;break;}
            }
            if(!hit)continue;
            const topId=findOwningTopDocId(id,state)||id;
            const g=groups.get(topId)||{topId,count:0};
            g.count++;
            groups.set(topId,g);
          }
          return Array.from(groups.values()).sort((a,b)=>b.count-a.count);
        }
        function renderBacklinkPanel(){
          const box=$('#srBacklinks');if(!box)return;
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();
          box.innerHTML='';
          const secTitle=box.closest('.sr-section')?.querySelector('.sr-title');

            box.appendChild(e);
            return;
          }
          const docTitle=stripHtml(doc.text||'').trim()||'鏈懡鍚?;
          const out=outgoingLinksOf(doc.id);
          const backs=backlinksOf(doc.id);
          if(secTitle)secTitle.lastChild.textContent='閾炬帴 / 鍙嶉摼锛堝嚭 '+out.size+' 路 鍥?'+backs.length+'锛?;
          const group=(label,count)=>{

            return g;
          };
          const icIn=`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10 13a5 5 0 0 0 7.5.5l3-3a5 5 0 0 0-7-7l-1.7 1.7"/><path d="M14 11a5 5 0 0 0-7.5-.5l-3 3a5 5 0 0 0 7 7l1.7-1.7"/></svg>`;
          const icOut=`<svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M7 17L17 7"/><path d="M9 7h8v8"/></svg>`;
          const mkRow=(title,targetId,{missing=false,create=false,count=null}={})=>{

            row.appendChild(t);

            if(missing){
              const b=document.createElement('span');b.className='bl-create';b.textContent='鍒涘缓';
              b.onclick=e=>{e.stopPropagation();const newId=actions.addNode(state.rootId,null,{text:title});if(newId)openNode(newId);else toast('鍒涘缓澶辫触');};
              row.appendChild(b);
            }
            row.onclick=()=>{if(targetId)openNode(targetId);};
            return row;
          };
          
          if(out.size){
            const g=group('鍑洪摼',out.size);
            Array.from(out.entries()).sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0],'zh')).forEach(([t,fromId])=>{
              const targetId=findNodeByTitle(t);
              g.appendChild(mkRow(t,targetId,{missing:!targetId}));
            });
            box.appendChild(g);
          }else{
            const g=group('鍑洪摼',0);
            const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='bl-empty';
            e.textContent='鏈枃妗ｈ繕娌℃湁 [[閾炬帴]]銆?;
            g.appendChild(e);box.appendChild(g);
          }
          
          if(backs.length){
            const g=group('鍙嶉摼',backs.length);
            backs.forEach(b=>{
              const src=N(b.topId);
              const title=src?stripHtml(src.text||'').trim():'鏈懡鍚?;
              g.appendChild(mkRow(title,b.topId,{count:b.count}));
            });
            box.appendChild(g);
          }else{
            const g=group('鍙嶉摼',0);
            const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='bl-empty';
            e.textContent='鏆傛棤鍏朵粬鏂囨。閾炬帴鍒版湰鏂囨。銆?;
            g.appendChild(e);box.appendChild(g);
          }
          const hint=document.createElement('div');hint.className='bl-hint';
          hint.textContent='鎻愮ず锛氱紪杈戜腑 Ctrl / 鈱?+ 鐐瑰嚮 [[閾炬帴]] 璺宠浆锛涚偣銆屽垱寤恒€嶇敓鎴愮己澶辩殑鑺傜偣銆?;
          box.appendChild(hint);
        }
        
        const REMIND_SENT_KEY='juju.remind.sent';
        const NOTIF_ICON='data:image/svg+xml,%3Csvg xmlns=\'http:
        let _remindBooted=false;
        function loadRemindSent(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(REMIND_SENT_KEY)||'{}')||{};}catch(e){return{};}}
        function saveRemindSent(map){try{localStorage.setItem(REMIND_SENT_KEY,JSON.stringify(map));}catch(e){} if(IS_DESKTOP)scheduleDesktopSave();}
        function remindersSupported(){return typeof Notification!=='undefined';}
        function requestReminderPermission(){
          if(!remindersSupported()){toast('褰撳墠娴忚鍣ㄤ笉鏀寔绯荤粺閫氱煡');return false;}
          if(Notification.permission==='granted')return true;
          if(Notification.permission==='denied'){toast('閫氱煡鏉冮檺宸茶娴忚鍣ㄦ嫆缁濓紝璇峰湪娴忚鍣ㄨ缃腑寮€鍚?);return false;}
          Notification.requestPermission().then(p=>{
            if(p==='granted'){toast('绯荤粺鎻愰啋宸插紑鍚?);refreshRemindStatus();}
            else toast('鏈幏寰楅€氱煡鏉冮檺锛屽埌鐐瑰皢浠呭湪搴旂敤鍐呮彁绀?);
          }).catch(()=>{toast('璇锋眰閫氱煡鏉冮檺澶辫触');});
          return false;
        }
        function sendSystemNotification(title,opts){
          if(!remindersSupported()||Notification.permission!=='granted')return false;
          try{
            const n=new Notification(title,Object.assign({icon:NOTIF_ICON,tag:'juju-remind'},opts||{}));
            n.onclick=()=>{try{window.focus();}catch(e){} try{n.close();}catch(e){} };
            return true;
          }catch(e){return false;}
        }
        let _reminderTimer=null; 
        function checkDueReminders(){
          if(!ui||!ui.reminderEnabled)return;
          const today=todayStr();
          const sent=loadRemindSent();
          for(const k in sent){if(String(sent[k])<today)delete sent[k];}
          const now=new Date();
          const nowMin=now.getHours()*60+now.getMinutes();
          const todos=calendarTodosOnDate(today).filter(n=>!n.done&&n.time);
          const due=[];
          todos.forEach(n=>{
            const parts=String(n.time).split(':');
            const hh=parseInt(parts[0],10),mm=parseInt(parts[1],10);
            if(isNaN(hh)||isNaN(mm))return;
            if(nowMin<hh*60+mm)return;
            if(sent[n.id]===today)return;
            due.push(n);
          });
          if(!due.length){saveRemindSent(sent);return;}
          if(!_remindBooted){
            _remindBooted=true;
            due.forEach(n=>{sent[n.id]=today;});
            saveRemindSent(sent);
            const names=due.slice(0,3).map(n=>stripHtml(n.text||'').slice(0,20)).join('銆?);
            const more=due.length>3?' 绛?'+due.length+' 鏉?:'';
            sendSystemNotification('JU 路 宸插埌鐐瑰緟鍔?,{body:names+more+' 宸插埌鐐规湭瀹屾垚',tag:'juju-summary'});
            toast('鈴?浠婃棩宸叉湁 '+due.length+' 鏉″緟鍔炲埌鐐规湭瀹屾垚');
            return;
          }
          
          due.forEach(n=>{sent[n.id]=today;});
          saveRemindSent(sent);
          const names=due.slice(0,3).map(n=>stripHtml(n.text||'').slice(0,20)).join('銆?);
          const more=due.length>3?' 绛?'+due.length+' 鏉?:'';
          sendSystemNotification('JU 路 宸插埌鐐瑰緟鍔?,{body:names+more+' 宸插埌鐐规湭瀹屾垚',tag:'juju-summary'});
          toast('鈴?浠婃棩宸叉湁 '+due.length+' 鏉″緟鍔炲埌鐐规湭瀹屾垚');
        }
        function updateRemindBadge(){
          const badge=$('#remindBadge');if(!badge)return;
          const today=todayStr();
          const count=calendarTodosOnDate(today).filter(n=>!n.done&&!isBlankNode(n)).length;
          badge.hidden=count===0;
          badge.textContent=count>99?'99+':String(count);
        }
        function renderRemindPanel(){
          const list=$('#remindList');if(!list)return;
          list.innerHTML='';
          const today=todayStr();
          const todos=calendarTodosOnDate(today).filter(n=>!isBlankNode(n)&&n.showInCal!==false);
          todos.sort((a,b)=>(a.done?1:0)-(b.done?1:0)||(a.time||'99:99').localeCompare(b.time||'99:99')||(a.createdAt-b.createdAt));
          if(!todos.length){
            const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='bl-empty';e.textContent='浠婃棩鏆傛棤寰呭姙';
            list.appendChild(e);
          }else{
            todos.forEach(n=>{
              const item=document.createElement('div');item.className='remind-item'+(n.done?' done':'');
              if(n.tags&&n.tags.length){
                const d=document.createElement('span');d.className='ri-tag';d.style.background=getTagColor(n.tags[0]);
                item.appendChild(d);
              }
              const t=document.createElement('span');t.className='ri-time';
              t.textContent=n.time||'--';
              if(!n.time)t.style.opacity='.45';
              item.appendChild(t);
              const txt=document.createElement('span');txt.className='ri-text';txt.textContent=stripHtml(n.text||'');
              item.appendChild(txt);
              item.title='鐐瑰嚮鎵撳紑璇ュ緟鍔?;
              item.onclick=()=>{
                toggleRemindPanel(false);
                ui.view='auto';ui.activeKey='nav:calendar';ui.selectedDate=today;
                ui.currentDocId=n.id;ui.focusId=n.id;
                saveUI();forceRender();
              };
              list.appendChild(item);
            });
          }
          refreshRemindStatus();
        }
        function refreshRemindStatus(){
          const st=$('#remindStatus');if(!st)return;
          const on=!!(ui.reminderEnabled&&remindersSupported()&&Notification.permission==='granted');
          st.classList.toggle('on',on);
          const label=st.querySelector('span:last-child');
          if(label)label.textContent=on?'绯荤粺鎻愰啋宸插紑鍚?:'绯荤粺鎻愰啋鏈紑鍚?+(remindersSupported()&&Notification.permission==='denied'?'锛堟潈闄愯鎷掞級':'');
        }
        function toggleRemindPanel(force){
          const p=$('#remindPanel');if(!p)return;
          const show=typeof force==='boolean'?force:!p.classList.contains('on');
          p.classList.toggle('on',show);
          if(show){
            
            placePop(p,320,420,$('#btnBell')||null);
            initDragClose(p,()=>toggleRemindPanel(false));
            ensureElInViewport(p);
            renderRemindPanel();
          }
        }
        function bindReminder(){
          const bell=$('#btnBell');
          if(bell)bell.onclick=()=>toggleRemindPanel();
          
          const gocal=$('#remindGoCal');
          if(gocal)gocal.onclick=()=>{
            toggleRemindPanel(false);
            ui.view='auto';ui.activeKey='nav:calendar';ui.selectedDate=todayStr();
            saveUI();forceRender();
          };
          const sw=$('#setReminder');
          if(sw){
            sw.classList.toggle('on',!!ui.reminderEnabled);
            sw.onclick=()=>{
              if(ui.reminderEnabled){
                ui.reminderEnabled=false;saveUI();sw.classList.remove('on');
                
                if(_reminderTimer){clearInterval(_reminderTimer);_reminderTimer=null;}
                toast('绯荤粺鎻愰啋宸插叧闂?);refreshRemindStatus();return;
              }
              if(!remindersSupported()){toast('褰撳墠娴忚鍣ㄤ笉鏀寔绯荤粺閫氱煡');return;}
              if(Notification.permission==='denied'){toast('閫氱煡鏉冮檺宸茶娴忚鍣ㄦ嫆缁濓紝璇峰厛鍦ㄦ祻瑙堝櫒璁剧疆涓紑鍚?);return;}
              ui.reminderEnabled=true;saveUI();sw.classList.add('on');
              
              if(!_reminderTimer){checkDueReminders();_reminderTimer=setInterval(checkDueReminders,30000);}
              if(Notification.permission==='granted'){toast('绯荤粺鎻愰啋宸插紑鍚?);}
              else{
                toast('姝ｅ湪璇锋眰绯荤粺閫氱煡鏉冮檺鈥?);
                Notification.requestPermission().then(p=>{
                  if(p!=='granted')toast('鏈幏寰楅€氱煡鏉冮檺锛屽埌鐐瑰皢浠呭湪搴旂敤鍐呮彁绀?);
                  else toast('绯荤粺鎻愰啋宸插紑鍚?);
                  refreshRemindStatus();
                }).catch(()=>{});
              }
              refreshRemindStatus();
            };
          }
        }
        function initReminder(){
          updateRemindBadge();
          refreshRemindStatus();
          if(ui.reminderEnabled){
            setTimeout(checkDueReminders,1500);
            
            if(!_reminderTimer)_reminderTimer=setInterval(checkDueReminders,30000);
          }
        }

        async function exportFullBackup(){
          try{
            const backup={
              app:'juju',ver:'fixed18',type:'full-backup',
              exportedAt:new Date().toISOString(),
              state:safeClone(state),
              ui:uiSnapshot(),
              graphpos:clone(_graphPositions||{}),
              versions:null
            };
            try{
              await VDB.ensure();
              
              const SNAP_BATCH=200;
              const snapRows=[];let snapLastKey=null;
              for(;;){
                const batch=await VDB.tx('snapshots','readonly',async(store)=>{
                  const range=snapLastKey===null?null:IDBKeyRange.lowerBound(snapLastKey,true);
                  const req=store.openCursor(range);
                  const arr=[];
                  await new Promise((res,rej)=>{
                    req.onsuccess=e=>{
                      const cur=e.target.result;
                      if(cur&&arr.length<SNAP_BATCH){arr.push(cur.value);snapLastKey=cur.key;cur.continue();}
                      else res();
                    };
                    req.onerror=()=>rej(req.error);
                  });
                  return arr;
                });
                snapRows.push.apply(snapRows,batch);
                if(!batch.length||batch.length<SNAP_BATCH)break;
              }
              backup.versions=snapRows;
            }catch(e){backup.versions=null;}
            download('JU-澶囦唤-'+todayStr()+'.json',JSON.stringify(backup,null,2),'application/json');
            toast('宸蹭笅杞藉畬鏁村浠斤紙鍚暟鎹?/ 鐣岄潰璁剧疆 / 鍥捐氨浣嶇疆 / 鍘嗗彶鐗堟湰锛?);
          }catch(e){
            toast('澶囦唤澶辫触锛?+(e.message||'鏈煡閿欒'));
          }
        }
        async function restoreVersions(list){
          if(!Array.isArray(list)||!list.length)return;
          try{
            await VDB.ensure();
            await VDB.tx('snapshots','readwrite',async(store)=>{
              list.forEach(s=>{if(s&&s.id)store.put(s);});
            });
          }catch(e){}
        }
        function nodeDepth(id){let d=0,cur=id;const _vis=new Set(); 
          while(cur&&N(cur)&&N(cur).parent){if(_vis.has(cur))break;_vis.add(cur);cur=N(cur).parent;if(cur===state.rootId)break;d++;}
          return d;}
        
        const COLORS_DAY=['#2E2218','#5A4B3B','#8B7C66','#9E2E24','#9E6F14','#2E59A7','#2E7D4F','#7B4BA8','#F2F6D0','#FBFCF0','#D6E0F0','#F5D6D2','#F5EBC8','#D6E2F5','#D2EEDC','#E8D6F5','#FFFFFF','#000000','#FF6B6B','#FFD93D','#6BCB77','#4D96FF','#B983FF','#FF9F45'];
        const COLORS_NIGHT=['#EDF2A8','#F2F7C8','#BCC87E','#F0A096','#F0D278','#8AB4F0','#8FD9A8','#D6B8F0','#3A2E23','#5A4B3B','#3B4A6E','#6E3B3B','#6E5E3B','#3B4A6E','#3B6E4A','#5E3B6E','#FFFFFF','#000000','#FF8A8A','#FFE066','#8FE09B','#6BAAFF','#C9A0FF','#FFB366'];
        let colorPickerEl=null;
        function openColorPicker(anchorEl,callback,current,opts){
          closeColorPicker();closeSizePicker();
          const isNight=document.documentElement.dataset.theme==='night';
          const palette=isNight?COLORS_NIGHT:COLORS_DAY;
          const picker=document.createElement('div');picker.className='color-picker';
          const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='cp-grid';
          const curLower=String(current||'').toLowerCase();
          const isTransparent=curLower==='transparent'||curLower==='rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)'||curLower==='rgba(0,0,0,0)'||!current;
          if(opts&&opts.transparent){
            
            const sw=document.createElement('div');sw.className='cp-swatch tp';
            sw.title='鏃犺儗鏅壊锛堥€忔槑锛?;
            if(isTransparent)sw.classList.add('on');
            sw.onclick=()=>{
              callback('transparent');
              picker.querySelectorAll('.cp-swatch').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('on'));sw.classList.add('on');
            };
            grid.appendChild(sw);
          }
          palette.forEach(c=>{
            const sw=document.createElement('div');sw.className='cp-swatch';
            if(!(opts&&opts.transparent)&&current&&current.toLowerCase()===c.toLowerCase())sw.classList.add('on');
            sw.style.background=c;sw.title=c;
            sw.onclick=()=>{
              callback(c);
              picker.querySelectorAll('.cp-swatch').forEach(s=>s.classList.remove('on'));sw.classList.add('on');
              const inp=picker.querySelector('input[type=color]');const hex=picker.querySelector('.cp-hex');
              if(inp)inp.value=c;if(hex)hex.value=c.toUpperCase();
            };
            grid.appendChild(sw);
          });
          picker.appendChild(grid);
          const customRow=document.createElement('div');customRow.className='cp-custom';customRow.innerHTML=`<label>鑷畾涔?/label>`;
          const input=document.createElement('input');input.type='color';input.value=(isTransparent?'#FBFCF0':(current||'#000000'));
          const hexInput=document.createElement('input');hexInput.type='text';hexInput.className='cp-hex';hexInput.value=input.value.toUpperCase();
          input.oninput=()=>{hexInput.value=input.value.toUpperCase();callback(input.value);};
          hexInput.oninput=()=>{if(/^#[0-9a-fA-F]{6}$/.test(hexInput.value)){input.value=hexInput.value;callback(hexInput.value);}};
          customRow.appendChild(input);customRow.appendChild(hexInput);
          picker.appendChild(customRow);
          const footer=document.createElement('div');footer.className='cp-footer';
          const preview=document.createElement('div');preview.className='cp-preview';preview.style.background=input.value;
          const okBtn=document.createElement('button');okBtn.className='btn primary';okBtn.textContent='纭畾';okBtn.onclick=()=>{callback(input.value);closeColorPicker();};
          const cancelBtn=document.createElement('button');cancelBtn.className='btn';cancelBtn.textContent='鍙栨秷';cancelBtn.onclick=closeColorPicker;
          footer.appendChild(preview);footer.appendChild(cancelBtn);footer.appendChild(okBtn);
          picker.appendChild(footer);
          document.body.appendChild(picker);
          picker.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
            if(e.target.closest('.cp-swatch'))e.preventDefault();
          });
          placePop(picker,260,300,anchorEl);
          initDragClose(picker,()=>closeColorPicker());
          colorPickerEl=picker;
          setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener('mousedown',onDocClickForPicker),0);
        }
        function closeColorPicker(){if(colorPickerEl){colorPickerEl.remove();colorPickerEl=null;}document.removeEventListener('mousedown',onDocClickForPicker);}
        function onDocClickForPicker(e){if(!e.target.closest('.color-picker'))closeColorPicker();}
        
        function clearBgFromSelection(){
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(!sel||sel.rangeCount===0||sel.isCollapsed){toast('璇峰厛閫変腑鏂囧瓧');return;}
          safeExec('hiliteColor','transparent');
          const range=sel.getRangeAt(0);
          const sc=range.commonAncestorContainer;
          const host=(sc.nodeType===1)?sc:sc.parentNode;
          if(!host||!host.closest||!host.closest('[contenteditable="true"]'))return;
          cleanupBg(host);
          toast('宸叉竻闄よ儗鏅鑹?);
        }
        function cleanupBg(root){
          const unwrap=[];const dead=[];
          (function walk(n){
            Array.from(n.children||[]).forEach(ch=>{
              walk(ch);
              if(ch.style){
                const v=(ch.style.backgroundColor||'').toLowerCase();
                if(v==='transparent'||v==='rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)'||v==='rgba(0,0,0,0)')ch.style.removeProperty('background-color');
              }
              if(!ch.getAttribute('style')&&(ch.tagName==='SPAN'||ch.tagName==='FONT'))unwrap.push(ch);
              if(EMPTY_INLINE_TAGS.has(ch.tagName)&&ch.children.length===0&&!ch.textContent)dead.push(ch);
            });
          })(root);
          unwrap.forEach(el=>{const p=el.parentNode;if(p){while(el.firstChild)p.insertBefore(el.firstChild,el);p.removeChild(el);}});
          dead.forEach(n=>{try{n.remove();}catch(e){}});
        }
        let sizePickerEl=null;
        
        const FONT_SIZE_RATIOS={'1':0.7,'2':0.85,'3':1.0,'4':1.2,'5':1.5,'6':2.0,'7':2.8};
        function currentBaseFontPx(){
          const v=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(document.documentElement).getPropertyValue('--font-size'));
          return (v&&v>0)?v:14;
        }
        function fontSizePxFor(level){
          const ratio=FONT_SIZE_RATIOS[level];
          if(!ratio)return null;
          return Number((currentBaseFontPx()*ratio).toFixed(1));
        }
        
        const EMPTY_INLINE_TAGS=new Set(['SPAN','FONT','B','I','U','S','EM','STRONG','MARK','DEL','INS','SMALL','SUB','SUP']);
        function cleanupEmptyInline(root){
          if(!root)return;
          const dead=[];
          (function walk(n){
            Array.from(n.children||[]).forEach(ch=>{
              if(EMPTY_INLINE_TAGS.has(ch.tagName)&&ch.children.length===0&&!ch.textContent)dead.push(ch);
              else walk(ch);
            });
          })(root);
          dead.forEach(n=>{try{n.remove();}catch(e){}});
        }
        
        function collectRangeIntersectElements(host,range){
          const out=[];
          (function walk(n){
            if(n.nodeType!==1)return;
            if(range.intersectsNode(n))out.push(n);
            Array.from(n.children||[]).forEach(walk);
          })(host);
          return out;
        }
        
        function unwrapStylelessSpans(host){
          const spans=[];
          (function walk(n){
            Array.from(n.children||[]).forEach(ch=>{
              if(ch.tagName==='SPAN'&&!(ch.getAttribute('style')||'').trim())spans.push(ch);
              walk(ch);
            });
          })(host);
          spans.forEach(el=>{
            const p=el.parentNode;if(!p)return;
            while(el.firstChild)p.insertBefore(el.firstChild,el);
            p.removeChild(el);
          });
        }
        function applyFontSize(level){
          const px=fontSizePxFor(level);if(!px)return;
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(!sel||sel.rangeCount===0||sel.isCollapsed){toast('璇峰厛鍦ㄦ鏂囦腑閫変腑瑕佽皟鏁村瓧鍙风殑鏂囨湰');return;}
          const range=sel.getRangeAt(0);
          const sc=range.commonAncestorContainer;
          const startEl=(sc.nodeType===1)?sc:sc.parentNode;
          const host=startEl&&startEl.closest?startEl.closest('[contenteditable="true"]'):null;
          if(!host){toast('璇峰厛鍦ㄦ鏂囦腑閫変腑鏂囧瓧');return;}
          
          collectRangeIntersectElements(host,range).forEach(el=>{
            if(el.style&&el.style.fontSize)el.style.removeProperty('font-size');
          });
          const span=document.createElement('span');
          span.style.fontSize=px+'px';
          try{
            range.surroundContents(span);
          }catch(e){
            
            try{span.appendChild(range.extractContents());range.insertNode(span);}
            catch(e2){toast('鏃犳硶璋冩暣璇ラ€夊尯瀛楀彿');return;}
          }
          unwrapStylelessSpans(host);
          cleanupEmptyInline(host);
        }
        
        function clearFontSizeFromSelection(){
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(!sel||sel.rangeCount===0||sel.isCollapsed){toast('璇峰厛鍦ㄦ鏂囦腑閫変腑鏂囧瓧');return;}
          const range=sel.getRangeAt(0);
          const sc=range.commonAncestorContainer;
          const startEl=(sc.nodeType===1)?sc:sc.parentNode;
          const host=startEl&&startEl.closest?startEl.closest('[contenteditable="true"]'):null;
          if(!host){toast('璇峰厛鍦ㄦ鏂囦腑閫変腑鏂囧瓧');return;}
          
          collectRangeIntersectElements(host,range).forEach(el=>{
            if(el.style&&el.style.fontSize)el.style.removeProperty('font-size');
          });
          unwrapStylelessSpans(host);
          cleanupEmptyInline(host);
          toast('宸叉竻闄ゅ瓧鍙凤紙鎭㈠姝ｆ枃澶у皬锛?);
        }
        function openSizePicker(anchorEl,callback){
          closeSizePicker();closeColorPicker();
          const picker=document.createElement('div');picker.className='color-picker';
          const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='sp-grid';
          
          let curPx=null;
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(sel&&sel.rangeCount&&!sel.isCollapsed){
            const r=sel.getRangeAt(0);
            const c=(r.commonAncestorContainer.nodeType===1)?r.commonAncestorContainer:r.commonAncestorContainer.parentElement;
            if(c)curPx=parseFloat(getComputedStyle(c).fontSize)||null;
          }
          [{v:'1',t:'灏?1'},{v:'2',t:'灏?2'},{v:'3',t:'姝ｆ枃 3'},{v:'4',t:'涓?4'},{v:'5',t:'澶?5'},{v:'6',t:'鐗瑰ぇ 6'},{v:'7',t:'鏈€澶?7'}].forEach(s=>{
            const px=fontSizePxFor(s.v);
            const btn=document.createElement('button');btn.className='sp-btn';
            btn.innerHTML=`<span>${s.t}</span><span class="sp-px">${px}px</span>`;
            if(curPx!=null&&Math.abs(curPx-px)<1.5)btn.classList.add('on');
            btn.onclick=()=>{callback(s.v);closeSizePicker();};
            grid.appendChild(btn);
          });
          picker.appendChild(grid);
          const clearBtn=document.createElement('button');clearBtn.className='sp-clear';clearBtn.textContent='娓呴櫎瀛楀彿锛堟仮澶嶆鏂囧ぇ灏忥級';
          clearBtn.onclick=()=>{clearFontSizeFromSelection();closeSizePicker();};
          picker.appendChild(clearBtn);
          document.body.appendChild(picker);
          picker.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>e.preventDefault());
          placePop(picker,180,230,anchorEl);
          initDragClose(picker,()=>closeSizePicker());
          sizePickerEl=picker;
          setTimeout(()=>document.addEventListener('mousedown',onDocClickForSizePicker),0);
        }
        function closeSizePicker(){if(sizePickerEl){sizePickerEl.remove();sizePickerEl=null;}document.removeEventListener('mousedown',onDocClickForSizePicker);}
        function onDocClickForSizePicker(e){if(!e.target.closest('.color-picker'))closeSizePicker();}
        
        let _fmtPainter={active:false,fmt:null,srcKey:null,btn:null};
        let _fpTimer=null;
        function selectionKey(){
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(!sel||sel.rangeCount===0)return '';
          const r=sel.getRangeAt(0);
          const path=n=>{
            let el=n.nodeType===1?n:(n.parentElement||null);
            const p=[];
            while(el&&el!==document.body){
              const parent=el.parentElement;
              p.unshift(parent?Array.from(parent.children).indexOf(el):-1);
              el=parent;
            }
            return p.join('.');
          };
          return path(r.startContainer)+'|'+r.startOffset+'|'+path(r.endContainer)+'|'+r.endOffset+'|'+sel.toString();
        }
        function captureFormat(){
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(!sel||sel.rangeCount===0||sel.isCollapsed)return null;
          const range=sel.getRangeAt(0);
          let node=range.startContainer;
          if(node.nodeType===3)node=node.parentElement;
          if(!node||node.nodeType!==1||!node.closest||!node.closest('[contenteditable="true"]'))return null;
          const root=node.closest('[contenteditable="true"]');
          const fmt={fontSize:null,color:null,bg:null,bold:false,italic:false,underline:false,strike:false};
          let el=node;
          while(el&&el!==root){
            const st=el.style||{};
            if(!fmt.fontSize&&st.fontSize)fmt.fontSize=st.fontSize;
            if(!fmt.color&&st.color)fmt.color=st.color;
            if(!fmt.bg&&st.backgroundColor)fmt.bg=st.backgroundColor;
            const cs=getComputedStyle(el);
            if(cs.fontWeight==='bold'||parseInt(cs.fontWeight)>=600)fmt.bold=true;
            if(cs.fontStyle==='italic')fmt.italic=true;
            const dec=cs.textDecorationLine||'';
            if(dec.includes('underline'))fmt.underline=true;
            if(dec.includes('line-through'))fmt.strike=true;
            el=el.parentElement;
          }
          if(!fmt.fontSize&&!fmt.color&&!fmt.bg&&!fmt.bold&&!fmt.italic&&!fmt.underline&&!fmt.strike)return null;
          return fmt;
        }
        function applyCapturedFormat(fmt){
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(!sel||sel.rangeCount===0||sel.isCollapsed)return false;
          const range=sel.getRangeAt(0);
          const sc=range.commonAncestorContainer;
          const host=(sc.nodeType===1)?sc:sc.parentNode;
          if(!host||!host.closest||!host.closest('[contenteditable="true"]'))return false;
          const span=document.createElement('span');
          if(fmt.fontSize)span.style.fontSize=fmt.fontSize;
          if(fmt.color)span.style.color=fmt.color;
          if(fmt.bg){
            const b=String(fmt.bg).toLowerCase();
            if(b!=='transparent'&&b!=='rgba(0, 0, 0, 0)'&&b!=='rgba(0,0,0,0)')span.style.backgroundColor=fmt.bg;
          }
          if(fmt.bold)span.style.fontWeight='bold';
          if(fmt.italic)span.style.fontStyle='italic';
          const dec=[];
          if(fmt.underline)dec.push('underline');
          if(fmt.strike)dec.push('line-through');
          if(dec.length)span.style.textDecoration=dec.join(' ');
          if(!span.getAttribute('style'))return false;
          try{range.surroundContents(span);}
          catch(e){
            try{span.appendChild(range.extractContents());range.insertNode(span);}
            catch(e2){return false;}
          }
          cleanupEmptyInline(host);
          return true;
        }
        function deactivatePainter(){
          _fmtPainter.active=false;_fmtPainter.fmt=null;_fmtPainter.srcKey=null;
          if(_fmtPainter.btn)_fmtPainter.btn.classList.remove('on');
          _fmtPainter.btn=null;
          if(_fpTimer){clearTimeout(_fpTimer);_fpTimer=null;}
        }
        function toggleFormatPainter(btn){
          if(_fmtPainter.active){deactivatePainter();toast('宸插彇娑堟牸寮忓埛');return;}
          const fmt=captureFormat();
          if(!fmt){toast('璇峰厛閫変腑甯︽牸寮忕殑鏂囧瓧锛堝鍔犵矖/棰滆壊/瀛楀彿锛?);return;}
          _fmtPainter={active:true,fmt:fmt,srcKey:selectionKey(),btn:btn};
          btn.classList.add('on');
          toast('宸叉嬀鍙栨牸寮忥紝璇烽€変腑鐩爣鏂囧瓧鍚庡皢鑷姩搴旂敤');
        }
        function onSelectionChangeForPainter(){
          if(!_fmtPainter.active)return;
          const sel=window.getSelection();
          if(!sel||sel.rangeCount===0||sel.isCollapsed)return;
          const t=String(sel);
          if(!t||!t.trim())return;
          const key=selectionKey();
          if(key===_fmtPainter.srcKey)return; 
          if(_fpTimer)clearTimeout(_fpTimer);
          _fpTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
            if(!_fmtPainter.active)return;
            const sel2=window.getSelection();
            if(!sel2||sel2.rangeCount===0||sel2.isCollapsed)return;
            if(selectionKey()===_fmtPainter.srcKey)return;
            const ok=applyCapturedFormat(_fmtPainter.fmt);
            deactivatePainter();
            toast(ok?'宸插簲鐢ㄦ牸寮忓埛':'鏃犳硶鍦ㄧ洰鏍囨枃瀛椾笂搴旂敤鏍煎紡');
          },250);
        }
        function onKeyForPainter(e){
          if(e.key==='Escape'&&_fmtPainter.active){deactivatePainter();toast('宸插彇娑堟牸寮忓埛');}
        }
        
        function renderStarredView(){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='files-wrap';
          const header=document.createElement('div');
          header.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin-bottom:14px';
          const back=document.createElement('button');
          back.className='btn';back.style.fontSize='.82em';
          back.textContent='鈫?杩斿洖';
          back.onclick=()=>{ui.view='auto';saveUI();safeNavigate(()=>{setActiveKey('nav:files');});};
          const title=document.createElement('div');
          title.style.cssText='font-size:1.05em;font-weight:700;color:var(--title)';
          title.textContent='鏄熸爣鏂囦欢';
          header.appendChild(back);header.appendChild(title);
          wrap.appendChild(header);
          const items=[];

          const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='files-grid';
          items.forEach(n=>grid.appendChild(renderFileCard(n.id,n,false)));
          wrap.appendChild(grid);return wrap;
        }

        function calStatsScope(){
          const now=new Date();
          const cm=ui.calMonth||{y:now.getFullYear(),m:now.getMonth()};
          const y=cm.y,m=cm.m;
          const calView=ui.calView||'month';
          if(calView==='year')return y+'骞?;
          if(calView==='week'){
            const selD=parseYmd(ui.selectedDate||todayStr());
            const dow=selD.getDay();
            const sun=new Date(selD.getFullYear(),selD.getMonth(),selD.getDate()-dow);
            const sat=new Date(sun.getFullYear(),sun.getMonth(),sun.getDate()+6);
            return sun.getFullYear()+'骞?+(sun.getMonth()+1)+'鏈?+sun.getDate()+'鏃?'+(sat.getMonth()+1)+'鏈?+sat.getDate()+'鏃?;
          }
          return y+'骞?+(m+1)+'鏈?;
        }
        function buildStatsBlock(){
          const now=new Date();
          const calView=ui.calView||'month';
          const cm=ui.calMonth||{y:now.getFullYear(),m:now.getMonth()};
          const y=cm.y,m=cm.m;
          
          const _dayIdx=new Map();
          for(const id in state.nodes){
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(n.id===state.rootId||n._deleted)continue;
            if(isFolder(n.id))continue;
            if(n.date){if(!_dayIdx.has(n.date))_dayIdx.set(n.date,[]);_dayIdx.get(n.date).push(n);}
          }
          const _dayStats=ds=>{
            const list=(_dayIdx.get(ds)||[]).filter(n=>!isBlankNode(n));
            return{done:list.filter(n=>n.done).length,total:list.length};
          };
          let days=[];
          if(calView==='year'){
            for(let mm=0;mm<12;mm++){
              const daysInMonth=new Date(y,mm+1,0).getDate();
              let total=0,done=0;
              for(let dd=1;dd<=daysInMonth;dd++){
                const s=_dayStats(ymd(new Date(y,mm,dd)));
                total+=s.total;done+=s.done;
              }
              days.push({done,total,label:(mm+1)+'鏈?});
            }
          }else if(calView==='week'){
            const selD=parseYmd(ui.selectedDate||todayStr());
            const dow=selD.getDay();
            const sun=new Date(selD.getFullYear(),selD.getMonth(),selD.getDate()-dow);
            for(let i=0;i<7;i++){
              const d=new Date(sun.getFullYear(),sun.getMonth(),sun.getDate()+i);
              const s=_dayStats(ymd(d));
              days.push({done:s.done,total:s.total,label:(d.getMonth()+1)+'/'+d.getDate()});
            }
          }else{
            const daysInMonth=new Date(y,m+1,0).getDate();
            for(let dd=1;dd<=daysInMonth;dd++){
              const d=new Date(y,m,dd);
              const s=_dayStats(ymd(d));
              days.push({done:s.done,total:s.total,label:String(dd)});
            }
          }
          const block=document.createElement('div');
          const totalDone=days.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.done,0);
          const totalAll=days.reduce((s,d)=>s+d.total,0);
          const rate=totalAll>0?Math.round(totalDone/totalAll*100):0;
          const activeDays=days.filter(d=>d.total>0).length;
          const summary=document.createElement('div');summary.style.cssText='display:flex;gap:8px;margin-bottom:8px;flex-wrap:wrap';
          [{label:'鎬诲畬鎴?,val:totalDone,cls:'var(--accent)'},{label:'鎬讳换鍔?,val:totalAll,cls:'var(--fg)'},{label:'瀹屾垚鐜?,val:rate+'%',cls:'var(--accent)'},{label:'娲昏穬澶╂暟',val:activeDays,cls:'var(--fg)'}].forEach(c=>{
            const card=document.createElement('div');
            card.style.cssText='flex:1;min-width:64px;padding:6px 8px;background:var(--bg-panel2);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px;text-align:center';
            card.innerHTML=`<div style="font-size:1.1em;font-weight:700;color:${c.cls}">${c.val}</div><div style="font-size:.66em;color:var(--fg-dim);margin-top:1px">${c.label}</div>`;
            summary.appendChild(card);
          });
          block.appendChild(summary);
          const chart=document.createElement('div');chart.className='stats-chart';
          chart.style.cssText='display:flex;align-items:flex-end;gap:3px;height:56px;padding:4px 0;border-top:1px solid var(--line-soft)';
          days.forEach(d=>{
            const h=d.total>0?Math.max(4,Math.round(d.done/d.total*100)):0;
            const bar=document.createElement('div');
            bar.style.cssText='flex:1;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:flex-end;height:100%;min-width:0;overflow:hidden';
            bar.innerHTML=`<span style="font-size:.6em;color:var(--fg-dim);margin-bottom:2px">${d.done}</span><div style="width:100%;max-width:22px;background:var(--accent);border-radius:2px 2px 0 0;min-height:3px;height:${h}%;opacity:${d.total>0?0.85:0.2}"></div><span style="font-size:.58em;color:var(--fg-dim);margin-top:2px">${d.label}</span>`;
            chart.appendChild(bar);
          });
          block.appendChild(chart);
          return block;
        }
        function renderStatsView(){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='files-wrap';wrap.style.padding='20px 24px';
          wrap.appendChild(buildStatsBlock());
          return wrap;
        }
        const TAG_COLORS=['#8A423B','#8F5230','#7A6E2E','#5F7A5C','#4E6D8C','#6E5C78','#7A5A2E','#556675'];
        function getTagColor(t){
          const c=state.tagColors&&state.tagColors[t];
          if(c)return c;
          let h=0;
          for(let i=0;i<t.length;i++)h=(h*31+t.charCodeAt(i))>>>0;
          
          return TAG_COLORS.length?TAG_COLORS[h%TAG_COLORS.length]:'#758596';
        }
        
        function tagStripText(n){
          let txt=stripHtml(n.text||'')||'(绌?';
          if(n.tags&&n.tags.length){
            n.tags.forEach(t=>{
              if(!t)return;
              const re=new RegExp('#\\s*'+t.replace(/[.*+?^${}()|[\]\\]/g,'\\$&')+'\\s*','g');
              txt=txt.replace(re,'');
            });
          }
          return txt;
        }
        function setTagColor(t,color){
          if(ui.locked){toast('璇峰厛瑙ｉ攣');return;}
          try{
            commit(s=>{if(!s.tagColors)s.tagColors={};s.tagColors[t]=color;},{snapshot:true}); 
          }catch(e){
            console.error('[JU] setTagColor commit',e);
            toast('棰滆壊璁剧疆澶辫触');
            return;
          }
          try{renderSidebar();}catch(e){console.error('[JU] setTagColor sidebar',e);}
          try{forceRender();}catch(e){console.error('[JU] setTagColor render',e);}
          toast('宸茶缃?#'+t+' 棰滆壊');
        }

        }

          const keys=_tagReady?Array.from(_tagIndexCache.keys()).sort():[];
          if(!_tagReady){
            const w=document.createElement('div');w.className='tp-item';
            w.style.color='var(--fg-dim)';w.textContent='锛堟爣绛剧储寮曟瀯寤轰腑鈥﹁绋嶅悗閲嶅紑锛?;
            p.appendChild(w);
          }else if(!keys.length){
            const em=document.createElement('div');em.className='tp-item';
            em.style.color='var(--fg-dim)';em.textContent='锛堟殏鏃犳爣绛撅細鍦ㄥぇ绾茶緭鍏?#鏍囩 鍗冲彲鍒涘缓锛?;
            p.appendChild(em);
          }
          const pick=(t)=>{
            
            if(!t.length)return;
            const owner=findOwningTopDocId(nodeId,state);
            
            commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[nodeId];if(!nn)return;
              nn._manualTags=[...new Set([...(nn._manualTags||[]),...t])];
              nn.tags=[...new Set([...nn._manualTags,...extractTags(stripHtml(nn.text||''))])];
              nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},
              {snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:owner||undefined});
            actions.logActivity('mod',nodeId);
            closeTagPicker();
            forceRender();
            toast('宸叉爣璁?#'+t[0]);
          };
          
          const clearTags=()=>{
            const owner=findOwningTopDocId(nodeId,state);
            commit(s=>{const nn=s.nodes[nodeId];if(!nn)return;
              nn._manualTags=[];
              nn.tags=[...new Set(extractTags(stripHtml(nn.text||'')))]; 
              nn.updatedAt=_nowSeq();},
              {snapshot:true,dirtyText:true,dirtyDocs:owner||undefined});
            actions.logActivity('mod',nodeId);
            closeTagPicker();
            forceRender();
            toast('宸茬Щ闄ゆ爣绛?);
          };
          keys.forEach(t=>{
            const it=document.createElement('div');it.className='tp-item';
            const dot=document.createElement('span');dot.className='sb-tag-dot';dot.style.background=getTagColor(t);
            it.appendChild(dot);
            const lab=document.createElement('span');lab.textContent='#'+t;
            it.appendChild(lab);
            it.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{e.stopPropagation();pick([t]);});
            p.appendChild(it);
          });
          
          const newRow=document.createElement('div');newRow.className='tp-item';
          const newInput=document.createElement('input');newInput.placeholder='鏂板缓鏍囩鍚嶁€?;
          newInput.style.cssText='flex:1;min-width:0;background:var(--bg-panel);border:1px solid var(--line);border-radius:6px;color:var(--fg);padding:4px 8px;font-size:.92em';
          const newBtn=document.createElement('button');newBtn.className='btn primary';newBtn.style.cssText='font-size:.82em;padding:4px 10px';newBtn.textContent='鍒涘缓';
          newRow.appendChild(newInput);newRow.appendChild(newBtn);
          const doCreate=()=>{
            const name=(newInput.value||'').trim().replace(/^#/,'');
            if(!name){toast('鏍囩鍚嶄笉鑳戒负绌?);newInput.focus();return;}
            _tagIndexCache=null;
            pick([name]);
          };
          newBtn.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{e.stopPropagation();e.preventDefault();doCreate();});
          newInput.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();doCreate();}});
          p.appendChild(newRow);
          const none=document.createElement('div');none.className='tp-item';
          none.style.color='var(--fg-dim)';
          none.textContent='鉁?鏃犳爣绛?;
          none.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{e.stopPropagation();clearTags();});
          p.appendChild(none);
          document.body.appendChild(p);
          placePop(p,196,320,anchor);
          initDragClose(p,()=>closeTagPicker());
          _tagPickerEl=p;
          setTimeout(()=>{
            window._tagPickerDocMousedown=e=>{if(!p.contains(e.target)&&!anchor.contains(e.target))closeTagPicker();};
            document.addEventListener('mousedown',window._tagPickerDocMousedown);
          },0);
        }

        function _tagScanStep(ids,i,deadline,map){
          while(i<ids.length){
            const id=ids[i];i++;
            const n=state.nodes[id];
            if(n&&!n._deleted&&!n._isFolder){
              const raw=stripHtml(n.text||'');
              const seen=new Set();
              let m;TAG_RE.lastIndex=0;
              while((m=TAG_RE.exec(raw))!==null){
                const t=m[1].slice(1);
                if(!t)continue;
                seen.add(t);
                if(!map.has(t))map.set(t,new Set());
                map.get(t).add(id);
              }
              
              if(Array.isArray(n.tags)&&n.tags.length){
                for(const t of n.tags){
                  if(!t||seen.has(t))continue;
                  
                  if(!(Array.isArray(n._manualTags)&&n._manualTags.includes(t)))continue;
                  seen.add(t);
                  if(!map.has(t))map.set(t,new Set());
                  map.get(t).add(id);
                }
              }
            }
            if(_l7Now()>=deadline)break;
          }
          return i;
        }
        function buildTagIndex(){
          if(_tagIndexCache)return _tagIndexCache;
          const map=new Map();
          const ids=Object.keys(state.nodes);
          let i=0;
          while(i<ids.length)i=_tagScanStep(ids,i,Infinity,map);
          _tagIndexCache=map;
          return map;
        }
        function calendarTagIndex(){
          const map=new Map();
          const calRootId=getCalendarRootId();
          const stack=[calRootId];const visited=new Set();
          while(stack.length){
            const pid=stack.pop();
            if(visited.has(pid))continue;
            visited.add(pid);
            const parent=N(pid);if(!parent)continue;
            (parent.children||[]).forEach(cid=>{
              const n=N(cid);
              if(!n||n._deleted)return;
              const seen=new Set();
              const raw=stripHtml(n.text||'');
              let m;TAG_RE.lastIndex=0;
              while((m=TAG_RE.exec(raw))!==null){const tt=m[1].slice(1);if(tt&&!seen.has(tt)){seen.add(tt);map.set(tt,(map.get(tt)||0)+1);}}
              (n.tags||[]).forEach(t=>{if(t&&!seen.has(t)){seen.add(t);map.set(t,(map.get(t)||0)+1);}});
              stack.push(cid);
            });
          }
          return map;
        }
        function nodeHasTag(n,tag){
          if(!n||!tag)return false;
          
          if((n.tags||[]).indexOf(tag)<0)return false;
          
          if(extractTags(stripHtml(n.text||'')).indexOf(tag)>=0)return true;
          return (n._manualTags||[]).indexOf(tag)>=0;
        }
        function nodeSearchHay(n){
          
          const cached=_searchHayCache.get(n.id);
          if(cached&&cached.updatedAt===n.updatedAt)return cached.hay;
          const hay=[
            stripHtml(n.text),
            n.note||'',
            (n.tags||[]).join(' '),
            Object.values(n.db||{}).map(v=>typeof v==='object'?formatTimeValue(v):String(v)).join(' ')
          ].join(' ').toLowerCase();
          _searchHayCache.set(n.id,{updatedAt:n.updatedAt,hay});
          return hay;
        }
        function renderTagsView(){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='files-wrap';wrap.style.padding='20px 24px';
          wrap.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:1.05em;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:14px;color:var(--title)">鏍囩杩囨护</div>';
          
          if(!_tagIndexCache){
            wrap.appendChild(l7Skeleton('tags',{mode:'chips',count:14,hint:'姝ｅ湪鏋勫缓鏍囩绱㈠紩鈥?,label:'鏍囩绱㈠紩'}));
            l7TagIndexWhenReady(function(){if(_l7IsTagsView())forceRender();});
            return wrap;
          }
          const tagIndex=buildTagIndex();
          const tags=Array.from(tagIndex.entries()).map(([t,ids])=>[t,ids.length]).sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]);
          if(!tags.length){const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='empty-state';e.textContent='鏆傛棤鏍囩锛屽湪鑺傜偣涓緭鍏?#鏍囩 鍗冲彲娣诲姞';wrap.appendChild(e);return wrap;}
          const cloud=document.createElement('div');cloud.style.cssText='display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px';
          tags.forEach(([t,count])=>{
            const chip=document.createElement('span');chip.className='tag-chip';

            chip.textContent=`#${t} (${count})`;
            chip.onclick=()=>{
              const ids=tagIndex.get(t)||[];

              w2.appendChild(backBtn);
              const h=document.createElement('div');h.style.cssText='font-size:1.05em;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:14px;color:var(--title)';

              items.forEach(n=>grid.appendChild(renderFileCard(n.id,n,false)));
              w2.appendChild(grid);c.appendChild(w2);
            };
            cloud.appendChild(chip);
          });
          wrap.appendChild(cloud);
          return wrap;
        }

        function renderTagNodes(t){
          
          if(!_tagIndexCache){

          const backBtn=document.createElement('button');backBtn.className='btn';backBtn.style.cssText='margin-bottom:14px;font-size:.85em';

          const grid=document.createElement('div');grid.className='files-grid';

          items.forEach(n=>grid.appendChild(renderFileCard(n.id,n,false)));
          w2.appendChild(grid);

        function renderHistoryView(){
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='files-wrap';
          wrap.appendChild(renderViewTitle('鐗堟湰鍘嗗彶'));
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();
          const isRoot=!doc||doc.id===state.rootId;
          if(isRoot){
            const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='empty-state';
            e.textContent='璇峰厛閫夋嫨涓€涓枃妗ｏ紙鏍圭洰褰曚笉鏀寔鐗堟湰鍘嗗彶锛?;
            wrap.appendChild(e);return wrap;
          }
          const loading=document.createElement('div');loading.className='empty-state';
          loading.textContent='鍔犺浇涓€?;
          wrap.appendChild(loading);
          loadVersionList(doc.id).then(list=>{
            if(!wrap.isConnected)return;
            loading.remove();
            if(!list.length){
              const e=document.createElement('div');e.className='empty-state';
              e.textContent='鏆傛棤鐗堟湰蹇収';
              wrap.appendChild(e);return;
            }
            [...list].reverse().forEach(v=>{
              const item=document.createElement('div');item.className='history-item';
              const d=new Date(v.ts);
              const ts=`${d.getFullYear()}-${pad(d.getMonth()+1)}-${pad(d.getDate())} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}`;
              const metaText=v._locked
                ? '馃敀 闇€瑙ｉ攣鏌ョ湅'
                : (v._corrupt ? '鈿?蹇収鎹熷潖' : Object.keys(v.nodes||{}).length+' 涓妭鐐?);
              item.innerHTML=`<div class="h-time">${esc(v.title||'鏈懡鍚?)} 路 ${ts}</div><div class="h-actions">${metaText}</div>`;
              item.onclick=()=>{
                if(v._locked){toast('璇峰厛瑙ｉ攣鍔犲瘑搴?);return;}
                if(v._corrupt){toast('璇ュ揩鐓у凡鎹熷潖锛屾棤娉曟仮澶?);return;}
                appConfirm(`鎭㈠鍒?${ts} 鐨勭増鏈紵褰撳墠淇敼灏嗚瑕嗙洊銆俙,()=>{
                  commit(s=>{
                    const before=s.nodes[doc.id];
                    const keep={
                      _isWord:before?before._isWord:undefined,
                      starred:before?before.starred:0,
                      private:before?before.private:false,
                      createdAt:before?before.createdAt:Date.now()
                    };
                    const sub=docSubtree(doc.id);
                    sub.forEach(id=>{
                      if(id!==doc.id){
                        delete s.nodes[id];
                        purgeNodeMeta(s,id);
                      }
                    });
                    const snapNodes=v.nodes||{};
                    for(const sid in snapNodes){
                      const cloned=clone(snapNodes[sid]);
                      if(cloned._deleted)delete cloned._deleted;
                      s.nodes[sid]=cloned;
                    }
                    
                    const liveIds=new Set(Object.keys(s.nodes));
                    for(const cid in ui.collapsedDocs){if(!liveIds.has(cid))delete ui.collapsedDocs[cid];}
                    
                    try{_searchHayCache.clear();}catch(e){}
                    const nd=s.nodes[doc.id];
                    if(nd){
                      if(keep._isWord!==undefined)nd._isWord=keep._isWord;
                      nd.starred=keep.starred;
                      nd.private=keep.private;
                      nd.createdAt=keep.createdAt;
                    }
                  },{snapshot:true});
                  forceRender();renderSidebar();
                  toast('宸叉仮澶?);
                });
              };
              wrap.appendChild(item);
            });
          }).catch(err=>{
            if(!wrap.isConnected)return;
            loading.textContent='鍔犺浇澶辫触锛?+(err.message||'鏈煡閿欒');
          });
          return wrap;
        }

        <script>
        "use strict";
        
        const APP_LOCALE=(typeof navigator!=='undefined'&&navigator.language)||'zh-CN';
        
        
        window.__juLog=[];
        const _errSeen=new Map();
        let _inReportError=false; 
        let _errRateWin=0,_errRateCount=0; 
        const _ERR_RATE_MAX=40;
        let _errSweepCount=0; 
        const _ERR_SWEEP_INTERVAL=64;
        const ERROR_ENDPOINT=(typeof window!=='undefined'&&window.__juConfig&&window.__juConfig.errorEndpoint)||null; 
        
        const _redactErrText=s=>String(s==null?'':s).replace(/["'`]([^"'`\n]{4,})["'`]/g,'"<redacted>"');
        function reportError(kind,payload){
          if(_inReportError)return; 
          _inReportError=true;
          try{
            payload=payload||{};
            
            const encNow=(typeof ui!=='undefined'&&!!ui.encrypted);
            const _p={...payload};
            if(encNow){
              if(_p.msg!=null)_p.msg='<enc-message>';
              if(_p.reason!=null)_p.reason='<enc-message>';
            }else{
              if(_p.msg!=null)_p.msg=_redactErrText(_p.msg);
              if(_p.reason!=null)_p.reason=_redactErrText(_p.reason);
            }
            
            const key=JSON.stringify([kind,payload.msg||payload.reason||'',payload.filename||'',payload.lineno]);
            const now=Date.now();
            
            if(++_errSweepCount>=_ERR_SWEEP_INTERVAL){
              _errSweepCount=0;
              for(const[k,t]of _errSeen){if(now-t>600000)_errSeen.delete(k);else break;}
            }else{
              const _oldestKey=_errSeen.keys().next().value;
              if(_oldestKey!==undefined){
                const _oldestT=_errSeen.get(_oldestKey);
                if(_oldestT!==undefined&&now-_oldestT>600000){
                  for(const[k,t]of _errSeen){if(now-t>600000)_errSeen.delete(k);else break;}
                }
              }
            }
            const last=_errSeen.get(key);
            if(last&&(now-last)<60000)return; 
            _errSeen.set(key,now);
            
            const _win=Math.floor(now/60000);
            if(_win!==_errRateWin){_errRateWin=_win;_errRateCount=0;}
            if(_errRateCount>=_ERR_RATE_MAX)return;
            _errRateCount++;
            
            const entry={kind,ts:now,reported:false,ctx:encNow?{
              view:null,docId:null,nodeCount:0,enc:true,
              ver:(typeof JU_VER!=='undefined'?JU_VER:'fixed23')
            }:{
              view:(typeof ui!=='undefined'&&ui)?ui.view:null,
              docId:(typeof ui!=='undefined'&&ui)?ui.currentDocId:null,
              nodeCount:(typeof state!=='undefined'&&state&&state.nodes)?Object.keys(state.nodes).length:0,
              enc:false,
              ver:(typeof JU_VER!=='undefined'?JU_VER:'fixed23')
            },..._p};
            
            if(entry.stack&&typeof entry.stack==='string'&&entry.stack.length>4096)entry.stack=entry.stack.slice(0,4096);
            window.__juLog.push(entry);
            if(window.__juLog.length>200)window.__juLog.shift();
            
            if(typeof IS_DESKTOP!=='undefined'&&IS_DESKTOP&&typeof desktopInvoke==='function'){
              try{desktopInvoke('report_error',{entry:JSON.stringify(entry)}).catch(e=>console.warn('[JU] report_error 妗岄潰钀界洏澶辫触',e));}catch(e){}
            }
            
            if(typeof ui!=='undefined'&&ui&&ui.errorReporting===false)return;
            if(!ERROR_ENDPOINT)return; 
            try{
              if(navigator.sendBeacon&&location.protocol!=='file:'){
                const ok=navigator.sendBeacon(ERROR_ENDPOINT,new Blob([JSON.stringify(entry)],{type:'application/json'}));
                if(ok)entry.reported=true;
              }
              
              if(!entry.reported&&typeof fetch==='function'){
                fetch(ERROR_ENDPOINT,{method:'POST',keepalive:true,headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(entry)})
                  .then(r=>{if(r.ok)entry.reported=true;}).catch(()=>{});
              }
            }catch(e){}
          }catch(e){}
          finally{_inReportError=false;}
        }
        
        window.__juFlushErrors=()=>{
          try{
            if(!ERROR_ENDPOINT)return;
            const pending=window.__juLog.filter(e=>!e.reported);
            if(!pending.length)return;
            fetch(ERROR_ENDPOINT,{method:'POST',keepalive:true,headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(pending)})
              .then(r=>{if(r.ok)pending.forEach(e=>{e.reported=true;});})
              .catch(()=>{});
          }catch(e){}
        };
        window.__juLogDownload=()=>{
          try{
            const blob=new Blob([JSON.stringify(window.__juLog,null,2)],{type:'application/json'});
            const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            a.download='ju-error-log.json';
            document.body.appendChild(a); 
            a.click();
            a.remove();
            URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href);
          }catch(e){}
        };
        window.addEventListener('error',e=>{
          
          if(e.error)e.preventDefault();
          reportError('error',{msg:e.message,stack:e.error&&e.error.stack,filename:e.filename,lineno:e.lineno});
          console.error('[JU]',e.error||e.message);
        });
        window.addEventListener('unhandledrejection',e=>{
          if(e.reason)e.preventDefault();
          reportError('unhandledrejection',{reason:String(e.reason),stack:e.reason&&e.reason.stack});
          console.error('[JU] unhandled rejection',e.reason);
        });
        
        function showLockOverlay(){

          const ov=document.createElement('div');
          ov.id='_juLock';

          ov.innerHTML='<div style="font-size:2em">馃敀</div>'
            +'<div style="font-size:1.1em;font-weight:700;color:var(--title)">JU 宸查攣瀹?/div>'
            +'<div style="color:var(--fg-dim);max-width:420px;line-height:1.8">鏁版嵁宸插姞瀵嗭紝褰撳墠澶勪簬閿佸畾鐘舵€侊紝涓嶄細鍐欏叆浠讳綍鍐呭銆?br>鐐瑰嚮涓嬫柟鎸夐挳閲嶆柊杈撳叆瀵嗙爜瑙ｉ攣銆?/div>';
          const btn=document.createElement('button');

          btn.onclick=()=>location.reload();
          ov.appendChild(btn);
          document.body.appendChild(ov);
        }
        function ensureElInViewport(el){
          if(!el)return;
          const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
          if(r.width<=0||r.height<=0)return;
          const cx=r.left+r.width/2,cy=r.top+r.height/2;
          const vw=window.innerWidth,vh=window.innerHeight;
          if(cx<0||cx>vw||cy<0||cy>vh){
            el.style.left="auto";el.style.top="auto";el.style.bottom="auto";
            if(el.id==="floatCal"){el.style.right="24px";el.style.top="calc(var(--topbar-h,48px) + 24px)";}
            else if(el.id==="pomoTimer"){el.style.left="calc(var(--sidebar-left-w) + 20px)";el.style.right="auto";el.style.bottom="20px";}
            else if(el.id==="settingsPanel"){el.style.left="calc(var(--sidebar-left-w) + 20px)";el.style.right="auto";el.style.bottom="20px";el.style.top="auto";}
          }
        }
        
        const POMO_WORK=25*60,POMO_BREAK=5*60;
        let pomoState={running:false,endAt:0,remaining:POMO_WORK,mode:'work',timer:null};
        function initPomo(){
          const el=$('#pomoTimer');if(!el)return;
          el.style.display=ui.pomoVisible?'':'none';
          $('#pomoStart').onclick=()=>togglePomo();
          $('#pomoReset').onclick=()=>resetPomo();
          const closeBtn=$('#pomoClose');
          if(closeBtn)closeBtn.onclick=()=>{el.style.display='none';ui.pomoVisible=false;saveUI();};
          const mi=$('#musicItem');
          if(mi)mi.onclick=()=>{if(ui.musicPlaying===MUSIC_TRACKS[0].id)stopMusic();else playTrack(MUSIC_TRACKS[0].id);};
          const mv=$('#musicVol');
          if(mv){
            mv.value=Math.round(musicVol*100);
            mv.oninput=e=>{
              musicVol=parseInt(e.target.value)/100;
              try{localStorage.setItem('juju.musicvol',String(musicVol));}catch(e){}
            };
          }
          let dragging=false,ox=0,oy=0;
          try{const p=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('juju.ftpos')||'null');if(p&&typeof p.x==='number'){el.style.left=p.x+'px';el.style.top=p.y+'px';el.style.right='auto';el.style.bottom='auto';}}catch(e){}
          ensureElInViewport(el);
          el.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
            if(e.target.closest('button')||e.target.closest('.music-item')||e.target.closest('input'))return;
            dragging=true;
            const r=el.getBoundingClientRect();
            ox=e.clientX-r.left;oy=e.clientY-r.top;
            el.style.right='auto';el.style.bottom='auto';
            el.style.left=r.left+'px';el.style.top=r.top+'px';
            e.preventDefault();
          });
          document.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
            if(!dragging)return;
            let x=e.clientX-ox,y=e.clientY-oy;
            x=Math.max(0,Math.min(window.innerWidth-60,x));
            y=Math.max(0,Math.min(window.innerHeight-40,y));
            el.style.left=x+'px';el.style.top=y+'px';
          });
          document.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>{
            if(!dragging)return;dragging=false;
            try{localStorage.setItem('juju.ftpos',JSON.stringify({x:parseInt(el.style.left)||0,y:parseInt(el.style.top)||0}));}catch(e){}
          });
          updatePomoDisplay();
          renderMusicList();
        }
        function togglePomo(){
          if(pomoState.running){
            clearInterval(pomoState.timer);pomoState.timer=null;pomoState.running=false;
            pomoState.remaining=Math.max(0,Math.round((pomoState.endAt-Date.now())/1000));
            $('#pomoStart').textContent='缁х画';$('#pomoTimer').classList.remove('active');
          }else{
            pomoState.running=true;
            pomoState.endAt=Date.now()+pomoState.remaining*1000;
            $('#pomoStart').textContent='鏆傚仠';$('#pomoTimer').classList.add('active');
            pomoState.timer=setInterval(()=>{
              const left=Math.max(0,Math.round((pomoState.endAt-Date.now())/1000));
              pomoState.remaining=left;
              updatePomoDisplay();
              if(left<=0){
                clearInterval(pomoState.timer);pomoState.timer=null;pomoState.running=false;
                if(pomoState.mode==='work'){pomoState.mode='break';pomoState.remaining=POMO_BREAK;toast('涓撴敞瀹屾垚锛佷紤鎭?鍒嗛挓');}
                else{pomoState.mode='work';pomoState.remaining=POMO_WORK;toast('浼戞伅缁撴潫锛屽紑濮嬩笅涓€涓笓娉?);}
                $('#pomoStart').textContent='寮€濮?;$('#pomoTimer').classList.remove('active');
                updatePomoDisplay();
              }
            },500);
          }
        }
        function resetPomo(){
          clearInterval(pomoState.timer);pomoState.timer=null;pomoState.running=false;
          pomoState.mode='work';pomoState.remaining=POMO_WORK;
          $('#pomoStart').textContent='寮€濮?;$('#pomoTimer').classList.remove('active');updatePomoDisplay();
        }
        function updatePomoDisplay(){
          const m=Math.floor(pomoState.remaining/60),s=pomoState.remaining%60;
          const t=$('#pomoTime');
          if(t)t.textContent=pad(m)+':'+pad(s);
        }
        
        function renderFloatCal(){
          const fc=$('#floatCal');if(!fc||fc.hidden)return;
          const now=new Date();
          if(!ui.fcMonth)ui.fcMonth={y:now.getFullYear(),m:now.getMonth()};
          const{y,m}=ui.fcMonth;
          const ts=todayStr();
          const title=$('#fcTitle');if(title)title.textContent=`${y}骞?{m+1}鏈坄;
          const days=$('#fcDays');if(!days)return;
          days.innerHTML='';
          const first=new Date(y,m,1);const startDow=first.getDay();
          const dim=new Date(y,m+1,0).getDate();const pdim=new Date(y,m,0).getDate();
          const total=Math.ceil((startDow+dim)/7)*7;
          for(let i=0;i<total;i++){
            const off=i-startDow+1;
            let d,out=false;
            if(off<1){d=new Date(y,m-1,pdim+off);out=true;}
            else if(off>dim){d=new Date(y,m+1,off-dim);out=true;}
            else d=new Date(y,m,off);
            const ds=ymd(d);
            const el=document.createElement('div');el.className='fc-day';
            if(out)el.classList.add('out');
            if(ds===ts)el.classList.add('today');
            if(calendarTodosOnDate(ds).length)el.classList.add('has');
            el.textContent=d.getDate();el.title=ds;
            el.onclick=()=>{
              ui.selectedDate=ds;
              const dObj=parseYmd(ds);
              ui.fcMonth={y:dObj.getFullYear(),m:dObj.getMonth()};
              saveUI();
              renderFloatCal();
              const cur=getCurrentDoc();
              if(cur&&viewOf(cur)===VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR)forceRender();
            };
            days.appendChild(el);
          }
          const list=$('#fcList');if(!list)return;
          list.innerHTML='';
          const showDate=ui.selectedDate||ts;
          const items=calendarTodosOnDate(showDate).filter(n=>!isBlankNode(n)||ui.focusId===n.id);
          if(!items.length){list.innerHTML='<div class="fc-empty">褰撴棩鏆傛棤寰呭姙</div>';}
          else{
            items.slice(0,10).forEach(n=>{
              const row=document.createElement('div');row.className='fc-todo'+(n.done?' done':'');
              const cb=document.createElement('input');cb.type='checkbox';cb.checked=!!n.done;
              cb.onchange=()=>{actions.toggleDone(n.id);renderFloatCal();forceRender();};
              const sp=document.createElement('span');sp.textContent=stripHtml(n.text)||'(绌?';
              row.appendChild(cb);row.appendChild(sp);
              list.appendChild(row);
            });
          }
        }
        function initFloatCalDrag(){
          const fc=$('#floatCal');if(!fc)return;
          const head=$('#fcHead');
          let dragging=false,ox=0,oy=0;
          try{const p=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('juju.fcpos')||'null');if(p&&typeof p.x==='number'){fc.style.left=p.x+'px';fc.style.top=p.y+'px';}else{fc.style.right='24px';fc.style.top='calc(var(--topbar-h,48px) + 24px)';}}catch(e){fc.style.right='24px';fc.style.top='calc(var(--topbar-h,48px) + 24px)';}
          ensureElInViewport(fc);
          const onMove=e=>{
            if(!dragging)return;
            let x=e.clientX-ox,y=e.clientY-oy;
            x=Math.max(0,Math.min(window.innerWidth-60,x));y=Math.max(0,Math.min(window.innerHeight-40,y));
            fc.style.left=x+'px';fc.style.top=y+'px';
          };
          const onUp=()=>{
            if(!dragging)return;dragging=false;
            document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onMove);document.removeEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
            try{localStorage.setItem('juju.fcpos',JSON.stringify({x:parseInt(fc.style.left)||0,y:parseInt(fc.style.top)||0}));}catch(e){}
          };
          head.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
            if(e.target.id==='fcClose')return;
            dragging=true;
            const r=fc.getBoundingClientRect();
            ox=e.clientX-r.left;oy=e.clientY-r.top;
            fc.style.right='auto';fc.style.left=r.left+'px';fc.style.top=r.top+'px';
            document.addEventListener('mousemove',onMove);document.addEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
            e.preventDefault();
          });
          $('#fcClose').onclick=()=>{
            $('#floatCal').hidden=true;ui.floatCal=false;saveUI();
            const bfc=$('#btnFloatCal');if(bfc)bfc.classList.remove('on');
          };
        }
        function initSettingsDrag(){
          const p=$('#settingsPanel');if(!p)return;
          const head=p.querySelector('.panel-head');
          if(!head)return;
          let dragging=false,ox=0,oy=0;
          try{const pos=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('juju.settingspos')||'null');if(pos&&typeof pos.x==='number'){p.style.left=pos.x+'px';p.style.top=pos.y+'px';p.style.bottom='auto';}}catch(e){}
          ensureElInViewport(p);
          head.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
            if(e.target.closest('button'))return;
            dragging=true;
            const r=p.getBoundingClientRect();
            ox=e.clientX-r.left;oy=e.clientY-r.top;
            p.style.bottom='auto';p.style.left=r.left+'px';p.style.top=r.top+'px';
            e.preventDefault();
          });
          document.addEventListener('mousemove',e=>{
            if(!dragging)return;
            let x=e.clientX-ox,y=e.clientY-oy;
            x=Math.max(0,Math.min(window.innerWidth-80,x));y=Math.max(0,Math.min(window.innerHeight-40,y));
            p.style.left=x+'px';p.style.top=y+'px';
          });
          document.addEventListener('mouseup',()=>{
            if(!dragging)return;dragging=false;
            try{localStorage.setItem('juju.settingspos',JSON.stringify({x:parseInt(p.style.left)||0,y:parseInt(p.style.top)||0}));}catch(e){}
          });
        }
        
        const MUSIC_TRACKS=[
          {id:'bach-goldberg',title:'鍝ュ痉鍫″彉濂忔洸',composer:'宸磋但',name:'Goldberg Variations, Aria',seq:[{n:'G3',d:1},{n:'F#3',d:.5},{n:'G3',d:.5},{n:'A3',d:1},{n:'B3',d:1},{n:'A3',d:.5},{n:'G3',d:.5},{n:'F#3',d:1},{n:'E3',d:1},{n:'D3',d:1.5},{n:'E3',d:.5},{n:'F#3',d:1},{n:'G3',d:2}]}
        ];
        const NOTE_FREQ={'C3':130.81,'C#3':138.59,'D3':146.83,'Eb3':155.56,'E3':164.81,'F3':174.61,'F#3':185.00,'G3':196.00,'A3':220.00,'Bb3':233.08,'B3':246.94,'C4':261.63,'C#4':277.18,'D4':293.66,'Eb4':311.13,'E4':329.63,'F4':349.23,'F#4':369.99,'G4':392.00,'A4':440.00,'Bb4':466.16,'B4':493.88,'C5':523.25,'D5':587.33,'E5':659.25};
        let audioCtx=null,musicStopFlag=false,currentTrackId=null,currentNodes=[],musicLoopTimer=null;
        let musicVol=(()=>{
          try{
            const v=parseFloat(localStorage.getItem('juju.musicvol'));
            return Number.isFinite(v)?Math.max(0,Math.min(1,v)):0.4;
          }catch(e){return 0.4;}
        })();
        function playTrack(id){
          stopMusic();
          const track=MUSIC_TRACKS.find(t=>t.id===id);if(!track)return;
          try{
            audioCtx=audioCtx||new(window.AudioContext||window.webkitAudioContext)();
            if(audioCtx.state==='suspended')audioCtx.resume();
            musicStopFlag=false;currentTrackId=id;ui.musicPlaying=id;
            renderMusicList();
            toast('姝ｅ湪鎾斁锛?+track.title);
            const trackDuration=track.seq.reduce((s,n)=>s+n.d*0.5,0);
            const GAP=1.5;
            const LOOKAHEAD=1.5;
            let nextCycleStart=audioCtx.currentTime+0.05;
            const scheduleCycle=startAt=>{
              let t=startAt;
              track.seq.forEach(note=>{
                const freq=NOTE_FREQ[note.n];
                if(!freq){t+=note.d*0.5;return;}
                const o=audioCtx.createOscillator();const g=audioCtx.createGain();
                o.type='sine';o.frequency.value=freq;
                g.gain.setValueAtTime(0,t);
                g.gain.linearRampToValueAtTime(musicVol*0.15,t+0.08);
                g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(musicVol*0.04,t+note.d*0.3);
                g.gain.exponentialRampToValueAtTime(0.0001,t+note.d*0.8);
                o.connect(g);g.connect(audioCtx.destination);
                o.start(t);o.stop(t+note.d*0.85);
                o.onended=()=>{
                  const i=currentNodes.indexOf(o);
                  if(i>=0)currentNodes.splice(i,1);
                  try{g.disconnect();}catch(e){}
                };
                currentNodes.push(o);t+=note.d*0.5;
              });
            };
            const schedule=()=>{
              if(musicStopFlag||currentTrackId!==id)return;
              while(nextCycleStart<audioCtx.currentTime+LOOKAHEAD){
                scheduleCycle(nextCycleStart);
                nextCycleStart+=trackDuration+GAP;
              }
              musicLoopTimer=setTimeout(schedule,300);
            };
            schedule();
          }catch(e){console.error(e);toast('娴忚鍣ㄤ笉鏀寔闊抽');}
        }
        function stopMusic(){
          musicStopFlag=true;currentTrackId=null;ui.musicPlaying=null;
          clearTimeout(musicLoopTimer);musicLoopTimer=null;
          currentNodes.forEach(n=>{try{n.stop();}catch(e){}});
          currentNodes=[];renderMusicList();
          
          try{if(audioCtx&&typeof audioCtx.suspend==='function')audioCtx.suspend();}catch(e){}
        }
        function renderMusicList(){
          const item=$('#musicItem');if(!item)return;
          const t=MUSIC_TRACKS[0];if(!t)return;
          const playing=ui.musicPlaying===t.id;
          item.classList.toggle('playing',playing);
          const ic=$('#musicPlayIc');if(ic)ic.textContent=playing?'鈴?:'鈻?;
        }

        function openAddToDbDialog(){

            },{snapshot:true});
