        function download(filename,content,mime){
          const blob=new Blob([content],{type:(mime||'text/plain')+';charset=utf-8'});
          const url=URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=filename;
          document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();
          
          setTimeout(()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(url);a.remove();},30000);
        }
        const MAX_IMPORT_BYTES=67108864; 
        function readFile(file){
          
          if(file&&typeof file.size==='number'&&file.size>MAX_IMPORT_BYTES){
            return Promise.reject(new Error('鏂囦欢瓒呰繃 64MB 涓婇檺锛?+Math.round(file.size/1048576)+'MB锛夛紝宸叉嫆缁濆鍏ヤ互閬垮厤鍗℃'));
          }
          return new Promise((res,rej)=>{
            const r=new FileReader();
            r.onload=()=>{
              const buf=r.result;
              let text;
              try{text=new TextDecoder('utf-8',{fatal:true}).decode(buf);}
              catch(e){
                try{text=new TextDecoder('gbk').decode(buf);}
                catch(e2){text=new TextDecoder('utf-8').decode(buf);}
              }
              res(text);
            };
            r.onerror=()=>rej(new Error('璇诲彇澶辫触'));
            r.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
          });
        }
        function walkTree(doc,visit){
          function step(id,depth){const n=N(id);if(!n)return;visit(n,id,depth);(n.children||[]).forEach(c=>step(c,depth+1));}
          step(doc.id,-1);
        }
        function toMarkdown(doc){
          let out='# '+(stripHtml(doc.text)||'鏈懡鍚?)+'\n\n';
          out+='> 瀵煎嚭鏃堕棿锛?+new Date().toLocaleString(APP_LOCALE)+'\n\n';
          walkTree(doc,(n,id,depth)=>{
            if(id===doc.id)return;
            const indent='  '.repeat(depth);
            let line=indent+'- '+(n.done?'[x] ':'[ ] ')+stripHtml(n.text).replace(/[\r\n]+/g,' ');
            if(n.date)line+=` \`馃搮 ${n.date}\``;
            out+=line+'\n';
          });
          return out;
        }
        function toOpml(doc){
          const escAttr=s=>String(s||'')
            .replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;')
            .replace(/[\r\n]+/g,' ').replace(/\t/g,' ');
          const INDENT='    ';
          let body='';
          function walk(id,depth){
            const n=N(id);
            if(n.id===doc.id){n.children.forEach(c=>walk(c,depth));return;}
            const pad=INDENT.repeat(depth);
            const attrs=[`text="${escAttr(stripHtml(n.text)||'(绌?')}"`,`_complete="${n.done?'true':'false'}"`];
            if(n.date)attrs.push(`_note="${escAttr(n.date)}"`);
            body+=pad+`<outline ${attrs.join(' ')}`;
            if(n.children.length){
              body+='>\n';
              n.children.forEach(c=>walk(c,depth+1));
              body+=pad+'</outline>\n';
            }else body+='/>\n';
          }
          walk(doc.id,1);
          return`<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<opml version="2.0"><head><title>${escAttr(stripHtml(doc.text)||'JU')}</title>\n<dateCreated>${new Date().toUTCString()}</dateCreated></head>\n<body>\n${body}  </body></opml>`;
        }
        function toHtml(doc){
          function renderChildren(id){
            const n=N(id);
            const kids=n.children.map(cid=>N(cid)).filter(Boolean);
            if(!kids.length)return'';
            let html='<ul>';
            kids.forEach(c=>{
              const cls=c.done?' class="done"':'';
              html+=`<li${cls}>${sanitizeHtml(c.text||'')}`;
              if(c.date)html+=` <span class="meta">馃搮 ${c.date}</span>`;
              html+=renderChildren(c.id);
              html+='</li>';
            });
            html+='</ul>';
            return html;
          }
          let body=renderChildren(doc.id);
          if(doc._isWord && doc.content){
            body=`<div class="word-content">${sanitizeHtml(doc.content)}</div>`+body;
          }
          return`<!DOCTYPE html>\n<html lang="zh-CN"><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${esc(stripHtml(doc.text)||'JU')}</title>\n<style>body{font-family:"Noto Sans SC","PingFang SC",sans-serif;font-size:15px;line-height:1.75;color:#24332B;background:#DFECD5;max-width:820px;margin:40px auto;padding:0 24px}ul{list-style:disc}li.done{text-decoration:line-through;opacity:.6}.meta{color:#33654C;font-size:.8em}</style></head>\n<body><h1>${esc(stripHtml(doc.text)||'')}</h1>${body}</body></html>`;
        }
        function docToXls(doc){
          
          const tsv=docToExcel(doc);
          const esc=function(v){return String(v).replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');};
          const rows=tsv.split('\n').filter(function(r){return r.length>0;});
          const body=rows.map(function(r){
            return '<Row>'+r.split('\t').map(function(c){return '<Cell><Data ss:Type="String">'+esc(c)+'</Data></Cell>';}).join('')+'</Row>';
          }).join('\n');
          return '<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?>\n<?mso-application progid="Excel.Sheet"?>\n'
            +'<Workbook xmlns="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet" xmlns:o="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:office" xmlns:x="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:excel" xmlns:ss="urn:schemas-microsoft-com:office:spreadsheet" xmlns:html="http:
            +'<Worksheet ss:Name="鏁版嵁"><Table>\n'+body+'\n</Table></Worksheet></Workbook>';
        }
        function docToExcel(doc){
          const rows=[];
          const isDb=doc.children.some(cid=>{const n=N(cid);return n&&n.db;});
          if(isDb){
            const fields=state.dbFields||['鏍囬'];
            rows.push(fields.map(f=>(typeof f==='string')?f:f.name).join('\t'));
            doc.children.forEach(cid=>{
              const n=N(cid);if(!n)return;
              const row=fields.map(f=>{
                const fn=(typeof f==='string')?f:f.name;
                if(fn===dbPrimaryField())return stripHtml(n.text).replace(/[\t\r\n]+/g,' '); 
                if(n.db&&n.db[fn]!==undefined){
                  const v=n.db[fn];
                  if(typeof v==='object')return formatTimeValue(v).replace(/[\t\r\n]+/g,' ');
                  return String(v).replace(/[\t\r\n]+/g,' ');
                }
                return'';
              });
              rows.push(row.join('\t'));
            });
          }else{
            rows.push('灞傜骇\t鍐呭\t瀹屾垚\t鏃ユ湡');
            if(doc._isWord && doc.content){
              const plain=stripHtml(doc.content).replace(/[\r\n\t]+/g,' ');
              if(plain) rows.push(['','[姝ｆ枃] '+plain,'',''].join('\t'));
            }
            walkTree(doc,(n,id,depth)=>{
              if(n.id===doc.id)return;
              rows.push([depth,stripHtml(n.text),n.done?'鉁?:'',n.date||''].map(x=>String(x).replace(/[\t\r\n]+/g,' ')).join('\t'));
            });
          }
          return'\ufeff'+rows.join('\n');
        }
        function foldIcs(line){
          const out=[];
          let buf='';
          let bufBytes=0;
          for(let i=0;i<line.length;i++){
            const ch=line[i];
            const c=line.charCodeAt(i);
            const bytes=c<128?1:(c<2048?2:3);
            if(bufBytes+bytes>75){
              out.push(buf);
              buf=' '+ch;
              bufBytes=1+bytes;
            }else{
              buf+=ch;
              bufBytes+=bytes;
            }
          }
          out.push(buf);
          return out.join('\r\n');
        }
        function outlineToIcs(doc){
          const escIcs=s=>String(s||'').replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/;/g,'\\;').replace(/,/g,'\\,').replace(/\r\n|\r|\n/g,'\\n');
          const stamp=new Date().toISOString().replace(/[-:]/g,'').split('.')[0]+'Z';
          const lines=['BEGIN:VCALENDAR','VERSION:2.0','PRODID:-
          lines.push('BEGIN:VTIMEZONE');
          lines.push('TZID:Asia/Shanghai');
          lines.push('BEGIN:STANDARD');
          lines.push('DTSTART:19700101T000000');
          lines.push('TZOFFSETFROM:+0800');
          lines.push('TZOFFSETTO:+0800');
          lines.push('END:STANDARD');
          lines.push('END:VTIMEZONE');
          walkTree(doc,(n,id)=>{
            if(id===doc.id||!n.date)return;
            const day=n.date.replace(/-/g,'');
            let dtstart,dtend,dateOnly=false;
            if(n.time){
              const [h,mi]=n.time.split(':').map(Number);
              dtstart=day+'T'+pad(h)+pad(mi)+'00';
              if(h>=23){
                const nextDay=new Date(parseYmd(n.date));
                nextDay.setDate(nextDay.getDate()+1);
                dtend=ymd(nextDay).replace(/-/g,'')+'T000000';
              }else{
                dtend=day+'T'+pad(h+1)+pad(mi)+'00';
              }
            }else{
              dateOnly=true;
              const dt=parseYmd(n.date);
              if(isNaN(dt.getTime()))return;
              dt.setDate(dt.getDate()+1);
              dtstart=day;
              dtend=ymd(dt).replace(/-/g,'');
            }
            lines.push('BEGIN:VEVENT');
            lines.push('UID:'+id+'@juju');
            lines.push('DTSTAMP:'+stamp);
            if(dateOnly){
              lines.push('DTSTART;VALUE=DATE:'+dtstart);
              lines.push('DTEND;VALUE=DATE:'+dtend);
            }else{
              lines.push('DTSTART;TZID=Asia/Shanghai:'+dtstart);
              lines.push('DTEND;TZID=Asia/Shanghai:'+dtend);
            }
            if(n.recur){
              const map={daily:'DAILY',weekly:'WEEKLY',monthly:'MONTHLY',yearly:'YEARLY'};
              const freq=map[n.recur.freq];
              if(freq){
                let rrule='RRULE:FREQ='+freq;
                const interval=n.recur.interval||1;
                if(interval>1)rrule+=';INTERVAL='+interval;
                if(n.recur.freq==='weekly'){
                  if(n.recur.weekday!=null){
                    const wd=['SU','MO','TU','WE','TH','FR','SA'][n.recur.weekday];
                    if(wd)rrule+=';BYDAY='+wd;
                  }
                  rrule+=';WKST=MO';
                }
                if(n.recurEnd){
                  rrule+=';UNTIL='+String(n.recurEnd).replace(/-/g,'')+'T235959Z';
                }else{
                  rrule+=';COUNT=100';
                }
                lines.push(rrule);
              }
            }
            lines.push('SUMMARY:'+escIcs(stripHtml(n.text)||'untitled'));
            lines.push('END:VEVENT');
          });
          lines.push('END:VCALENDAR');
          return lines.map(foldIcs).join('\r\n');
        }
        function downloadMindmapSvg(doc){
          const{NW,NH,GX,GY}=mmLayoutConsts(); 
          const layout=layoutMindmap(doc.id); 
          const all=layout.nodes;
          if(!all.length)return '<svg xmlns="http:
          const rootNode=layout.rootNode;
          const W=all.reduce((a,n)=>Math.max(a,n.x),0)+NW+80;
          const H=all.reduce((a,n)=>Math.max(a,n.y+n.h/2),0)+80;
          const isNight=document.documentElement.dataset.theme==='night';
          const bg=isNight?'#0F2440':'#DFECD5';
          const fg=isNight?'#E7F1FA':'#24332B';
          const nodeBg=isNight?'#1C5A90':'#EAF3E1';
          const nodeStroke=isNight?'#8FB8E0':'#4A7A5E';
          const rootBg=isNight?'#5E9ED8':'#33654C';
          const lineColor=isNight?'#8FB8E0':'#2E4B3A';
          let svg=`<svg xmlns="http:
          (function edges(nd){
            nd.kids.forEach(k=>{
              const p1x=nd.x+NW,p1y=nd.y,p2x=k.x,p2y=k.y,mx=(p1x+p2x)/2;
              svg+=`<path d="M${p1x},${p1y} C${mx},${p1y} ${mx},${p2y} ${p2x},${p2y}" fill="none" stroke="${lineColor}" stroke-width="1.5"/>`;
              edges(k);
            });
          })(rootNode);
          all.forEach(nd=>{
            const n=N(nd.id);
            const isRoot=nd.id===doc.id;
            const fill=isRoot?rootBg:nodeBg;
            const stroke=isRoot?rootBg:nodeStroke;
            const txt=String((isRoot?stripHtml(doc.text):(n?stripHtml(n.text):''))||'');
            const h=nd.h||NH;
            
            const charsPerLine=16;
            const lines=[];
            let cur='';
            for(const ch of txt){cur+=ch;if(cur.length>=charsPerLine){lines.push(cur);cur='';}}
            if(cur)lines.push(cur);
            if(!lines.length)lines.push('');
            svg+=`<rect x="${nd.x}" y="${nd.y-h/2}" width="${NW}" height="${h}" rx="7" fill="${fill}" stroke="${stroke}" stroke-width="1.5"/>`;
            const lineH=Math.round(18*Math.max(0.8,currentBaseFontPx()/14));
            const startY=nd.y-(lines.length-1)*lineH/2+4;
            lines.forEach((ln,i)=>{
              svg+=`<text x="${nd.x+11}" y="${startY+i*lineH}" fill="${isRoot?'#fff':fg}">${esc(ln)}</text>`;
            });
          });
          svg+='</svg>';
          return svg;
        }
        function doDownload(type){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();if(!doc){toast('璇峰厛閫夋嫨鏂囨。');return;}
          const name=(stripHtml(doc.text)||'鏈懡鍚?).replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,'_');
          switch(type){
            case 'template':{
              const tree=[];
              function toTree(id){
                const n=N(id);
                return{
                  text:n.text, done:n.done, date:n.date,
                  time:n.time||null, tags:(n.tags||[]).slice(),
                  note:n.note||null, recur:n.recur||null,
                  priority:n.priority||0, starred:n.starred||0,
                  color:n.color||null, showInCal:n.showInCal!==false,
                  db:n.db?clone(n.db):null,
                  children:n.children.map(toTree)
                };
              }
              doc.children.forEach(cid=>tree.push(toTree(cid)));
              actions.addTemplate(name,tree);
              toast('宸蹭繚瀛樹负妯℃澘');
              break;
            }
            case 'md':download(`${name}-${todayStr()}.md`,toMarkdown(doc),'text/markdown');break;
            case 'opml':download(`${name}-${todayStr()}.opml`,toOpml(doc),'text/xml');break;
            case 'html':case 'word':download(`${name}-${todayStr()}.html`,toHtml(doc),'text/html');break;
            case 'excel':download(`${name}-${todayStr()}.xls`,docToXls(doc),'application/vnd.ms-excel');break;
            case 'png':{
              const svgStr=downloadMindmapSvg(doc);
              const svgBlob=new Blob([svgStr],{type:'image/svg+xml;charset=utf-8'});
              const svgUrl=URL.createObjectURL(svgBlob);
              const img=new Image();
              img.onload=()=>{
                const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');
                const w=Math.max(img.width||800,800),h=Math.max(img.height||600,600);
                canvas.width=w*2;canvas.height=h*2;
                const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d');
                const isNight=document.documentElement.dataset.theme==='night';
                ctx.fillStyle=isNight?'#0F2440':'#DFECD5';
                ctx.fillRect(0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
                ctx.drawImage(img,0,0,canvas.width,canvas.height);
                URL.revokeObjectURL(svgUrl);
                canvas.toBlob(pngBlob=>{
                  if(!pngBlob){download(`${name}-${todayStr()}.svg`,svgStr,'image/svg+xml');return;}
                  const url=URL.createObjectURL(pngBlob);
                  const a=document.createElement('a');a.href=url;a.download=`${name}-${todayStr()}.png`;
                  document.body.appendChild(a);a.click();a.remove();
                  setTimeout(()=>URL.revokeObjectURL(url),500);
                  toast('宸蹭笅杞?PNG');
                },'image/png');
              };
              img.onerror=()=>{URL.revokeObjectURL(svgUrl);download(`${name}-${todayStr()}.svg`,svgStr,'image/svg+xml');};
              img.src=svgUrl;
              break;
            }
          }
        }
        function showPrintPreview(){
          const doc=getCurrentDoc();
          if(!doc){toast('璇峰厛閫夋嫨鏂囨。');return;}
          const c=$('#content');c.innerHTML='';
          const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='print-preview';
          const h=document.createElement('h1');h.textContent=stripHtml(doc.text)||'鏈懡鍚?;wrap.appendChild(h);
          const metaCb=$('#printWithMeta');
          if(metaCb&&metaCb.checked){
            const meta=document.createElement('div');
            meta.style.cssText='font-size:.8em;color:var(--fg-dim);margin-bottom:16px;padding-bottom:12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-soft);';
            meta.innerHTML=`鑺傜偣 ${docNodeCount(doc.id)} 路 瀛楁暟 ${docWordCount(doc.id)} 路 鏇存柊 ${new Date(doc.updatedAt).toLocaleString(APP_LOCALE)}`;
            wrap.appendChild(meta);
          }
          if(doc._isWord && doc.content){
            const wb=document.createElement('div');
            wb.className='node-print';
            wb.innerHTML=sanitizeHtml(doc.content);
            wrap.appendChild(wb);
          }
          function render(id,depth){
            const n=N(id);
            if(n.id!==doc.id){
              const item=document.createElement('div');
              item.className='node-print'+(n.done?' done':'');
              item.style.paddingLeft=(depth*20)+'px';
              item.textContent=(n.done?'鉁?':'')+(stripHtml(n.text)||'(绌?');
              wrap.appendChild(item);
            }
            const nd=n.id===doc.id?1:depth+1;
            n.children.forEach(c=>render(c,nd));
          }
          doc.children.forEach(c=>render(c,1));
          c.appendChild(wrap);
        }
        function doPrint(){
          const mode=document.querySelector('input[name="printMode"]:checked')?.value||'all';
          if(mode==='selection'){
            const sel=window.getSelection();
            if(!sel||sel.isCollapsed){toast('璇峰厛閫変腑鍐呭');return;}
            const range=sel.getRangeAt(0);
            const frag=range.cloneContents();
            const tmp=document.createElement('div');tmp.className='print-preview';
            const h=document.createElement('h1');h.textContent='閫夊畾鍐呭';
            tmp.appendChild(h);tmp.appendChild(frag);
            const c=$('#content');
            c.innerHTML='';c.appendChild(tmp);
            toast('鍑嗗鎵撳嵃鈥?);
            setTimeout(()=>{window.print();forceRender();},250);
            return;
          }
          if(mode==='all'){
            const c=$('#content');
            const wrap=document.createElement('div');wrap.className='print-preview';
            const root=N(state.rootId);
            const docs=root.children.map(cid=>N(cid)).filter(n=>n&&!n._isCalRoot&&!n._isDbRoot&&!n._isFolder&&!n.dbRoot&&!n._deleted);
            const metaCb=$('#printWithMeta');
            if(!docs.length){
              const empty=document.createElement('div');empty.textContent='鏆傛棤鍙墦鍗扮殑鏂囨。';wrap.appendChild(empty);
              c.innerHTML='';c.appendChild(wrap);
              toast('鍑嗗鎵撳嵃鈥?);
              setTimeout(()=>{window.print();forceRender();},250);
            }else{
              
              const PRINT_BATCH=20;
              let _di=0;
              const finishBatch=()=>{
                c.innerHTML='';c.appendChild(wrap);
                toast('鍑嗗鎵撳嵃鈥?);
                setTimeout(()=>{window.print();forceRender();},250);
              };
              (function renderBatch(){
                const _end=Math.min(_di+PRINT_BATCH,docs.length);
                for(;_di<_end;_di++){
                const doc=docs[_di];
                const h=document.createElement('h1');h.textContent=stripHtml(doc.text)||'鏈懡鍚?;wrap.appendChild(h);
                if(metaCb&&metaCb.checked){
                  const meta=document.createElement('div');
                  meta.style.cssText='font-size:.8em;color:var(--fg-dim);margin-bottom:16px;padding-bottom:12px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--line-soft);';
                  meta.textContent=`鑺傜偣 ${docNodeCount(doc.id)} 路 瀛楁暟 ${docWordCount(doc.id)} 路 鏇存柊 ${new Date(doc.updatedAt).toLocaleString(APP_LOCALE)}`;
                  wrap.appendChild(meta);
                }
                if(doc._isWord && doc.content){
                  const wb=document.createElement('div');
                  wb.className='node-print';
                  wb.innerHTML=sanitizeHtml(doc.content);
                  wrap.appendChild(wb);
                }
                function render(id,depth){
                  const nn=N(id);
                  if(nn.id!==doc.id){
                    const item=document.createElement('div');
                    item.className='node-print'+(nn.done?' done':'');
                    item.style.paddingLeft=(depth*20)+'px';
                    item.textContent=(nn.done?'鉁?':'')+(stripHtml(nn.text)||'(绌?');
                    wrap.appendChild(item);
                  }
                  const nd=nn.id===doc.id?1:depth+1;
                  nn.children.forEach(cc=>render(cc,nd));
                }
                doc.children.forEach(cc=>render(cc,1));
                }
                if(_di<docs.length){setTimeout(renderBatch,0);return;}
                finishBatch();
              })();
            }
            return;
          }
          showPrintPreview();
          toast('鍑嗗鎵撳嵃鈥?);
          setTimeout(()=>{window.print();forceRender();},250);
        }
        function exportICS(){
          const calRootId=getCalendarRootId();
          const calDoc=N(calRootId);
          
          download((stripHtml(calDoc.text)||'calendar').replace(/[\\/:*?"<>|]/g,'_')+'.ics',outlineToIcs(calDoc),'text/calendar');
          toast('宸蹭笅杞?ICS');
        }
        
        function importOutline(text){
          const lines=text.split(/\r?\n/);
          const entries=[];
          let inCode=false;
          const allIndents=lines.map(l=>{
            const m=l.match(/^([ \t]*)\S/);
            if(!m)return Infinity;
            const n=m[1].replace(/\t/g,'  ').length;
            return n>0?n:Infinity;
          }).filter(Number.isFinite);
          const unit=allIndents.length?allIndents.reduce((a,b)=>a<b?a:b):2;
          for(let i=0;i<lines.length;i++){
            const line=lines[i];
            if(/^```/.test(line.trim())){inCode=!inCode;continue;}
            if(inCode||!line.trim())continue;
            const m=line.match(/^([ \t]*)(.*)$/);if(!m)continue;
            const rawIndent=m[1].replace(/\t/g,'  ').length;
            let content=m[2];let level=-1;
            const hMatch=content.match(/^(#{1,6})\s+(.*)$/);
            if(hMatch){level=hMatch[1].length-1;content=hMatch[2];}
            content=content.replace(/^[-*+]\s+/,'').replace(/^\d+\.\s+/,'');
            let done=false;
            if(/^\[x\]\s*/i.test(content)){done=true;content=content.replace(/^\[x\]\s*/i,'');}
            else if(/^\[\s\]\s*/i.test(content))content=content.replace(/^\[\s\]\s*/i,'');
            let date=null;
            const dm=content.match(/馃搮\s*(\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2})/);
            if(dm){date=dm[1];content=content.replace(/馃搮\s*\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}/g,'');}
            content=content.trim();
            if(!content)continue;
            const effIndent=level>=0?level:Math.round(rawIndent/unit);
            entries.push({indent:effIndent,text:content,done,date});
          }
          if(!entries.length)return 0;
          let count=0,firstId=null;
          commit(s=>{
            const stack=[{indent:-1,id:s.rootId}];
            for(const e of entries){
              while(stack.length>1&&stack[stack.length-1].indent>=e.indent)stack.pop();
              const parentId=stack[stack.length-1].id;
              const n=addNodeTo(s,parentId,null,{text:e.text,done:e.done,date:e.date});
              if(!n)continue;
              count++;
              if(!firstId)firstId=n.id;
              stack.push({indent:e.indent,id:n.id});
            }
          },{snapshot:true});
          const uniqueIndents=new Set(allIndents);
          const hint=uniqueIndents.size>4
            ?'锛堢缉杩涗笉瑙勬暣锛屽眰绾у彲鑳戒笉鍑嗭級'
            :unit>2
              ?'锛堢缉杩涘崟浣?'+unit+' 绌烘牸锛?
              :'';
          if(count>0&&firstId)actions.logActivity('add',firstId);
          toast(`宸插鍏?${count} 涓妭鐐?{hint}`);
          return count;
        }
        function importOpml(text){
          const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(text,'text/xml');
          if(doc.querySelector('parsererror'))throw new Error('OPML 瑙ｆ瀽澶辫触');
          const body=doc.querySelector('body');if(!body)return 0;
          let count=0,firstId=null;
          commit(s=>{
            function walk(el,parentId){
              const raw=(el.getAttribute('text')||el.getAttribute('title')||'').trim();
              const childOutlines=Array.from(el.children).filter(c=>c.tagName&&c.tagName.toLowerCase()==='outline');
              if(!raw&&!childOutlines.length)return;
              let content=raw||'(绌?',done=false,date=null;
              if(el.getAttribute('_complete')==='true')done=true;
              const note=el.getAttribute('_note');
              if(note&&/^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}$/.test(note))date=note;
              const n=addNodeTo(s,parentId,null,{text:content,done,date});
              if(!n)return;
              count++;
              if(!firstId)firstId=n.id;
              childOutlines.forEach(c=>walk(c,n.id));
            }
            Array.from(body.children).forEach(c=>{if(c.tagName&&c.tagName.toLowerCase()==='outline')walk(c,s.rootId);});
          },{snapshot:true});
          if(count>0&&firstId)actions.logActivity('add',firstId);
          return count;
        }
        function importHtml(text){
          const doc=new DOMParser().parseFromString(text,'text/html');
          doc.querySelectorAll('script,style,head,nav,footer,header,title').forEach(el=>el.remove());
          const body=doc.body;if(!body)return 0;
          const entries=[];
          function walk(el,depth){
            for(const child of el.children){
              const tag=child.tagName.toLowerCase();
              if(/^h([1-6])$/.test(tag)){const t=child.textContent.trim();if(t)entries.push({depth:Math.max(0,parseInt(tag[1],10)-1),text:t});}
              else if(tag==='ul'||tag==='ol')walk(child,depth+1);
              else if(tag==='li'){
                const own=Array.from(child.childNodes).filter(n=>n.nodeType===3||(n.nodeType===1&&!/^(ul|ol)$/i.test(n.tagName))).map(n=>n.textContent).join('').trim();
                if(own)entries.push({depth,text:own});
                for(const sub of child.children){if(/^(ul|ol)$/i.test(sub.tagName))walk(sub,depth+1);}
              }else if(tag==='p'||tag==='div'){
                const hasBlocks=Array.from(child.children).some(c=>/^(div|p|ul|ol|li|h[1-6])$/i.test(c.tagName));
                if(hasBlocks)walk(child,depth);
                else{
                  const t=child.textContent.trim();
                  if(t){
                    if(t.length<900)entries.push({depth,text:t});
                    else{
                      const parts=t.split(/(?<=[銆傦紒锛燂紱.!?;])/).map(s=>s.trim()).filter(Boolean);
                      parts.forEach(p=>entries.push({depth,text:p}));
                    }
                  }
                }
              }
            }
          }
          walk(body,0);
          if(!entries.length)return 0;
          let count=0,firstId=null;
          commit(s=>{
            const stack=[{depth:-1,id:s.rootId}];
            for(const e of entries){
              let content=e.text.replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
              if(!content)continue;
              while(stack.length>1&&stack[stack.length-1].depth>=e.depth)stack.pop();
              const parentId=stack[stack.length-1].id;
              const n=addNodeTo(s,parentId,null,{text:content});
              if(!n)continue;
              count++;
              if(!firstId)firstId=n.id;
              stack.push({depth:e.depth,id:n.id});
            }
          },{snapshot:true});
          if(count>0&&firstId)actions.logActivity('add',firstId);
          return count;
        }
        
        
        function stripDangerKeys(v){
          if(!v||typeof v!=='object')return v;
          if(Array.isArray(v)){for(const item of v)stripDangerKeys(item);return v;}
          for(const k of ['__proto__','constructor','prototype']){
            if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(v,k))delete v[k];
          }
          for(const k in v){
            if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(v,k))stripDangerKeys(v[k]);
          }
          return v;
        }
        function validateImportedTree(p){
          if(!p||typeof p!=='object')return{ok:false,err:'涓嶆槸鏈夋晥瀵硅薄'};
          stripDangerKeys(p); 
          if(!p.nodes||typeof p.nodes!=='object')return{ok:false,err:'缂哄皯 nodes 瀛楁'};
          if(!p.rootId||!p.nodes[p.rootId])return{ok:false,err:'缂哄皯鏍硅妭鐐?};
          
          const visited=new Set();
          const onStack=new Set();
          const stack=[p.rootId];
          while(stack.length){
            const id=stack[stack.length-1];
            const n=p.nodes[id];
            if(!n)return{ok:false,err:'寮曠敤浜嗕笉瀛樺湪鐨勮妭鐐癸細'+id};
            onStack.add(id);
            if(!Array.isArray(n.children)){onStack.delete(id);stack.pop();continue;}
            let advanced=false;
            for(const cid of n.children){
              if(typeof cid!=='string')return{ok:false,err:'瀛愯妭鐐?ID 闈炲瓧绗︿覆'};
              if(!p.nodes[cid])return{ok:false,err:'瀛愯妭鐐瑰紩鐢ㄤ笉瀛樺湪锛?+cid};
              if(onStack.has(cid))return{ok:false,err:'妫€娴嬪埌鑺傜偣寮曠敤鐜矾锛堣妭鐐?'+cid+'锛?};
              if(visited.has(cid))continue;
              visited.add(cid);
              stack.push(cid);
              advanced=true;
              break;
            }
            if(!advanced){onStack.delete(id);stack.pop();}
          }
          
          for(const id in p.nodes){
            if(visited.has(id))continue;
            const seen=new Set();
            let cur=id;
            const chain=[];
            while(cur&&!seen.has(cur)){
              if(!p.nodes[cur])break;
              seen.add(cur);chain.push(cur);
              cur=p.nodes[cur].parent;
            }
            if(cur&&seen.has(cur))return{ok:false,err:'妫€娴嬪埌婕傛诞瀛愭爲鐜矾锛堣妭鐐?'+cur+'锛?};
          }
          return{ok:true};
        }
        async function handleImportFiles(files){
          let total=0;const errors=[];
          for(const f of files){
            try{
              const text=await readFile(f);
              const ext=(f.name.split('.').pop()||'').toLowerCase();
              if(ext==='ics'){importICS(text);total++;continue;}
              if(ext==='opml'){total+=importOpml(text);continue;}
              if(ext==='db'||ext==='sqlite'||ext==='sqlite3'){
                if(!IS_DESKTOP){errors.push(f.name+'锛?db 浠呮闈㈢増鏀寔锛屾祻瑙堝櫒鐗堣瀵煎叆 JSON 澶囦唤');continue;}
                try{
                  const buf=await readFileArrayBuffer(f);
                  
                  await desktopInvoke('import_db',{bytes:new Uint8Array(buf)});
                  const ok=await desktopReloadIntoApp();
                  if(ok)toast('宸蹭粠 SQLite 鏂囦欢鎭㈠锛堝鍏ュ墠宸茶嚜鍔ㄥ浠藉綋鍓嶅簱锛?);
                  else errors.push(f.name+'锛氬鍏ョ殑鏁版嵁搴撲负绌烘垨涓嶅彲璇?);
                }catch(err){errors.push(f.name+'锛?+(err.message||'瀵煎叆澶辫触'));}
                continue;
              }
              if(ext==='docx'){errors.push(f.name+'锛?docx 涓轰簩杩涘埗鏍煎紡锛岃鍙﹀瓨涓?.html 鎴?.md 鍚庡鍏?);continue;}
              if(ext==='html'||ext==='htm'||ext==='doc'){total+=importHtml(text);continue;}
              if(ext==='json'){
                let p;
                try{p=JSON.parse(text);}catch(perr){errors.push(f.name+'锛欽SON 瑙ｆ瀽澶辫触');continue;}
                const isFull=!!(p&&p.app==='juju'&&p.type==='full-backup'&&p.state&&p.state.nodes&&p.state.rootId);
                const restoreState=isFull?p.state:p;
                if(!restoreState||typeof restoreState!=='object'||!restoreState.nodes||typeof restoreState.nodes!=='object'||!restoreState.rootId){
                  errors.push(f.name+'锛氫笉鏄湁鏁堢殑JU澶囦唤锛堢己灏?nodes 鎴?rootId锛?);
                  continue;
                }
                
                const v=validateImportedTree(restoreState);
                if(!v.ok){errors.push(f.name+'锛?+v.err);continue;}
                
                const normRestore=migrateStateObject(restoreState);
                if(!normRestore){errors.push(f.name+'锛氬浠界粨鏋勬棤娉曚慨澶?);continue;}
                try{
                  localStorage.setItem('juju.backup.preimport',JSON.stringify({ts:Date.now(),state}));
                }catch(e){
                  toast('澶囦唤褰撳墠鏁版嵁澶辫触锛屽凡鍙栨秷瀵煎叆');
                  continue;
                }
                let brokenRefs=0;
                for(const id in restoreState.nodes){
                  const n=restoreState.nodes[id];
                  if(!n||!Array.isArray(n.children))continue;
                  n.children.forEach(cid=>{if(!restoreState.nodes[cid])brokenRefs++;});
                }
                const ok=await new Promise(res=>{
                  appConfirm(
                    brokenRefs>0
                      ? `澶囦唤涓瓨鍦?${brokenRefs} 涓柇瑁傜殑瀛愯妭鐐瑰紩鐢紝瀵煎叆鍚庤繖浜涜妭鐐瑰皢涓嶅彲瑙併€備粛瑕佺户缁紵`
                      : (isFull?'瀹屾暣澶囦唤灏嗚鐩栧綋鍓嶆暟鎹紙鍚晫闈㈣缃?/ 鍥捐氨浣嶇疆 / 鍘嗗彶鐗堟湰锛屽凡鑷姩澶囦唤褰撳墠鏁版嵁锛?:'瀵煎叆 JSON 澶囦唤浼氳鐩栧綋鍓嶆暟鎹紙宸茶嚜鍔ㄥ浠斤級锛岀‘瀹氱户缁紵'),
                    ()=>res(true),
                    ()=>res(false));
                });
                if(!ok)continue;
                initStore(normRestore);
                ensureCalendarRoot();
                if(isFull){
                  if(p.ui&&typeof p.ui==='object')applyUIFields(p.ui);
                  if(p.graphpos&&typeof p.graphpos==='object'){_graphPositions=p.graphpos;saveGraphPositions();}
                  if(Array.isArray(p.versions))await restoreVersions(p.versions);
                  toast('宸叉仮澶嶅畬鏁村浠?);
                }else{
                  toast('宸叉仮澶嶅浠?);
                }
                saveUI();
                renderSidebar();
                forceRender();
                flushPersistNow();
                schedulePersist();
                return;
              }
              total+=importOutline(text);
            }catch(err){errors.push(f.name+'锛?+(err.message||'鏈煡閿欒'));}
          }
          renderSidebar();forceRender();
          if(errors.length)toast('閮ㄥ垎澶辫触锛?+errors.join('锛?));
          else if(total>0)toast(`宸插鍏?${files.length} 涓枃浠讹紝鍏?${total} 涓妭鐐筦);
          else toast('鏈瘑鍒埌鍙鍏ョ殑鍐呭');
        }
        function importICS(text){
          text=String(text||'').replace(/\r\n[ \t]|\n[ \t]|\r[ \t]/g,'');
          const unescIcs=s=>String(s||'')
            .replace(/\\n/gi,'\n')
            .replace(/\\,/g,',')
            .replace(/\\;/g,';')
            .replace(/\\\\/g,'\\');
          const events=text.split('BEGIN:VEVENT');
          const calRootId=getCalendarRootId();
          const items=[];
          events.slice(1).forEach(ev=>{
            const summary=unescIcs((ev.match(/SUMMARY[^:]*:([^\r\n]+)/)||[])[1]||'imported');
            const dtstart=(ev.match(/DTSTART[^:]*:([^\r\n]+)/)||[])[1]||'';
            if(dtstart&&dtstart.length>=8){
              const y=dtstart.slice(0,4),m=dtstart.slice(4,6),d=dtstart.slice(6,8);
              const dateStr=y+'-'+m+'-'+d;
              let timeStr=null;
              const tMatch=dtstart.match(/T(\d{2})(\d{2})(\d{2})?(Z)?/);
              if(tMatch){
                let hh=parseInt(tMatch[1],10),mm=parseInt(tMatch[2],10);
                if(tMatch[4]==='Z'){
                  const utc=new Date(Date.UTC(parseInt(y,10),parseInt(m,10)-1,parseInt(d,10),hh,mm));
                  hh=utc.getHours();mm=utc.getMinutes();
                }
                timeStr=pad(hh)+':'+pad(mm);
              }
              const opts={text:summary.trim(),date:dateStr,showInCal:true};
              if(timeStr)opts.time=timeStr;
              items.push(opts);
            }
          });
          if(items.length){
            commit(s=>{
              items.forEach(opts=>addNodeTo(s,calRootId,null,opts));
            },{snapshot:true});
          }
          forceRender();
          toast('宸插鍏?'+items.length+' 涓簨浠?);
        }
        function openImportDialog(){
          const modal=document.createElement('div');modal.className='modal-overlay';
          
          modal.innerHTML=`<div class="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true"><div class="modal-head"><span>鈫?瀵煎叆鍐呭</span><button class="modal-close">鉁?/button></div><div class="modal-body"><div id="impDrop" style="border:2px dashed var(--line);border-radius:10px;padding:24px 20px;text-align:center;cursor:pointer;margin-bottom:12px"><div style="font-size:2em;color:var(--icon)">鈫?/div><div style="font-weight:600;margin-top:6px">鎷栨斁鏂囦欢鍒版澶?/div><div style="font-size:.82em;color:var(--fg-dim);margin-top:3px">鎴栫偣鍑婚€夋嫨鏂囦欢锛堝彲澶氶€夛級</div><input type="file" id="impFile" multiple accept=".md,.markdown,.txt,.opml,.html,.htm,.doc,.json,.ics,.db,.sqlite,.sqlite3" style="display:none"></div></div></div>`;
          document.body.appendChild(modal);
          
          _modalStack.push(modal);
          const _isTopImp=()=>_modalStack[_modalStack.length-1]===modal;
          const _popImp=()=>{const i=_modalStack.indexOf(modal);if(i>=0)_modalStack.splice(i,1);};
          const onImpKey=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&_isTopImp())close();};
          const close=()=>{document.removeEventListener('keydown',onImpKey);modal.remove();_popImp();};
          document.addEventListener('keydown',onImpKey);
          modal.querySelector('.modal-close').onclick=close;
          modal.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===modal&&_isTopImp())close();};
          const drop=modal.querySelector('#impDrop');
          const fileInput=modal.querySelector('#impFile');
          drop.onclick=()=>fileInput.click();
          fileInput.onchange=()=>{if(fileInput.files&&fileInput.files.length){handleImportFiles(Array.from(fileInput.files)).then(close);}};
          ['dragenter','dragover'].forEach(ev=>drop.addEventListener(ev,e=>{e.preventDefault();drop.style.borderColor='var(--accent)';drop.style.background='var(--accent-soft)';}));
          ['dragleave','drop'].forEach(ev=>drop.addEventListener(ev,e=>{e.preventDefault();drop.style.borderColor='var(--line)';drop.style.background='';}));
          drop.addEventListener('drop',e=>{e.preventDefault();if(e.dataTransfer.files&&e.dataTransfer.files.length){handleImportFiles(Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files)).then(close);}});
        }

        function bindMainToggles(){
          
          const L=$('#btnToggleLeft'),R=$('#btnToggleRight');
          const syncL=()=>{const sb=$('#sidebarLeft');const c=sb.classList.contains('collapsed');ui.sidebarLeftCollapsed=c;document.body.classList.toggle('sb-left-collapsed',c);L.classList.toggle('collapsed',c);L.title=c?'灞曞紑宸︿晶鏍?:'鏀惰捣宸︿晶鏍?;L.setAttribute('aria-pressed',c?'false':'true');};
          const syncR=()=>{const sb=$('#sidebarRight');const c=sb.classList.contains('collapsed');ui.sidebarRightCollapsed=c;document.body.classList.toggle('sb-right-collapsed',c);R.classList.toggle('collapsed',c);R.title=c?'灞曞紑鍙充晶鏍?:'鏀惰捣鍙充晶鏍?;R.setAttribute('aria-pressed',c?'false':'true');};
          L.onclick=()=>{$('#sidebarLeft').classList.toggle('collapsed');syncL();saveUI();};
          R.onclick=()=>{$('#sidebarRight').classList.toggle('collapsed');syncR();saveUI();};
          syncL();syncR();
          
          (function(){
            const handle=$('#sidebarResize');if(!handle)return;
            let startX=0,startW=0,resizing=false;
            handle.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
              e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
              resizing=true;startX=e.clientX;
              startW=document.getElementById('sidebarLeft').offsetWidth;
              handle.classList.add('dragging');document.body.classList.add('sb-resizing');
              document.addEventListener('mousemove',onMove);
              document.addEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
            });
            function onMove(e){
              if(!resizing)return;
              const cap=Math.min(480,window.innerWidth*0.3);
              let w=startW+(e.clientX-startX);
              w=Math.max(140,Math.min(cap,w));
              
              handle.classList.toggle('at-limit',w>=cap-1);
              document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-left-w',w+'px');
              ui.sidebarLeftWidth=w;
            }
            function onUp(){
              if(!resizing)return;resizing=false;
              handle.classList.remove('dragging');document.body.classList.remove('sb-resizing');
              document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onMove);
              document.removeEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
              saveUI();
            }
          })();
          
          (function(){
            const handle=$('#sidebarRightResize');if(!handle)return;
            let startX=0,startW=0,resizing=false;
            handle.addEventListener('mousedown',e=>{
              e.preventDefault();e.stopPropagation();
              resizing=true;startX=e.clientX;
              startW=document.getElementById('sidebarRight').offsetWidth;
              handle.classList.add('dragging');document.body.classList.add('sb-resizing');
              document.addEventListener('mousemove',onMove);
              document.addEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
            });
            function onMove(e){
              if(!resizing)return;
              const cap=Math.min(340,window.innerWidth*0.25);
              let w=startW+(startX-e.clientX);
              w=Math.max(180,Math.min(cap,w));
              handle.classList.toggle('at-limit',w>=cap-1);
              document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-right-w',w+'px');
              ui.sidebarRightWidth=w;
            }
            function onUp(){
              if(!resizing)return;resizing=false;
              handle.classList.remove('dragging');document.body.classList.remove('sb-resizing');
              document.removeEventListener('mousemove',onMove);
              document.removeEventListener('mouseup',onUp);
              saveUI();
            }
          })();
        }

        function tickClock(){
          const d=new Date();
          const el=$('#tbClock');
          if(el)el.textContent=`${d.getFullYear()}.${d.getMonth()+1}.${d.getDate()} ${pad(d.getHours())}:${pad(d.getMinutes())}:${pad(d.getSeconds())}`;

        const VDB_NAME='juju-versions';
        const VDB_VERSION=1;
        const VDB_CHANNEL='juju-vdb';
        
        let _vdbUnavailableToastShown=false;
        function toastVDBUnavailable(){
          if(_vdbUnavailableToastShown)return;
          _vdbUnavailableToastShown=true;
          try{toast('鐗堟湰鍘嗗彶鍦ㄥ綋鍓嶇幆澧冧笉鍙敤');}catch(e){}
        }
        const VDB={
          db:null,opening:null,writable:true,bc:null,_bcReady:false,__tabId:null,unavailable:false,
          initChannel(){
            if(this._bcReady)return;
            this._bcReady=true;
            try{this.bc=new BroadcastChannel(VDB_CHANNEL);this.bc.onmessage=(e)=>this._onBcMessage(e.data);}
            catch(e){this.bc=null;}
          },
          _tabId(){if(!this.__tabId)this.__tabId=Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,8);return this.__tabId;},
          _broadcast(msg){if(this.bc){try{this.bc.postMessage(msg);}catch(e){}}},
          _onBcMessage(msg){
            if(!msg||!msg.type)return;
            if(msg.type==='upgrade-request'){
              if(this.db){
                try{this.db.close();}catch(e){}
                this.db=null;
                this.writable=false;
                this._broadcast({type:'closed',from:this._tabId()});
                setTimeout(()=>this.ensure().catch(()=>{}),400);
              }
            }
          },
          ensure(){
            if(this.db)return Promise.resolve(this.db);
            if(this.opening)return this.opening;
            this.initChannel();
            this.opening=new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{
              let blockedTimer=null;
              let settled=false;
              let req;
              try{req=indexedDB.open(VDB_NAME,VDB_VERSION);}
              catch(e){this.opening=null;this.unavailable=true;toastVDBUnavailable();reject(e);return;} 
              req.onupgradeneeded=(e)=>{
                const db=e.target.result;
                if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('snapshots')){
                  const os=db.createObjectStore('snapshots',{keyPath:'id',autoIncrement:true});
                  os.createIndex('docId','docId',{unique:false});
                  os.createIndex('ts','ts',{unique:false});
                }
                if(!db.objectStoreNames.contains('meta')){
                  db.createObjectStore('meta',{keyPath:'k'});
                }
              };
              req.onsuccess=()=>{
                clearTimeout(blockedTimer);
                if(settled){req.result.close();return;}
                settled=true;
                const db=req.result;
                this.db=db;
                this.writable=true;
                db.onversionchange=()=>{
                  try{db.close();}catch(e){}
                  this.db=null;
                  this.writable=false;
                  this._broadcast({type:'closed',from:this._tabId()});
                  setTimeout(()=>this.ensure().catch(()=>{}),500);
                };
                db.onclose=()=>{this.db=null;this.writable=false;};
                this.opening=null;
                resolve(db);
              };
              req.onerror=()=>{
                clearTimeout(blockedTimer);
                this.opening=null;
                this.unavailable=true; 
                if(!settled){settled=true;toastVDBUnavailable();reject(req.error);}
              };
              req.onblocked=()=>{
                this.writable=false;
                this._broadcast({type:'upgrade-request',v:VDB_VERSION});
                toast('鍙︿竴涓爣绛鹃〉姝ｅ湪浣跨敤鐗堟湰搴擄紝姝ｅ湪绛夊緟鍏堕噴鏀锯€?);
                blockedTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
                  if(!settled)toast('鐗堟湰搴撹鍏跺畠鏍囩椤靛崰鐢紝璇峰叧闂叾瀹僇U鏍囩椤靛悗鍒锋柊');
                },8000);
              };
            }).finally(()=>{this.opening=null;});
            return this.opening;
          },
          async tx(storeNames,mode,fn){
            const db=await this.ensure();
            
            if(mode==='readwrite'&&!this.writable){
              const err=new Error('VDB readonly: store locked by another tab');
              err.code='VDB_READONLY';
              throw err;
            }
            const tx=db.transaction(storeNames,mode);
            const stores=Array.isArray(storeNames)?storeNames.map(n=>tx.objectStore(n)):[tx.objectStore(storeNames)];
            const result=await fn(...stores,tx);
            await new Promise((res,rej)=>{
              tx.oncomplete=()=>res();
              tx.onerror=()=>rej(tx.error);
              tx.onabort=()=>rej(tx.error||new Error('tx aborted'));
            });
            return result;
          },
          close(){if(this.db){try{this.db.close();}catch(e){}this.db=null;}},
          destroy(){
            this.close();
            if(this.bc){try{this.bc.close();}catch(e){}this.bc=null;}
            this._bcReady=false;
            return new Promise((res)=>{
              const req=indexedDB.deleteDatabase(VDB_NAME);
              req.onsuccess=req.onerror=req.onblocked=()=>res();
            });
          }
        };
        function idbReq(req){
          return new Promise((res,rej)=>{req.onsuccess=()=>res(req.result);req.onerror=()=>rej(req.error);});
        }
        let _versSalt=null;
        let _versSaltPromise=null;
        async function getVersSalt(){
          if(_versSalt)return _versSalt;
          if(_versSaltPromise)return _versSaltPromise;
          _versSaltPromise=(async()=>{
            const row=await VDB.tx('meta','readonly',(store)=>idbReq(store.get('salt')));
            if(row&&row.v){_versSalt=b64ToBuf(row.v);return _versSalt;}
            const salt=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(16));
            await VDB.tx('meta','readwrite',(store)=>{store.put({k:'salt',v:bufToB64(salt)});});
            _versSalt=salt;
            return salt;
          })();
          return _versSaltPromise;
        }
        async function writeSnapshot(docId,ts,title,nodes){
          let record={docId,ts,title};
          if(ui.encrypted && ui.encKey){
            const salt=await getVersSalt();
            const iv=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
            const key=await deriveKey(ui.encKey,salt,ENC_ITER);
            const plain=new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(nodes));
            const cipher=await crypto.subtle.encrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv},key,plain);
            record.enc=true;
            record.iv=bufToB64(iv);
            record.cipher=bufToB64(cipher);
          }else{
            record.enc=false;
            record.nodes=nodes;
          }
          if(IS_DESKTOP){
            
            try{
              await desktopInvoke('save_snapshot',{docId,ts,title:title||'',data:JSON.stringify(record)});
            }catch(e){console.warn('[JU] snapshot save failed',e);}
            return;
          }
          await VDB.tx('snapshots','readwrite',async(store)=>{
            store.add(record);
            const idx=store.index('docId');
            const keys=await new Promise((res,rej)=>{
              const out=[];
              const req=idx.openCursor(IDBKeyRange.only(docId));
              req.onsuccess=()=>{
                const cur=req.result;
                if(!cur){res(out);return;}
                out.push(cur.primaryKey);
                cur.continue();
              };
              req.onerror=()=>rej(req.error);
            });
            const keep=30;
            const toDelete=keys.slice(0,Math.max(0,keys.length-keep));
            for(const k of toDelete)store.delete(k);
          });
        }
        async function listSnapshots(docId){
          if(IS_DESKTOP){
            let rows=[];
            try{
              const raw=await desktopInvoke('list_snapshots',{docId});
              rows=(typeof raw==='string'?JSON.parse(raw):raw)||[];
            }catch(e){return [];}
            rows.sort((a,b)=>a.ts-b.ts);
            const out=[];
            for(const r of rows){
              let rec=null;
              try{rec=typeof r.data==='string'?JSON.parse(r.data):r.data;}catch(e){rec=null;}
              if(!rec){out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:{},_corrupt:true});continue;}
              if(!rec.enc){out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:repairSnapshotNodes(rec.nodes)});continue;}
              if(!ui.encKey){out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:{},_locked:true});continue;}
              try{
                const salt=await getVersSalt();
                const key=await deriveKey(ui.encKey,salt,ENC_ITER);
                const plain=await crypto.subtle.decrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv:b64ToBuf(rec.iv)},key,b64ToBuf(rec.cipher));
                out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:repairSnapshotNodes(JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(plain)))});
              }catch(e){
                out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:{},_corrupt:true});
              }
            }
            return out;
          }
          let rows;
          try{
            rows=await VDB.tx('snapshots','readonly',(store)=>{
              const idx=store.index('docId');
              return idbReq(idx.getAll(IDBKeyRange.only(docId)));
            });
          }catch(e){toastVDBUnavailable();return [];} 
          rows.sort((a,b)=>a.ts-b.ts);
          const out=[];
          for(const r of rows){
            if(!r.enc){out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:repairSnapshotNodes(r.nodes)});continue;}
            if(!ui.encKey){out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:{},_locked:true});continue;}
            try{
              const salt=await getVersSalt();
              const key=await deriveKey(ui.encKey,salt,ENC_ITER);
              const plain=await crypto.subtle.decrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv:b64ToBuf(r.iv)},key,b64ToBuf(r.cipher));
              out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:repairSnapshotNodes(JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(plain)))});
            }catch(e){
              out.push({ts:r.ts,title:r.title,nodes:{},_corrupt:true});
            }
          }
          return out;
        }
        async function migrateVersionsFromLS(){
          try{
            const flag=await VDB.tx('meta','readonly',(store)=>idbReq(store.get('migrated')));
            if(flag&&flag.v)return;
          }catch(e){return;}
          let plain={};
          try{plain=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(VERS_KEY)||'{}');}catch(e){}
          const encRaw=localStorage.getItem(VERS_ENC_KEY);
          if(encRaw&&ui.encKey){
            try{
              const dec=await decryptState(JSON.parse(encRaw),ui.encKey);
              Object.assign(plain,dec);
            }catch(e){}
          }
          const hasData=Object.keys(plain).some(k=>(plain[k]||[]).length);
          if(hasData){
            try{
              await VDB.tx('snapshots','readwrite',(store)=>{store.clear();});
              for(const docId in plain){
                for(const snap of plain[docId]||[]){
                  await writeSnapshot(docId,snap.ts,snap.title,snap.nodes);
                }
              }
            }catch(e){console.warn('migrate versions failed',e);}
          }
          await VDB.tx('meta','readwrite',(store)=>{store.put({k:'migrated',v:1});});
          try{
            localStorage.removeItem(VERS_KEY);
            localStorage.removeItem(VERS_ENC_KEY);
          }catch(e){}
        }
        let _versionRunning=false;
        let _versionRetryQueued=false; 
        async function takeVersionSnapshot(){
          if(_versionRunning){_versionRetryQueued=true;return;} 
          _versionRunning=true;
          try{
            const docId=ui._lastTouchedDocId;
            ui._lastTouchedDocId=null;
            if(!docId)return;
            const doc=N(docId);
            if(!doc||doc.id===state.rootId)return;
            const sub=docSubtree(docId);
            const nodes={};
            sub.forEach(id=>{
              const n=clone(state.nodes[id]);
              if(n){delete n._deleted;nodes[id]=n;}
            });
            await writeSnapshot(docId,Date.now(),stripHtml(doc.text||'鏈懡鍚?),nodes);
          }catch(e){
            console.warn('snapshot failed',e); 
            
            try{if(e&&e.code==='VDB_READONLY')toast('鐗堟湰搴撴殏鏃惰鍏跺畠鏍囩鍗犵敤锛屽皢鍦ㄧ┖闂插悗琛ュ瓨蹇収');}catch(_){}
            
            try{if(VDB.unavailable)toastVDBUnavailable();}catch(_){}
          }finally{
            _versionRunning=false;
            
            if(_versionRetryQueued){_versionRetryQueued=false;takeVersionSnapshot();}
          }
        }
        async function loadVersionList(docId){
          try{return await listSnapshots(docId);}
          catch(e){
            
            console.warn('loadVersionList failed',e);
            toastVDBUnavailable();
            return [];
          }
        }
