        const JU_VER='fixed23';
        const STATE_VER=18;
        const AUTO_BACKUP_KEY='juju.autobackup.v18';
        function migrateStateObject(st){
        
          try{
            if(!st||typeof st!=='object')return null;
            if(!st.nodes||typeof st.nodes!=='object')return null;
            if(!st.rootId||!st.nodes[st.rootId]){
              let rootId=null;
              for(const id in st.nodes){
                const n=st.nodes[id];
                if(n&&(n._isRoot||n.text==='鎴戠殑鏂囨。')){rootId=id;break;}
              }
              if(!rootId){
                for(const id in st.nodes){
                  const n=st.nodes[id];
                  if(n&&(!n.parent||n.parent===id)){rootId=id;break;}
                }
              }
              if(!rootId)return null;
              st.rootId=rootId;
            }
            const root=st.nodes[st.rootId];
            if(!root||typeof root!=='object')return null;
            if(typeof root.children==='undefined')root.children=[];
            stripDangerKeys(st.nodes); 
            for(const id in st.nodes){
              const n=st.nodes[id];
              if(!n||typeof n!=='object'){delete st.nodes[id];continue;}
              if(n.parent===id)delete n.parent;
              if(!Array.isArray(n.children))n.children=[];
              n.children=n.children.filter(c=>c&&c!==id&&c!==st.rootId&&st.nodes[c]);
            }
            const v=validateImportedTree(st);
            if(!v.ok){
              
              const trashedIds=new Set();
              if(Array.isArray(st.trashed))st.trashed.forEach(t=>{
                if(!t)return;
                if(t.rootId)trashedIds.add(t.rootId);
                if(Array.isArray(t.ids))t.ids.forEach(i=>{if(i)trashedIds.add(i);});
              });
              const reach=new Set();const queue=[st.rootId];const seen=new Set();
              while(queue.length){
                const id=queue.shift();
                if(seen.has(id))continue;seen.add(id);
                const n=st.nodes[id];if(!n)continue;
                reach.add(id);
                (n.children||[]).forEach(c=>{if(st.nodes[c]&&!seen.has(c))queue.push(c);});
              }
              for(const id in st.nodes){
                if(reach.has(id))continue;
                if(trashedIds.has(id))continue; 
                const n=st.nodes[id];
                
                if(n&&typeof n==='object'){
                  n.parent=st.rootId;
                  if(!st.nodes[st.rootId].children.includes(id))st.nodes[st.rootId].children.push(id);
                  continue;
                }
                delete st.nodes[id];
              }
              const v2=validateImportedTree(st);
              if(!v2.ok)return null;
            }
            if(typeof st.tagColors==='undefined')st.tagColors={};
            if(typeof st.dbFields==='undefined')st.dbFields=['鏍囬','浼樺厛绾?,'鐘舵€?,'鏃堕棿'];
            if(typeof st.activity==='undefined')st.activity={};
            
            for(const ds in st.activity){
              if(!st.activity[ds]||typeof st.activity[ds]!=='object'){st.activity[ds]={add:[],mod:[],del:[]};continue;}
              for(const k of ['add','mod','del']){
                if(!Array.isArray(st.activity[ds][k]))st.activity[ds][k]=[];
              }
            }
            if(typeof st._rev!=='number')st._rev=parseInt(st._rev,10)||0;
            if(typeof st.updatedAt!=='number')st.updatedAt=Date.now();
            
            try{
              const lc=Array.isArray(st._localCreated)?st._localCreated.filter((id,i,a)=>typeof id==='string'&&st.nodes[id]&&a.indexOf(id)===i):[];
              st._localCreated=lc;
            }catch(e){try{st._localCreated=[];}catch(e2){}}
            
            try{
              if(st._tombstones&&typeof st._tombstones==='object'){
                const _now=Date.now();
                for(const k in st._tombstones){const _ts=+st._tombstones[k];if(!_ts||_now-_ts>30000)delete st._tombstones[k];}
                if(!Object.keys(st._tombstones).length)delete st._tombstones;
              }
            }catch(e){}
            if(typeof st._ver==='undefined')st._ver=STATE_VER;
            else st._ver=STATE_VER;
            return st;
          }catch(e){
            console.error('[JU] migrateStateObject failed',e);
            return null;
          }
        }
        
        function migrateOrFallback(st){
          const r=migrateStateObject(st);
          if(!r&&st){try{toast('妫€娴嬪埌鏁版嵁鎹熷潖涓旀棤娉曡嚜鍔ㄤ慨澶嶏紝宸查噸缃负榛樿鐘舵€?);}catch(e){}}
          return r||defaultState();
        }
        function repairSnapshotNodes(nodes){  if(!nodes||typeof nodes!=='object')return {};  const out={};
          for(const id in nodes){
            const n=nodes[id];
            if(!n||typeof n!=='object')continue;
            if(!Array.isArray(n.children))n.children=[];
            n.children=n.children.filter(c=>c&&c!==id&&nodes[c]);
            out[id]=n;
          }
          return out;
        }
        let _lastAutoBackupTs=0; 
        function saveAutoBackup(force){
          try{
            if(ui.locked)return;
            const now=Date.now();
            
            if(!force&&now-_lastAutoBackupTs<10000)return;
            
            if(ui.encrypted){
              try{saveEncryptedAutoBackup();}catch(e){}
              _lastAutoBackupTs=now;
              return;
            }
            
            const cur=getState();
            if(!cur||!cur.nodes||!cur.rootId)return;
            
            const mainLen=((localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY)||'').length)||0;
            if(mainLen>3500000){
              try{toast('瀛樺偍绌洪棿鎺ヨ繎涓婇檺锛屽凡鏆傚仠鑷姩澶囦唤锛岃灏藉揩瀵煎嚭澶囦唤');}catch(e){}
              return;
            }
            const payload={state:clone(cur),ui:uiSnapshot(),graphpos:clone(_graphPositions||{}),ts:now};
            localStorage.setItem(AUTO_BACKUP_KEY,JSON.stringify(payload));
            _lastAutoBackupTs=now;
          }catch(e){
            console.warn('[JU] saveAutoBackup failed',e);
            try{reportError('autobackup',{msg:'鑷姩澶囦唤澶辫触锛?+String((e&&e.message)||e)});}catch(_){}
          }
        }
        
        async function saveEncryptedAutoBackup(){
          try{
            if(!(ui&&ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey))return;
            const cur=getState();
            if(!cur||!cur.nodes||!cur.rootId)return;
            const salt=await getVersSalt();
            const iv=crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(12));
            const key=await deriveKey(ui.encKey,salt,ENC_ITER);
            const plain=new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(cur));
            const cipher=await crypto.subtle.encrypt({name:'AES-GCM',iv},key,plain);
            const record={docId:'__autobackup__',ts:Date.now(),title:'鑷姩澶囦唤锛堝姞瀵嗭級',enc:true,iv:bufToB64(iv),cipher:bufToB64(cipher)};
            if(IS_DESKTOP){
              try{await desktopInvoke('save_snapshot',{docId:'__autobackup__',ts:record.ts,title:record.title,data:JSON.stringify(record)});}catch(e){}
              return;
            }
            await VDB.tx('snapshots','readwrite',async(store)=>{
              store.add(record);
              const idx=store.index('docId');
              const keys=await new Promise((res,rej)=>{
                const out=[];
                const req=idx.openCursor(IDBKeyRange.only('__autobackup__'));
                req.onsuccess=()=>{
                  const cur2=req.result;
                  if(!cur2){res(out);return;}
                  out.push(cur2.primaryKey);
                  cur2.continue();
                };
                req.onerror=()=>rej(req.error);
              });
              const keep=5;
              const toDelete=keys.slice(0,Math.max(0,keys.length-keep));
              for(const k of toDelete)store.delete(k);
            });
          }catch(e){
            try{reportError('encbackup',{msg:'鍔犲瘑鑷姩澶囦唤澶辫触锛?+String((e&&e.message)||e)});}catch(_){}
          }
        }
        
        function backupOnClose(){
          if(ui&&ui.autoBackupOnClose===false)return;
          try{saveAutoBackup(true);}catch(e){}
        }
        function loadAutoBackup(){
          try{
            const raw=localStorage.getItem(AUTO_BACKUP_KEY);
            if(!raw)return null;
            const p=JSON.parse(raw);
            if(!p||!p.state||!p.state.nodes)return null;
            return p;
          }catch(e){return null;}
        }
        
        let _dsSaveTimer=null,_dsSaving=false,_dsDirty=false,_dsSaveSeq=0;
        function scheduleDesktopSave(){
          if(!IS_DESKTOP)return;
          clearTimeout(_dsSaveTimer);
          _dsSaveTimer=setTimeout(()=>{flushDesktopSave();},400);
        }
        async function flushDesktopSave(){
          if(!IS_DESKTOP||ui.locked)return;
          if(ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey)return; 
          if(_dsSaving){_dsDirty=true;return;}
          _dsSaving=true;
          const seq=++_dsSaveSeq;
          try{
            await desktopInvoke('save_state',{payload:JSON.stringify(buildDesktopPayload())});
            if(seq!==_dsSaveSeq){_dsDirty=false;flushDesktopSave();}
          }catch(e){
            console.error('[JU] desktop save failed',e);
            try{toast('妗岄潰瀛樺偍淇濆瓨澶辫触锛?+(e.message||'鏈煡閿欒'));}catch(_){}
          }finally{
            _dsSaving=false;
            if(_dsDirty){_dsDirty=false;flushDesktopSave();}
          }
        }
        function buildDesktopPayload(){
          const st=state||{};
          const stateMeta={};
          for(const k in st){if(k==='nodes')continue;stateMeta[k]=st[k];}
          const nodes={};
          for(const id in (st.nodes||{})){nodes[id]=st.nodes[id];}
          return {stateMeta,nodes,ui:uiSnapshot(),graphpos:_graphPositions||{},remindSent:loadRemindSent(),ver:JU_VER};
        }
        async function desktopReloadIntoApp(){
          const snap=await dsLoadState();
          if(!snap||!snap.hasData||!snap.state||!snap.state.nodes)return false;
          const norm=migrateStateObject(snap.state);
          if(!norm){console.warn('[JU] desktopReloadIntoApp: migrate failed, falling back to default state');try{reportError('desktop-migrate',{msg:'妗岄潰蹇収杩佺Щ澶辫触'});}catch(e){}return false;}
          initStore(norm);
          ensureCalendarRoot();
          if(snap.ui&&typeof snap.ui==='object')applyUIFields(snap.ui);
          if(snap.graphpos&&typeof snap.graphpos==='object')_graphPositions=snap.graphpos;
          try{_calIndexCache=null;_calRecurCache=null;}catch(e){}
          renderSidebar();forceRender();flushPersistNow();
          return true;
        }
        async function dsLoadState(){
          const raw=await desktopInvoke('load_state',{});
          return typeof raw==='string'?JSON.parse(raw):raw;
        }
        function readFileArrayBuffer(file){
          return new Promise((res,rej)=>{
            const r=new FileReader();
            r.onload=()=>res(r.result);
            r.onerror=()=>rej(new Error('璇诲彇澶辫触'));
            r.readAsArrayBuffer(file);
          });
        }
        
        function systemInfo(){
          return {
            platform:navigator.platform||'unknown',
            
            userAgent:(function(){const ua=navigator.userAgent||'';const m=ua.match(/(HeadlessChrome|Chromium|Chrome|Edg|Firefox|Safari|Version)\/([\d.]+)/);return m?m[0]:ua.slice(0,40);})(),
            isDesktop:IS_DESKTOP,
            ver:JU_VER,
            mode:IS_DESKTOP?'sqlite':'localStorage+IndexedDB',
            nodeCount:state?Object.keys(state.nodes).length:0,
            errorCount:window.__juLog?window.__juLog.length:0,
            theme:document.documentElement.dataset.theme||'day'
          };
        }
        function openFeedbackDialog(){
          const sys=systemInfo();
          const modal=document.createElement('div');modal.className='modal-overlay';
          modal.innerHTML=`<div class="modal" role="dialog" aria-modal="true" style="max-width:600px"><div class="modal-head"><span>鍙嶉涓庨敊璇笂鎶?/span><button class="modal-close">鉁?/button></div><div class="modal-body">
            <div class="field"><label>闂鎻忚堪锛堝彲閫夛級</label><textarea id="fbNote" rows="3" placeholder="鎻忚堪浣犻亣鍒扮殑闂鈥? style="width:100%;padding:6px 8px;border:1px solid var(--line-soft);border-radius:5px;background:var(--bg-mid);color:var(--fg);resize:vertical"></textarea></div>
            <div style="font-size:.76em;color:var(--fg-dim);line-height:1.8;margin:8px 0;word-break:break-all">閿欒鏃ュ織 <b>${sys.errorCount}</b> 鏉?路 杩愯妯″紡 <b>${esc(sys.mode)}</b> 路 鑺傜偣 <b>${sys.nodeCount}</b> 路 鐗堟湰 ${sys.ver}<br>${esc(sys.platform)} 路 ${esc(sys.userAgent)}</div>
            <div style="display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px">
              <button class="btn" id="fbExport">瀵煎嚭閿欒鏃ュ織</button>
              <button class="btn" id="fbCopy">澶嶅埗鏃ュ織</button>
              <button class="btn primary" id="fbSend">瀵煎嚭鍙嶉鍖?/button>
            </div>
            <div style="font-size:.72em;color:var(--fg-dim);margin-top:8px">鍙嶉鍖呬负鏈湴 JSON 鏂囦欢锛屽寘鍚敊璇棩蹇椾笌绯荤粺淇℃伅锛涜闅忓伐鍗曚竴骞跺彂閫佺粰寮€鍙戣€呫€傛湭閰嶇疆涓婃姤绔偣鏃朵笉浼氳嚜鍔ㄨ仈缃戙€?/div>
          </div></div>`;
          document.body.appendChild(modal);
          
          _modalStack.push(modal);
          const _isTopFb=()=>_modalStack[_modalStack.length-1]===modal;
          const onFbKey=e=>{if(e.key==='Escape'&&_isTopFb())close();};
          
          const close=()=>{document.removeEventListener('keydown',onFbKey);modal.remove();const i=_modalStack.indexOf(modal);if(i>=0)_modalStack.splice(i,1);};
          document.addEventListener('keydown',onFbKey);
          modal.querySelector('.modal-close').onclick=close;
          modal.onclick=e=>{if(e.target===modal&&_isTopFb())close();};
          modal.querySelector('#fbExport').onclick=()=>{try{window.__juLogDownload&&window.__juLogDownload();}catch(e){} toast('宸插鍑洪敊璇棩蹇?);};
          modal.querySelector('#fbCopy').onclick=()=>{
            const txt=JSON.stringify(window.__juLog||[],null,2);
            if(navigator.clipboard&&navigator.clipboard.writeText)navigator.clipboard.writeText(txt).then(()=>toast('宸插鍒?)).catch(()=>toast('澶嶅埗澶辫触'));
            else toast('娴忚鍣ㄤ笉鏀寔鍓创鏉?);
          };
          modal.querySelector('#fbSend').onclick=()=>{
            const note=modal.querySelector('#fbNote').value||'';
            const pkg={ts:Date.now(),note,sys,log:window.__juLog||[]};
            download('JU-鍙嶉-'+todayStr()+'.json',JSON.stringify(pkg,null,2),'application/json');
            const ep=(typeof window!=='undefined'&&window.__juConfig&&window.__juConfig.reportEndpoint);
            if(ep){
              try{fetch(ep,{method:'POST',keepalive:true,headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(pkg)}).then(r=>{if(r.ok)toast('鍙嶉鍖呭凡鍙戦€?);else toast('鍙嶉鍖呭凡瀵煎嚭锛堝彂閫佸け璐ワ級');}).catch(()=>toast('鍙嶉鍖呭凡瀵煎嚭锛堟湭鑱旂綉锛?));}catch(e){toast('鍙嶉鍖呭凡瀵煎嚭');}
            }else toast('鍙嶉鍖呭凡瀵煎嚭锛堢函鏈湴锛屾湭鑱旂綉锛?);
          };
        }
        
        function loadState(){
          try{
            const raw=localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY);
            if(raw){
              const plain=JSON.parse(raw);
              if(plain&&plain.nodes&&typeof plain.nodes==='object'){
                
                return plain;
              }
            }
          }catch(e){}
          return null;
        }
        async function boot(){
          let initial=null;
          
          try{localStorage.removeItem(PENDING_KEY);}catch(e){}
          IS_DESKTOP=detectDesktop();
          if(IS_DESKTOP){
            try{
              const snap=await dsLoadState();
              if(snap&&snap.encStore){
                
                ui.encrypted=true;
                let st=null;
                try{st=await promptUnlock(JSON.parse(snap.encStore));}catch(e){st=null;}
                if(st){
                  ui.locked=false;
                  initial=migrateOrFallback(st);
                }else{
                  ui.encKey=null;ui.locked=true;
                  initial=defaultState();
                }
                loadUI();
              }else if(snap&&snap.hasData&&snap.state&&snap.state.nodes){
                initial=migrateOrFallback(snap.state);
                if(snap.ui&&typeof snap.ui==='object'){applyUIFields(snap.ui);}
                if(snap.ui&&typeof snap.ui.theme==='string')document.documentElement.dataset.theme=snap.ui.theme;
                if(snap.graphpos&&typeof snap.graphpos==='object')_graphPositions=snap.graphpos;
                if(snap.remindSent&&typeof snap.remindSent==='object'){try{localStorage.setItem(REMIND_SENT_KEY,JSON.stringify(snap.remindSent));}catch(e){}}
                ui.desktopUiApplied=true;
                applyScale();
              }else{
                
                if(hasEncryptedStore()){
                  initial=await promptUnlock();
                  if(!initial){
                    ui.encrypted=true;
                    ui.encKey=null;
                    ui.locked=true;
                    initial=defaultState();
                  }else{
                    ui.locked=false;
                    initial=migrateOrFallback(initial);
                  }
                }else{
                  const legacy=loadState();
                  const ab=loadAutoBackup();
                  const src=legacy?migrateStateObject(legacy):(ab?migrateStateObject(ab.state):null);
                  if(!legacy&&ab&&ab.ui)applyUIFields(ab.ui);
                  initial=src||defaultState();
                  if(!legacy&&ab)toast('涓绘暟鎹笉鍙锛屽凡浠庤嚜鍔ㄥ浠芥仮澶?);
                }
                loadUI();
              }
            }catch(e){
              console.error('[JU] desktop load failed, fallback browser mode',e);
              IS_DESKTOP=false;
              ui.desktopUiApplied=false;
              initial=null;
            }
          }
          if(!initial){
            if(hasEncryptedStore()){
              initial=await promptUnlock();
              if(!initial){
                
                ui.encrypted=true;
                ui.encKey=null;
                ui.locked=true;
                initial=defaultState();
              }else{
                ui.locked=false;
                initial=migrateOrFallback(initial);
              }
            }else{
              const stored=loadState();
              if(stored){
                initial=migrateOrFallback(stored);
              }else{
                const ab=loadAutoBackup();
                initial=ab?migrateOrFallback(ab.state):defaultState();
                if(ab&&!stored)toast('涓诲簱涓嶅彲璇伙紝宸茶嚜鍔ㄦ仮澶嶅埌鏈€杩戜竴娆″浠?);
              }
            }
          }
          
          if(initial&&typeof initial.tagColors==='undefined')initial.tagColors={};
          
          try{
            const pendRaw=sessionStorage.getItem('juju.pending.v1');
            if(pendRaw){
              const pend=JSON.parse(pendRaw);
              if(pend&&pend.state&&pend.state.nodes&&(pend.ts+pend.ttl)>Date.now()){
                initial=pend.state;
                if(typeof initial.tagColors==='undefined')initial.tagColors={};
              }
              sessionStorage.removeItem('juju.pending.v1');
            }
          }catch(e){}
          initStore(initial);
          ensureCalendarRoot();
          try{await VDB.ensure();}catch(e){}
          try{await migrateVersionsFromLS();}catch(e){}
          
          try{flushPersistNow();}catch(e){}
          await loadUIEncrypted();
          loadUI();
          const initTheme=document.documentElement.dataset.theme||'day';
          applyTheme(initTheme);
          if(!ui.currentDocId||!state.nodes[ui.currentDocId]){
            const root=state.nodes[state.rootId];
            const first=root.children.map(id=>state.nodes[id]).find(n=>n&&!n._isCalRoot&&!n._isDbRoot);
            ui.currentDocId=first?first.id:state.rootId;
          }
          
          if(window.innerWidth<=768){ui.sidebarLeftCollapsed=true;ui.sidebarRightCollapsed=true;}
          if(ui.sidebarLeftCollapsed){$('#sidebarLeft').classList.add('collapsed');$('#btnToggleLeft').classList.add('collapsed');document.body.classList.add('sb-left-collapsed');}
          if(ui.sidebarLeftWidth&&ui.sidebarLeftWidth!==230){document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-left-w',Math.min(ui.sidebarLeftWidth,window.innerWidth*0.35)+'px');} 
          if(ui.sidebarRightWidth&&ui.sidebarRightWidth!==250){document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--sidebar-right-w',Math.min(ui.sidebarRightWidth,window.innerWidth*0.25)+'px');} 
          if(ui.sidebarRightCollapsed){$('#sidebarRight').classList.add('collapsed');$('#btnToggleRight').classList.add('collapsed');}
          if(ui.floatCal){$('#floatCal').hidden=false;ensureElInViewport($('#floatCal'));}
          bindTopbar();bindSidebar();bindTools();bindMainToggles();bindRightSidebar();bindSettings();bindReminder();
          initPomo();initFloatCalDrag();initSettingsDrag();
          if(!ui.activeKey){
            const cur=ui.currentDocId?state.nodes[ui.currentDocId]:null;
            const v=cur?viewOf(cur):VIEW_KIND.LIBRARY_ROOT;
            if(v===VIEW_KIND.CALENDAR)setActiveKey('nav:calendar');
            else if(v===VIEW_KIND.DATABASE)setActiveKey('nav:database');
            else if(cur)setActiveKey('node:'+cur.id);
            else setActiveKey('nav:files');
          }
          subscribe(()=>{scheduleRender();});
          
          try{
            STORE_BC_INSTANCE=new BroadcastChannel(STORE_BC_NAME);
            STORE_BC_INSTANCE.onmessage=(e)=>{
              const d=e.data||{};
              
              if((d.updatedAt||0)<((state&&state.updatedAt)||0))return;
              
              try{
                if(ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey){
                  
                  const _mergeEnc=payload=>{if(payload)decryptState(payload,ui.encKey).then(st=>{if(st&&st.nodes)mergeRemoteIfNewer(st);}).catch(()=>{});};
                  if(IS_DESKTOP){
                    
                    dsLoadState().then(snap=>{
                      if(snap&&snap.encStore){try{_mergeEnc(JSON.parse(snap.encStore));}catch(e){}}
                    }).catch(()=>{});
                  }else{
                    _mergeEnc(readEncryptedStore());
                  }
                  return;
                }
                const raw=localStorage.getItem(STORE_KEY);
                onRemoteStoreChange(raw);
              }catch(err){}
            };
          }catch(e){STORE_BC_INSTANCE=null;}
          window.addEventListener('storage',e=>{
            if(e.key===STORE_KEY)onRemoteStoreChange(e.newValue);
            else if(e.key===ENC_KEY&&ui&&ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey){
              
              const payload=readEncryptedStore();
              if(payload)decryptState(payload,ui.encKey).then(st=>{if(st&&st.nodes)mergeRemoteIfNewer(st);}).catch(()=>{});
            }
          });
          let _snapTimer=null;
          let _lastSnapAt=0;
          const SNAP_IDLE_MS=2000;
          const SNAP_MIN_INTERVAL=60000;
          subscribe(()=>{
            clearTimeout(_snapTimer);
            const sinceLast=Date.now()-_lastSnapAt;
            const wait=sinceLast>=SNAP_MIN_INTERVAL?SNAP_IDLE_MS:Math.max(SNAP_IDLE_MS,SNAP_MIN_INTERVAL-sinceLast);
            _snapTimer=setTimeout(()=>{
              _lastSnapAt=Date.now();
              takeVersionSnapshot();
            },wait);
          });
          
          let _clockTimer=null;
          const _startClock=()=>{tickClock();if(!_clockTimer)_clockTimer=setInterval(tickClock,1000);};
          const _stopClock=()=>{if(_clockTimer){clearInterval(_clockTimer);_clockTimer=null;}};
          _startClock();
          
          let _recoveringPending=false;
          const _recoverPendingOnce=()=>{
            if(_recoveringPending)return;
            if(!(typeof recoverPendingEncrypted==='function'))return;
            _recoveringPending=true;
            Promise.resolve(recoverPendingEncrypted()).catch(()=>{}).then(()=>{_recoveringPending=false;});
          };
          $('#content').addEventListener('scroll',e=>{ui._lastScroll=e.target.scrollTop;},{passive:true});
          renderMain();renderFloatCal();updateRightSidebar();updateSidebarActiveState();
          updateEncryptSwitch();
          initReminder();
          
          recoverPendingEncrypted();
          if(ui.locked)showLockOverlay();
          document.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
            if((e.ctrlKey||e.metaKey)&&e.key.toLowerCase()==='k'){
              const ae=document.activeElement;
              if(ae&&(ae.isContentEditable||ae.tagName==='INPUT'||ae.tagName==='TEXTAREA'))return;
              e.preventDefault();
              const inp=$('#searchInput');
              if(inp){inp.focus();inp.select();}
            }
          });
          window.addEventListener('beforeunload',flushBeforeUnload);
          
          window.addEventListener('blur',()=>{
            document.querySelectorAll('.sb-row.dragging,.sb-row.drop-before,.sb-row.drop-after,.sb-row.drop-child').forEach(r=>{r.classList.remove('dragging','drop-before','drop-after','drop-child');});
            document.body.classList.remove('sb-resizing');
          });
          function flushBeforeUnload(){
            try{
              const ae=document.activeElement;
              if(ae&&ae.blur&&(ae.isContentEditable||ae.tagName==='INPUT'||ae.tagName==='TEXTAREA')){
                ae.blur();
              }
            }catch(e){}
            try{_flushPendingSession();}catch(e){} 
            try{flushPersistNow();}catch(e){}
            try{window.__juFlushErrors&&window.__juFlushErrors();}catch(e){} 
            
            try{if(_logTimers&&_logTimers.size!==0){console.warn('[JU] 鍏抽〉鍓嶄粛鏈?'+_logTimers.size+' 鏉℃棩蹇楀緟钀界洏');flushPendingLogs();}}catch(e){}
            try{backupOnClose();}catch(e){} 
          }
          document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{
            if(document.hidden){
              _stopClock(); 
              try{
                const ae=document.activeElement;
                if(ae&&ae.blur&&(ae.isContentEditable||ae.tagName==='INPUT'||ae.tagName==='TEXTAREA'))ae.blur();
              }catch(e){}
              try{_flushPendingSession();}catch(e){} 
              
              try{window.__juFlushErrors&&window.__juFlushErrors();}catch(e){}
              
              if(ui&&ui.encrypted&&ui.encKey){
                try{
                  clearTimeout(_encFallbackTimer);_encFallbackTimer=null;
                  encryptState(getState(),ui.encKey).then(payload=>{
                    _lastEncPayload=payload;
                    try{sessionStorage.setItem(PENDING_SYNC_KEY,JSON.stringify({payload,ts:Date.now(),ttl:60000}));}catch(e){}
                  }).catch(()=>{});
                }catch(e){}
              }
              try{backupOnClose();}catch(e){} 
            }else{
              _startClock(); 
              
              if(document.visibilityState==='visible')_recoverPendingOnce();
            }
            flushPersistNow();
            if(!_graphWorker)return;
            const w=_graphWorker;
            if(!w.ready)return;
            if(document.hidden)w.pause();
            else w.resume();
          });
        }
        boot();
        </script>
        <script>
        
        (function(){
          function getItems(container,itemSel,cfg){
            return [].filter.call(container.querySelectorAll(itemSel),()=>{
              if(cfg.expandedOnly&&!container.classList.contains('open'))return false;
              return true;
            });
          }
          function refreshRoving(container,itemSel,cfg){
            const items=getItems(container,itemSel,cfg);
            if(!items.length)return;
            let cur=items[0];
            if(cfg.cols){
              cur=container.querySelector('.cal-cell.selected')||container.querySelector('.cal-cell.today')||items[0];
            }
            items.forEach(x=>x.tabIndex=-1);
            cur.tabIndex=0;
          }
          function setupRoving(container,itemSel,cfg){
            if(!container._rovingNav){
              container._rovingNav=true;
              container.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
                const items=getItems(container,itemSel,cfg);
                if(!items.length)return;
                const cur=document.activeElement;
                const idx=items.indexOf(cur);
                if(idx<0)return;
                let nxt=-1;
                if(cfg.cols){
                  if(e.key==='ArrowRight')nxt=(idx+1)%items.length;
                  else if(e.key==='ArrowLeft')nxt=(idx-1+items.length)%items.length;
                  else if(e.key==='ArrowDown')nxt=Math.min(items.length-1,idx+cfg.step);
                  else if(e.key==='ArrowUp')nxt=Math.max(0,idx-cfg.step);
                }else{
                  if(e.key==='ArrowDown')nxt=Math.min(items.length-1,idx+1);
                  else if(e.key==='ArrowUp')nxt=Math.max(0,idx-1);
                }
                if(nxt>=0&&nxt!==idx){
                  e.preventDefault();
                  items.forEach((x,i)=>x.tabIndex=i===nxt?0:-1);
                  items[nxt].focus();
                  return;
                }
                if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();if(typeof cur.click==='function')cur.click();}
              });
            }
            refreshRoving(container,itemSel,cfg);
          }
          const GRID_CFG={cols:7,step:7};
          const TREE_CFG={vertical:true,expandedOnly:true};
          function decorate(el){
            if(!el||el.nodeType!==1)return;
            if(el.querySelectorAll){
              [].forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.sb-arrow:not(.leaf)'),x=>{if(!x.getAttribute('aria-label'))x.setAttribute('aria-label','灞曞紑鎴栨姌鍙犲瓙椤?);});
              [].forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.drag-handle'),x=>{if(!x.getAttribute('aria-label'))x.setAttribute('aria-label','鎷栧姩鎺掑簭鎴栫Щ鍔?);});
              [].forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.cal-sel-arrow'),x=>{if(x.getAttribute('aria-hidden')!=='true')x.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');});
              [].forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.cal-sel'),x=>{if(!x.hasAttribute('tabindex'))x.tabIndex=0;});
              [].forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.cal-grid'),x=>setupRoving(x,'.cal-cell',GRID_CFG));
              [].forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.sb-subtree'),x=>setupRoving(x,'.sb-row:not(.sb-empty)',TREE_CFG));
              [].forEach.call(el.querySelectorAll('.sb-row:not(.sb-empty)'),x=>{
                if(x.closest&&x.closest('.sb-subtree'))return;
                if(!x.hasAttribute('tabindex'))x.tabIndex=0;
                if(!x._kbEnter){
                  x._kbEnter=true;
                  x.addEventListener('keydown',e=>{
                    if(e.key==='Enter'||e.key===' '){e.preventDefault();if(typeof x.click==='function')x.click();}
                  });
                }
              });
            }
            if(el.classList&&(el.classList.contains('cal-grid')))setupRoving(el,'.cal-cell',GRID_CFG);
            if(el.classList&&(el.classList.contains('sb-subtree')))setupRoving(el,'.sb-row:not(.sb-empty)',TREE_CFG);
            if(el.classList&&el.classList.contains('cal-sel')&&!el.hasAttribute('tabindex'))el.tabIndex=0;
            const sub=el.closest&&el.closest('.sb-subtree');
            if(sub)refreshRoving(sub,'.sb-row:not(.sb-empty)',TREE_CFG);
          }
          
          const queue=[];let rafId=0;
          function flush(){
            rafId=0;
            while(queue.length)decorate(queue.shift());
          }
          function schedule(n){queue.push(n);if(!rafId)rafId=requestAnimationFrame(flush);}
          decorate(document);
          const mo=new MutationObserver(muts=>{
            for(const m of muts){
              if(m.type==='attributes'&&m.attributeName==='class'){
                const t=m.target;
                if(t&&t.nodeType===1&&t.classList){
                  if(t.classList.contains('sb-subtree')||t.classList.contains('cal-grid'))schedule(t);
                  else if(t.closest){const sub=t.closest('.sb-subtree');if(sub)schedule(sub);}
                }
                continue;
              }
              for(const n of m.addedNodes){
                if(n&&n.nodeType===1)schedule(n);
              }
            }
          });
          mo.observe(document.body,{childList:true,subtree:true,attributes:true,attributeFilter:['class']});
        })();
        </script>
        <script>
        
        (function () {
        'use strict';
        
        var L9_BASE_URL = (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.baseURI)
          ? document.baseURI
          : ((typeof location !== 'undefined' && location.href) ? location.href : 'file:
        
        var Buffer = globalThis.Buffer || (function () {
          var toU8 = function (x) { return (x instanceof Uint8Array) ? x : new Uint8Array(x); };
          var enc = function (x) { var u8 = toU8(x); var s = ''; for (var i = 0; i < u8.length; i++) s += String.fromCharCode(u8[i]); return btoa(s); };
          var dec = function (str) { var bin = atob(str); var u8 = new Uint8Array(bin.length); for (var i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) u8[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i); return u8; };
          return {
            from: function (a, e) {
              if (typeof a === 'string') return (e === 'base64') ? dec(a) : new TextEncoder().encode(a);
              return { toString: function (x) { return x === 'base64' ? enc(a) : String(a); } };
            },
          };
        })();
        
        
        const _nodeWebCrypto = (typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' && globalThis.crypto) ? globalThis.crypto : {};
        
        const _crypto = (globalThis.crypto && globalThis.crypto.subtle) ? globalThis.crypto : _nodeWebCrypto;
        const _subtle = _crypto.subtle;
        
        
        const ENC_ITER = 60000;
        const SALT_BYTES = 16;
        const IV_BYTES = 12;
        
        const _b64 = (u8) => Buffer.from(u8).toString('base64');
        const _unb64 = (s) => new Uint8Array(Buffer.from(String(s), 'base64'));
        
        
        function newSalt() { return _crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(SALT_BYTES)); }
        
        
        async function deriveKey(password, salt, iter = ENC_ITER) {
          const base = await _subtle.importKey(
            'raw', new TextEncoder().encode(String(password)), 'PBKDF2', false, ['deriveKey'],
          );
          return _subtle.deriveKey(
            { name: 'PBKDF2', salt, iterations: iter, hash: 'SHA-256' },
            base, { name: 'AES-GCM', length: 256 }, false, ['encrypt', 'decrypt'],
          );
        }
        
        
        async function encryptState(data, password, salt) {
          const s = salt instanceof Uint8Array ? salt : newSalt();
          const iv = _crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(IV_BYTES));
          const key = await deriveKey(password, s);
          const plain = new TextEncoder().encode(JSON.stringify(data));
          const cipher = await _subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv }, key, plain);
          return { v: 1, salt: _b64(s), iv: _b64(iv), cipher: _b64(cipher), iter: ENC_ITER, updatedAt: Date.now() };
        }
        
        
        async function decryptState(payload, password) {
          const salt = _unb64(payload.salt);
          const iv = _unb64(payload.iv);
          const key = await deriveKey(password, salt, payload.iter || ENC_ITER);
          const plain = await _subtle.decrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv }, key, _unb64(payload.cipher));
          return JSON.parse(new TextDecoder().decode(plain));
        }
        
        
        async function encryptBatch(batch, password, salt) {
          const s = salt instanceof Uint8Array ? salt : newSalt();
          const key = await deriveKey(password, s);
          const changed = [];
          for (const it of batch.changed || []) {
            const iv = _crypto.getRandomValues(new Uint8Array(IV_BYTES));
            const cipher = await _subtle.encrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv }, key, new TextEncoder().encode(String(it.data)));
            changed.push({ id: it.id, data: JSON.stringify({ v: 1, iv: _b64(iv), cipher: _b64(cipher) }) });
          }
          return {
            changed,
            deleted: (batch.deleted || []).slice(),
            enc: true,
            salt: _b64(s),
            iter: ENC_ITER,
            seq: batch.seq,
            baseRev: batch.baseRev,
          };
        }
        
        
        async function decryptBatch(encBatch, password) {
          const key = await deriveKey(password, _unb64(encBatch.salt), encBatch.iter || ENC_ITER);
          const changed = [];
          for (const it of encBatch.changed || []) {
            const o = JSON.parse(it.data);
            const plain = await _subtle.decrypt({ name: 'AES-GCM', iv: _unb64(o.iv) }, key, _unb64(o.cipher));
            changed.push({ id: it.id, data: new TextDecoder().decode(plain) });
          }
          return { changed, deleted: (encBatch.deleted || []).slice(), seq: encBatch.seq, baseRev: encBatch.baseRev };
        }
        
        
        function containsPlaintext(batch, needle) {
          const n = String(needle);
          for (const it of (batch && batch.changed) || []) {
            if (String(it && it.data).includes(n)) return true;
          }
          return false;
        }
        
        
        class KeyStore {
          constructor() { this._key = null; this._salt = null; this._pwd = null; }
          get locked() { return !this._key; }
          get salt() { return this._salt; }
          
          async unlock(password, salt) {
            const s = salt instanceof Uint8Array
              ? salt
              : (this._salt && this._pwd === password ? this._salt : newSalt());
            this._key = await deriveKey(password, s);
            this._salt = s;
            this._pwd = password;
            return this._key;
          }
          lock() { this._key = null; return this; }
        }
        
        
        
        
        
        const L9_MAX_PER_BATCH = 500;
        
        const L9_PATCH_MAX = 2000;
        
        const L9_FAIL_CAP = 50;
        
        const L9_CHECKPOINT_EVERY = 32;
        
        const L9_METRIC_CAP = 256;
        
        
        function nowMs() {
          const perf = typeof globalThis !== 'undefined' ? globalThis.performance : undefined;
          if (perf && typeof perf.now === 'function') return perf.now();
          if (typeof process !== 'undefined' && process.hrtime && typeof process.hrtime.bigint === 'function') {
            return Number(process.hrtime.bigint()) / 1e6;
          }
          return Date.now();
        }
        
        
        function percentile95(samples) {
          if (!Array.isArray(samples) || samples.length === 0) return NaN;
          const sorted = samples.slice().sort((a, b) => a - b);
          const rank = Math.ceil(0.95 * sorted.length); 
          const idx = Math.min(sorted.length - 1, Math.max(0, rank - 1));
          return sorted[idx];
        }
        
        
        function isBatch(b) {
          return !!b
            && Array.isArray(b.changed) && Array.isArray(b.deleted)
            && b.changed.every((it) => it && typeof it.id === 'string' && typeof it.data === 'string')
            && b.deleted.every((id) => typeof id === 'string')
            && typeof b.seq === 'number'
            && typeof b.baseRev === 'number';
        }
        
        
        function buildPersistBatch(dirtyNodes, removedIds) {
          const dirty = dirtyNodes instanceof Map ? dirtyNodes : new Map(Object.entries(dirtyNodes || {}));
          const removed = removedIds instanceof Set ? removedIds : new Set(removedIds || []);
          if (dirty.size + removed.size > L9_PATCH_MAX) return { reason: 'over-threshold', batches: [] };
        
          const deleted = Array.from(removed).filter(Boolean);
          const delSet = new Set(deleted);
          const changedIds = Array.from(dirty.keys()).filter((id) => id && !delSet.has(id));
          if (!changedIds.length && !deleted.length) return { reason: 'empty', batches: [] };
        
          const batches = [];
          const n = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(changedIds.length / L9_MAX_PER_BATCH));
          for (let i = 0; i < n; i++) {
            const slice = changedIds.slice(i * L9_MAX_PER_BATCH, (i + 1) * L9_MAX_PER_BATCH);
            const changed = slice.map((id) => ({ id, data: JSON.stringify(dirty.get(id)) }));
            batches.push({ changed, deleted: i === 0 ? deleted.slice() : [] });
          }
          return { reason: null, batches };
        }
        
        
        function equalProjection(a, b) {
          const ma = a instanceof Map ? a : new Map(Object.entries(a || {}));
          const mb = b instanceof Map ? b : new Map(Object.entries(b || {}));
          if (ma.size !== mb.size) return false;
          for (const [k, v] of ma) { if (!mb.has(k) || mb.get(k) !== v) return false; }
          return true;
        }
        
        
        class InMemoryBackend {
          constructor() {
            this._committed = new Map();
            this.failNext = 0;          
            this._crash = null;          
            this._lastSeq = 0;
          }
          
          crashBeforeCommit(fn) { this._crash = typeof fn === 'function' ? fn : null; }
          
          async transact(batch) {
            if (typeof this._crash === 'function') this._crash('begin', batch); 
            const buf = new Map();
            for (const it of batch.changed || []) buf.set(it.id, it.data);
            const dels = (batch.deleted || []).slice();
            if (typeof this._crash === 'function') this._crash('apply', batch); 
            if (this.failNext > 0) { this.failNext--; throw new Error('backend unavailable'); }
            
            for (const [k, v] of buf) this._committed.set(k, v);
            for (const id of dels) this._committed.delete(id);
            this._lastSeq = Math.max(this._lastSeq, batch.seq | 0);
            return { ok: true, seq: batch.seq | 0, changed: (batch.changed || []).length, deleted: dels.length };
          }
          
          async saveState(snapshot) {
            const next = new Map();
            const nodes = (snapshot && snapshot.nodes) || {};
            for (const id of Object.keys(nodes)) next.set(id, JSON.stringify(nodes[id]));
            if (this.failNext > 0) { this.failNext--; throw new Error('backend unavailable'); }
            this._committed = next; 
            return { ok: true, rows: next.size };
          }
          rows() { return new Map(this._committed); }
        }
        
        
        class PersistStore {
          constructor({ backend, failCap = L9_FAIL_CAP, checkpointEvery = L9_CHECKPOINT_EVERY, latencyCap = L9_METRIC_CAP } = {}) {
            this.backend = backend || new InMemoryBackend();
            this.failCap = failCap;
            this.checkpointEvery = checkpointEvery;
            this.latencyCap = latencyCap; 
            this.failures = [];          
            this.mode = 'patch';         
            this.seq = 0;                
            this.sinceFull = 0;          
            this.rev = 0;                
            this.checkpointPending = false;
            this._lastAcceptedSeq = 0;   
            this._latency = [];          
            this._latencyTotal = 0;      
          }
          
          recordLatency(ms) {
            const v = Number(ms);
            if (!Number.isFinite(v) || v < 0) return this;
            this._latency.push(v);
            if (this._latency.length > this.latencyCap) {
              this._latency.splice(0, this._latency.length - this.latencyCap);
            }
            this._latencyTotal++;
            return this;
          }
          
          p95() { return percentile95(this._latency); }
          stats() {
            return {
              mode: this.mode, seq: this.seq, sinceFull: this.sinceFull, rev: this.rev,
              pending: this.checkpointPending, failures: this.failures.length,
              
              p95: this.p95(), latencySamples: this._latencyTotal,
            };
          }
          
          failure(phase, op, err) {
            this.failures.push({
              phase: String(phase || ''), op: String(op == null ? '' : op),
              error: String((err && err.message) || err), ts: Date.now(),
            });
            if (this.failures.length > this.failCap) this.failures.splice(0, this.failures.length - this.failCap);
            this.mode = 'full';
          }
          setMode(m) { this.mode = m === 'full' ? 'full' : 'patch'; return this.mode; }
          
          async applyBatch(batch) {
            if (!isBatch(batch)) { this.failure('validate', 'patch_nodes', new Error('invalid batch shape')); return { ok: false }; }
            if (this._lastAcceptedSeq > 0 && (batch.seq | 0) <= this._lastAcceptedSeq) {
              return { ok: true, replay: true }; 
            }
            const t0 = nowMs(); 
            try {
              const ack = await this.backend.transact(batch);
              this._lastAcceptedSeq = batch.seq | 0;
              this.seq++;
              this.sinceFull++;
              this.rev++;
              if (this.sinceFull >= this.checkpointEvery) this.checkpointPending = true;
              return { ok: true, ack };
            } catch (e) {
              this.failure('patch', 'patch_nodes', e);
              return { ok: false };
            } finally {
              this.recordLatency(nowMs() - t0); 
            }
          }
          
          async flushNow(snapshot) {
            const t0 = nowMs(); 
            try {
              await this.backend.saveState(snapshot);
              this.rev++;
              this.sinceFull = 0;
              this.checkpointPending = false;
              this.mode = 'patch';
              return { ok: true };
            } catch (e) {
              this.failure('checkpoint', 'save_state', e);
              return { ok: false };
            } finally {
              this.recordLatency(nowMs() - t0); 
            }
          }
          
          projection() { return this.backend.rows(); }
          
          snapshot() {
            const out = {};
            for (const [id, json] of this.backend.rows()) {
              try { out[id] = JSON.parse(json); } catch (e) {  }
            }
            return out;
          }
        }
        
        
        
        
        const _TAG_RE = /#([\w\u4e00-\u9fff-]+)/g;
        const _WIKI_RE = /\[\[([^\]]+)\]\]/g;
        const _EN_RE = /[a-z0-9]+/g;
        const _CJK_RE = /[\u4e00-\u9fff\u3400-\u4dbf]/g;
        
        const _stripHtml = (s) => String(s || '').replace(/<[^>]*>/g, '');
        const _normTitle = (s) => String(s || '').trim().toLowerCase();
        
        
        function nodeAllTags(n) {
          const seen = new Set();
          if (!n) return seen;
          const raw = _stripHtml(n.text);
          let m;
          _TAG_RE.lastIndex = 0;
          while ((m = _TAG_RE.exec(raw)) !== null) { if (m[1]) seen.add(m[1]); }
          if (Array.isArray(n.tags) && n.tags.length) {
            for (const t of n.tags) {
              if (!t || seen.has(t)) continue;
              if (!(Array.isArray(n._manualTags) && n._manualTags.includes(t))) continue;
              seen.add(t);
            }
          }
          return seen;
        }
        
        
        function tokenize(text) {
          const out = new Set();
          if (!text) return out;
          const t = _stripHtml(text).toLowerCase();
          let m;
          _EN_RE.lastIndex = 0;
          while ((m = _EN_RE.exec(t)) !== null) { if (m[0].length >= 1 && m[0].length <= 32) out.add(m[0]); }
          _CJK_RE.lastIndex = 0;
          while ((m = _CJK_RE.exec(t)) !== null) { out.add(m[0]); }
          return out;
        }
        
        
        function extractWikiTitles(text) {
          const out = new Set();
          if (!text) return out;
          _WIKI_RE.lastIndex = 0;
          let m;
          while ((m = _WIKI_RE.exec(String(text))) !== null) {
            const title = _normTitle(m[1]);
            if (title) out.add(title);
          }
          return out;
        }
        
        const _pack = (map) => {
          const o = {};
          for (const k of Array.from(map.keys()).sort()) o[k] = Array.from(map.get(k)).sort();
          return o;
        };
        
        
        class IndexLayer {
          constructor() {
            this.cal = new Map();
            this.tag = new Map();
            this.search = new Map();
            this.backlink = new Map();
          }
          
          build(state) {
            this.cal.clear(); this.tag.clear(); this.search.clear(); this.backlink.clear();
            const nodes = (state && state.nodes) || {};
            for (const id of Object.keys(nodes)) {
              const n = nodes[id];
              if (!n || n._deleted) continue;
              this._add(id, n);
            }
            return this;
          }
          _push(map, key, id) {
            let s = map.get(key);
            if (!s) { s = new Set(); map.set(key, s); }
            s.add(id);
          }
          _pull(map, key, id) {
            const s = map.get(key);
            if (!s) return;
            s.delete(id);
            if (!s.size) map.delete(key);
          }
          _add(id, n) {
            if (n.date) this._push(this.cal, n.date, id);
            for (const t of nodeAllTags(n)) this._push(this.tag, t, id);
            for (const w of tokenize(n.text)) this._push(this.search, w, id);
            for (const title of extractWikiTitles(n.text)) this._push(this.backlink, title, id);
          }
          _remove(id, n) {
            if (n.date) this._pull(this.cal, n.date, id);
            for (const t of nodeAllTags(n)) this._pull(this.tag, t, id);
            for (const w of tokenize(n.text)) this._pull(this.search, w, id);
            for (const title of extractWikiTitles(n.text)) this._pull(this.backlink, title, id);
          }
          
          update(changedIds, fromState, toState) {
            const fromNodes = (fromState && fromState.nodes) || {};
            const toNodes = (toState && toState.nodes) || {};
            let count = 0;
            for (const id of changedIds || []) {
              if (!id) continue;
              const fromN = fromNodes[id];
              const toN = toNodes[id];
              if (fromN && !fromN._deleted) this._remove(id, fromN);
              if (toN && !toN._deleted) this._add(id, toN);
              count++;
            }
            return { ok: true, count };
          }
          byDate(d) { return new Set(this.cal.get(d) || []); }
          byTag(t) { return new Set(this.tag.get(t) || []); }
          lookupTerm(w) { return new Set(this.search.get(w) || []); }
          backlinksOf(title) { return new Set(this.backlink.get(_normTitle(title)) || []); }
          
          snapshot() {
            return { cal: _pack(this.cal), tag: _pack(this.tag), search: _pack(this.search), backlink: _pack(this.backlink) };
          }
        }
        
        
        
        const REQ = {
          PATCH: 'patch_nodes',   
          SAVE: 'save_state',     
          LOAD: 'load_state',     
          PING: 'ping',
          DISPOSE: 'dispose',
        };
        const RES = {
          ACK: 'ack',
          RESULT: 'result',
          ERROR: 'error',
          READY: 'ready',         
        };
        
        
        function makeRequest(id, type, payload) { return { id, type, payload }; }
        
        
        function isRequest(m) {
          return !!m && typeof m === 'object'
            && (typeof m.id === 'number' || typeof m.id === 'string')
            && typeof m.type === 'string';
        }
        
        
        function makeAck(id, extra) { return Object.assign({ id, type: RES.ACK }, extra || {}); }
        
        
        function makeError(id, err) {
          return { id, type: RES.ERROR, error: String((err && err.message) || err) };
        }
        
        
        
        
        const RESPONSE_TYPES = [RES.ACK, RES.RESULT, RES.ERROR];
        
        
        function makeResult(id, extra) { return Object.assign({ id, type: RES.RESULT }, extra || {}); }
        
        
        function isResponse(m) {
          return !!m && typeof m === 'object'
            && RESPONSE_TYPES.indexOf(m.type) !== -1
            && (typeof m.id === 'number' || typeof m.id === 'string');
        }
        
        
        function makeDispose(id) { return { id, type: REQ.DISPOSE, payload: null }; }
        
        
        function isDispose(m) { return !!m && typeof m === 'object' && m.type === REQ.DISPOSE; }
        
        
        
        
        
        
        const ADAPTER_KINDS = ['idb', 'invoke'];
        
        
        class _CoreBackend {
          constructor() {
            this._rows = new Map();   
            this.failNext = 0;        
            this.closed = false;      
          }
          
          setFailNext(n) { this.failNext = Math.max(0, n | 0); return this; }
          close() { this.closed = true; return this; }
          _gate() {
            if (this.closed) throw new Error('adapter closed');
            if (this.failNext > 0) { this.failNext--; throw new Error('backend unavailable'); }
          }
          async write(entries) {
            this._gate();
            const buf = new Map();
            for (const e of entries || []) {
              if (!e || typeof e.id !== 'string' || typeof e.data !== 'string') throw new Error('invalid entry');
              buf.set(e.id, e.data);
            }
            for (const [k, v] of buf) this._rows.set(k, v);
            return { ok: true, written: buf.size };
          }
          async read(ids) {
            this._gate();
            const want = ids ? Array.from(ids) : Array.from(this._rows.keys());
            const rows = {}; const missing = [];
            for (const id of want) {
              if (this._rows.has(id)) rows[id] = this._rows.get(id);
              else missing.push(id);
            }
            return { ok: true, rows, missing };
          }
          async del(ids) {
            this._gate();
            let n = 0;
            for (const id of ids || []) { if (this._rows.delete(id)) n++; }
            return { ok: true, deleted: n };
          }
          async query(opts) {
            this._gate();
            const o = opts || {};
            const prefix = typeof o.prefix === 'string' ? o.prefix : null;
            const ids = Array.from(this._rows.keys()).filter((id) => !prefix || id.indexOf(prefix) === 0).sort();
            const rows = {};
            for (const id of ids) rows[id] = this._rows.get(id);
            return { ok: true, ids, rows };
          }
          
          async transact(batch) {
            this._gate();
            if (!isBatch(batch)) throw new Error('invalid batch shape');
            const buf = new Map();
            for (const it of batch.changed || []) buf.set(it.id, it.data);
            const dels = (batch.deleted || []).slice();
            for (const [k, v] of buf) this._rows.set(k, v);
            for (const id of dels) this._rows.delete(id);
            return { ok: true, seq: batch.seq | 0, changed: (batch.changed || []).length, deleted: dels.length };
          }
          
          async saveState(snapshot) {
            this._gate();
            const nodes = (snapshot && snapshot.nodes) || {};
            const next = new Map();
            for (const id of Object.keys(nodes)) next.set(id, JSON.stringify(nodes[id]));
            this._rows = next;
            return { ok: true, rows: next.size };
          }
          rows() { return new Map(this._rows); }
        }
        
        
        class IdbStyleAdapter {
          constructor() { this.kind = 'idb'; this._core = new _CoreBackend(); }
          
          _request(fn) { return Promise.resolve().then(() => fn()); }
          write(entries) { return this._request(() => this._core.write(entries)); }
          read(ids) { return this._request(() => this._core.read(ids)); }
          del(ids) { return this._request(() => this._core.del(ids)); }
          query(opts) { return this._request(() => this._core.query(opts)); }
          transact(batch) { return this._request(() => this._core.transact(batch)); }
          saveState(snapshot) { return this._request(() => this._core.saveState(snapshot)); }
          rows() { return this._core.rows(); }
          setFailNext(n) { this._core.setFailNext(n); return this; }
          close() { this._core.close(); return this; }
        }
        
        
        class InvokeStyleAdapter {
          constructor() { this.kind = 'invoke'; this._core = new _CoreBackend(); this.calls = []; }
          
          _invoke(cmd, args) {
            this.calls.push({ cmd });
            const wire = args === undefined ? {} : JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(args));
            return Promise.resolve().then(() => this._dispatch(cmd, wire));
          }
          _dispatch(cmd, wire) {
            const c = this._core;
            switch (cmd) {
              case 'l9_write': return c.write(wire.entries);
              case 'l9_read': return c.read(wire.ids);
              case 'l9_delete': return c.del(wire.ids);
              case 'l9_query': return c.query(wire.opts);
              case 'l9_transact': return c.transact(wire.batch);
              case 'l9_save_state': return c.saveState(wire.snapshot);
              default: throw new Error('unknown command: ' + String(cmd));
            }
          }
          write(entries) { return this._invoke('l9_write', { entries }); }
          read(ids) { return this._invoke('l9_read', { ids }); }
          del(ids) { return this._invoke('l9_delete', { ids }); }
          query(opts) { return this._invoke('l9_query', { opts }); }
          transact(batch) { return this._invoke('l9_transact', { batch }); }
          saveState(snapshot) { return this._invoke('l9_save_state', { snapshot }); }
          rows() { return this._core.rows(); }
          setFailNext(n) { this._core.setFailNext(n); return this; }
          close() { this._core.close(); return this; }
        }
        
        
        function createAdapter(kind, opts) {
          if (kind === 'idb') return new IdbStyleAdapter(opts);
          if (kind === 'invoke') return new InvokeStyleAdapter(opts);
          throw new Error('unknown adapter kind: ' + String(kind));
        }
        
        
        
        
        
        
        function createSyncFallback(store) {
          return async function syncFallback(msg) {
            const type = msg && msg.type;
            try {
              if (type === REQ.PATCH) return await store.applyBatch(msg.payload);
              if (type === REQ.SAVE) return await store.flushNow(msg.payload && msg.payload.snapshot);
              if (type === REQ.LOAD) return { ok: true, snapshot: store.snapshot() };
              if (type === REQ.PING) return { ok: true, pong: true };
              if (type === REQ.DISPOSE) return { ok: true, disposed: true }; 
              return { ok: false, error: 'unknown-type:' + String(type) };
            } catch (e) {
              return { ok: false, error: String((e && e.message) || e) };
            }
          };
        }
        
        
        async function syncFallback(store, msg) { return createSyncFallback(store)(msg); }
        
        
        async function _handleInWorker(store, msg) {
          try {
            if (msg.type === REQ.PATCH) return makeAck(msg.id, await store.applyBatch(msg.payload));
            if (msg.type === REQ.SAVE) return makeAck(msg.id, await store.flushNow(msg.payload && msg.payload.snapshot));
            if (msg.type === REQ.LOAD) return makeAck(msg.id, { snapshot: store.snapshot() });
            if (msg.type === REQ.PING) return makeAck(msg.id, { pong: true });
            if (msg.type === REQ.DISPOSE) return makeAck(msg.id, { ok: true, disposed: true });
            return makeError(msg.id, new Error('unknown-type:' + String(msg.type)));
          } catch (e) {
            return makeError(msg.id, e);
          }
        }
        
        
        
        function createInProcessWorker(store) {
          let alive = true;
          const worker = {
            onmessage: null,
            
            terminate() { alive = false; },
            postMessage(msg) {
              if (!alive) return;
              setTimeout(() => {
                if (!alive) return;
                
                if (msg && msg.type === REQ.DISPOSE) {
                  if (typeof worker.onmessage === 'function') {
                    worker.onmessage({ data: makeAck(msg.id, { ok: true, disposed: true }) });
                  }
                  alive = false;
                  return;
                }
                _handleInWorker(store, msg).then((res) => {
                  if (alive && typeof worker.onmessage === 'function') worker.onmessage({ data: res });
                });
              }, 0);
            },
          };
          
          setTimeout(() => {
            if (alive && typeof worker.onmessage === 'function') worker.onmessage({ data: { type: RES.READY } });
          }, 0);
          return worker;
        }
        
        
        
        
        const L9_WORKER_SRC = `'use strict';
        
        const REQ = { PATCH: 'patch_nodes', SAVE: 'save_state', LOAD: 'load_state', PING: 'ping', DISPOSE: 'dispose' };
        const RES = { ACK: 'ack', RESULT: 'result', ERROR: 'error', READY: 'ready' };
        const rows = new Map();
        let lastSeq = 0;
        function ack(id, extra) {
          const o = { id: id, type: RES.ACK };
          if (extra) { for (const k in extra) o[k] = extra[k]; }
          return o;
        }
        function fail(id, e) { return { id: id, type: RES.ERROR, error: String((e && e.message) || e) }; }
        function applyPatch(id, payload) {
          const p = payload || {};
          const seq = (p.seq | 0);
          if (lastSeq > 0 && seq <= lastSeq) return ack(id, { ok: true, replay: true, seq: seq });
          const changed = p.changed || [];
          const deleted = p.deleted || [];
          for (const it of changed) rows.set(it.id, it.data);
          for (const de of deleted) rows.delete(de);
          lastSeq = Math.max(lastSeq, seq);
          return ack(id, { ok: true, seq: seq, changed: changed.length, deleted: deleted.length });
        }
        function saveState(id, snapshot) {
          const nodes = (snapshot && snapshot.nodes) || {};
          const next = new Map();
          for (const de of Object.keys(nodes)) next.set(de, JSON.stringify(nodes[de]));
          rows.clear();
          for (const entry of next) rows.set(entry[0], entry[1]);
          return ack(id, { ok: true, rows: next.size });
        }
        function loadState(id) {
          const snap = {};
          for (const entry of rows) { try { snap[entry[0]] = JSON.parse(entry[1]); } catch (e) {} }
          return ack(id, { ok: true, snapshot: snap });
        }
        let closed = false;
        function handle(msg) {
          const id = msg && msg.id;
          const type = msg && msg.type;
          try {
            if (closed) return fail(id, new Error('disposed'));
            if (type === REQ.PATCH) return applyPatch(id, msg.payload);
            if (type === REQ.SAVE) return saveState(id, msg.payload && msg.payload.snapshot);
            if (type === REQ.LOAD) return loadState(id);
            if (type === REQ.PING) return ack(id, { ok: true, pong: true });
            if (type === REQ.DISPOSE) return ack(id, { ok: true, disposed: true });
            return fail(id, new Error('unknown-type:' + String(type)));
          } catch (e) { return fail(id, e); }
        }
        self.onmessage = function (ev) {
          const msg = ev && ev.data;
          const res = handle(msg);
          self.postMessage(res);
          
          if (msg && msg.type === REQ.DISPOSE) { closed = true; self.close(); }
        };
        self.postMessage({ type: RES.READY });
        `;
        
        
        function isBrowserWorkerAvailable() {
          const hasApi = typeof Worker === 'function'
            && typeof Blob === 'function'
            && typeof URL !== 'undefined'
            && typeof URL.createObjectURL === 'function';
          const inBrowser = typeof window !== 'undefined';
          return hasApi && inBrowser;
        }
        
        
        function createBlobWorkerUrl() {
          if (!isBrowserWorkerAvailable()) return null;
          const blob = new Blob([L9_WORKER_SRC], { type: 'text/javascript' });
          return URL.createObjectURL(blob);
        }
        
        
        function createRealWorker() {
          if (!isBrowserWorkerAvailable()) return null;
          let url = null;
          try {
            url = createBlobWorkerUrl();
            
            return new Worker(url);
          } catch (e) {
            if (url && typeof URL.revokeObjectURL === 'function') { try { URL.revokeObjectURL(url); } catch (_e) {  } }
            return null;
          }
        }
        
        
        function createWorkerSkeleton(store) {
          const real = createRealWorker();
          if (real) return real;
          return createInProcessWorker(store);
        }
        
        
        class PersistWorkerBridge {
          constructor({ store, worker = null, mode = 'sync', timeoutMs = 5000 } = {}) {
            this.store = store;
            this.worker = worker || null;
            this.mode = (mode === 'worker' || mode === 'shadow') ? mode : 'sync';
            this.timeoutMs = timeoutMs;
            this.ready = false;
            this._id = 0;
            this._pending = new Map();
            
            this.disposed = false;       
            this._terminated = false;    
            this.unknownResponses = 0;   
          }
          
          async ensure() {
            if (this.worker) this.worker.onmessage = (ev) => this._onMessage(ev);
            return !!this.worker;
          }
          
          setMode(m) {
            this.mode = (m === 'worker' || m === 'shadow') ? m : 'sync';
            if (this.mode === 'sync') this.ready = false;
            return this.mode;
          }
          
          async request(type, payload) {
            if (this.disposed) throw new Error('bridge disposed');
            if (this.mode === 'sync' || !this.worker || typeof this.worker.postMessage !== 'function') {
              return createSyncFallback(this.store)({ type, payload });
            }
            const id = ++this._id;
            return new Promise((resolve) => {
              const fallback = () => { createSyncFallback(this.store)({ type, payload }).then(resolve); };
              const timer = setTimeout(() => { this._pending.delete(id); fallback(); }, this.timeoutMs);
              this._pending.set(id, { resolve, timer });
              try {
                this.worker.postMessage({ id, type, payload });
              } catch (e) {
                clearTimeout(timer);
                this._pending.delete(id);
                fallback();
              }
            });
          }
          
          _onMessage(ev) {
            const d = ev && ev.data;
            if (!d || typeof d !== 'object') return;
            if (d.type === RES.READY) { this.ready = true; return; }
            const p = this._pending.get(d.id);
            if (!p) { this.unknownResponses++; return; }
            clearTimeout(p.timer);
            this._pending.delete(d.id);
            p.resolve(d);
          }
          
          terminate() {
            if (this._terminated) return this;
            this._terminated = true;
            if (this.worker && typeof this.worker.terminate === 'function') {
              try { this.worker.terminate(); } catch (e) {  }
            }
            if (this.worker) this.worker.onmessage = null;   
            for (const p of this._pending.values()) clearTimeout(p.timer);
            this._pending.clear();
            this.ready = false;
            return this;
          }
          
          async dispose() {
            if (this.disposed) return { ok: true, disposed: true, already: true };
            this.disposed = true;
            let ack = null;
            if (this.worker && !this._terminated && typeof this.worker.postMessage === 'function') {
              const id = ++this._id;
              ack = await new Promise((resolve) => {
                const timer = setTimeout(() => {
                  this._pending.delete(id);
                  resolve({ ok: false, error: 'dispose-timeout' });
                }, this.timeoutMs);
                this._pending.set(id, { resolve, timer });
                try {
                  this.worker.postMessage({ id, type: REQ.DISPOSE, payload: null });
                } catch (e) {
                  clearTimeout(timer);
                  this._pending.delete(id);
                  resolve({ ok: false, error: String((e && e.message) || e) });
                }
              });
            }
            this.terminate();   
            return ack || { ok: true, disposed: true };
          }
        }
        
        
        var L9_PILLARS_MODULES = Object.freeze({
          persistStore: Object.freeze({ L9_MAX_PER_BATCH, L9_PATCH_MAX, L9_FAIL_CAP, L9_CHECKPOINT_EVERY, L9_METRIC_CAP, percentile95, isBatch, buildPersistBatch, equalProjection, InMemoryBackend, PersistStore }),
          cryptoStore: Object.freeze({ ENC_ITER, SALT_BYTES, IV_BYTES, newSalt, deriveKey, encryptState, decryptState, encryptBatch, decryptBatch, containsPlaintext, KeyStore }),

        const SENTINEL_KEY = '__juL9ShadowSentinel';
        const NS_KEY = '__juL9Shadow';
        const SENTINEL_ID = 'l9-shadow/v1';
        
        
        const HOST_MARKERS = Object.freeze(['state', '__juPersistProbe', '__juIndexWorker']);
        
        
        const PILLARS = Object.freeze({
          persistStore: './persist-store.mjs',

          workerProtocol: './worker-protocol.mjs',

        let _pillarUrls = null;
        
        function pillarUrls() {
          if (_pillarUrls) return _pillarUrls;
          const out = {};
          for (const [k, spec] of Object.entries(PILLARS)) out[k] = new URL(spec, L9_BASE_URL).href;
          _pillarUrls = Object.freeze(out);
          return _pillarUrls;
        }
        
        let _baseDir;
        let _baseResolved = false;
        
        async function pillarBaseDir() {
          if (_baseResolved) return _baseDir;
          
          _baseDir = null;
          _baseResolved = true;
          return _baseDir;
        }
        
        
        async function loadPillars() {
          
          return L9_PILLARS_MODULES;

        function isProductHost(host) {
          if (!host || (typeof host !== 'object' && typeof host !== 'function')) return false;
          for (const m of HOST_MARKERS) {
            try { if (m in host) return true; } catch {  }
          }
          return false;
        }
        
        
        function hasSentinel(host = globalThis) {
          try {
            const s = host && host[SENTINEL_KEY];
            return !!(s && s.id === SENTINEL_ID);
          } catch {
            return false;
          }
        }
        
        
        function shadowStatus(host = globalThis) {
          let s = null;
          try { s = host ? host[SENTINEL_KEY] : null; } catch { s = null; }
          if (!s || s.id !== SENTINEL_ID) {
            return Object.freeze({ attached: false, sentinel: false, id: SENTINEL_ID });
          }
          return Object.freeze({
            attached: true,
            sentinel: true,
            id: s.id,
            version: s.version,
            attachedAt: s.attachedAt,
            attachCount: s.attachCount,
            namespace: s.namespace,
            pillars: Array.isArray(s.pillars) ? s.pillars.slice() : [],
            paths: s.paths,
            pillarBaseDir: s.pillarBaseDir,
          });
        }
        
        const _result = (o) => Object.freeze(Object.assign({ attached: false, sentinel: false, id: SENTINEL_ID }, o));
        
        
        async function attachShadow(host = globalThis, opts = {}) {
          
          if (!isProductHost(host)) return _result({ reason: 'no-product' });
        
          
          if (hasSentinel(host)) {
            return _result({ attached: true, sentinel: true, reason: 'already-attached', attachCount: host[SENTINEL_KEY].attachCount });
          }
        
          
          const loader = typeof opts.loader === 'function' ? opts.loader : loadPillars;
          let pillars;
          try {
            pillars = await loader();
          } catch (e) {
            return _result({ reason: 'no-l9', error: String((e && e.message) || e) });
          }
        
          const paths = opts.paths || pillarUrls();
          const baseDir = await pillarBaseDir();
        
          
          try {
            let ns = host[NS_KEY];
            if (!ns || typeof ns !== 'object') {
              ns = {};
              Object.defineProperty(host, NS_KEY, { value: ns, writable: true, enumerable: false, configurable: true });
            }
            ns.id = SENTINEL_ID;
            ns.version = SENTINEL_ID;
            ns.modules = pillars;
            ns.paths = paths;
        
            const sentinel = {
              id: SENTINEL_ID,
              version: SENTINEL_ID,
              attachedAt: Date.now(),
              attachCount: 1,
              namespace: NS_KEY,
              pillars: Object.keys(pillars),
              paths,
              pillarBaseDir: baseDir,
            };
            Object.defineProperty(host, SENTINEL_KEY, { value: sentinel, writable: true, enumerable: false, configurable: true });
            return _result({ attached: true, sentinel: true, reason: 'attached', attachCount: 1 });
          } catch (e) {
            
            try { delete host[SENTINEL_KEY]; } catch {  }
            try { delete host[NS_KEY]; } catch {  }
            return _result({ reason: 'install-failed', error: String((e && e.message) || e) });
          }
        }
        
        
        function detachShadow(host = globalThis) {
          if (!host || (typeof host !== 'object' && typeof host !== 'function')) return false;
          let present = false;
          try { present = !!(host[SENTINEL_KEY] || host[NS_KEY]); } catch { return false; }
          try { delete host[SENTINEL_KEY]; } catch {  }
          try { delete host[NS_KEY]; } catch {  }
          return present;
        }
        
        
        Promise.resolve()
          .then(function () { return attachShadow(globalThis); })
          .then(function (r) { globalThis.__juL9ShadowAttachResult = r; })
          .catch(function () {  });
        })();

        (function () {

          var tries = 0;

            var mods = null;

              var ns = globalThis.__juL9Shadow;
              mods = ns && ns.modules;

            if (mods && mods.persistStore && mods.backendAdapters) { install(mods); return; }
            
            if (tries++ < 400) setTimeout(waitShadow, 25);
          }
        
          function install(mods) {
            try {

              var ps = mods.persistStore;
              var ba = mods.backendAdapters;

              function migrateLegacy(raw) {
                return Promise.resolve().then(function () {
                  var obj = raw;
                  if (typeof raw === 'string') {
                    try { obj = JSON.parse(raw); } catch (e) { return { ok: false, error: 'parse' }; }
                  }
                  var nodes = (obj && obj.nodes) || {};
                  var have = backend.rows();
                  var ids = Object.keys(nodes);
                  var entries = [];
                  var migrated = 0;
                  var already = 0;
                  for (var i = 0; i < ids.length; i++) {
                    var id = ids[i];
                    var json = JSON.stringify(nodes[id]);
                    if (have.has(id) && have.get(id) === json) { already++; continue; }
                    entries.push({ id: id, data: json });
                    migrated++;
                  }
                  return Promise.resolve(backend.write(entries)).then(function () {
                    return { ok: true, migrated: migrated, already: already, ids: ids };
                  });
                });
              }
        
              var api = {
                version: VERSION,
                flag: FLAG,
                backend: backend,
                store: store,
                
                applyBatch: function (batch) { return store.applyBatch(batch); },
                flushNow: function (snapshot) { return store.flushNow(snapshot); },
                projection: function () { return store.projection(); },
                
                buildPersistBatch: ps.buildPersistBatch,
                migrateLegacy: migrateLegacy,
              };
              Object.defineProperty(globalThis, KEY, {
                value: api, writable: true, enumerable: false, configurable: true,
              });
            } catch (e) {  }
          }
        
          waitShadow();
        })();
